local function Notify(title, content, delay)
    Chloex:MakeNotify({
        Title = title or "BOLONG-HUB",
        Description = "Info",
        Content = content or "",
        Color = ACCENT_COLOR,
        Time = 0.4,
        Delay = delay or 2,
    })
end

-- ============================================================
--  ITEM ESP DATA

local ITEM_IMAGES = {
    ["Adrenaline Shot"] = "rbxassetid://135388781922226",
    ["Bandage"] = "rbxassetid://97791520639443",
    ["Flashlight"] = "rbxassetid://103299939715311",
    ["Gate"] = "rbxassetid://131249244284700",
    ["Holy Water"] = "rbxassetid://86130208614143",
    ["Motion Tracker"] = "rbxassetid://92303584765773",
    ["Parrying Dagger"] = "rbxassetid://76822757630703",
    ["Riot Shield"] = "rbxassetid://95718705901699",
    ["Shadow Clone"] = "rbxassetid://134088840518889",
    ["Twist of Fate"] = "rbxassetid://98397448432071",
    ["WaxBound Candle"] = "rbxassetid://110413686590821",
}

local function GetItemImage(itemName)
    if not itemName or type(itemName) ~= "string" then return nil end
    itemName = itemName:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
    
    local img = ITEM_IMAGES[itemName]
    if img then return img end
    
    for name, id in pairs(ITEM_IMAGES) do
        if name:lower() == itemName:lower() then
            return id
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local BASE_SPEED = 17
local _FAKE_NAME_POOL = { "BOLONGHUB" }

local espFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
espFolder.Name = "__BolongESP__"
espFolder.Parent = workspace

local function GetEspFolder()
    if espFolder and espFolder.Parent then return espFolder end
    espFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
    espFolder.Name = "__BolongESP__"
    espFolder.Parent = workspace
    return espFolder
end

-- ============================================================
--  MASTER HEARTBEAT LOOP
-- ============================================================
local MasterTasks = {}

local function RegisterTask(name, interval, fn)
    MasterTasks[#MasterTasks + 1] = { name=name, interval=interval, timer=0, fn=fn }
end

-- ============================================================
--  KILLER NO COOLDOWN SYSTEM (Hidden, Abysswalker, Masked) 
-- ============================================================
local killerNoCooldownEnabled = false
local originalTaskDelay
local _bypassDelays = { [25] = true, [17] = true, [30] = true, [67.8] = true, [2] = true, [4] = true, [0.25] = true, [70] = true, [0.65] = true }
local _bypassDelaysMayers = { [1] = true, [10] = true }

local _activeKillerKey, _activeKillerT = nil, 0
local function GetActiveKillerKey()
    local now = tick()
    if now - _activeKillerT < 2 and _activeKillerKey ~= nil then
        return _activeKillerKey
    end
    local key = nil
    pcall(function()
        local attr = localPlayer:GetAttribute("SelectedKiller")
        local name = tostring(attr or ""):lower()
        if name:find("hidden") then
            key = "hidden"
        elseif name:find("mayers") or name:find("stalker") or name:find("michael") then
            key = "mayers"
        end
        if not key then
            local rs = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
            local killRemotes = rs:FindFirstChild("Remotes") and rs.Remotes:FindFirstChild("Killers")
            if killRemotes then
                if killRemotes:FindFirstChild("Stalker") then key = "mayers"
                elseif killRemotes:FindFirstChild("Hidden") then key = "hidden"
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    _activeKillerKey, _activeKillerT = key, now
    return key
end

local function SetupKillerNoCooldownHook()
    if originalTaskDelay then return end
    if not hookfunction then
        Notify("No Cooldown", "Executor tidak support hookfunction", 2)
        return
    end
    local ok, err = pcall(function()
        local hookFn = newcclosure or function(f) return f end
        originalTaskDelay = hookfunction(task.delay, hookFn(function(delayTime, callback)
            if not originalTaskDelay then
                return task.delay and task.delay(0, callback) or task.spawn(callback)
            end
            if not killerNoCooldownEnabled then
                return originalTaskDelay(delayTime, callback)
            end
            if _bypassDelays[delayTime] then
                return originalTaskDelay(0, callback)
            end
            if _bypassDelaysMayers[delayTime] and GetActiveKillerKey() == "mayers" then
                return originalTaskDelay(0, callback)
            end
            return originalTaskDelay(delayTime, callback)
        end))
    end)
    if not ok then
        Notify("No Cooldown", "Gagal hook task.delay: " .. tostring(err), 2)
        originalTaskDelay = nil
    end
end

local function RemoveKillerNoCooldownHook()
    if originalTaskDelay then
        if restorefunction then
            pcall(restorefunction, task.delay)
        end
        originalTaskDelay = nil
    end
end

-- ============================================================
--  ROLE MANAGEMENT
-- ============================================================
local function getRole(player)
    return STATE.playerRoles[player] or "survivor"
end

local function refreshRole(player)
    local ok, teamName = pcall(function() return player.Team and player.Team.Name:lower() or "" end)
    STATE.playerRoles[player] = (ok and teamName:find("killer")) and "killer" or "survivor"
end

-- ============================================================
--  MAYERS (STALKER) NO COOLDOWN SYSTEM
-- ============================================================
local FireMayersDashNoCooldown, GetNearestSurvivorForMayers, StartMayersMobileHook, StopMayersMobileHook
do
        function GetNearestSurvivorForMayers(maxDist)
            maxDist = maxDist or 5
            local char = localPlayer.Character
            local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not hrp then return nil, math.huge end
            local best, bestD = nil, maxDist + 0.01
            for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if p ~= localPlayer and p.Character then
                    local th = p.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local hum = p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if th and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                        local d = (th.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                        if d < bestD then best, bestD = p.Character, d end
                    end
                end
            end
            return best, bestD
        end

        function FireMayersDashNoCooldown()
            if not (STATE.mayersNoCooldownEnabled or killerNoCooldownEnabled) then return end
            if GetActiveKillerKey() ~= "mayers" then return end
            if STATE.mayersDashLock then return end
            local char = localPlayer.Character
            if not char then return end
            local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not (hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0) then return end
            STATE.mayersDashLock = true
            task.delay(0.35, function() STATE.mayersDashLock = false end)
            local dashTrack = nil
            pcall(function()
                local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
                anim.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://77477445889320"
                local aHum = hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator") or hum
                dashTrack = aHum:LoadAnimation(anim)
                dashTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Action
                dashTrack:Play()
            end)
            local rs = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
            pcall(function() rs.Remotes.SoundPlayer:FireServer("132736711620405", hrp, 0.3, 70) end)
            pcall(function() rs.Remotes.Attacks.BasicAttack:FireServer(true) end)
            pcall(function()
                local ev = rs:FindFirstChild("Remotes") and rs.Remotes:FindFirstChild("Killers") and rs.Remotes.Killers:FindFirstChild("Stalker") and rs.Remotes.Killers.Stalker:FindFirstChild("ConsumeReady")
                if ev then ev:FireServer() end
            end)
            task.spawn(function()
                local t0 = tick()
                local found = false
                while tick() - t0 < 2 and not found do
                    RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
                    local target, dist = GetNearestSurvivorForMayers(5)
                    if target and dist <= 5 then
                        pcall(function()
                            local grabEv = rs.Remotes.Killers.Stalker:FindFirstChild("grab")
                            if grabEv then grabEv:FireServer(target) end
                        end)
                        if dashTrack then pcall(function() dashTrack:Stop(0.1) end) end
                        found = true
                        break
                    end
                end
                if dashTrack then pcall(function() dashTrack:Stop(0.1) end) end
            end)
        end

        RegisterTask("MayersStalkWhileMoving", 0.2, function()
            if not STATE.mayersStalkWhileMovingEnabled then return end
            pcall(function()
                local rs = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
                local stalkerFolder = rs:FindFirstChild("Remotes") and rs.Remotes:FindFirstChild("Killers") and rs.Remotes.Killers:FindFirstChild("Stalker")
                local ev = stalkerFolder and stalkerFolder:FindFirstChild("StartStalking")
                if not ev then return end
                for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if p ~= localPlayer and p.Character then
                        local hum = p.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        if hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                            pcall(function() ev:FireServer(p) end)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end)

            STATE.mayersQConn = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gp)
                if gp then return end
                if input.KeyCode ~= Enum.KeyCode.Q then return end
                if not (STATE.mayersNoCooldownEnabled or killerNoCooldownEnabled) then return end
                pcall(FireMayersDashNoCooldown)
            end)

        local function Mayers_IsMove2Button(inst)
            if not (inst and inst:IsA("GuiButton")) then return false end
            if inst.Name ~= "move2" then return false end
            local parent = inst.Parent
            if not (parent and parent.Name == "Controls") then return false end
            local gp = parent.Parent
            if not (gp and gp.Name == "Slasher-mob") then return false end
            return true
        end

        local function Mayers_HookMove2Button(btn)
            if not STATE.mayersNoCooldownEnabled then return end
            if GetActiveKillerKey() ~= "mayers" then return end
            if not Mayers_IsMove2Button(btn) then return end
            if STATE.mayersHookedButtons[btn] then return end
            STATE.mayersHookedButtons[btn] = true
            pcall(function() btn.Active = true end)
            local function doDash()
                if not STATE.mayersNoCooldownEnabled then return end
                pcall(FireMayersDashNoCooldown)
            end
            local c1 = btn.Activated:Connect(doDash)
            table.insert(STATE.mayersMove2Conns, c1)
        end

        local function Mayers_ScanMove2Buttons()
            if not STATE.mayersNoCooldownEnabled then return end
            if GetActiveKillerKey() ~= "mayers" then return end
            local pg = localPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") or PlayerGui
            if not pg then return end
            pcall(function()
                local slasher = pg:FindFirstChild("Slasher-mob")
                if slasher then
                    local controls = slasher:FindFirstChild("Controls")
                    if controls then
                        local move2 = controls:FindFirstChild("move2")
                        if move2 then Mayers_HookMove2Button(move2) end
                        for _, child in ipairs(controls:GetDescendants()) do
                            if child.Name == "move2" then Mayers_HookMove2Button(child) end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            if STATE.mayersGuiWatchConn then pcall(function() STATE.mayersGuiWatchConn:Disconnect() end) end
            STATE.mayersGuiWatchConn = pg.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                if child.Name == "Slasher-mob" then
                    task.wait(0.5)
                    Mayers_ScanMove2Buttons()
                    if STATE.mayersMove2WatchConn then pcall(function() STATE.mayersMove2WatchConn:Disconnect() end) end
                    STATE.mayersMove2WatchConn = child.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(desc)
                        if desc.Name == "move2" then Mayers_HookMove2Button(desc) end
                    end)
                    table.insert(STATE.mayersMove2Conns, STATE.mayersMove2WatchConn)
                end
            end)
            table.insert(STATE.mayersMove2Conns, STATE.mayersGuiWatchConn)
            pcall(function()
                local slasher = pg:FindFirstChild("Slasher-mob")
                if slasher and not STATE.mayersMove2WatchConn then
                    STATE.mayersMove2WatchConn = slasher.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(desc)
                        if desc.Name == "move2" then Mayers_HookMove2Button(desc) end
                    end)
                    table.insert(STATE.mayersMove2Conns, STATE.mayersMove2WatchConn)
                end
            end)
        end

        function StartMayersMobileHook()
            Mayers_ScanMove2Buttons()
        end

        function StopMayersMobileHook()
            for _, conn in ipairs(STATE.mayersMove2Conns) do pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) end
            STATE.mayersMove2Conns = {}
            STATE.mayersHookedButtons = {}
            if STATE.mayersGuiWatchConn then pcall(function() STATE.mayersGuiWatchConn:Disconnect() end) STATE.mayersGuiWatchConn = nil end
            if STATE.mayersMove2WatchConn then pcall(function() STATE.mayersMove2WatchConn:Disconnect() end) STATE.mayersMove2WatchConn = nil end
        end
end

-- ============================================================
--  INFINITE LUNGE (Killer)
-- ============================================================
local function RemoveInfiniteLunge()
    if STATE.infiniteLungeConn then
        STATE.infiniteLungeConn:Disconnect()
        STATE.infiniteLungeConn = nil
    end

    if STATE.origLungeBoost ~= nil then
        local char = localPlayer.Character
        if char then
            pcall(function() char:SetAttribute("lungeboost", STATE.origLungeBoost) end)
        end
        STATE.origLungeBoost = nil
    end

    if STATE.lungeDetectConns then
        for i = 1, #STATE.lungeDetectConns do
            local conn = STATE.lungeDetectConns[i]
            if conn then
                pcall(function() conn:Enable() end)
            end
        end
        STATE.lungeDetectConns = nil
    end
end

local function SetupInfiniteLunge()
    if getRole(localPlayer) ~= "killer" then return end
    if STATE.infiniteLungeConn then return end

    local char = localPlayer.Character

    if char then
        pcall(function()
            STATE.origLungeBoost = char:GetAttribute("lungeboost")
            char:SetAttribute("lungeboost", 999999)
        end)
    end

    pcall(function()
        local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        local attacks = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Attacks")
        local detect = attacks and attacks:FindFirstChild("LungeDetect")
        if detect and detect:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
            local conns = getconnections(detect.OnClientEvent)
            local saved = {}
            for i = 1, #conns do
                local conn = conns[i]
                pcall(function()
                    conn:Disable()
                    saved[#saved + 1] = conn
                end)
            end
            STATE.lungeDetectConns = saved
        end
    end)

    STATE.infiniteLungeConn = localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
        if not CONFIG.infiniteLungeEnabled then return end
        pcall(function()
            STATE.origLungeBoost = newChar:GetAttribute("lungeboost")
            newChar:SetAttribute("lungeboost", 999999)
        end)
    end)
end

-- ============================================================
--  ANTI BLIND SYSTEM
-- ============================================================
local ToggleAntiAfk
do
    local _blindGuiCache = {}
    local function checkBlindGui(gui)
        if gui.Name == "Blind" then
            _blindGuiCache[gui] = true
            if gui:IsA("ScreenGui") then
                gui.Enabled = false
            elseif gui:IsA("GuiObject") then
                gui.Visible = false
                gui.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            end
        end
    end

    local _antiBlindHooked = false
    local function SetupAntiBlindHook()
        if _antiBlindHooked then return end
        local ok = pcall(function()
            assert(typeof(hookmetamethod) == "function", "hookmetamethod missing")
            assert(typeof(getnamecallmethod) == "function", "getnamecallmethod missing")
            assert(typeof(checkcaller) == "function", "checkcaller missing")
            local r  = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
            local i  = r and r:FindFirstChild("Items")
            local fl = i and i:FindFirstChild("Flashlight")
            local gb = fl and fl:FindFirstChild("GotBlinded")
            if not (gb and gb:IsA("RemoteEvent")) then return end

            local oldNamecall
            oldNamecall = hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", function(self, ...)
                if not checkcaller()
                    and CONFIG.antiBlindEnabled
                    and rawequal(self, gb)
                    and getnamecallmethod() == "FireServer"
                    and getRole(localPlayer) == "killer"
                then
                    return
                end
                return oldNamecall(self, ...)
            end)
            _antiBlindHooked = true
        end)
        return ok
    end
    getgenv().SetupAntiBlindHook = SetupAntiBlindHook

    PlayerGui.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(desc)
        if CONFIG.antiBlindEnabled then checkBlindGui(desc) end
    end)

    RegisterTask("AntiBlindForce", 1.0, function()
        if not CONFIG.antiBlindEnabled then return end
        SetupAntiBlindHook()
        for gui, _ in pairs(_blindGuiCache) do
            if not gui.Parent then
                _blindGuiCache[gui] = nil
            else
                checkBlindGui(gui)
            end
        end
    end)

    -- ============================================================
    --  ANTI AFK SYSTEM
    -- ============================================================
    local antiAfkConn
    function ToggleAntiAfk(state)
        if state then
            if not antiAfkConn then
                antiAfkConn = localPlayer.Idled:Connect(function()
                    VirtualUser:CaptureController()
                    VirtualUser:ClickButton2(Vector2.new())
                end)
            end
        else
            if antiAfkConn then
                antiAfkConn:Disconnect()
                antiAfkConn = nil
            end
        end
    end
end

-- ============================================================
--  SHARED SPEAR VELOCITY ESTIMATOR
-- ============================================================
local Spear_EstVel, Spear_PingSec
do
    local Spear_PingObj = nil
    function Spear_PingSec()
        if Spear_PingObj == nil then
            pcall(function()
                Spear_PingObj = game:GetService("Stats").Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]
            end)
        end
        local v = 0
        if Spear_PingObj ~= nil then
            pcall(function() v = Spear_PingObj:GetValue() end)
        end
        v = tonumber(v) or 0
        return math.clamp(v / 1000, 0, 1)
    end
    function Spear_EstVel(st, char, posNow)
        local now = tick()
        if st.char ~= char then
            st.char = char
            st.hist = {}
            st.sm = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            st.turn = 0
        end
        if st.turn == nil then st.turn = 0 end
        local hist = st.hist
        table.insert(hist, { pos = posNow, t = now })
        while #hist > 1 and hist[1].t < now - 0.4 do table.remove(hist, 1) end
        if #hist >= 2 then
            local a = hist[#hist - 1]
            local b = hist[#hist]
            local dt = b.t - a.t
            if dt > 0.001 then
                local dp = (b.pos - a.pos) / dt
                local inst = Vector3.new(dp.X, 0, dp.Z)
                local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if root and root:IsA("BasePart") then
                    local ok, av = pcall(function() return root.AssemblyLinearVelocity end)
                    if ok and typeof(av) == "Vector3" then
                        local ha = Vector3.new(av.X, 0, av.Z)
                        if ha.Magnitude > 0.5 and ha.Magnitude < 100 then
                            inst = inst:Lerp(ha, 0.5)
                        end
                    end
                end
                if inst.Magnitude > 24 then inst = inst.Unit * 24 end
                local turnInst = 0
                if st.sm.Magnitude > 2 and inst.Magnitude > 2 then
                    local d = st.sm:Dot(inst) / (st.sm.Magnitude * inst.Magnitude)
                    if CONFIG.spearDamping and d < 0 then inst = inst * 0.7 end
                    turnInst = math.clamp(1 - d, 0, 1)
                end
                st.turn = st.turn * 0.7 + turnInst * 0.3
                st.sm = st.sm:Lerp(inst, 0.45)
            end
        end
        local hvel = Vector3.new(st.sm.X, 0, st.sm.Z)
        if hvel.Magnitude > 24 then hvel = hvel.Unit * 24 end
        st.sm = hvel
        local stab = 1 - math.clamp(st.turn, 0, 1)
        return hvel, stab
    end
end

-- ============================================================
--  CAMERA VEIL SYSTEM
-- ============================================================
local StartCameraVeil, StopCameraVeil, CV_ScanAttackButtons, CV_ClearLock
do
    local CVVelSt = { hist = {}, char = nil, sm = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0) }
    local function GetSafeGuiParent()
        local ok, parent = pcall(function() if gethui then return gethui() end return game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
        return (ok and parent) or PlayerGui
    end
    local function CV_GetAimPos(char)
        if not char then return nil end
        local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if head then return head.Position end
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if root then return root.Position end
        local ut = char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
        if ut then return ut.Position end
        local t = char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
        if t then return t.Position end
        return char.PrimaryPart and char.PrimaryPart.Position or nil
    end
    local function CV_GetTargetVelocity(rootPart)
        if not rootPart then return Vector3.new(0, 0, 0) end
        local vel = rootPart.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local horizontal = Vector3.new(vel.X, 0, vel.Z)
        if horizontal.Magnitude > 65 then horizontal = horizontal.Unit * 65 end
        return horizontal
    end
    local function CV_GetLaunchPosition()
        local char = localPlayer.Character
        local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not root then return nil end
        local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        local lookVector = (cam and cam.CFrame.LookVector) or root.CFrame.LookVector
        return root.Position + lookVector * 3 + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0)
    end
    local function CV_GetEffectiveSpearSpeed(distance)
        local speed = tonumber(STATE.CV_LastSpearSpeed) or tonumber(CONFIG.cameraVeilSpearSpeed) or 220
        return math.clamp(speed, 35, 800)
    end
    local function CV_EstimateProjectileHeightAtDistance(horizontalDist, angle, speed, gravity)
        local cosA = math.cos(angle)
        if cosA <= 0.015 then return nil, nil end
        local travelTime = horizontalDist / (speed * cosA)
        if travelTime ~= travelTime or travelTime <= 0 then return nil, nil end
        local frameDt = 1 / 60
        local discreteDropFix = 0.5 * gravity * frameDt * travelTime
        local height = speed * math.sin(angle) * travelTime - (0.5 * gravity * travelTime * travelTime) - discreteDropFix
        return height, travelTime
    end
    local function CV_ScorePitch(horizontalDist, heightDiff, angle, speed, gravity)
        local predictedHeight, travelTime = CV_EstimateProjectileHeightAtDistance(horizontalDist, angle, speed, gravity)
        if not predictedHeight or not travelTime then return nil end
        if travelTime < 0.025 or travelTime > 4 then return nil end
        local heightError = math.abs(predictedHeight - heightDiff)
        local highArcPenalty = math.max(angle - RAD_42, 0) * 0.35
        local timePenalty = math.max(travelTime - 1.3, 0) * 0.25
        return heightError + highArcPenalty + timePenalty, heightError, travelTime
    end
    local function CV_SolvePitchByDistanceHeight(launchPos, targetPos, speed, gravity)
        local delta = targetPos - launchPos
        local horizontal = Vector3.new(delta.X, 0, delta.Z)
        local horizontalDist = horizontal.Magnitude
        local heightDiff = delta.Y
        if delta.Magnitude <= 0.001 then return nil end
        if horizontalDist <= 0.35 or gravity <= 0.001 then return delta.Unit, math.clamp(delta.Magnitude / speed, 0.025, 4) end
        local horizontalDir = horizontal.Unit
        local lineAngle = math.atan2(heightDiff, horizontalDist)
        local minAngle = math.max(-RAD_28, lineAngle - RAD_18)
        local maxAngle = RAD_72
        local bestAngle, bestScore, bestError, bestTime = nil, math.huge, math.huge, nil
        local farShot = horizontalDist > 80
        local samples = farShot and 40 or 24
        local goodEnough = farShot and 0.08 or GOOD_ENOUGH_ERROR
        for i = 0, samples do
            local angle = minAngle + (maxAngle - minAngle) * (i / samples)
            local score, heightError, travelTime = CV_ScorePitch(horizontalDist, heightDiff, angle, speed, gravity)
            if score and score < bestScore then
                bestScore, bestError, bestAngle, bestTime = score, heightError, angle, travelTime
                if bestError < goodEnough then break end
            end
        end
        if bestAngle then
            local range = (maxAngle - minAngle) / samples * 2.5
            local maxRefineIters = (bestError < goodEnough) and 1 or (farShot and 5 or 3)
            for _ = 1, maxRefineIters do
                local localBestAngle, localBestScore, localBestError, localBestTime = bestAngle, bestScore, bestError, bestTime
                for j = -3, 3 do
                    local angle = math.clamp(bestAngle + range * (j / 3), minAngle, maxAngle)
                    local score, heightError, travelTime = CV_ScorePitch(horizontalDist, heightDiff, angle, speed, gravity)
                    if score and score < localBestScore then
                        localBestScore, localBestError, localBestAngle, localBestTime = score, heightError, angle, travelTime
                    end
                end
                bestAngle, bestScore, bestError, bestTime = localBestAngle, localBestScore, localBestError, localBestTime
                if bestError < goodEnough then break end
                range = range * 0.38
            end
        end
        if not bestAngle then
            local directTime = math.clamp(horizontalDist / speed, 0.025, 4)
            local lift = math.min(0.5 * gravity * directTime * directTime, 15)
            local aimPos = targetPos + Vector3.new(0, lift, 0)
            local fallback = aimPos - launchPos
            if fallback.Magnitude <= 0.001 then return nil end
            return fallback.Unit, directTime
        end
        local dir = horizontalDir * math.cos(bestAngle) + Vector3.new(0, math.sin(bestAngle), 0)
        if dir.Magnitude <= 0.001 then return nil end
        return dir.Unit, bestTime
    end
    local function CV_SolveBallisticDirection(targetPos)
        local launchPos = CV_GetLaunchPosition()
        if not launchPos or not targetPos then return nil end
        local speed = CV_GetEffectiveSpearSpeed((targetPos - launchPos).Magnitude)
        local gMult = math.max(tonumber(STATE.CV_LastGravityMult) or tonumber(CONFIG.cameraVeilGravityMult) or 1, 0)
        local gravity = _cachedGravity * gMult
        return CV_SolvePitchByDistanceHeight(launchPos, targetPos, speed, gravity)
    end
    local function CV_GetBallisticAimDirection(headPos, targetVelocity)
        if not headPos then return nil end
        local launchPos = CV_GetLaunchPosition()
        if launchPos then
            local directDelta = headPos - launchPos
            local directDist = directDelta.Magnitude
            if directDist < 6 then
                if directDist < 0.001 then return nil end
                local spd = tonumber(STATE.CV_LastSpearSpeed) or tonumber(CONFIG.cameraVeilSpearSpeed) or 220
                return directDelta.Unit, headPos, math.clamp(directDist / math.max(spd, 1), 0.01, 1)
            end
        end
        local fc = STATE.CV_frameCount
        local framesSinceLastSolve = fc - STATE.CV_ballisticFrame
        local movedLittle = STATE.CV_lastSolvedHeadPos and (headPos - STATE.CV_lastSolvedHeadPos).Magnitude < POSITION_SKIP_THRESHOLD
        if framesSinceLastSolve < BALLISTIC_THROTTLE and movedLittle and STATE.CV_lastSolvedDir then
            return STATE.CV_lastSolvedDir, STATE.CV_lastSolvedPredicted, STATE.CV_lastSolvedTime
        end
        local predictedPos = headPos
        local aimDir, travelTime = CV_SolveBallisticDirection(predictedPos)
        if aimDir and travelTime and targetVelocity and targetVelocity.Magnitude > 1.25 then
            local leadFactor = 0.68 + 0.32 * math.clamp(travelTime / 0.6, 0, 1)
            local shotDist = (launchPos and headPos) and (headPos - launchPos).Magnitude or 0
            local maxLead = math.max(shotDist * 0.4, 4)
            local pingExtra = Spear_PingSec() * (tonumber(CONFIG.spearPingLead) or 1.0)
            local leadTime = math.clamp(travelTime * leadFactor, 0, 1.1)
            local lead = targetVelocity * ((leadTime + pingExtra) * leadScale)
            if lead.Magnitude > 32 then lead = lead.Unit * 32 end
            if lead.Magnitude > maxLead then lead = lead.Unit * maxLead end
            local candidate = headPos + lead
            local newDir, newTime = CV_SolveBallisticDirection(candidate)
            if newDir and newTime then aimDir, travelTime, predictedPos = newDir, newTime, candidate end
            if newDir and newTime and newTime > 0.35 then
                local leadTime2 = math.clamp(newTime * leadFactor, 0, 1.1)
                local lead2 = targetVelocity * ((leadTime2 + pingExtra) * leadScale)
                if lead2.Magnitude > 32 then lead2 = lead2.Unit * 32 end
                if lead2.Magnitude > maxLead then lead2 = lead2.Unit * maxLead end
                local candidate2 = headPos + lead2
                local newDir2, newTime2 = CV_SolveBallisticDirection(candidate2)
                if newDir2 and newTime2 then aimDir, travelTime, predictedPos = newDir2, newTime2, candidate2 end
            end
        end
        STATE.CV_ballisticFrame = fc
        STATE.CV_lastSolvedHeadPos = headPos
        STATE.CV_lastSolvedDir = aimDir
        STATE.CV_lastSolvedPredicted = predictedPos
        STATE.CV_lastSolvedTime = travelTime
        return aimDir, predictedPos, travelTime
    end
    local CV_IsSpearMode
    local function CV_GetClosestTarget()
        local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not cam then return nil end
        local camPos, camDir = cam.CFrame.Position, cam.CFrame.LookVector
        local bestPlayer, bestAngle = nil, RAD_30
        local myChar = localPlayer.Character
        local myRoot = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not myRoot then return nil end
        for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if player ~= localPlayer then
                local role = getRole(player)
                if role == "survivor" then
                    local char = player.Character
                    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if char and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                        local headPos = CV_GetAimPos(char)
                        if headPos then
                            local dist = (headPos - myRoot.Position).Magnitude
                            if dist <= CONFIG.cameraVeilMaxDistance then
                                local toTarget = headPos - camPos
                                if toTarget.Magnitude > 0.001 then
                                    local angle = math.acos(math.clamp(camDir:Dot(toTarget.Unit), -1, 1))
                                    if angle < bestAngle then bestAngle = angle; bestPlayer = player end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return bestPlayer
    end
    local function CV_EnsureSnapLine()
        if STATE.CV_SnapGui and STATE.CV_SnapGui.Parent and STATE.CV_SnapLine and STATE.CV_SnapLine.Parent and STATE.CV_SnapDot and STATE.CV_SnapDot.Parent then return end
        local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        gui.Name = "CameraVeil_SnapLine"
        gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true; gui.ResetOnSpawn = false; gui.Parent = GetSafeGuiParent()
        local line = Instance.new("Frame")
        line.Name = "Line"; line.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5); line.BorderSizePixel = 0
        line.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 120); line.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
        line.Visible = false; line.Parent = gui
        local dot = Instance.new("Frame")
        dot.Name = "Dot"; dot.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5); dot.BorderSizePixel = 0
        dot.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 120); dot.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        dot.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(7, 7); dot.Visible = false; dot.Parent = gui
        local dotCorner = Instance.new("UICorner"); dotCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0); dotCorner.Parent = dot
        STATE.CV_SnapGui = gui; STATE.CV_SnapLine = line; STATE.CV_SnapDot = dot
    end
    local function CV_UpdateSnapLine(targetPos)
        if not CONFIG.cameraVeilSnapLine or not STATE.CV_Enabled then
            if STATE.CV_SnapLine then STATE.CV_SnapLine.Visible = false end
            if STATE.CV_SnapDot then STATE.CV_SnapDot.Visible = false end
            return
        end
        if not CV_IsSpearMode() then
            if STATE.CV_SnapLine then STATE.CV_SnapLine.Visible = false end
            if STATE.CV_SnapDot then STATE.CV_SnapDot.Visible = false end
            return
        end
        local fc = STATE.CV_frameCount
        if targetPos and (fc - STATE.CV_snapLineFrame) < 2 then return end
        STATE.CV_snapLineFrame = fc
        local ok = pcall(function()
            CV_EnsureSnapLine()
            local line = STATE.CV_SnapLine; local dot = STATE.CV_SnapDot; local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
            if not line or not cam then if line then line.Visible = false end if dot then dot.Visible = false end return end
            local displayPos = targetPos
            local isLocked = (STATE.CV_CurrentTarget ~= nil and STATE.CV_WasHolding)
            if not isLocked then
                local previewTarget = CV_GetClosestTarget()
                if previewTarget then
                    local char = previewTarget.Character
                    if char then displayPos = CV_GetAimPos(char) end
                else displayPos = nil end
            end
            if not displayPos then line.Visible = false; if dot then dot.Visible = false end return end
            local screenPos, onScreen = cam:WorldToViewportPoint(displayPos)
            if not onScreen or screenPos.Z <= 0 then line.Visible = false; if dot then dot.Visible = false end return end
            local viewport = cam.ViewportSize
            local startPos = Vector2.new(viewport.X * 0.5, viewport.Y * 0.5)
            local endPos = Vector2.new(screenPos.X, screenPos.Y)
            local delta = endPos - startPos; local length = delta.Magnitude
            if length < 2 then line.Visible = false; if dot then dot.Visible = false end return end
            local lineColor = isLocked and Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 120) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 0)
            local lineAlpha = isLocked and 0.08 or 0.35
            line.BackgroundColor3 = lineColor; line.BackgroundTransparency = lineAlpha
            line.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(length, isLocked and 2 or 1)
            line.Position = UDim2.fromOffset((startPos.X + endPos.X) * 0.5, (startPos.Y + endPos.Y) * 0.5)
            line.Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(delta.Y, delta.X)); line.Visible = true
            if dot then
                dot.BackgroundColor3 = lineColor; dot.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(endPos.X, endPos.Y); dot.Visible = true
            end
        end)
        if not ok then
            if STATE.CV_SnapLine then STATE.CV_SnapLine.Visible = false end
            if STATE.CV_SnapDot then STATE.CV_SnapDot.Visible = false end
        end
    end
    function CV_ClearLock()
        STATE.CV_CurrentTarget = nil; STATE.CV_WasHolding = false
        STATE.CV_LastStableDir = nil; STATE.CV_acquireStartFrame = nil
        CV_UpdateSnapLine(nil)
    end
    CV_IsSpearMode = function()
        local fc = STATE.CV_frameCount
        if fc == STATE.CV_spearModeFrame then return STATE.CV_cachedSpearMode end
        STATE.CV_spearModeFrame = fc
        local char = localPlayer.Character
        local result = false
        if char then
            local ok, v = pcall(char.GetAttribute, char, "spearmode")
            result = ok and v == true
        end
        STATE.CV_cachedSpearMode = result
        return result
    end
    local function CV_IsHoldingAttack()
        if not CV_IsSpearMode() then return false end
        return STATE.CV_HoldingPC or STATE.CV_HoldingMobile
    end
    local function CV_Step()
        STATE.CV_frameCount = STATE.CV_frameCount + 1
        if not STATE.CV_Enabled then return end
        local holding = CV_IsHoldingAttack()
        if not holding then
            if STATE.CV_WasHolding then CV_ClearLock() else CV_UpdateSnapLine(nil) end
            return
        end
        if not STATE.CV_WasHolding then
            STATE.CV_LastStableDir = nil
            STATE.CV_CurrentTarget = CV_GetClosestTarget()
            STATE.CV_WasHolding = true
            STATE.CV_acquireStartFrame = STATE.CV_frameCount
        end
        if STATE.CV_acquireStartFrame and (STATE.CV_frameCount - STATE.CV_acquireStartFrame) < ACQUIRE_GRACE_FRAMES then
            local reacquired = CV_GetClosestTarget()
            if reacquired then STATE.CV_CurrentTarget = reacquired end
        end
        local target = STATE.CV_CurrentTarget
        if not target then STATE.CV_WasHolding = false; return end
        local char = target.Character
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local role = getRole(target)
        if not char or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 or role ~= "survivor" then
            STATE.CV_WasHolding = false; STATE.CV_CurrentTarget = nil; return
        end
        local headPos = CV_GetAimPos(char)
        if not headPos then STATE.CV_WasHolding = false; STATE.CV_CurrentTarget = nil; return end
        local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        local myChar = localPlayer.Character
        local localRoot = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not cam or not localRoot then return end
        if (headPos - localRoot.Position).Magnitude > CONFIG.cameraVeilMaxDistance then
            STATE.CV_WasHolding = false; STATE.CV_CurrentTarget = nil; return
        end
        local targetVelocity, stabNow = nil, 1
        if CONFIG.spearPrediction then
            targetVelocity, stabNow = Spear_EstVel(CVVelSt, char, headPos)
        else
            CVVelSt.char, CVVelSt.hist, CVVelSt.sm = nil, {}, Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            CVVelSt.turn = 0
            targetVelocity = CV_GetTargetVelocity(char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
        end
        local leadScale = 1
        if CONFIG.spearDamping then leadScale = 0.55 + 0.45 * math.clamp(stabNow or 1, 0, 1) end
        local desiredDir = CV_GetBallisticAimDirection(headPos, targetVelocity)
        local dirValid = desiredDir and desiredDir.Magnitude > 0.001 and desiredDir.Y < RAD_p95 and desiredDir.Y > RAD_n95
        if dirValid then
            if STATE.CV_LastStableDir then
                local angleDelta = math.acos(math.clamp(STATE.CV_LastStableDir:Dot(desiredDir.Unit), -1, 1))
                if angleDelta < RAD_45 then STATE.CV_LastStableDir = desiredDir.Unit end
            else
                STATE.CV_LastStableDir = desiredDir.Unit
            end
            desiredDir = STATE.CV_LastStableDir
        elseif STATE.CV_LastStableDir then
            desiredDir = STATE.CV_LastStableDir
        else
            desiredDir = cam.CFrame.LookVector
        end
        local cameraPos = cam.CFrame.Position
        local smoothness = math.clamp(CONFIG.cameraVeilSmoothness or 1, 0.05, 1)
        local newDir = cam.CFrame.LookVector:Lerp(desiredDir, smoothness)
        if newDir.Magnitude > 0.001 then
            cam.CFrame = CFrame.new(cameraPos, cameraPos + newDir.Unit)
            CV_UpdateSnapLine(headPos)
        end
    end
    local function CV_HasAncestorNamed(inst, name)
        local current = inst and inst.Parent
        while current do
            if current.Name == name then return true end
            current = current.Parent
        end
        return false
    end
    local function CV_IsSpearAttackButton(inst)
        if not (inst and inst:IsA("GuiButton")) then return false end
        if inst.Name ~= "attack" then return false end
        if not CV_HasAncestorNamed(inst, "Slasher-mob") then return false end
        if not CV_HasAncestorNamed(inst, "Control") and not CV_HasAncestorNamed(inst, "Controls") then return false end
        return true
    end
    local function CV_HookAttackButton(btn)
        if not CV_IsSpearAttackButton(btn) then return end
        if STATE.CV_HookedButtons[btn] then return end
        STATE.CV_HookedButtons[btn] = true
        local function beginHold()
            if not STATE.CV_Enabled or not CV_IsSpearMode() then return end
            STATE.CV_HoldingMobile = true
        end
        local function endHold() STATE.CV_HoldingMobile = false end
        local inputBeganConn = btn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            local t = input.UserInputType
            if t ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch and t ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then return end
            beginHold()
            local releaseConn
            releaseConn = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(ended)
                if ended == input then endHold(); if releaseConn then releaseConn:Disconnect() end end
            end)
        end)
        table.insert(STATE.CV_Connections, inputBeganConn)
        local inputEndedConn = btn.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            local t = input.UserInputType
            if t ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch and t ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then return end
            endHold()
        end)
        table.insert(STATE.CV_Connections, inputEndedConn)
    end
    function CV_ScanAttackButtons()
        local pg = localPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") or PlayerGui
        if not pg then return end
        pcall(function()
            local slasher = pg:FindFirstChild("Slasher-mob", true)
            if slasher then
                local control = slasher:FindFirstChild("Control") or slasher:FindFirstChild("Controls")
                if control then
                    local function walkControl(parent)
                        for _, child in ipairs(parent:GetChildren()) do
                            CV_HookAttackButton(child)
                            walkControl(child)
                        end
                    end
                    walkControl(control)
                end
            end
        end)
        if STATE.CV_WatchConn then STATE.CV_WatchConn:Disconnect() end
        STATE.CV_WatchConn = pg.DescendantAdded:Connect(CV_HookAttackButton)
        table.insert(STATE.CV_Connections, STATE.CV_WatchConn)
    end
    local function CV_HookVisualizeRemote()
        if STATE.CV_VisualConn then return end
        local ok, remote = pcall(function()
            local rs = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
            local remotes = rs:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
            local mechanics = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Mechanics")
            return mechanics and mechanics:FindFirstChild("visualize")
        end)
        if ok and remote and remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
            STATE.CV_VisualConn = remote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(ownerChar, spearDir, spearSpeed, gravityMult)
                if ownerChar ~= localPlayer.Character then return end
                if type(spearSpeed) == "number" and spearSpeed >= 15 and spearSpeed <= 800 then STATE.CV_LastSpearSpeed = spearSpeed end
                if type(gravityMult) == "number" and gravityMult > 0.2 and gravityMult <= 5 then STATE.CV_LastGravityMult = gravityMult end
            end)
            table.insert(STATE.CV_Connections, STATE.CV_VisualConn)
        end
    end
    function StartCameraVeil()
        if STATE.CV_Enabled then return end
        STATE.CV_Enabled = true
        STATE.CV_HoldingPC = false; STATE.CV_HoldingMobile = false
        CV_ClearLock(); CV_HookVisualizeRemote(); CV_ScanAttackButtons()
        STATE.CV_InputBeganConn = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 and CV_IsSpearMode() then STATE.CV_HoldingPC = true end
        end)
        table.insert(STATE.CV_Connections, STATE.CV_InputBeganConn)
        STATE.CV_InputEndedConn = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then STATE.CV_HoldingPC = false end
        end)
        table.insert(STATE.CV_Connections, STATE.CV_InputEndedConn)
        pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(STATE.CV_RenderStepName) end)
        pcall(function() RunService:BindToRenderStep(STATE.CV_RenderStepName, Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 2, CV_Step) end)
    end
    function StopCameraVeil()
        STATE.CV_LastSpearSpeed = nil; STATE.CV_LastGravityMult = nil
        STATE.CV_Enabled = false
        STATE.CV_HoldingPC = false; STATE.CV_HoldingMobile = false
        CV_ClearLock()
        STATE.CV_frameCount = 0; STATE.CV_snapLineFrame = -99; STATE.CV_spearModeFrame = -99; STATE.CV_cachedSpearMode = false
        STATE.CV_ballisticFrame = -99; STATE.CV_lastSolvedHeadPos = nil; STATE.CV_lastSolvedDir = nil
        STATE.CV_lastSolvedPredicted = nil; STATE.CV_lastSolvedTime = nil
        pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep(STATE.CV_RenderStepName) end)
        for _, conn in ipairs(STATE.CV_Connections) do pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) end
        STATE.CV_Connections = {}; STATE.CV_HookedButtons = {}
        STATE.CV_InputBeganConn = nil; STATE.CV_InputEndedConn = nil; STATE.CV_WatchConn = nil; STATE.CV_VisualConn = nil
    end
end

-- ============================================================
--  ESP HELPERS
-- ============================================================
local CreatePlayerESP
local CreatePlayerOutline
local UpdatePlayerEspDistances
local RefreshAllESP, ForceRefreshAllESP, GetCamPosition, AttachESPToChar, RemovePlayerESP, AttachOutlineToChar, ValidateAndReattachESP, CreateBillboardTag
do
    function CreateBillboardTag(text, color, size, textSize)
        local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        bb.AlwaysOnTop = true; bb.Size = size or UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 40); bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3.5, 0)
        local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        lbl.Name = "Label"; lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0); lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        lbl.Text = text; lbl.TextColor3 = color; lbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
        lbl.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0); lbl.TextSize = textSize or 22
        lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold; lbl.RichText = true; lbl.Parent = bb
        return bb
    end

    local function GetEspDistance(isKiller)
        return isKiller and CONFIG.killerMaxDistance or CONFIG.survivorMaxDistance
    end

    function RefreshAllESP()
        for player, esp in pairs(STATE.espObjects) do
            if esp and esp.billboard and esp.billboard.Parent then
                local role = getRole(player); local isKiller = role == "killer"
                esp.nameLabel.Visible = (isKiller and CONFIG.killerShowName) or (not isKiller and CONFIG.survivorShowName)
                esp.nameLabel.Text = STATE.pnameEnabled and (STATE.pnameFakeNames[player] or player.Name) or player.Name
                esp.billboard.MaxDistance = GetEspDistance(isKiller)
                if esp.itemBillboard then esp.itemBillboard.MaxDistance = GetEspDistance(isKiller) end
            end
        end
        for player, hl in pairs(STATE.outlineObjects) do
            if hl and hl.Parent then
                local role = getRole(player); local isKiller = role == "killer"
                local color = isKiller and CONFIG.killerColor or CONFIG.survivorColor
                local showOutline = (isKiller and CONFIG.killerShowOutline) or (not isKiller and CONFIG.survivorShowOutline)
                local outlineOnly = (isKiller and CONFIG.killerOutlineOnly) or (not isKiller and CONFIG.survivorOutlineOnly)
                hl.FillColor = color; hl.OutlineColor = color; hl.Enabled = showOutline
                hl.FillTransparency = outlineOnly and 1 or CONFIG.fillTransparency
            end
        end
        UpdatePlayerEspDistances()
    end

    local function RebuildPlayerESP(player)
        if player == localPlayer or not player or not player.Parent then return end
        local oldEsp = STATE.espObjects[player]
        if oldEsp then
            if oldEsp.billboard and oldEsp.billboard.Parent then oldEsp.billboard:Destroy() end
            if oldEsp.itemBillboard and oldEsp.itemBillboard.Parent then oldEsp.itemBillboard:Destroy() end
        end
        local oldHl = STATE.outlineObjects[player]
        if oldHl and oldHl.Parent then oldHl:Destroy() end
        STATE.espObjects[player] = nil
        STATE.outlineObjects[player] = nil
        CreatePlayerESP(player)
        CreatePlayerOutline(player)
    end

    function ForceRefreshAllESP()
        for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if player ~= localPlayer then RebuildPlayerESP(player) end
        end
    end

    function GetCamPosition()
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if cam then return cam.CFrame.Position end
        return nil
    end

    UpdatePlayerEspDistances = function()
        local camPos = GetCamPosition()
        if not camPos then return end
        for player, esp in pairs(STATE.espObjects) do
            if player and player.Parent then
                local char = player.Character
                local root = char and (char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso") or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Head"))
                if root then
                    local dist = (root.Position - camPos).Magnitude
                    local isKiller = getRole(player) == "killer"
                    local maxD = GetEspDistance(isKiller)
                    local within = dist <= maxD

                    local hl = STATE.outlineObjects[player]
                    if hl then
                        local showOutline = (isKiller and CONFIG.killerShowOutline) or (not isKiller and CONFIG.survivorShowOutline)
                        local want = showOutline and within
                        if hl.Enabled ~= want then hl.Enabled = want end
                    end
                    if esp then
                        if esp.billboard then
                            local showName = (isKiller and CONFIG.killerShowName) or (not isKiller and CONFIG.survivorShowName)
                            local wantB = within and showName
                            if esp.billboard.Enabled ~= wantB then esp.billboard.Enabled = wantB end
                            esp.billboard.MaxDistance = maxD
                        end
                        if esp.itemBillboard and CONFIG.espItemEnabled then
                            if esp.itemBillboard.Enabled ~= within then esp.itemBillboard.Enabled = within end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    function AttachESPToChar(player, char)
        local esp = STATE.espObjects[player]
        if not esp then return end
        if not char or not char.Parent or player.Character ~= char then return end
    
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            or char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
            or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
            or char:FindFirstChild("Head")
    
        if not root then
            task.spawn(function()
                local ok, waitedRoot = pcall(function() return char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 3) end)
                if ok and waitedRoot and waitedRoot.Parent and char.Parent and player.Character == char then
                    if esp.billboard then 
                        esp.billboard.Adornee = waitedRoot 
                        esp.billboard.Enabled = true 
                    end
                    if esp.itemBillboard then 
                        esp.itemBillboard.Adornee = waitedRoot 
                        esp.itemBillboard.Enabled = true
                    end
                end
            end)
            return
        end
    
        if esp.billboard then
            esp.billboard.Adornee = root
            esp.billboard.Enabled = true
        end
        if esp.itemBillboard then
            esp.itemBillboard.Adornee = root
            esp.itemBillboard.Enabled = true
        end
    
        local role = getRole(player); local isKiller = role == "killer"
        esp.nameLabel.Visible = (isKiller and CONFIG.killerShowName) or (not isKiller and CONFIG.survivorShowName)
        esp.nameLabel.Text = STATE.pnameEnabled and (STATE.pnameFakeNames[player] or player.Name) or player.Name
    end

    function RemovePlayerESP(player)
        if STATE.espObjects[player] then
            if STATE.espObjects[player].billboard then 
                STATE.espObjects[player].billboard:Destroy() 
            end
            if STATE.espObjects[player].itemBillboard then 
                STATE.espObjects[player].itemBillboard:Destroy() 
            end
            STATE.espObjects[player] = nil
        end
        if STATE.outlineObjects[player] then
            STATE.outlineObjects[player]:Destroy(); STATE.outlineObjects[player] = nil
        end
        STATE.playerRoles[player] = nil
        if STATE.playerTeamConns[player] then
            STATE.playerTeamConns[player]:Disconnect(); STATE.playerTeamConns[player] = nil
        end
    end

    function AttachOutlineToChar(player, char)
        local hl = STATE.outlineObjects[player]
        if not hl then return end
        if not char or not char.Parent or player.Character ~= char then return end
    
        local role = getRole(player); local isKiller = role == "killer"
        local color = isKiller and CONFIG.killerColor or CONFIG.survivorColor
        local showOutline = (isKiller and CONFIG.killerShowOutline) or (not isKiller and CONFIG.survivorShowOutline)
        local outlineOnly = (isKiller and CONFIG.killerOutlineOnly) or (not isKiller and CONFIG.survivorOutlineOnly)
    
        hl.Adornee = char
        hl.FillColor = color
        hl.OutlineColor = color
        hl.FillTransparency = outlineOnly and 1 or CONFIG.fillTransparency
        hl.OutlineTransparency = 0
        hl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
        hl.Enabled = showOutline
    end

    function ValidateAndReattachESP(player)
        if player == localPlayer then return end
        if not player or not player.Parent then return end
    
        local esp = STATE.espObjects[player]
        local hl = STATE.outlineObjects[player]
        if not esp and not hl then return end

        if (esp and esp.billboard and not esp.billboard.Parent) or (hl and not hl.Parent) then
            RebuildPlayerESP(player)
            return
        end
    
        local char = player.Character
        if not char or not char.Parent then
            if esp and esp.billboard then esp.billboard.Adornee = nil end
            if esp and esp.itemBillboard then esp.itemBillboard.Adornee = nil end
            if hl then hl.Adornee = nil; hl.Enabled = false end
            return
        end
    
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            or char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
            or char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
            or char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if not root then return end

        local camPos = GetCamPosition()
        local within = true
        if camPos then
            local dist = (root.Position - camPos).Magnitude
            local isKillerR = getRole(player) == "killer"
            within = dist <= GetEspDistance(isKillerR)
        end
        local isKillerR2 = getRole(player) == "killer"
        local showNameWant = (isKillerR2 and CONFIG.killerShowName) or (not isKillerR2 and CONFIG.survivorShowName)
        local showOutlineWant = (isKillerR2 and CONFIG.killerShowOutline) or (not isKillerR2 and CONFIG.survivorShowOutline)

        if esp and esp.billboard then
            if esp.billboard.Adornee ~= root then esp.billboard.Adornee = root end
            local wantB = within and showNameWant
            if esp.billboard.Enabled ~= wantB then esp.billboard.Enabled = wantB end
        end
        if esp and esp.itemBillboard then
            if esp.itemBillboard.Adornee ~= root then esp.itemBillboard.Adornee = root end
            if esp.itemBillboard.Enabled ~= within then esp.itemBillboard.Enabled = within end
        end
    
        if hl then
            if hl.Adornee ~= char then
                AttachOutlineToChar(player, char)
            else
                local wantH = within and showOutlineWant
                if hl.Enabled ~= wantH then hl.Enabled = wantH end
            end
        end
    end

    CreatePlayerESP = function(player)
        if player == localPlayer then return end
        refreshRole(player)
        local role = getRole(player)
    
        local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        billboard.Name = "ESP_BB_" .. player.Name; billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
        billboard.Size = UDim2.new(0, 150, 0, 18)
        billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3.5, 0)
        billboard.MaxDistance = GetEspDistance(role == "killer"); billboard.Parent = GetEspFolder()
    
        local nameLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        nameLabel.Name = "NameLabel"; nameLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        nameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        nameLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255); nameLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
        nameLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0); nameLabel.TextSize = 12
        nameLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        nameLabel.Text = STATE.pnameEnabled and (STATE.pnameFakeNames[player] or player.Name) or player.Name
        nameLabel.Visible = (role == "killer" and CONFIG.killerShowName) or (role ~= "killer" and CONFIG.survivorShowName)
        nameLabel.Parent = billboard
    
        local itemBillboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        itemBillboard.Name = "ESP_Item_" .. player.Name
        itemBillboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
    
        itemBillboard.Size = UDim2.new(1.5, 0, 1.5, 0) 
    
        itemBillboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, -5, 0) 
        itemBillboard.MaxDistance = GetEspDistance(role == "killer")
        itemBillboard.Parent = GetEspFolder()
    
        local itemImage = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
        itemImage.Name = "ItemImage"
        itemImage.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        itemImage.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        itemImage.Visible = false
        itemImage.Parent = itemBillboard
    
        STATE.espObjects[player] = { 
            billboard = billboard, 
            nameLabel = nameLabel, 
            itemBillboard = itemBillboard,
            itemImage = itemImage 
        }
    
        if player.Character then AttachESPToChar(player, player.Character) end
        if not STATE.playerTeamConns[player] then
            STATE.playerTeamConns[player] = player:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Team"):Connect(function()
                refreshRole(player)
                RefreshAllESP()
                task.delay(0.5, function() ValidateAndReattachESP(player) end)
                task.delay(2, function() ValidateAndReattachESP(player) end)
            end)
        end
    end

    CreatePlayerOutline = function(player)
        if player == localPlayer then return end
        local hl = Instance.new("Highlight"); hl.Parent = GetEspFolder()
        STATE.outlineObjects[player] = hl
        if player.Character then AttachOutlineToChar(player, player.Character) end
    end
end

-- ============================================================
--  OBJECT ESP SYSTEM
-- ============================================================
local RefreshAllObjESP, ApplyAntiLoopWindow, DisableAntiLoopWindow, DropAllPallets, ApplyGhostGateToObj
do
    local function GetObjValue(obj, name)
        if typeof(obj) ~= "Instance" then return nil end
        local attr = obj:GetAttribute(name)
        if attr ~= nil then return attr end
        local child = obj:FindFirstChild(name)
        if child and child:IsA("ValueBase") then return child.Value end
        return nil
    end

    local function ApplyObjHighlight(object, color)
        if not object or not object.Parent then return end
        local h = object:FindFirstChild("__BolongHL__")
        if not h then
            h = Instance.new("Highlight"); h.Name = "__BolongHL__"; h.Adornee = object
            h.FillTransparency = 1; h.OutlineTransparency = 0; h.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
            h.FillColor = color; h.OutlineColor = color; h.Parent = object; return
        end
        if h.FillColor ~= color then h.FillColor = color; h.OutlineColor = color end
        if not h.Enabled then h.Enabled = true end
    end

    local function RemoveObjHighlight(object)
        if not object then return end
        local h = object:FindFirstChild("__BolongHL__")
        if h then h:Destroy() end
    end

    local function UpdateGeneratorProgress(generator)
        if not generator or not generator.Parent then return true end
        if STATE.completedGenerators[generator] then return true end
    
        local percent = generator:GetAttribute("RepairProgress") or generator:GetAttribute("Progress") or 0
        local isCompleted = (percent >= 100) or (generator:GetAttribute("Completed") == true) or (generator:GetAttribute("IsCompleted") == true) or (generator:GetAttribute("Done") == true)
    
        local billboard = generator:FindFirstChild("__BolongGenProgress__")
    
        if isCompleted then
            if billboard then billboard:Destroy() end
            RemoveObjHighlight(generator)
            generator:SetAttribute("__BolongGenLastPct__", nil)
            generator:SetAttribute("__BolongGenLastInfo__", nil)
            STATE.completedGenerators[generator] = true
            STATE.genIndices[generator] = nil
            return isCompleted
        end
    
        -- === LOGIKA OUTLINE (ESP GENERATOR) ===
        if CONFIG.espGeneratorEnabled then
            ApplyObjHighlight(generator, CONFIG.espGeneratorColor)
        else
            RemoveObjHighlight(generator)
        end
    
        -- === LOGIKA TEXT LABEL (PROGRESS GEN) ===
        if CONFIG.espGeneratorProgressGen then
            local playersCount = generator:GetAttribute("PlayersRepairingCount") or 0
            local kickCount = generator:GetAttribute("kickcount") or 0
        
            local roundedPct = math.floor(percent + 0.5)
            local lastPct = generator:GetAttribute("__BolongGenLastPct__")
            local lastInfo = generator:GetAttribute("__BolongGenLastInfo__")
            local genIndex = STATE.genIndices[generator] or 1
        
            local currentInfo = string.format("%d_%d_%d_%s_%d", roundedPct, playersCount, kickCount, tostring(CONFIG.espGenShowPK), genIndex)
        
            if lastPct == roundedPct and lastInfo == currentInfo and billboard then return false end
        
            generator:SetAttribute("__BolongGenLastPct__", roundedPct)
            generator:SetAttribute("__BolongGenLastInfo__", currentInfo)
        
            local cp = math.clamp(percent, 0, 100)
            local dynamicColor = (cp < 50) and CONFIG.espGeneratorColor:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 0), cp / 50) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 0):Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(100, 255, 80), (cp - 50) / 50)
            local hexColor = dynamicColor:ToHex()
        
            local genName = string.format("GEN%d", genIndex)
            local percentStr = string.format("%d%%", roundedPct)
        
            local targetPart = generator:FindFirstChild("GeneratorBody", true) 
                            or generator:FindFirstChild("defaultMaterial", true) 
                            or (generator:IsA("Model") and generator.PrimaryPart) 
                            or generator:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
        
            if not targetPart then return false end
        
            local genHex = "F4D03F"
        
            local displayText = string.format(
                '<font size="9" color="#%s">%s</font> <font color="#555555">│</font> <font color="#%s">%s</font>',
                genHex, genName, hexColor, percentStr
            )
        
            if CONFIG.espGenShowPK then
                displayText = displayText .. string.format(' <font color="#555555">│</font> <font color="#76D7C4">P:%d</font> <font color="#555555">│</font> <font color="#FF6B6B">K:%d</font>', playersCount, kickCount)
            end
        
            if not billboard then
                billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
                billboard.Name = "__BolongGenProgress__"
                billboard.Adornee = targetPart
                billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
                billboard.LightInfluence = 0
                billboard.ResetOnSpawn = false
                billboard.MaxDistance = 260
                billboard.Size = UDim2.new(0, 100, 0, 14)
            
                local yOffset = (targetPart.Size.Y / 2) + 3.5
                billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, yOffset, 0) 
            
                billboard.Parent = generator
            
                local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                lbl.Name = "Label"
                lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
                lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack 
                lbl.TextSize = 11 
                lbl.RichText = true
                lbl.Text = displayText
                lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
                lbl.Parent = billboard
            
                local textStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
                textStroke.Thickness = 0.8 
                textStroke.Transparency = 0.4
                textStroke.Color = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
                textStroke.Parent = lbl
            else
                if billboard.Adornee ~= targetPart then billboard.Adornee = targetPart end
            
                local yOffset = (targetPart.Size.Y / 2) + 3.5
                billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, yOffset, 0)
            
                local lbl = billboard:FindFirstChild("Label")
                if lbl then lbl.Text = displayText end
            end
        else
            if billboard then 
                billboard:Destroy()
                generator:SetAttribute("__BolongGenLastPct__", nil) 
                generator:SetAttribute("__BolongGenLastInfo__", nil)
            end
        end
        return false
    end

    local function SetupGeneratorWatcher(generator)
        local function onProgress()
            if not CONFIG.espGeneratorEnabled and not CONFIG.espGeneratorProgressGen then return end
            UpdateGeneratorProgress(generator)
        end
    
        pcall(function()
            generator:GetAttributeChangedSignal("RepairProgress"):Connect(onProgress)
            generator:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Progress"):Connect(onProgress)
            generator:GetAttributeChangedSignal("Completed"):Connect(onProgress)
            generator:GetAttributeChangedSignal("IsCompleted"):Connect(onProgress)
            generator:GetAttributeChangedSignal("PlayersRepairingCount"):Connect(onProgress)
            generator:GetAttributeChangedSignal("kickcount"):Connect(onProgress)
        end)
    end

    local function SetupPalletWatcher(pallet, meta)
        local function checkState()
            local isDropped = GetObjValue(pallet, "Dropped") or GetObjValue(pallet, "IsDropped")
            local isBroken = GetObjValue(pallet, "Broken") or GetObjValue(pallet, "IsBroken") or GetObjValue(pallet, "Destroyed")
            if isDropped or isBroken or meta.isFake then
                RemoveObjHighlight(pallet)
                for i, p in ipairs(STATE.cachedMapObjects.Pallets) do
                    if p == pallet then table.remove(STATE.cachedMapObjects.Pallets, i); break end
                end
                STATE.cachedPalletMeta[pallet] = nil
            end
        end
        for _, attr in ipairs({"Dropped","IsDropped","Broken","IsBroken","Destroyed"}) do
            pallet:GetAttributeChangedSignal(attr):Connect(checkState)
        end
        pallet.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            if child:IsA("ValueBase") and (child.Name=="Dropped" or child.Name=="IsDropped" or child.Name=="Broken" or child.Name=="IsBroken" or child.Name=="Destroyed") then
                child.Changed:Connect(checkState)
            end
        end)
    end

    function ApplyGhostGateToObj(gate, enabled)
        if not gate or not gate.Parent then return end
        local parts = gate:IsA("BasePart") and {gate} or {}
        if not gate:IsA("BasePart") then
            local function collectParts(parent)
                for _, child in ipairs(parent:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("BasePart") then table.insert(parts, child) end
                    collectParts(child)
                end
            end
            collectParts(gate)
        end
        for _, part in ipairs(parts) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                if enabled then
                    if not STATE.ghostGateOriginals[part] then
                        STATE.ghostGateOriginals[part] = { CanCollide = part.CanCollide, Transparency = part.Transparency }
                    end
                    part.CanCollide = false
                    part.Transparency = 0.6
                else
                    if STATE.ghostGateOriginals[part] then
                        part.CanCollide = STATE.ghostGateOriginals[part].CanCollide
                        part.Transparency = STATE.ghostGateOriginals[part].Transparency
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local function HandleMapDescendantAdded(desc)
        local n = desc.Name
        if n == "Generator" then
            table.insert(STATE.cachedMapObjects.Generators, desc)
            if not STATE.genIndices[desc] then
                if next(STATE.genIndices) == nil then
                    STATE.nextGenIndex = 1
                end
                STATE.genIndices[desc] = STATE.nextGenIndex
                STATE.nextGenIndex = STATE.nextGenIndex + 1
            end
            if CONFIG.espGeneratorEnabled then UpdateGeneratorProgress(desc); SetupGeneratorWatcher(desc) end
        elseif n == "Hook" then
            table.insert(STATE.cachedMapObjects.Hooks, desc)
            local parts = {}
            local m = desc:FindFirstChild("Model")
            if m then
                local function collectMeshParts(parent)
                    for _, child in ipairs(parent:GetChildren()) do
                        if child:IsA("MeshPart") then table.insert(parts, child) end
                        collectMeshParts(child)
                    end
                end
                collectMeshParts(m)
            end
            STATE.cachedHookMeshParts[desc] = parts
            if CONFIG.espHookEnabled then
                if #parts > 0 then for _, p in ipairs(parts) do ApplyObjHighlight(p, CONFIG.espHookColor) end
                else ApplyObjHighlight(desc, CONFIG.espHookColor) end
            end
        elseif n == "Gate" then
            table.insert(STATE.cachedMapObjects.Gates, desc)
            if CONFIG.espGateEnabled then ApplyObjHighlight(desc, CONFIG.espGateColor) end
            if STATE.ghostGateEnabled then ApplyGhostGateToObj(desc, true) end
        elseif n == "Pallet" or n == "Palletwrong" then
            table.insert(STATE.cachedMapObjects.Pallets, desc)
            local nLower = n:lower()
            local part = (desc:IsA("Model") and desc.PrimaryPart) or desc:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true) or (desc:IsA("BasePart") and desc)
            local fake = nLower:find("fake") or nLower:find("broken") or nLower:find("destroyed")
            local meta = { part = part, isFake = fake and true or false }
            STATE.cachedPalletMeta[desc] = meta
            if CONFIG.espPalletEnabled and not meta.isFake then ApplyObjHighlight(desc, CONFIG.espPalletColor) end
            SetupPalletWatcher(desc, meta)
        end
    end

    local function HandleMapDescendantRemoving(desc)
        local n = desc.Name
        if n == "Generator" then
            for i, v in ipairs(STATE.cachedMapObjects.Generators) do
                if v == desc then table.remove(STATE.cachedMapObjects.Generators, i); break end
            end
            STATE.genIndices[desc] = nil
            STATE.completedGenerators[desc] = nil
            RemoveObjHighlight(desc)
            local b = desc:FindFirstChild("__BolongGenProgress__")
            if b then b:Destroy() end
            if next(STATE.genIndices) == nil then
                STATE.nextGenIndex = 1
            end
        elseif n == "Hook" then
            for i, v in ipairs(STATE.cachedMapObjects.Hooks) do
                if v == desc then table.remove(STATE.cachedMapObjects.Hooks, i); break end
            end
            local parts = STATE.cachedHookMeshParts[desc]
            if parts then for _, p in ipairs(parts) do RemoveObjHighlight(p) end else RemoveObjHighlight(desc) end
            STATE.cachedHookMeshParts[desc] = nil
        elseif n == "Gate" then
            for i, v in ipairs(STATE.cachedMapObjects.Gates) do
                if v == desc then table.remove(STATE.cachedMapObjects.Gates, i); break end
            end
            RemoveObjHighlight(desc)
        elseif n == "Pallet" or n == "Palletwrong" then
            for i, v in ipairs(STATE.cachedMapObjects.Pallets) do
                if v == desc then table.remove(STATE.cachedMapObjects.Pallets, i); break end
            end
            RemoveObjHighlight(desc)
            STATE.cachedPalletMeta[desc] = nil
        end
    end

    local function ScanMapObjects(map)
        if not map then return end
        STATE.cachedMapObjects = { Generators={}, Pallets={}, Hooks={}, Gates={} }
        STATE.cachedPalletMeta = {}; STATE.cachedHookMeshParts = {}; STATE.completedGenerators = {}
        STATE.genIndices = {}; STATE.nextGenIndex = 1
        for _, obj in ipairs(map:GetDescendants()) do HandleMapDescendantAdded(obj) end
    end

    local function SetupMapListeners(map)
        if not map then return end
        ScanMapObjects(map)
        map.DescendantAdded:Connect(HandleMapDescendantAdded)
        map.DescendantRemoving:Connect(HandleMapDescendantRemoving)
        STATE.objEspInitialized = true
    end

    -- ============================================================
    --  WINDOW ESP SYSTEM
    -- ============================================================
    STATE.windowEspObjects = {}
    STATE._windowParts = {}
    STATE._windowPartMap = {}

    local function CreateWindowESP(part)
        if not part or not part.Parent or STATE.windowEspObjects[part] then return end
    
        local color = CONFIG.espWindowColor
    
        local adornment = Instance.new("BoxHandleAdornment")
        adornment.Name = "WindowESP_Box"
        adornment.Adornee = part
        adornment.Color3 = color
        adornment.Transparency = 0.3
        adornment.Size = part.Size
        adornment.AlwaysOnTop = true
        adornment.ZIndex = 10
        adornment.Parent = GetEspFolder()
    
        STATE.windowEspObjects[part] = adornment
    end

    local function RemoveWindowESP(part)
        local adornment = STATE.windowEspObjects[part]
        if adornment then
            pcall(function() adornment:Destroy() end)
            STATE.windowEspObjects[part] = nil
        end
    end

    local function GetWindowTarget(desc)
        if typeof(desc) ~= "Instance" then return nil end
        local n = string.lower(desc.Name)
    
        if n == "window" and desc:IsA("Model") then
            local bottom = desc:FindFirstChild("Bottom", true)
            if bottom and bottom:IsA("BasePart") then 
                return bottom 
            end
            return desc.PrimaryPart
        end
    
        if n == "bottom" and desc:IsA("BasePart") then
            if desc.Parent and string.lower(desc.Parent.Name) == "window" then
                return desc
            end
        end
    
        return nil
    end

    local function HandleWindowDescendant(desc)
        local targetPart = GetWindowTarget(desc)
        if targetPart then
            if not STATE._windowPartMap[targetPart] then
                STATE._windowPartMap[targetPart] = true
                table.insert(STATE._windowParts, targetPart)
            end
            if CONFIG.espWindowEnabled and not STATE.windowEspObjects[targetPart] then
                CreateWindowESP(targetPart)
            end
        end
    end

    Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(desc)
        HandleWindowDescendant(desc)
    end)

    Workspace.DescendantRemoving:Connect(function(desc)
        local targetPart = GetWindowTarget(desc) or desc
        if STATE._windowPartMap[targetPart] then
            STATE._windowPartMap[targetPart] = nil
            for i, e in ipairs(STATE._windowParts) do
                if e == targetPart then table.remove(STATE._windowParts, i) break end
            end
        end
        if STATE.windowEspObjects[targetPart] then
