            RemoveWindowESP(targetPart)
        end
    end)

    local function RescanAllWindowsESP()
        for part, adornment in pairs(STATE.windowEspObjects) do
            if not part or not part.Parent then
                RemoveWindowESP(part)
            end
        end
    
        if CONFIG.espWindowEnabled then
            for _, target in ipairs(STATE._windowParts) do
                if target and target.Parent and not STATE.windowEspObjects[target] then
                    CreateWindowESP(target)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        task.wait(3)
        for _, obj in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            local target = GetWindowTarget(obj)
            if target and not STATE._windowPartMap[target] then
                STATE._windowPartMap[target] = true
                table.insert(STATE._windowParts, target)
            end
        end
        RescanAllWindowsESP()
    end)

    function RefreshAllObjESP()
        if not STATE.objEspInitialized then return end
    
        -- === BAGIAN GENERATOR ===
        local isGenEspActive = CONFIG.espGeneratorEnabled or CONFIG.espGeneratorProgressGen
        if isGenEspActive then
            local newActive = {}
            for _, obj in ipairs(STATE.cachedMapObjects.Generators) do
                if obj and obj.Parent then
                    local done = UpdateGeneratorProgress(obj)
                    if not done then table.insert(newActive, obj) end
                end
            end
            STATE.cachedMapObjects.Generators = newActive
        else
            for _, obj in ipairs(STATE.cachedMapObjects.Generators) do
                if obj and obj.Parent then
                    RemoveObjHighlight(obj)
                    local b = obj:FindFirstChild("__BolongGenProgress__")
                    if b then b:Destroy() end
                    obj:SetAttribute("__BolongGenLastPct__", nil)
                end
            end
        end

        -- === BAGIAN PALLET ===
        if CONFIG.espPalletEnabled then
            for _, pallet in ipairs(STATE.cachedMapObjects.Pallets) do
                if pallet and pallet.Parent then
                    local meta = STATE.cachedPalletMeta[pallet]
                    if meta and not meta.isFake then ApplyObjHighlight(pallet, CONFIG.espPalletColor) end
                end
            end
        else
            for _, pallet in ipairs(STATE.cachedMapObjects.Pallets) do
                if pallet then RemoveObjHighlight(pallet) end
            end
        end

        -- === BAGIAN HOOK ===
        if CONFIG.espHookEnabled then
            for _, hook in ipairs(STATE.cachedMapObjects.Hooks) do
                if hook and hook.Parent then
                    local parts = STATE.cachedHookMeshParts[hook]
                    if parts then for _, p in ipairs(parts) do ApplyObjHighlight(p, CONFIG.espHookColor) end
                    else ApplyObjHighlight(hook, CONFIG.espHookColor) end
                end
            end
        else
            for _, hook in ipairs(STATE.cachedMapObjects.Hooks) do
                if hook and hook.Parent then
                    local parts = STATE.cachedHookMeshParts[hook]
                    if parts then for _, p in ipairs(parts) do RemoveObjHighlight(p) end
                    else RemoveObjHighlight(hook) end
                end
            end
        end

        -- === BAGIAN GATE ===
        if CONFIG.espGateEnabled then
            for _, gate in ipairs(STATE.cachedMapObjects.Gates) do
                if gate and gate.Parent then ApplyObjHighlight(gate, CONFIG.espGateColor) end
            end
        else
            for _, gate in ipairs(STATE.cachedMapObjects.Gates) do
                if gate and gate.Parent then RemoveObjHighlight(gate) end
            end
        end

        -- === BAGIAN WINDOW ESP ===
        if CONFIG.espWindowEnabled then
            RescanAllWindowsESP()
            for part, adornment in pairs(STATE.windowEspObjects) do
                if part and part.Parent and adornment and adornment.Parent then
                    pcall(function()
                        local color = CONFIG.espWindowColor
                        adornment.Color3 = color
                        adornment.Size = part.Size
                    end)
                else
                    RemoveWindowESP(part)
                end
            end
        else
            for part, adornment in pairs(STATE.windowEspObjects) do
                RemoveWindowESP(part)
            end
        end
    end

    -- ============================================================
    --  ANTI LOOP WINDOW SYSTEM
    -- ============================================================
    STATE._antiLoopWindowCache = {}
    STATE._antiLoopWindowMap = {}
    Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(desc)
        if typeof(desc) ~= "Instance" then return end
        if not (desc:IsA("BasePart") or desc:IsA("Model")) then return end
        local n = desc.Name
        if n ~= "Window" and n ~= "VaultPoint" and not string.find(n, "Window") then return end
        if STATE._antiLoopWindowMap[desc] then return end
        STATE._antiLoopWindowMap[desc] = true
        table.insert(STATE._antiLoopWindowCache, desc)
    end)
    Workspace.DescendantRemoving:Connect(function(desc)
        if not STATE._antiLoopWindowMap[desc] then return end
        STATE._antiLoopWindowMap[desc] = nil
        for i, e in ipairs(STATE._antiLoopWindowCache) do
            if e == desc then table.remove(STATE._antiLoopWindowCache, i) break end
        end
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        local map = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
        if map then
            for _, desc in ipairs(map:GetDescendants()) do
                if typeof(desc) == "Instance" and (desc:IsA("BasePart") or desc:IsA("Model")) then
                    local n = desc.Name
                    if n == "Window" or n == "VaultPoint" or string.find(n, "Window") then
                        if not STATE._antiLoopWindowMap[desc] then
                            STATE._antiLoopWindowMap[desc] = true
                            table.insert(STATE._antiLoopWindowCache, desc)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    local function GetAllWindows()
        local windows = {}
        for _, w in ipairs(STATE._antiLoopWindowCache) do
            if w and w.Parent then table.insert(windows, w) end
        end
        for _, tagged in ipairs(CollectionService:GetTagged("VaultPoint")) do
            local exists = false
            for _, w in ipairs(windows) do
                if w == tagged then exists = true break end
            end
            if not exists then table.insert(windows, tagged) end
        end
        return windows
    end

    function ApplyAntiLoopWindow()
        if not STATE.antiLoopWindowEnabled then return end
    
        local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        if not remotes then return end
        local windowRemoteFolder = remotes:FindFirstChild("Window")
        if not windowRemoteFolder then return end
        local vaultEvent = windowRemoteFolder:FindFirstChild("VaultEvent")
        if not vaultEvent then return end

        task.spawn(function()
            task.wait(1)
        
            local windows = GetAllWindows()
        
            for _, windowObj in ipairs(windows) do
                task.spawn(function()
                    pcall(function()
                        vaultEvent:FireServer(windowObj, true)
                    end)
                end)
                task.wait(0.05)
            end
        end)
    end

    function DisableAntiLoopWindow()
        local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        if not remotes then return end
        local windowRemoteFolder = remotes:FindFirstChild("Window")
        if not windowRemoteFolder then return end
    
        local vaultCompleteEvent = windowRemoteFolder:FindFirstChild("VaultCompleteEvent")
        local vaultCompleteEventPart1 = windowRemoteFolder:FindFirstChild("VaultCompleteEventpart1")

        task.spawn(function()
            local windows = GetAllWindows()
        
            for _, windowObj in ipairs(windows) do
                task.spawn(function()
                    pcall(function()
                        if vaultCompleteEvent then
                            vaultCompleteEvent:FireServer(windowObj, false)
                        end
                        if vaultCompleteEventPart1 then
                            vaultCompleteEventPart1:FireServer()
                        end
                    end)
                end)
                task.wait(0.05)
            end
        end)
    end

    do
        local existingMap = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
        if existingMap then 
            SetupMapListeners(existingMap) 
            ApplyAntiLoopWindow()
        end
        workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            if child.Name == "Map" then 
                task.wait(2)
                SetupMapListeners(child) 
                ApplyAntiLoopWindow()
            end
        end)
        workspace.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
            if child.Name == "Map" then
                STATE.cachedMapObjects = { Generators={}, Pallets={}, Hooks={}, Gates={} }
                STATE.cachedPalletMeta = {}; STATE.cachedHookMeshParts = {}
                STATE.completedGenerators = {}; STATE.objEspInitialized = false
                STATE.genIndices = {}; STATE.nextGenIndex = 1
            end
        end)
    end

    RegisterTask("GenESPUpdater", 0.2, function()
        if not CONFIG.espGeneratorEnabled and not CONFIG.espGeneratorProgressGen then return end
        for _, obj in ipairs(STATE.cachedMapObjects.Generators) do
            if obj and obj.Parent and not STATE.completedGenerators[obj] then UpdateGeneratorProgress(obj) end
        end
    end)

    function DropAllPallets()
        if not getnilinstances then 
            Notify("Error", "Executor tidak support getnilinstances()", 2)
            return 
        end
    
        task.spawn(function()
            local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
            if not remotes then return end
            local palletRemote = remotes:FindFirstChild("Pallet")
            palletRemote = palletRemote and palletRemote:FindFirstChild("PalletDropEvent")
            if not palletRemote then return end
        
            local palletPoints = {}
            local foundNames = {}
        
            local ok, nilInstances = pcall(getnilinstances)
            if ok and type(nilInstances) == "table" then
                for _, obj in ipairs(nilInstances) do
                    if typeof(obj) == "Instance" then
                        local name = obj.Name
                        if name == "PalletPointSlide" or name == "palletDropPoint" or name == "PalletDropPoint" or name == "PalletPoint" then
                            if not foundNames[obj] then
                                table.insert(palletPoints, obj)
                                foundNames[obj] = true
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        
            if STATE.palletPointsCache then
                for _, obj in ipairs(STATE.palletPointsCache) do
                    if obj and obj.Parent and not foundNames[obj] then
                        table.insert(palletPoints, obj)
                        foundNames[obj] = true
                    end
                end
            end
        
            if #palletPoints == 0 then
                return
            end
            for _, point in ipairs(palletPoints) do
                pcall(function()
                    palletRemote:FireServer(point)
                end)
            end
        end)
    end
    local function AddPalletPointObj(obj)
        if typeof(obj) ~= "Instance" then return end
        local name = obj.Name
        if name ~= "PalletPointSlide" and name ~= "palletDropPoint" and name ~= "PalletDropPoint" and name ~= "PalletPoint" then return end
        if not STATE.palletPointsCache then STATE.palletPointsCache = {} end
        if STATE._palletPointMap[obj] then return end
        STATE._palletPointMap[obj] = true
        table.insert(STATE.palletPointsCache, obj)
    end
    STATE._palletPointMap = {}
    Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(AddPalletPointObj)
    Workspace.DescendantRemoving:Connect(function(obj)
        if not STATE._palletPointMap[obj] then return end
        STATE._palletPointMap[obj] = nil
        if STATE.palletPointsCache then
            for i, e in ipairs(STATE.palletPointsCache) do
                if e == obj then table.remove(STATE.palletPointsCache, i) break end
            end
        end
    end)
    if getnilinstances then
        local ok, nilInstances = pcall(getnilinstances)
        if ok and type(nilInstances) == "table" then
            for _, obj in ipairs(nilInstances) do AddPalletPointObj(obj) end
        end
    end
    for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do AddPalletPointObj(obj) end

    RegisterTask("AutoDropNearbyPallets", 0.2, function()
        if not STATE.autoDropNearbyPallets then return end
        if STATE.autoDropCooldown then return end
    
        local char = localPlayer.Character
        if not char then return end
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not root then return end
    
        local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        if not remotes then return end
        local palletRemote = remotes:FindFirstChild("Pallet")
        palletRemote = palletRemote and palletRemote:FindFirstChild("PalletDropEvent")
        if not palletRemote then return end

        local rootPos = root.Position
    
        if not STATE.palletPointsCache then
            STATE.palletPointsCache = {}
            STATE._palletPointMap = {}
            if getnilinstances then
                local ok, nilInstances = pcall(getnilinstances)
                if ok and type(nilInstances) == "table" then
                    for _, obj in ipairs(nilInstances) do AddPalletPointObj(obj) end
                end
            end
            for _, obj in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do AddPalletPointObj(obj) end
        end
    
        if tick() - (STATE.lastPalletPointScan or 0) > 5 then
            STATE.lastPalletPointScan = tick()
            if getnilinstances then
                local ok, nilInstances = pcall(getnilinstances)
                if ok and type(nilInstances) == "table" then
                    for _, obj in ipairs(nilInstances) do AddPalletPointObj(obj) end
                end
            end
        end
    
        local closestPoint = nil
        local closestDist = math.huge
    
        for i = #STATE.palletPointsCache, 1, -1 do
            local obj = STATE.palletPointsCache[i]
            if not obj or not obj.Parent then
                STATE._palletPointMap[obj] = nil
                table.remove(STATE.palletPointsCache, i)
            else
                local pos = nil
                pcall(function()
                    if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
                        pos = obj.Position
                    elseif obj:IsA("Model") then
                        pos = obj:GetPivot().Position
                    elseif obj:IsA("Attachment") then
                        pos = obj.WorldPosition
                    end
                end)
            
                if not pos then
                    local part = obj:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
                    if part then pos = part.Position end
                end
            
                if pos then
                    local dist = (pos - rootPos).Magnitude
                    if dist < closestDist then
                        closestDist = dist
                        closestPoint = obj
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    
        if closestPoint and closestDist <= 4 then
            pcall(function()
                palletRemote:FireServer(closestPoint)
            end)
            for i, obj in ipairs(STATE.palletPointsCache) do
                if obj == closestPoint then
                    STATE._palletPointMap[obj] = nil
                    table.remove(STATE.palletPointsCache, i)
                    break
                end
            end
        
            STATE.autoDropCooldown = true
            task.delay(2, function()
                STATE.autoDropCooldown = false
            end)
        end
    end)
end

-- ============================================================
--  GAME SLOW DETECTOR (Parry / Item / Cooldown Slow)
-- ============================================================
local IsGameSlowActive, IsPlayerDoingAction
do
    local function SetupGameSlowDetector()
        task.spawn(function()
            local ok, mechanics = pcall(function()
                return game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Remotes", 10):WaitForChild("Mechanics", 10)
            end)
            if not ok or not mechanics then return end

            local slow = mechanics:FindFirstChild("Slow")
            if slow and slow:IsA("BindableEvent") then
                slow.Event:Connect(function(_, dur, extra)
                    local total = (tonumber(dur) or 0) + (tonumber(extra) or 0)
                    if total > 0 then STATE.gameSlowUntil = tick() + total + 0.1 end
                end)
            end

            local slowServer = mechanics:FindFirstChild("Slowserver")
            if slowServer and slowServer:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                slowServer.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(_, dur)
                    local total = tonumber(dur) or 0
                    if total > 0 then STATE.gameSlowUntil = tick() + total + 0.1 end
                end)
            end
        end)
    end

    function IsGameSlowActive()
        return tick() < STATE.gameSlowUntil
    end

    function IsPlayerDoingAction()
        local char = localPlayer.Character
        if not char then return false end
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if root and CollectionService:HasTag(root, "doing action") then return true end
        return false
    end

    SetupGameSlowDetector()
end

-- ============================================================
--  SHOW HOOK COUNTER SYSTEM (KillerTab)
-- ============================================================
do
    local function HookCounter_GetCount(player)
        if not player then return 0 end
        local ok, val = pcall(function() return player:GetAttribute("HookCount") end)
        if ok and typeof(val) == "number" then return val end
        local v = player:FindFirstChild("HookCount")
        if v and v:IsA("ValueBase") then return tonumber(v.Value) or 0 end
        local char = player.Character
        if char then
            local ok2, cval = pcall(function() return char:GetAttribute("HookCount") end)
            if ok2 and typeof(cval) == "number" then return cval end
            local cv = char:FindFirstChild("HookCount")
            if cv and cv:IsA("ValueBase") then return tonumber(cv.Value) or 0 end
        end
        return 0
    end

    local function HookCounter_Update(enabled)
        local playerGui = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        if not playerGui then return end
        for _, gui in ipairs(playerGui:GetChildren()) do
            if gui:IsA("ScreenGui") and gui.Name:match("%-mob$") then
                local frame = gui:FindFirstChild("Frame")
                if frame then
                    for i = 1, 5 do
                        local survivorFrame = frame:FindFirstChild("Survivor" .. i)
                        local imageLabel = survivorFrame and survivorFrame:FindFirstChild("ImageLabel")
                        local textLabel = survivorFrame and survivorFrame:FindFirstChild("TextLabel")
                        if imageLabel and textLabel then
                            local labelName = "Bolong_CustomHookCounter"
                            local customLabel = imageLabel:FindFirstChild(labelName)
                            local counter = imageLabel:FindFirstChild("Counter")
                            local function HideSlot(cacheKey)
                                if counter then pcall(function() counter.Visible = false end) end
                                if customLabel then customLabel.Visible = false end
                                if cacheKey then STATE.hookCounterCache[cacheKey] = nil end
                            end

                            if not enabled then
                                HideSlot(nil)
                                continue
                            end

                            local rawName = textLabel.Text and textLabel.Text:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or ""
                            local lower = rawName:lower()
                            local isEmptyText = (rawName == "" or lower == "waiting" or lower:find("waiting") or lower == "empty" or rawName:match("^Survivor%d+$"))
                            local isFrameVisible = true
                            pcall(function()
                                if survivorFrame.Visible == false then isFrameVisible = false end
                                if imageLabel.Image == "" or imageLabel.Image == "rbxasset://textures/ui/GuiImagePlaceholder.png" then
                                end
                            end)
                            if not isFrameVisible then
                                HideSlot(nil)
                                continue
                            end

                            local player = nil
                            if not isEmptyText then
                                for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                                    if p.Name == rawName or p.DisplayName == rawName then
                                        player = p
                                        break
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                            if not player then
                                HideSlot(nil)
                                continue
                            end

                            local hookCount = HookCounter_GetCount(player)
                            STATE.hookCounterCache[player.Name] = hookCount

                            if hookCount <= 0 then
                                if counter then pcall(function() counter.Visible = false end) end
                                if not customLabel then
                                    customLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                                    customLabel.Name = labelName
                                    customLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.35, 0)
                                    customLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.65, 0)
                                    customLabel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                                    customLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
                                    customLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                                    customLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                                    customLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
                                    customLabel.TextScaled = true
                                    customLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
                                    customLabel.BorderSizePixel = 0
                                    customLabel.Parent = imageLabel
                                end
                                customLabel.Visible = true
                                customLabel.Text = "Hooks: 0"
                                customLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                                continue
                            end

                            if counter then pcall(function() counter.Visible = true end) end
                            if not customLabel then
                                customLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                                customLabel.Name = labelName
                                customLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0.35, 0)
                                customLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.65, 0)
                                customLabel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                                customLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
                                customLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                                customLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                                customLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
                                customLabel.TextScaled = true
                                customLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
                                customLabel.BorderSizePixel = 0
                                customLabel.Parent = imageLabel
                            end
                            customLabel.Visible = true
                            if hookCount >= 3 then
                                customLabel.Text = "DEAD"
                                customLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 75, 75)
                            elseif hookCount == 2 then
                                customLabel.Text = "Hooks: 2"
                                customLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 140, 0)
                            else
                                customLabel.Text = "Hooks: 1"
                                customLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(210, 130, 255)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local function HookCounter_SetupPlayer(player)
        if not player or STATE.hookCounterAttrConns[player] then return end
        pcall(function()
            local conn = player:GetAttributeChangedSignal("HookCount"):Connect(function()
                STATE.hookCounterDirty = true
            end)
            STATE.hookCounterAttrConns[player] = conn
        end)
        pcall(function()
            local v = player:FindFirstChild("HookCount")
            if v and v:IsA("ValueBase") then
                local c2 = v.Changed:Connect(function() STATE.hookCounterDirty = true end)
                STATE.hookCounterAttrConns[player.Name .. "_val"] = c2
            end
        end)
        if player.Character then
            pcall(function()
                local cc = player.Character:GetAttributeChangedSignal("HookCount"):Connect(function()
                    STATE.hookCounterDirty = true
                end)
                STATE.hookCounterAttrConns[player.Name .. "_char"] = cc
            end)
        end
        pcall(function()
            player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
                if not CONFIG.showHookCounterEnabled then return end
                task.wait(0.5)
                pcall(function()
                    local cc = char:GetAttributeChangedSignal("HookCount"):Connect(function()
                        STATE.hookCounterDirty = true
                    end)
                    STATE.hookCounterAttrConns[player.Name .. "_char"] = cc
                end)
                STATE.hookCounterDirty = true
            end)
        end)
    end

    local function HookCounter_TeardownAllConns()
        for k, conn in pairs(STATE.hookCounterAttrConns) do
            pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
            STATE.hookCounterAttrConns[k] = nil
        end
        if STATE.hookCounterGuiConn then
            pcall(function() STATE.hookCounterGuiConn:Disconnect() end)
            STATE.hookCounterGuiConn = nil
        end
        if STATE.hookCounterPlayerAddedConn then
            pcall(function() STATE.hookCounterPlayerAddedConn:Disconnect() end)
            STATE.hookCounterPlayerAddedConn = nil
        end
        if STATE.hookCounterPlayerRemovingConn then
            pcall(function() STATE.hookCounterPlayerRemovingConn:Disconnect() end)
            STATE.hookCounterPlayerRemovingConn = nil
        end
    end

    local function HookCounter_Enable()
        if STATE.hookCounterGuiConn then return end
        STATE.hookCounterDirty = true
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            HookCounter_SetupPlayer(p)
        end
        STATE.hookCounterPlayerAddedConn = Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(p)
            task.wait(0.5)
            HookCounter_SetupPlayer(p)
            STATE.hookCounterDirty = true
        end)
        STATE.hookCounterPlayerRemovingConn = Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(p)
            STATE.hookCounterCache[p.Name] = nil
            local c = STATE.hookCounterAttrConns[p]
            if c then pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end); STATE.hookCounterAttrConns[p]=nil end
            local c2 = STATE.hookCounterAttrConns[p.Name .. "_val"]
            if c2 then pcall(function() c2:Disconnect() end); STATE.hookCounterAttrConns[p.Name .. "_val"]=nil end
            local c3 = STATE.hookCounterAttrConns[p.Name .. "_char"]
            if c3 then pcall(function() c3:Disconnect() end); STATE.hookCounterAttrConns[p.Name .. "_char"]=nil end
            STATE.hookCounterDirty = true
        end)
        local pg = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        if pg then
            STATE.hookCounterGuiConn = pg.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                if child:IsA("ScreenGui") and child.Name:match("%-mob$") then
                    STATE.hookCounterDirty = true
                end
                if child.Name == "Frame" and child.Parent and child.Parent.Name:match("%-mob$") then
                    STATE.hookCounterDirty = true
                end
            end)
            local descConn = pg.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(desc)
                if desc.Name == "Survivor1" or desc.Name == "ImageLabel" then
                    local top = desc:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("ScreenGui")
                    if top and top.Name:match("%-mob$") then
                        STATE.hookCounterDirty = true
                    end
                end
            end)
            STATE.hookCounterAttrConns["_guiDesc"] = descConn
        end
        HookCounter_Update(true)
    end

    local function HookCounter_Disable()
        HookCounter_TeardownAllConns()
        STATE.hookCounterCache = {}
        STATE.hookCounterDirty = true
        HookCounter_Update(false)
        local playerGui = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        if playerGui then
            for _, gui in ipairs(playerGui:GetChildren()) do
                if gui:IsA("ScreenGui") and gui.Name:match("%-mob$") then
                    local frame = gui:FindFirstChild("Frame")
                    if frame then
                        for i = 1, 5 do
                            local survivorFrame = frame:FindFirstChild("Survivor" .. i)
                            local imageLabel = survivorFrame and survivorFrame:FindFirstChild("ImageLabel")
                            local customLabel = imageLabel and imageLabel:FindFirstChild("Bolong_CustomHookCounter")
                            if customLabel then pcall(function() customLabel:Destroy() end) end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    RegisterTask("HookCounter", 0.5, function()
        if not CONFIG.showHookCounterEnabled then return end
        if not STATE.hookCounterDirty then
            local hasChange = false
            for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                local cur = HookCounter_GetCount(p)
                if STATE.hookCounterCache[p.Name] ~= cur then hasChange = true break end
            end
            if not hasChange then return end
            STATE.hookCounterDirty = true
        end
        STATE.hookCounterDirty = false
        HookCounter_Update(true)
    end)

    _G.Bolong_SetShowHookCounter = function(enabled)
        CONFIG.showHookCounterEnabled = enabled and true or false
        if CONFIG.showHookCounterEnabled then
            HookCounter_Enable()
        else
            HookCounter_Disable()
        end
    end
    getgenv().Bolong_SetShowHookCounter = _G.Bolong_SetShowHookCounter
    STATE._HookCounter_Enable = HookCounter_Enable
    STATE._HookCounter_Disable = HookCounter_Disable
    STATE._HookCounter_Update = HookCounter_Update
end

-- ============================================================
--  MOVEMENT SYSTEM
-- ============================================================
local _GetDesiredSpeed, _ApplySpeed, _StartAntiFallSlow, _StopAntiFallSlow, _StartNoSlowdown, _StopNoSlowdown, SetupMovementForChar
do
    function _GetDesiredSpeed()
        if STATE.SpeedBoost then
            return BASE_SPEED * (1 + math.clamp(tonumber(STATE.speedMult) or 0.3, 0, 2))
        end
        return BASE_SPEED
    end

    function _ApplySpeed(hum, desired)
        if not hum or not hum.Parent then return end
        if math.abs(hum.WalkSpeed - desired) > 0.05 then
            pcall(function() hum.WalkSpeed = desired end)
        end
    end

    local function _HookFallRemote()
        task.spawn(function()
            local ok, remote = pcall(function()
                return game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):WaitForChild("Remotes", 10):WaitForChild("Mechanics", 10):WaitForChild("Fall", 10)
            end)
            if not ok or not remote then return end
            local oldNamecall
            oldNamecall = hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", function(self, ...)
                local method = getnamecallmethod()
                if STATE.AntiFallSlow and method == "FireServer" and rawequal(self, remote) then return end
                return oldNamecall(self, ...)
            end)
        end)
    end

    function _StartAntiFallSlow(hum)
        if STATE.movConns.antiFall then STATE.movConns.antiFall:Disconnect() end
        _HookFallRemote()
        if not hum then return end
        pcall(function() hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, false) end)
        STATE.movConns.antiFall = hum.StateChanged:Connect(function(_, newState)
            if not STATE.AntiFallSlow then return end
            if newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed or newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp or newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then
                pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) end)
                _ApplySpeed(hum, _GetDesiredSpeed())
            end
        end)
    end

    function _StopAntiFallSlow(hum)
        if STATE.movConns.antiFall then STATE.movConns.antiFall:Disconnect(); STATE.movConns.antiFall = nil end
        if hum and hum.Parent then pcall(function() hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, true) end) end
    end

    function _StartNoSlowdown(hum)
        if STATE.movConns.noSlow then STATE.movConns.noSlow:Disconnect(); STATE.movConns.noSlow = nil end
        if not hum then return end
        if getRole(localPlayer) == "killer" then return end
        STATE.movConns.noSlow = hum:GetPropertyChangedSignal("WalkSpeed"):Connect(function()
            if not STATE.NoSlowdown then return end
            if getRole(localPlayer) == "killer" then return end
        
            local char = localPlayer.Character
            if STATE.safeModeSpeed and char and (char:GetAttribute("IsHooked") or char:GetAttribute("IsCarried") or hum.Health <= 50) then
                return
            end
            if STATE.safeModeNoSlow and (IsGameSlowActive() or IsPlayerDoingAction()) then
                return
            end
        
            local desired = _GetDesiredSpeed()
            if hum.WalkSpeed < desired - 0.05 then pcall(function() hum.WalkSpeed = desired end) end
        end)
    end

    function _StopNoSlowdown()
        if STATE.movConns.noSlow then STATE.movConns.noSlow:Disconnect(); STATE.movConns.noSlow = nil end
    end

    function SetupMovementForChar(char)
        if not char then return end
        local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 5)
        if not hum then return end
        if STATE.AntiFallSlow then _StartAntiFallSlow(hum) end
        if STATE.NoSlowdown and getRole(localPlayer) ~= "killer" then _StartNoSlowdown(hum) end
        if STATE.SpeedBoost then
            local desired = _GetDesiredSpeed()
            task.wait(0.15)
            if not (STATE.safeModeSpeed and (IsGameSlowActive() or IsPlayerDoingAction())) then
                _ApplySpeed(hum, desired)
            end
        end
    end

    localPlayer:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Team"):Connect(function()
        refreshRole(localPlayer)
        if getRole(localPlayer) == "killer" and STATE.movConns.noSlow then
            STATE.movConns.noSlow:Disconnect(); STATE.movConns.noSlow = nil
        end
        if getRole(localPlayer) ~= "killer" then
            RemoveInfiniteLunge()
        end
    end)
end

-- ============================================================
--  MOONWALK LOCK SYSTEM (SMOOTH ROTATION)
-- ============================================================
local CreateMoonwalkGui
do
    function CreateMoonwalkGui()
        if STATE.moonwalkGui then
            if STATE.moonwalkGui.Parent then return end
            STATE.moonwalkGui = nil
        end
    
        local PlayerGui = localPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
        local Gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        Gui.Name = "BolongHubMoonwalk"
        Gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
        Gui.Enabled = false 
        Gui.Parent = PlayerGui

        local Frame = Instance.new("Frame")
        Frame.Name = "MoonwalkBtns"
        Frame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1,1)
        Frame.Position = UDim2.new(1,-18,1,-170)
        Frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(46,96)
        Frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        Frame.Parent = Gui

        local function CreateButton(rotation, y)
            local Btn = Instance.new("ImageButton")
            Btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            Btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
            Btn.AutoButtonColor = false
            Btn.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5,0)
            Btn.Position = UDim2.new(0.5,0,0,y)
            Btn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(42,42)
            Btn.Image = "rbxassetid://125598796341580"
            Btn.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice
            Btn.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170,170,170)
            Btn.ImageTransparency = 0.25
            Btn.Rotation = rotation
            Btn.Parent = Frame
            return Btn
        end

        local Forward = CreateButton(-90,0)
        local Backward = CreateButton(90,50)

        local function setupBtnHold(btn, dir)
            btn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                    STATE.moonwalkMobileDir = dir
                    btn.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    btn.ImageTransparency = 0
                end
            end)
            btn.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
                if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                    STATE.moonwalkMobileDir = 0
                    btn.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170,170,170)
                    btn.ImageTransparency = 0.25
                end
            end)
        end

        setupBtnHold(Forward, 1)
        setupBtnHold(Backward, -1)

        STATE.moonwalkGui = Gui
    end

    RegisterTask("MoonwalkGuiGuard", 0.5, function()
        if not CONFIG.moonwalkEnabled then return end
        if not STATE.moonwalkGui or not STATE.moonwalkGui.Parent then
            CreateMoonwalkGui()
            if STATE.moonwalkGui then STATE.moonwalkGui.Enabled = true end
        elseif not STATE.moonwalkGui.Enabled then
            STATE.moonwalkGui.Enabled = true
        end
    end)

    -- ============================================================
    --  MOONWALK LOCK SYSTEM (SMOOTH PHYSICS-BASED)
    -- ============================================================
    local function SetupMoonwalkAlign(root)
        if root:FindFirstChild("BolongMoonwalkAlign") then return root:FindFirstChild("BolongMoonwalkAlign") end
    
        local att = Instance.new("Attachment")
        att.Name = "BolongMoonwalkAtt"
        att.Parent = root

        local alignOri = Instance.new("AlignOrientation")
        alignOri.Name = "BolongMoonwalkAlign"
        alignOri.Mode = Enum.OrientationAlignmentMode.OneAttachment
        alignOri.Attachment0 = att
        alignOri.MaxTorque = 6000000
        alignOri.Responsiveness = 65
        alignOri.Parent = root

        return alignOri
    end

    local function RemoveMoonwalkAlign(root)
        local alignOri = root:FindFirstChild("BolongMoonwalkAlign")
        local att = root:FindFirstChild("BolongMoonwalkAtt")
        if alignOri then alignOri:Destroy() end
        if att then att:Destroy() end
    end

    RegisterTask("MoonwalkLock", 0, function(dt)
        if not CONFIG.moonwalkEnabled then return end

        local char = localPlayer.Character
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera

        if not hum or not root or not cam then return end

        local targetDir = 0
        if STATE.moonwalkPCForward then targetDir = 1 end
        if STATE.moonwalkPCBackward then targetDir = -1 end
        if STATE.moonwalkMobileDir ~= 0 then targetDir = STATE.moonwalkMobileDir end

        if targetDir == 0 then
            if not hum.AutoRotate then hum.AutoRotate = true end
            RemoveMoonwalkAlign(root)
            STATE.moonwalkYc = 0
            return
        end

        hum.AutoRotate = false
        local alignOri = SetupMoonwalkAlign(root)

        local camLook = cam.CFrame.LookVector
        local flatCam = Vector3.new(camLook.X, 0, camLook.Z)
    
        if flatCam.Magnitude > 0.001 then
            flatCam = flatCam.Unit
            local targetLook = (targetDir == 1) and flatCam or -flatCam

            -- ============================================================
            --  CLASSIC
            -- ============================================================
            if (CONFIG.moonwalkMode or "Classic") == "Classic" then
                alignOri.Responsiveness = 15
                STATE.moonwalkYc = 0
                local targetCFrame = CFrame.lookAt(Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), targetLook)

                alignOri.CFrame = targetCFrame
            else
                alignOri.Responsiveness = 65
                local baseYaw = math.atan2(targetLook.X, targetLook.Z)

                local swayOffset = 0
                if hum.MoveDirection.Magnitude > 0.01 then
                    local swaySpeed = CONFIG.moonwalkSwaySpeed or 6
                    local swayAmp = CONFIG.moonwalkSwayAmp or 0.55
                    local shaking = 0.06
                    local interval = 1.2 / swaySpeed
                    if tick() - STATE.moonwalkWc >= interval then
                        STATE.moonwalkWc = tick()
                        STATE.moonwalkXc = -STATE.moonwalkXc
                    end
                    local targetSway = STATE.moonwalkXc * swayAmp
                    local lerpFactor = math.clamp(dt * 14, 0, 1)
                    STATE.moonwalkYc = STATE.moonwalkYc + (targetSway - STATE.moonwalkYc) * lerpFactor
                    local jitter = (math.random() - 0.5) * shaking
                    swayOffset = STATE.moonwalkYc + jitter
                else
                    STATE.moonwalkYc = 0
                    swayOffset = 0
                end

                local finalYaw = baseYaw + swayOffset
                local targetCFrame = CFrame.Angles(0, finalYaw, 0)
                alignOri.CFrame = targetCFrame
            end
        end
    end)
end

-- ============================================================
--  GOD MODE
-- ============================================================
local function StartGodMode()
    STATE.godEnabled = true; STATE.godLastHP = nil
    local char = localPlayer.Character
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then
        pcall(function() hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, false) end)
        pcall(function() hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, false) end)
        pcall(function() hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, false) end)
    end
end

local function StopGodMode()
    STATE.godEnabled = false; STATE.godLastHP = nil
    local char = localPlayer.Character
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then
        pcall(function() hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead, true) end)
        pcall(function() hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll, true) end)
        pcall(function() hum:SetStateEnabled(Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown, true) end)
    end
end

RegisterTask("GodMode", 0.2, function()
    if not STATE.godEnabled then return end
    local char = localPlayer.Character
    if not char then return end
    local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not hum then return end
    local maxHP = hum.MaxHealth
    local curHP = hum.Health
    if STATE.godLastHP ~= nil and curHP < STATE.godLastHP and curHP > 0 then
        pcall(function() hum.Health = maxHP end)
    end
    STATE.godLastHP = hum.Health
    pcall(function()
        local st = hum:GetState()
        if st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Dead then
            hum.Health = maxHP; hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running); STATE.godLastHP = maxHP
        elseif st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown then
            hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
        end
    end)
end)

-- ============================================================
--  AUTO GENERATOR
-- ============================================================
local PressSkill
do
        local KS_State = { active = false, target = nil, generator = nil }
    local KS_StartRemote = ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.KillerPerks.kingscourge:WaitForChild("KingScourgeStart")
    local KS_EndRemote = ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.KillerPerks.kingscourge:WaitForChild("KingScourgeEnd")

    KS_StartRemote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(gen, point, hitCount)
        KS_State.active = true; KS_State.target = point; KS_State.generator = gen
    end)
    KS_EndRemote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function()
        KS_State.active = false; KS_State.target = nil; KS_State.generator = nil
    end)

    local function GetSkillCheck()
        for _, name in ipairs({"SkillCheckPromptGui", "SkillCheckPromptGui-con"}) do
            local gui = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild(name, true)
            if gui then
                local check = gui:FindFirstChild("Check", true)
                if check and check.Visible then
                    return check:FindFirstChild("Line", true), check:FindFirstChild("Goal", true)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local _cachedActionBtn = nil
    local function FindActionButton()
        if _cachedActionBtn and _cachedActionBtn.Parent then return _cachedActionBtn end
        local survivorMob = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("Survivor-mob", true)
        if not survivorMob then return nil end
        local controls = survivorMob:FindFirstChild("Controls", true)
        if not controls then return nil end
        local action = controls:FindFirstChild("action")
        if action and action:IsA("GuiButton") then _cachedActionBtn = action; return action end
        action = controls:FindFirstChild("Gui-mob")
        if action and action:IsA("GuiButton") then _cachedActionBtn = action; return action end
        return nil
    end

    function PressSkill()
        local actionBtn = FindActionButton()
        if actionBtn and type(firesignal) == "function" then
            firesignal(actionBtn.MouseButton1Down)
            task.delay(0.05, function()
                if actionBtn and actionBtn.Parent then
                    firesignal(actionBtn.MouseButton1Up)
                    firesignal(actionBtn.MouseButton1Click)
                end
            end)
            return
        end
        local btn = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("check", true)
        if btn and btn:IsA("GuiObject") and btn.Visible then
            local p = btn.AbsolutePosition; local s = btn.AbsoluteSize
            local inset = GuiService:GetGuiInset()
            local x = p.X + (s.X / 2) + inset.X; local y = p.Y + (s.Y / 2) + inset.Y
            pcall(function()
                VirtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(x, y, 0, true, game, 1)
                task.wait(0.01)
                VirtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(x, y, 0, false, game, 1)
            end)
        else
            VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.Space, false, game)
            task.wait()
            VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.Space, false, game)
        end
    end

    RegisterTask("Generator", 0, function()
        if not CONFIG.autoGenerator then return end
        local line, goal = GetSkillCheck()
        if not (line and goal) then
            STATE.instantLastVisible = false
            STATE.lastGoalRot = nil
            STATE.prevLr = nil
            return
        end
    
        local gr = goal.Rotation
        local lr = line.Rotation
        local now = tick()
    
        local cooldown = KS_State.active and 0.05 or 0.1
        if now - STATE.lastPressTime < cooldown then 
            STATE.prevLr = lr
            return 
        end

        if CONFIG.autoGeneratorMode == "Instant" then
            if not STATE.instantLastVisible or gr ~= STATE.lastGoalRot then
                line.Rotation = gr + 109
                STATE.lastGoalRot = gr
                STATE.instantLastVisible = true
                STATE.lastPressTime = now
                STATE.lastSkillHit = now
                PressSkill()
            end

        else
            local diff = (lr - gr) % 360
        
            local prevDiff = -1
            if STATE.prevLr and STATE.lastGoalRot == gr then
                prevDiff = (STATE.prevLr - gr) % 360
            end
            STATE.lastGoalRot = gr
        
            local zoneStart, zoneEnd
            if CONFIG.autoGeneratorMode == "Perfect" then
                zoneStart = 102
                zoneEnd = 116
            elseif CONFIG.autoGeneratorMode == "Normal" then
                zoneStart = 116
                zoneEnd = 159
            elseif CONFIG.autoGeneratorMode == "Random" then
                if not STATE.randomIsNeutral then
                    zoneStart = 102; zoneEnd = 116
                else
                    zoneStart = 116; zoneEnd = 159
                end
            else
                return
            end

            local inZone = diff >= zoneStart and diff <= zoneEnd
        
            local skippedZone = prevDiff >= 0 and prevDiff < zoneStart and diff > zoneEnd
        
            if inZone or skippedZone then
                if skippedZone then
                    line.Rotation = gr + (zoneStart + zoneEnd) / 2
                end
            
                STATE.lastPressTime = now
                STATE.lastSkillHit = now
                PressSkill()
            
                if CONFIG.autoGeneratorMode == "Random" then
                    STATE.randomIsNeutral = not STATE.randomIsNeutral
                end
            end
        end
    
        STATE.prevLr = lr
    end)

    RegisterTask("GenBtnRefresh", 2.0, function()
        if not CONFIG.autoGenerator then return end
        if not _cachedActionBtn or not _cachedActionBtn.Parent then FindActionButton() end
    end)
end

-- ============================================================
--  GENERATOR BOOST (GENBOOST)
-- ============================================================
function IsGeneratorPoint(obj)
    if not obj then return false end
    local original = obj:GetAttribute("OriginalName")
    if original and string.find(tostring(original):lower(), "generatorpoint") then return true end
    if string.find(obj.Name, "^GeneratorPoint%d+$") then return true end
    return false
end

function CollectGeneratorPoints(gen)
    local points = {}
    if not gen then return points end
    local seen = {}
    local function consider(obj)
        if seen[obj] then return end
        if IsGeneratorPoint(obj) then
            seen[obj] = true
            table.insert(points, obj)
        end
    end
    local function collectGenPoints(parent)
        for _, child in ipairs(parent:GetChildren()) do
            consider(child)
            collectGenPoints(child)
        end
    end
    collectGenPoints(gen)
    table.sort(points, function(a, b)
        local an = a:GetAttribute("OriginalName") or a.Name
        local bn = b:GetAttribute("OriginalName") or b.Name
        return tostring(an) < tostring(bn)
    end)
    return points
end

function FindGenForPoint(point)
    if not point then return nil end
    local current = point
    for _ = 1, 6 do
        local parent = current.Parent
        if not parent then break end
        if parent:IsA("Model") or parent:IsA("Folder") then
            local name = parent.Name:lower()
            if string.find(name, "generator") or string.find(name, "gens") then
                return parent
            end
            for _, child in ipairs(parent:GetChildren()) do
                if IsGeneratorPoint(child) then return parent end
            end
        end
        current = parent
    end
    for _, gen in ipairs(STATE.cachedMapObjects.Generators) do
        if gen and gen.Parent and point:IsDescendantOf(gen) then return gen end
    end
    return nil
end

function GetAllGenerators()
    local gens = {}
    local seen = {}
    local function add(gen)
        if gen and gen.Parent and not seen[gen] then
            seen[gen] = true
            table.insert(gens, gen)
        end
    end
    for _, gen in ipairs(STATE.cachedMapObjects.Generators) do add(gen) end
    local map = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
    if map then
        for _, folderName in ipairs({"Generators", "Generator", "Gens"}) do
            local folder = map:FindFirstChild(folderName)
            if folder then
                for _, gen in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do add(gen) end
            end
        end
    end
    return gens
end

function GetPointPosition(point)
    if point:IsA("BasePart") then return point.Position end
    local part = point:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
    if part then return part.Position end
    return nil
end

function PerformGenBoost()
    task.spawn(function()
        local ok, err = pcall(function()
            local char = localPlayer.Character
            if not char then
                Notify("GenBoost", "Character not found!", 2)
                return
            end
            local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not hrp then
                Notify("GenBoost", "Character HumanoidRootPart not found!", 2)
                return
            end

            local checkInterractable = char:FindFirstChild("CheckInterractable")
            local isRepairing = checkInterractable and checkInterractable:GetAttribute("isRepairing") or false
            if not isRepairing then
                Notify("GenBoost", "Repair a generator first!", 2)
                return
            end

            local gens = GetAllGenerators()
            local currentPoint = nil
            local bestDist = math.huge
            for _, gen in ipairs(gens) do
                for _, point in ipairs(CollectGeneratorPoints(gen)) do
                    local pos = GetPointPosition(point)
                    local dist = pos and (pos - hrp.Position).Magnitude or math.huge
                    if dist < bestDist then
                        bestDist = dist
                        currentPoint = point
                    end
                end
            end

            local gen = currentPoint and FindGenForPoint(currentPoint)
            if not gen then
                local fallbackPoint = nil
                local fallbackDist = math.huge
                for _, g in ipairs(gens) do
                    for _, point in ipairs(CollectGeneratorPoints(g)) do
                        local pos = GetPointPosition(point)
                        local dist = pos and (pos - hrp.Position).Magnitude or math.huge
                        if dist < fallbackDist then
                            fallbackDist = dist
                            fallbackPoint = point
                        end
                    end
                end
                if fallbackPoint then
                    currentPoint = fallbackPoint
                    gen = FindGenForPoint(fallbackPoint)
                end
            end

            if not gen then
                Notify("GenBoost", "Could not find current generator!", 2)
                return
            end

            local points = CollectGeneratorPoints(gen)
            if #points == 0 then
                Notify("GenBoost", "No generator points resolved!", 2)
                return
            end

            local repairEvent = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
                and ReplicatedStorage.Remotes:FindFirstChild("Generator")
                and ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.Generator:FindFirstChild("RepairEvent")
            if not repairEvent then repairEvent = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("RepairEvent", true) end
            if not repairEvent then
                Notify("GenBoost", "RepairEvent remote not found!", 2)
                return
            end

            local userPoint = currentPoint
            local found = false
            for _, p in ipairs(points) do
                if p == userPoint then found = true break end
            end
            if not userPoint or not found then userPoint = points[1] end

            for _, p in ipairs(points) do
                if p ~= userPoint then
                    repairEvent:FireServer(p, true)
                    task.wait(0.4)
                    repairEvent:FireServer(userPoint, true)
                    task.wait(0.15)
                    repairEvent:FireServer(userPoint, false)
                    task.wait(0.15)
                end
            end
            for _, p in ipairs(points) do
                if p ~= userPoint then
                    repairEvent:FireServer(p, true)
                    task.wait(0.3)
                end
            end
            repairEvent:FireServer(userPoint, false)
            task.wait(0.2)
            local tpPos = GetPointPosition(userPoint)
            if tpPos and hrp then
                pcall(function() hrp.Anchored = false end)
                pcall(function() hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(tpPos + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0)) end)
            end
            task.wait(0.3)
            pcall(PressSkill)

            Notify("GenBoost", "Successfully applied generator Boost!", 2)
        end)
        if not ok then
            Notify("GenBoost", "Critical Error: " .. tostring(err), 2)
        end
    end)
end

function DestroyGenBoostButton()
    if STATE.genBoostCrouchConn then
        pcall(function() STATE.genBoostCrouchConn:Disconnect() end)
        STATE.genBoostCrouchConn = nil
    end
    if STATE.genBoostGui then
        pcall(function() STATE.genBoostGui:Destroy() end)
        STATE.genBoostGui = nil
    end
end

function BuildGenBoostGui(crouch)
    if not crouch or STATE.genBoostGui then return end
    local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    gui.Name = "BolongHubGenBoost"
    gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    gui.Parent = localPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

    local btn = Instance.new("ImageButton")
    btn.Name = "GenBoostBtn"
    btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    btn.AutoButtonColor = false
    btn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(76, 76)
    btn.Image = "rbxassetid://129980991442403"
    btn.ImageTransparency = 0.5
    btn.Draggable = false
    btn.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
    btn.Position = UDim2.new(
        crouch.Position.X.Scale,
        crouch.Position.X.Offset + 100,
        crouch.Position.Y.Scale,
        crouch.Position.Y.Offset
    )
    btn.Parent = gui

    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(PerformGenBoost)
    btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        btn.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 128, 0)
        task.delay(0.3, function()
            btn.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        end)
    end)

    STATE.genBoostGui = gui
end

function CreateGenBoostButton()
    if STATE.genBoostGui then return end

    local pGui = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
    if pGui then
        local sMob = pGui:FindFirstChild("Survivor-mob")
        if sMob then
            local ctrl = sMob:FindFirstChild("Controls")
            local crouch = ctrl and ctrl:FindFirstChild("crouch")
            if crouch and crouch:IsA("GuiButton") then
                BuildGenBoostGui(crouch)
                return
            end
        end
    end

    if STATE.genBoostCrouchConn then return end
    pGui = pGui or localPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
    STATE.genBoostCrouchConn = pGui.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
        if child and child.Name == "Survivor-mob" then
            if STATE.genBoostCrouchConn then
                STATE.genBoostCrouchConn:Disconnect()
                STATE.genBoostCrouchConn = nil
            end
            task.spawn(function()
                local ctrl = child:WaitForChild("Controls", 5)
                local crouch = ctrl and ctrl:WaitForChild("crouch", 5)
                if crouch and crouch:IsA("GuiButton") and CONFIG.genBoostEnabled then
                    BuildGenBoostGui(crouch)
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
end

-- ============================================================
--  KILLER WARN
-- ============================================================
RegisterTask("KillerWarn", 0.1, function()
    if not CONFIG.warnEnabled then
        local localChar = localPlayer.Character
        local myRoot = localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if myRoot then
            local warnGui = myRoot:FindFirstChild("KillerWarn")
            if warnGui then warnGui:Destroy() end
        end
        return
    end
    local localChar = localPlayer.Character
    local myRoot = localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not myRoot then return end
    local closestKillerDist = math.huge
    for player, esp in pairs(STATE.espObjects) do
        if player and player.Parent then
            local role = getRole(player)
            if role == "killer" then
                local char = player.Character
                local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if root then
                    local dist = (root.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude
                    if dist < closestKillerDist then closestKillerDist = dist end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local warnGui = myRoot:FindFirstChild("KillerWarn")
    if closestKillerDist <= CONFIG.warnDist1 then
        local txt, col
        if closestKillerDist <= CONFIG.warnDist3 then txt = "!!!"; col = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
        elseif closestKillerDist <= CONFIG.warnDist2 then txt = "!!"; col = Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 0)
        else txt = "!"; col = Color3.fromRGB(255, 160, 0) end
        if not warnGui then
            warnGui = CreateBillboardTag(txt, col, UDim2.new(0, 40, 0, 40), 22)
            warnGui.Name = "KillerWarn"; warnGui.Parent = myRoot
        else
            warnGui.Label.Text = txt; warnGui.Label.TextColor3 = col
        end
    elseif warnGui then
        warnGui:Destroy()
    end
end)

-- ============================================================
--  STUN TIMER SYSTEM
-- ============================================================
local StartStunTimer, StopStunTimer
do
    local STUN_TRACKS = { StunAnimation=true, WallHitStun=true, WallHitStun2=true, WipeMachete=true, Parried=true }
    local STUN_VM_IDS = {
        stunnedvm = "rbxassetid://108650759135855",
        parriedvm = "rbxassetid://87457855797515",
        wallhitvm = "rbxassetid://109632692854327",
        wipevm = "rbxassetid://71906161598748",
    }
    local STUN_TEXT_COLOR = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 60)
    STATE.stunTimerPulse = 0

    local function PreloadStunLengths()
        if STATE.stunTimerLens.stunnedvm then return true end
        local animator = nil
        pcall(function()
            local char = localPlayer.Character
            local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum then animator = hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator") end
        end)
        if not animator then return false end
        for key, assetId in pairs(STUN_VM_IDS) do
            if not STATE.stunTimerLens[key] then
                pcall(function()
                    local anim = Instance.new("Animation")
                    anim.AnimationId = assetId
                    local tr = animator:LoadAnimation(anim)
                    if tr and tr.Length and tr.Length > 0 then
                        STATE.stunTimerLens[key] = tr.Length
                    end
                    pcall(function() anim:Destroy() end)
                end)
            end
        end
        return STATE.stunTimerLens.stunnedvm ~= nil
    end

    local function RetryPreloadStunLengths()
        task.spawn(function()
            for _ = 1, 5 do
                if not STATE.stunTimerEnabled then return end
                local ok = false
                pcall(function() ok = PreloadStunLengths() end)
                if ok then return end
                task.wait(2)
            end
        end)
    end

    local function SeedStunFallback(dur)
        if not dur or dur <= 0 then return end
        local untilT = tick() + dur
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p ~= localPlayer and p.Parent and getRole(p) == "killer" then
                local cur = STATE.stunTimerForced[p]
                if not cur or cur < untilT then
                    STATE.stunTimerForced[p] = untilT
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local function DestroyStunBoard(player)
        local bb = STATE.stunTimerBoards[player]
        if bb then pcall(function() bb:Destroy() end) end
        STATE.stunTimerBoards[player] = nil
        STATE.stunTimerForced[player] = nil
        STATE.stunTimerAnim[player] = nil
    end

    local function DestroyAllStunBoards()
        local todo = {}
        for player, _ in pairs(STATE.stunTimerBoards) do todo[#todo + 1] = player end
        for i = 1, #todo do DestroyStunBoard(todo[i]) end
        for player, _ in pairs(STATE.stunTimerForced) do STATE.stunTimerForced[player] = nil end
        STATE.stunTimerPulse = 0
    end

    local function GetCachedStunAnimator(player, char)
        local an = STATE.stunTimerAnim[player]
        if an and an.Parent then return an end
        an = nil
        pcall(function()
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum then an = hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator") end
        end)
        STATE.stunTimerAnim[player] = an
        return an
    end

    local function GetKillerStunRemaining(player, char)
        local animator = GetCachedStunAnimator(player, char)
        if not animator then return 0 end
        local ok, tracks = pcall(function() return animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks() end)
        if not ok or type(tracks) ~= "table" then return 0 end
        local best = 0
        for i = 1, #tracks do
            local tr = tracks[i]
            local nm = nil
            local spd = 1
            local isPlay, len, pos = false, 0, 0
            pcall(function()
                nm = tr.Name
                local a = tr.Animation
                if a then
                    if not STUN_TRACKS[nm] and a.Name and STUN_TRACKS[a.Name] then
                        nm = a.Name
                    end
                    if not STUN_TRACKS[nm] and a.AnimationId then
                        local id = tostring(a.AnimationId)
                        for _, assetId in pairs(STUN_VM_IDS) do
                            if id == assetId or id:find(assetId:match("%d+"), 1, true) then
                                nm = "__vmid__"
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                isPlay = tr.IsPlaying
                len = tr.Length or 0
                pos = tr.TimePosition or 0
                spd = tr.Speed or 1
            end)
            if nm and (STUN_TRACKS[nm] or nm == "__vmid__") and isPlay then
                if spd == 0 then spd = 1 end
                local rem = (len - pos) / math.abs(spd)
                if rem > best then best = rem end
            end
        end
        return best
    end

    local function GetStunBoard(player, head)
        local bb = STATE.stunTimerBoards[player]
        if bb and bb.Parent and bb.Adornee and bb.Adornee.Parent then
            if bb.Adornee ~= head then
                pcall(function() bb.Adornee = head end)
            end
            return bb
        end
        if bb then pcall(function() bb:Destroy() end) end
        bb = CreateBillboardTag("Stun 0.0s", STUN_TEXT_COLOR, UDim2.new(0, 110, 0, 30), 18)
        bb.Name = "StunTimer"
        pcall(function() bb.MaxDistance = STATE.stunTimerMaxDist end)
        pcall(function()
            bb.Adornee = head
            bb.Parent = head
            bb:SetAttribute("__t", "")
        end)
        STATE.stunTimerBoards[player] = bb
        return bb
    end

    local function ConnectStunEvents()
        if STATE.stunTimerConnected then return end
        STATE.stunTimerConnected = true
        pcall(function()
            local rs = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
            if not rs then return end
            local pallet = rs:FindFirstChild("Pallet")
            local jason = pallet and pallet:FindFirstChild("Jason")
            local stunEv = jason and jason:FindFirstChild("Stun")
            if stunEv and stunEv:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                table.insert(STATE.stunTimerConns, stunEv.OnClientEvent:Connect(function()
                    if STATE.stunTimerEnabled then
                        STATE.stunTimerPulse = tick()
                        if not STATE.stunTimerLens.stunnedvm then RetryPreloadStunLengths() end
                        SeedStunFallback(STATE.stunTimerLens.stunnedvm or 2.5)
                    end
                end))
            end
            local overEv = jason and jason:FindFirstChild("Stunover")
            if overEv and overEv:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                table.insert(STATE.stunTimerConns, overEv.OnClientEvent:Connect(function()
                    if STATE.stunTimerEnabled then
                        for player, _ in pairs(STATE.stunTimerForced) do STATE.stunTimerForced[player] = nil end
                        DestroyAllStunBoards()
                    end
                end))
            end
            local attacks = rs:FindFirstChild("Attacks")
            local afterEv = attacks and attacks:FindFirstChild("AfterAttack")
            if afterEv and afterEv:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                table.insert(STATE.stunTimerConns, afterEv.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(atkType)
                    if not STATE.stunTimerEnabled then return end
                    if atkType == "Parried" then
                        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                            if p ~= localPlayer and p.Parent and getRole(p) == "killer" then
                                STATE.stunTimerForced[p] = tick() + 4
                            end
                        end
                    elseif atkType == "WallHitStun" or atkType == "WallHitStun2" then
                        STATE.stunTimerPulse = tick()
                        if not STATE.stunTimerLens.wallhitvm then RetryPreloadStunLengths() end
                        SeedStunFallback(STATE.stunTimerLens.wallhitvm or 2.5)
                    elseif atkType == "WipeMachete" then
                        STATE.stunTimerPulse = tick()
                        if not STATE.stunTimerLens.wipevm then RetryPreloadStunLengths() end
                        SeedStunFallback(STATE.stunTimerLens.wipevm or 2.5)
                    end
                end))
            end
            local killers = rs:FindFirstChild("Killers")
            local hidden = killers and killers:FindFirstChild("Hidden")
            local endlag = hidden and hidden:FindFirstChild("endlag")
            if endlag and endlag:IsA("BindableEvent") then
                table.insert(STATE.stunTimerConns, endlag.Event:Connect(function()
                    if STATE.stunTimerEnabled then
                        STATE.stunTimerPulse = tick()
                        if not STATE.stunTimerLens.wipevm then RetryPreloadStunLengths() end
                        SeedStunFallback(STATE.stunTimerLens.wipevm or 2.5)
                    end
                end))
            end
            local gameFolder = rs:FindFirstChild("Game")
            local awardEv = gameFolder and gameFolder:FindFirstChild("PlayerActionEvent")
            if awardEv and awardEv:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                table.insert(STATE.stunTimerConns, awardEv.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(message)
                    if STATE.stunTimerEnabled and message == "Stunned the Killer" then
                        STATE.stunTimerPulse = tick()
                        if not STATE.stunTimerLens.stunnedvm then RetryPreloadStunLengths() end
                        SeedStunFallback(STATE.stunTimerLens.stunnedvm or 2.5)
                    end
                end))
            end
        end)
    end

    function StartStunTimer()
        STATE.stunTimerEnabled = true
        ConnectStunEvents()
        RetryPreloadStunLengths()
    end

    function StopStunTimer()
        STATE.stunTimerEnabled = false
        DestroyAllStunBoards()
    end

    RegisterTask("StunTimer", 0.1, function()
        if not STATE.stunTimerEnabled then
            if next(STATE.stunTimerBoards) ~= nil then DestroyAllStunBoards() end
            return
        end
        local localChar = localPlayer.Character
        local myRoot = localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local myPos = nil
        if myRoot then
            myPos = myRoot.Position
        else
            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            if cam then myPos = cam.CFrame.Position end
        end
        if not myPos then return end
        local now = tick()
        local maxD = STATE.stunTimerMaxDist
        for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if player ~= localPlayer and player.Parent and getRole(player) == "killer" then
                local char = player.Character
                local head = char and (char:FindFirstChild("Head") or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
                if char and char.Parent and head and head.Parent then
                    local dist = (head.Position - myPos).Magnitude
                    if dist <= maxD then
                        local remaining = 0
                        local forced = STATE.stunTimerForced[player]
                        if forced and forced > now then remaining = forced - now end
                        local trackRem = GetKillerStunRemaining(player, char)
                        if trackRem > remaining then remaining = trackRem end
                        if remaining > 0.05 then
                            local bb = GetStunBoard(player, head)
                            if bb then
                                if not bb.Enabled then bb.Enabled = true end
                                local txt = string.format("Stun %.1fs", remaining)
                                local last = bb:GetAttribute("__t")
                                if last ~= txt then
                                    bb:SetAttribute("__t", txt)
                                    local lbl = bb:FindFirstChild("Label")
                                    if lbl then lbl.Text = txt end
                                end
                            end
                        else
                            if now - (STATE.stunTimerPulse or 0) > 0.35 then
                                DestroyStunBoard(player)
                            end
                        end
                    else
                        local bb = STATE.stunTimerBoards[player]
                        if bb and bb.Enabled then bb.Enabled = false end
                    end
                else
                    DestroyStunBoard(player)
                end
            end
        end
        local orphans = nil
        for player, _ in pairs(STATE.stunTimerBoards) do
            if not player.Parent then
                orphans = orphans or {}
                orphans[#orphans + 1] = player
            end
        end
        if orphans then
            for i = 1, #orphans do DestroyStunBoard(orphans[i]) end
        end
    end)
end

RegisterTask("ESPValidator", 1, function()
    if not espFolder or not espFolder.Parent then
        ForceRefreshAllESP()
        return
    end
    for player, _ in pairs(STATE.espObjects) do
        ValidateAndReattachESP(player)
    end
    for player, _ in pairs(STATE.outlineObjects) do
        if not STATE.espObjects[player] then
            ValidateAndReattachESP(player)
        end
    end
end)

RegisterTask("EspDistCull", 0.25, function()
    if STATE.espObjects then UpdatePlayerEspDistances() end
end)
-- ============================================================
--  ITEM ESP UPDATER
-- ============================================================
RegisterTask("ItemESP", 0.2, function()
    if not CONFIG.espItemEnabled then
        for _, esp in pairs(STATE.espObjects) do
            if esp and esp.itemImage and esp.itemImage.Visible then
                esp.itemImage.Visible = false
            end
        end
        return
    end

    local localChar = localPlayer.Character
    local localRoot = localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

    for player, esp in pairs(STATE.espObjects) do
        if esp and esp.billboard and esp.billboard.Parent and esp.itemBillboard then
            
            if getRole(player) == "killer" then
                if esp.itemImage.Visible then esp.itemImage.Visible = false end
                continue
            end

            local char = player.Character
            local itemName = nil
            
            if char then
                local itemAttr = char:GetAttribute("EquippedItem") or char:GetAttribute("Equippedltem")
                if type(itemAttr) == "string" then itemName = itemAttr
                elseif typeof(itemAttr) == "Instance" then itemName = itemAttr.Name end
            end
            
            if not itemName then
                local pAttr = player:GetAttribute("EquippedItem") or player:GetAttribute("Equippedltem")
                if type(pAttr) == "string" then itemName = pAttr
                elseif typeof(pAttr) == "Instance" then itemName = pAttr.Name end
            end
