            local img = GetItemImage(itemName)
            
            if img then
                if esp.itemImage.Image ~= img then
                    esp.itemImage.Image = img
                end
                
                -- === LOGIKA UKURAN BERDASARKAN JARAK ===
                if localRoot and char then
                    local targetRoot = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if targetRoot then
                        local dist = (localRoot.Position - targetRoot.Position).Magnitude
                        
                        local size = 1.5 + ((dist / 200) * 2)
                        
                        size = math.clamp(size, 1.5, 3.5)
                        
                        esp.itemBillboard.Size = UDim2.new(size, 0, size, 0)
                    end
                end
                -- =======================================

                esp.itemImage.Visible = true
            else
                if esp.itemImage.Visible then
                    esp.itemImage.Visible = false
                end
            end
        elseif esp and esp.itemImage then
            if esp.itemImage.Visible then esp.itemImage.Visible = false end
        end
    end
end)

-- ============================================================
--  SCP / ZOMBIE ESP
-- ============================================================
local RemoveScpEsp, GetScpModelRoot, RefreshZombieCache, RefreshScpEsp
local zombieCache, zombieCacheTime
do
    local function IsScpModelName(name)
        if type(name) ~= "string" or name == "" then return false end
        local low = name:lower()
        if low:find("zombie") then return true end
        if low == "scp" then return true end
        for i = 1, 9 do
            if low == ("scp" .. i) or low:find("^scp%-" .. i) then return true end
        end
        return false
    end

    function RemoveScpEsp(model)
        local entry = STATE.scpEspObjects[model]
        if entry then
            if entry.highlight and entry.highlight.Parent then entry.highlight:Destroy() end
            if entry.billboard and entry.billboard.Parent then entry.billboard:Destroy() end
            STATE.scpEspObjects[model] = nil
        end
    end

    local function CreateScpEsp(model)
        local hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
        hl.Name = "__BolongScpHl__"; hl.Adornee = model
        hl.FillColor = CONFIG.espScpColor; hl.OutlineColor = CONFIG.espScpColor
        hl.FillTransparency = 1; hl.OutlineTransparency = 0
        hl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
        hl.Enabled = true; hl.Parent = GetEspFolder()

        local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
        billboard.Name = "__BolongScpBb__"; billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
        billboard.Size = UDim2.new(0, 140, 0, 18)
        billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)
        billboard.MaxDistance = CONFIG.espScpDistance
        billboard.Parent = GetEspFolder()

        local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        lbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0; lbl.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
        lbl.TextSize = 12; lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        lbl.Text = CONFIG.espScpShowName and "zombie" or ""
        lbl.Parent = billboard

        STATE.scpEspObjects[model] = { highlight = hl, billboard = billboard, label = lbl }
    end

    function GetScpModelRoot(model)
        local root = model.PrimaryPart
        if not root then
            local found = model:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if found and found:IsA("BasePart") then root = found end
        end
        if not root then
            for _, child in ipairs(model:GetChildren()) do
                if child:IsA("BasePart") then root = child break end
            end
        end
        return root
    end

    zombieCache = {}
    zombieCacheTime = 0

    local function SetupZombieCache()
        if STATE._zombieCacheReady then return end
        STATE._zombieCacheReady = true
        local zombieMap = {}
        local function AddZombieModel(model)
            if not model:IsA("Model") then return end
            if localPlayer.Character and model == localPlayer.Character then return end
            for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if plr.Character and model == plr.Character then return end
            end
            if not IsScpModelName(model.Name) then return end
            if zombieMap[model] then return end
            zombieMap[model] = true
            table.insert(zombieCache, model)
            if CONFIG.espScpEnabled and not STATE.scpEspObjects[model] then
                CreateScpEsp(model)
            end
        end
        local function RemoveZombieModel(model)
            if not zombieMap[model] then return end
            zombieMap[model] = nil
            for i, existing in ipairs(zombieCache) do
                if existing == model then
                    table.remove(zombieCache, i)
                    break
                end
            end
            RemoveScpEsp(model)
        end
        Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(AddZombieModel)
        Workspace.DescendantRemoving:Connect(RemoveZombieModel)
        local playerChars = {}
        for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if plr.Character then playerChars[plr.Character] = true end
        end
        for _, model in ipairs(workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if model:IsA("Model") and not playerChars[model] and IsScpModelName(model.Name) then
                zombieMap[model] = true
                table.insert(zombieCache, model)
            end
        end
        zombieCacheTime = tick()
    end
    function RefreshZombieCache()
        SetupZombieCache()
        zombieCacheTime = tick()
    end
    SetupZombieCache()

    function RefreshScpEsp()
        if not STATE.scpEspObjects then return end
        local camPos = GetCamPosition()
        local stale = {}
        for model, entry in pairs(STATE.scpEspObjects) do
            if not model or not model.Parent then
                stale[#stale + 1] = model
            else
                local within = true
                if camPos then
                    local root = GetScpModelRoot(model)
                    if root then
                        within = (root.Position - camPos).Magnitude <= CONFIG.espScpDistance
                    end
                end
                if entry.highlight and entry.highlight.Parent then
                    if entry.highlight.FillColor ~= CONFIG.espScpColor then
                        entry.highlight.FillColor = CONFIG.espScpColor
                        entry.highlight.OutlineColor = CONFIG.espScpColor
                    end
                    if entry.highlight.Enabled ~= within then entry.highlight.Enabled = within end
                end
                if entry.billboard then
                    entry.billboard.MaxDistance = CONFIG.espScpDistance
                    if entry.billboard.Enabled ~= within then entry.billboard.Enabled = within end
                    if entry.label then
                        local wantName = CONFIG.espScpShowName and "zombie" or ""
                        if entry.label.Text ~= wantName then entry.label.Text = wantName end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        for i = 1, #stale do RemoveScpEsp(stale[i]) end
    end

    RegisterTask("ScpESP", 0.5, function()
        if not CONFIG.espScpEnabled then
            for model, _ in pairs(STATE.scpEspObjects) do RemoveScpEsp(model) end
            return
        end

        local stale = {}
        for model, _ in pairs(STATE.scpEspObjects) do
            if not model or not model.Parent then stale[#stale + 1] = model end
        end
        for i = 1, #stale do RemoveScpEsp(stale[i]) end

        for i = 1, #zombieCache do
            local model = zombieCache[i]
            if model and model.Parent and not STATE.scpEspObjects[model] then
                CreateScpEsp(model)
            end
        end

        RefreshScpEsp()
    end)
end

-- ============================================================
--  SPEED BOOST
-- ============================================================
local SB_SetEnabled
do
    local function SB_ApplyNow()
        if not STATE.SpeedBoost then return end
        local char = localPlayer.Character
        if not char then return end
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum then return end

        if STATE.safeModeSpeed and (char:GetAttribute("IsHooked") or char:GetAttribute("IsCarried") or hum.Health <= 50) then
            return
        end
        if STATE.safeModeSpeed and (IsGameSlowActive() or IsPlayerDoingAction()) then
            return
        end

        if STATE.speedBoostPauseCrouch and char:GetAttribute("Crouching") then
            return
        end

        local desired = _GetDesiredSpeed()
        if desired <= 0 then return end
        local bonus = math.clamp(tonumber(STATE.speedMult) or 0.3, 0, 2)
        local jitterAmp = BASE_SPEED * bonus
        STATE._jitterFlip = not STATE._jitterFlip
        local target = STATE._jitterFlip and (desired + jitterAmp) or desired
        if math.abs(hum.WalkSpeed - target) > 0.1 then
            pcall(function() hum.WalkSpeed = target end)
        end
    end

    RegisterTask("SpeedBoost", 0, function()
        SB_ApplyNow()
    end)

    function SB_SetEnabled(on)
        STATE.SpeedBoost = on and true or false
        local char = localPlayer.Character
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not on then
            if hum then _ApplySpeed(hum, BASE_SPEED) end
            return
        end
        if hum then SB_ApplyNow() end
    end

    STATE.SB_Retarget = function()
        SB_ApplyNow()
    end
    STATE.SB_RefreshAll = function()
        SB_ApplyNow()
    end
end

-- ============================================================
--  SPEED BOOST V2
-- ============================================================
local SB2_SetEnabled
do
    local attrBaseline   = {}
    local attrLastWrite  = {}
    local attrMonitorCon = {}
    local sb2TeamWarned  = false

    local function WriteSpeedAttr(model, value)
        if not model or not model.Parent then return end
        local child = model:FindFirstChild("speedboost")
        if child and child:IsA("ValueBase") then
            child.Value = value
            return
        end
        model:SetAttribute("speedboost", value)
    end

    local function TeamFilterAllowsBoost()
        local team = localPlayer.Team
        local teamName = team and team.Name
        local filter = STATE.sb2TeamFilter or "Both"
        if filter == "Both" then
            return teamName == "Survivors" or teamName == "Killer"
        end
        return teamName == filter
    end

    local function ApplySpeedAttrToModel(model)
        if not model or not model.Parent then return end
        local baseValue = attrBaseline[model] or 1
        local finalValue
        if STATE.sb2Enabled and TeamFilterAllowsBoost() then
            local mult = math.clamp(tonumber(STATE.sb2Mult) or 1.3, 1, 3)
            if STATE.sb2CountPerks then
                finalValue = baseValue + (mult - 1)
            else
                finalValue = mult
            end
        else
            if STATE.sb2CountPerks then
                finalValue = baseValue
            else
                finalValue = 1
            end
        end
        attrLastWrite[model] = finalValue
        WriteSpeedAttr(model, finalValue)
    end

    local function MonitorSpeedAttr(model)
        if attrMonitorCon[model] then return end
        local current = model:GetAttribute("speedboost") or 1
        attrBaseline[model] = current
        attrLastWrite[model] = current
        attrMonitorCon[model] = model.AttributeChanged:Connect(function(attrName)
            if attrName ~= "speedboost" then return end
            local newValue = model:GetAttribute("speedboost") or 1
            local lastWrite = attrLastWrite[model]
            if lastWrite and math.abs(newValue - lastWrite) < 0.001 then return end
            attrBaseline[model] = newValue
            pcall(function() ApplySpeedAttrToModel(model) end)
        end)
    end

    local function ApplySpeedAttrToAll()
        if STATE.sb2Enabled and not TeamFilterAllowsBoost() then
            if not sb2TeamWarned then
                sb2TeamWarned = true
                Notify("Speed Boost V2", "Hanya work di dalam match, tidak work di lobby", 2)
            end
        else
            sb2TeamWarned = false
        end

        local playerName = localPlayer.Name
        if not playerName then return end

        local models = {}
        if localPlayer.Character then
            table.insert(models, localPlayer.Character)
        end
        local worldModel = Workspace:FindFirstChild(playerName)
        if worldModel and worldModel:IsA("Model") and not table.find(models, worldModel) then
            table.insert(models, worldModel)
        end
        local climbFolders = { "climb_obsessing", "climb_collisoning", "climb_collisioning", "climb_colliding" }
        for _, folderName in ipairs(climbFolders) do
            local folder = Workspace:FindFirstChild(folderName)
            local model = folder and folder:FindFirstChild(playerName)
            if model and model:IsA("Model") and not table.find(models, model) then
                table.insert(models, model)
            end
        end

        for model, conn in pairs(attrMonitorCon) do
            if not model or not model.Parent then
                pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
                attrMonitorCon[model] = nil
                attrBaseline[model] = nil
                attrLastWrite[model] = nil
            end
        end

        for _, model in ipairs(models) do
            MonitorSpeedAttr(model)
            ApplySpeedAttrToModel(model)
        end
    end

    RegisterTask("SpeedBoostV2", 0.35, function()
        if STATE.sb2Enabled or next(attrMonitorCon) ~= nil then
            ApplySpeedAttrToAll()
        end
    end)

    function SB2_SetEnabled(on)
        on = on and true or false
        if on and STATE.SpeedBoost and SB_SetEnabled then
            SB_SetEnabled(false)
            if STATE.SB_SyncToggle then pcall(STATE.SB_SyncToggle, false) end
        end
        STATE.sb2Enabled = on
        if not on then
            for model, baseValue in pairs(attrBaseline) do
                if model and model.Parent then
                    attrLastWrite[model] = baseValue
                    WriteSpeedAttr(model, baseValue)
                end
            end
        end
        ApplySpeedAttrToAll()
    end

    STATE.SB2_Refresh = function()
        ApplySpeedAttrToAll()
    end
end

-- ============================================================
--  HOLD LOCK DETECTION
-- ============================================================
do
    local function IsTouchInsideButton(btn, pos)
        if not btn or not btn.Parent then return false end
        local ap = btn.AbsolutePosition; local as = btn.AbsoluteSize
        local cx = ap.X + as.X / 2; local cy = ap.Y + as.Y / 2
        local r = math.min(as.X, as.Y) / 2 * 0.8
        local dx = pos.X - cx; local dy = pos.Y - cy
        return (dx*dx + dy*dy) <= (r*r)
    end

    local function HookMobButton(btn)
        if not btn:IsA("ImageButton") then return end
        if btn.Name ~= "Gui-mob" then return end
        if STATE._hookedMobButtons[btn] then return end
        STATE._hookedMobButtons[btn] = true
        UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
            if not IsTouchInsideButton(btn, input.Position) then return end
            STATE.HoldLockActive = true
            local conn
            conn = UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(ended)
                if ended == input then
                    STATE.HoldLockActive = false
                    conn:Disconnect()
                end
            end)
        end)
    end

    local _KILLER_MOB_WHITELIST = {
        ["Slasher-mob"] = true,
        ["Masked-mob"]  = true,
        ["Hidden-mob"]  = true,
        ["Killer-mob"]  = true,
    }

    local function IsSlasherAttackButton(btn)
        if not (btn and btn:IsA("GuiButton")) then return false end
        if btn.Name ~= "attack" then return false end
        local hasMob, hasControl = false, false
        local p = btn.Parent
        while p do
            if _KILLER_MOB_WHITELIST[p.Name] then hasMob = true end
            if p.Name == "Controls" or p.Name == "Control" then hasControl = true end
            p = p.Parent
        end
        return hasMob and hasControl
    end

    local function HookSlasherAttackButton(btn)
        if STATE._hookedSlasherButtons[btn] then return end
        STATE._hookedSlasherButtons[btn] = true
        btn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                STATE.HoldLockActive = true
            end
        end)
        btn.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                STATE.HoldLockActive = false
            end
        end)
    end

    local function ScanForMobButtons(parent)
        local function scanMobScreen(screen)
            if not screen then return end
            local mobBtn = screen:FindFirstChild("Gui-mob", true)
            if mobBtn and mobBtn:IsA("ImageButton") then HookMobButton(mobBtn) end
            local control = screen:FindFirstChild("Control") or screen:FindFirstChild("Controls")
            if control then
                local function walkControl(parent)
                    for _, child in ipairs(parent:GetChildren()) do
                        if IsSlasherAttackButton(child) then HookSlasherAttackButton(child) end
                        walkControl(child)
                    end
                end
                walkControl(control)
            end
        end
        for mobName, _ in pairs(_KILLER_MOB_WHITELIST) do
            scanMobScreen(parent:FindFirstChild(mobName))
        end
        parent.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(desc)
            if desc:IsA("ImageButton") and desc.Name == "Gui-mob" then HookMobButton(desc) end
            if IsSlasherAttackButton(desc) then HookSlasherAttackButton(desc) end
        end)
    end

    task.spawn(function()
        local pg = localPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
        ScanForMobButtons(pg)
    end)

    UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
        local isKiller = getRole(localPlayer) == "killer"
        if isKiller then
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then STATE.HoldLockActive = true end
        else
            if not gameProcessed and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then STATE.HoldLockActive = true end
        end
        if not gameProcessed and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1 and input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonL2 then STATE.HoldLockActive = true end
    end)

    UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 then 
            STATE.HoldLockActive = false 
        end
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1 and input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonL2 then STATE.HoldLockActive = false end
    end)

    RegisterTask("HoldLock", 0.2, function()
        if not CONFIG.cameralockEnabled then return end
        if STATE.HoldLockActive then return end
        local char = localPlayer.Character
        if char and char:GetAttribute("Aiming") == true then
            STATE.HoldLockActive = true
        end
    end)
end

-- ============================================================
--  CAMERALOCK
-- ============================================================
local StartCameralock, StopCameralock
do
    local function GetTorsoPosition(char)
        if not char then return nil end
        if CONFIG.cameralockAimPart == "Head" then
            local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
            if head then return head.Position end
        end
        local upperTorso = char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
        if upperTorso then return upperTorso.Position end
        local torso = char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
        if torso then return torso.Position end
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if root then 
            return root.Position + (CONFIG.cameralockAimPart == "Head" and Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0) or Vector3.new(0, 1.2, 0)) 
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function IsValidAimTarget(player, char, hum)
        if not char or not hum or hum.Health <= 0 then return false end
    
        if hum.Health < 50 then return false end
    
        if char:GetAttribute("IsHooked") then return false end
        if char:GetAttribute("IsCarried") then return false end
        if player:GetAttribute("IsHooked") then return false end
        if player:GetAttribute("IsCarried") then return false end
    
        local state = hum:GetState()
        if state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding then return false end
        if state == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics and hum.PlatformStand then return false end
    
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if root and root.Anchored then return false end
    
        return true
    end

    local function GetClosestTarget(currentTarget)
        local myChar = localPlayer.Character
        local myRoot = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not myRoot then return nil end
        local myPos = myRoot.Position
        local HYSTERESIS = 0.1
        local bestTarget = nil
        local bestDist = CONFIG.cameralockMaxDistance

        if CONFIG.cameralockTargetType == "Zombie" then
            if currentTarget and typeof(currentTarget) == "Instance" and not currentTarget:IsA("Player") then
                local root = GetScpModelRoot(currentTarget)
                if root and root:IsA("BasePart") and currentTarget.Parent then
                    local dist = (root.Position - myPos).Magnitude
                    if dist <= CONFIG.cameralockMaxDistance then
                        bestTarget = currentTarget
                        bestDist = dist - HYSTERESIS
                    end
                end
            end
            if tick() - zombieCacheTime > 0.5 then RefreshZombieCache() end
            for i = 1, #zombieCache do
                local model = zombieCache[i]
                if model and model.Parent then
                    local root = GetScpModelRoot(model)
                    if root and root:IsA("BasePart") then
                        local dist = (root.Position - myPos).Magnitude
                        if dist < bestDist then bestDist = dist; bestTarget = model end
                    end
                end
            end
            return bestTarget
        end

        if currentTarget and currentTarget.Character then
            local ctChar = currentTarget.Character
            local ctHum = ctChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local ctPos = GetTorsoPosition(ctChar)
            local ctRoleOk = true
            if CONFIG.cameralockTargetType == "Killer" and getRole(currentTarget) ~= "killer" then
                ctRoleOk = false
            elseif CONFIG.cameralockTargetType == "Survivor" and getRole(currentTarget) ~= "survivor" then
                ctRoleOk = false
            end
            if ctRoleOk and IsValidAimTarget(currentTarget, ctChar, ctHum) and ctPos then
                local ctDist = (ctPos - myPos).Magnitude
                if ctDist <= CONFIG.cameralockMaxDistance then
                    bestTarget = currentTarget
                    bestDist = ctDist - HYSTERESIS
                end
            end
        end
    
        for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if player == localPlayer then continue end
            local char = player.Character
            if not char then continue end
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not IsValidAimTarget(player, char, hum) then continue end
        
            local torsoPos = GetTorsoPosition(char)
            if not torsoPos then continue end
            local role = getRole(player)
            if CONFIG.cameralockTargetType == "Killer" and role ~= "killer" then continue end
            if CONFIG.cameralockTargetType == "Survivor" and role ~= "survivor" then continue end
            local dist = (torsoPos - myPos).Magnitude
            if dist < bestDist then bestDist = dist; bestTarget = player end
        end
        return bestTarget
    end

    function StartCameralock()
        if STATE.aimConn then STATE.aimConn:Disconnect() end
        STATE.aimTargetCache = nil; STATE.aimTargetT = 0; STATE.aimLastStableDir = nil
        STATE.aimConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
            if not CONFIG.cameralockEnabled then return end
            if CONFIG.cameralockLockMode == "Hold to Lock" and not STATE.HoldLockActive then
                STATE.aimTargetCache = nil; STATE.aimLastStableDir = nil; return
            end
            local myChar = localPlayer.Character
            if not myChar then return end
            local myRoot = myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if not myRoot then return end
            STATE.aimTargetT = STATE.aimTargetT + dt
            if STATE.aimTargetT >= 0.15 then
                STATE.aimTargetT = 0
                STATE.aimTargetCache = GetClosestTarget(STATE.aimTargetCache)
            end
            local target = STATE.aimTargetCache
            if not target then STATE.aimLastStableDir = nil; return end
        
            local torsoPos
            if typeof(target) == "Instance" and not target:IsA("Player") then
                local modelRoot = GetScpModelRoot(target)
                if not modelRoot or not modelRoot:IsA("BasePart") or not target.Parent then
                    STATE.aimTargetCache = nil; STATE.aimLastStableDir = nil; return
                end
                torsoPos = modelRoot.Position
            else
                local targetChar = target.Character
                if not targetChar then STATE.aimTargetCache = nil; STATE.aimLastStableDir = nil; return end
                local targetHum = targetChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")

                if not IsValidAimTarget(target, targetChar, targetHum) then 
                    STATE.aimTargetCache = nil; STATE.aimLastStableDir = nil; return 
                end

                torsoPos = GetTorsoPosition(targetChar)
            end
            if not torsoPos then STATE.aimTargetCache = nil; STATE.aimLastStableDir = nil; return end
            local _cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            if not _cam then return end
            local camCFrame = _cam.CFrame
            local cameraPos = camCFrame.Position
            local currentDir = camCFrame.LookVector
            local smoothness = CONFIG.cameralockSmoothness
            local rawVec = torsoPos - cameraPos
            local camDist = rawVec.Magnitude
            local playerToTarget = (torsoPos - myRoot.Position).Magnitude
            local MIN_LOCK_DIST = 4.0
            local desiredDir
            if camDist >= MIN_LOCK_DIST and playerToTarget >= 1.5 then
                desiredDir = rawVec.Unit; STATE.aimLastStableDir = desiredDir
            elseif STATE.aimLastStableDir then
                desiredDir = STATE.aimLastStableDir
            else
                desiredDir = currentDir
            end
            local newDir = currentDir:Lerp(desiredDir, smoothness)
            if newDir.Magnitude < 0.001 then return end
            _cam.CFrame = CFrame.new(cameraPos, cameraPos + newDir)
            local hum = myChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum and not hum.AutoRotate then
                local camLook = _cam.CFrame.LookVector
                local myPos = myRoot.Position
                local lookFlat = Vector3.new(camLook.X, 0, camLook.Z)
                if lookFlat.Magnitude > 0.001 then
                    local currentAngle = math.atan2(myRoot.CFrame.LookVector.X, myRoot.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
                    local desiredAngle = math.atan2(lookFlat.X, lookFlat.Z)
                    local diff = desiredAngle - currentAngle
                    diff = ((diff + math.pi) % (2 * math.pi)) - math.pi
                    local finalAngle = currentAngle + diff * 0.15
                    local finalLookX = math.sin(finalAngle)
                    local finalLookZ = math.cos(finalAngle)
                    local finalTarget = myPos + Vector3.new(finalLookX, 0, finalLookZ) * 900
                    myRoot.CFrame = CFrame.new(myPos, Vector3.new(finalTarget.X, myPos.Y, finalTarget.Z))
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    function StopCameralock()
        if STATE.aimConn then STATE.aimConn:Disconnect(); STATE.aimConn = nil end
        STATE.aimTargetCache = nil; STATE.aimLastStableDir = nil
    end
end

-- ============================================================
--  CAMERA ZOOM & FOV
-- ============================================================
local function applyCameraZoom(value)
    if STATE.originalMaxZoom == nil then STATE.originalMaxZoom = localPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance end
    localPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance = value
end

local function restoreCameraZoom()
    if STATE.originalMaxZoom ~= nil then localPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance = STATE.originalMaxZoom end
    STATE.originalMaxZoom = nil
end

-- ============================================================
--  CAMERA STIFFNESS NATIF
-- ============================================================
local function GetSmoothCamera(char)
    if not char then return nil end
    local ok, sc = pcall(function() return char:FindFirstChild("SmoothCamera") end)
    if ok and sc then return sc end
    return nil
end

local function applyCamStiffness(value)
    local sc = GetSmoothCamera(localPlayer.Character)
    if not sc then return false end
    if STATE.origCamStiffness == nil then
        local cur = 8
        pcall(function() cur = sc:GetAttribute("CameraStiffness") or 8 end)
        STATE.origCamStiffness = cur
    end
    pcall(function() sc:SetAttribute("CameraStiffness", value) end)
    return true
end

local function restoreCamStiffness()
    if STATE.origCamStiffness ~= nil then
        local sc = GetSmoothCamera(localPlayer.Character)
        if sc then pcall(function() sc:SetAttribute("CameraStiffness", STATE.origCamStiffness) end) end
    end
    STATE.origCamStiffness = nil
end

do
    localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
        task.spawn(function()
            task.wait(1)
            if CONFIG.camStiffEnabled and localPlayer.Character == char then
                applyCamStiffness(CONFIG.camStiffValue)
            end
        end)
    end)

    RegisterTask("CamStiffness", 0.5, function()
        if not CONFIG.camStiffEnabled then return end
        local sc = GetSmoothCamera(localPlayer.Character)
        if not sc then return end
        local curOk, cur = pcall(function() return sc:GetAttribute("CameraStiffness") end)
        if curOk and cur ~= CONFIG.camStiffValue then
            pcall(function() sc:SetAttribute("CameraStiffness", CONFIG.camStiffValue) end)
        end
    end)
end

local function applyCustomFov(value)
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if not cam then return end
    if STATE.originalFOV == nil then STATE.originalFOV = cam.FieldOfView end
    cam.FieldOfView = value
end

local function restoreCustomFov()
    local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
    if cam and STATE.originalFOV then cam.FieldOfView = STATE.originalFOV end
    STATE.originalFOV = nil
end

do
    localPlayer:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CameraMaxZoomDistance"):Connect(function()
        if CONFIG.cameraZoomEnabled and localPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance ~= CONFIG.cameraZoomValue then
            localPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance = CONFIG.cameraZoomValue
        end
    end)

    local function SetupFovLock()
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not cam then return end
        cam:GetPropertyChangedSignal("FieldOfView"):Connect(function()
            if CONFIG.lockFovEnabled and cam.FieldOfView ~= CONFIG.customFovValue then
                cam.FieldOfView = CONFIG.customFovValue
            end
        end)
    end
    SetupFovLock()
end


-- ============================================================
--  ASPECT RATIO / STRETCHED RES
-- ============================================================
local AR_Cur = { m = 1, n = 1 }
local function AR_Stop()
    pcall(function() RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("BOLONG_Aspect") end)
    AR_Cur.m, AR_Cur.n = 1, 1
end

local function AR_Start()
    AR_Stop()
    AR_Cur.m, AR_Cur.n = 1, 1
    pcall(function()
        RunService:UnbindFromRenderStep("BOLONG_Aspect")
        RunService:BindToRenderStep("BOLONG_Aspect", Enum.RenderPriority.Camera.Value + 2, function(dt)
            if not CONFIG.aspectEnabled then return end
            local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
            if not cam then return end
            local vs = cam.ViewportSize
            if vs.Y < 1 then return end
            local native = vs.X / vs.Y
            local targetRatio = tonumber(CONFIG.aspectRatio) or (16 / 9)
            local m, n = 1, 1
            if math.abs(targetRatio - native) > 0.03 then
                if targetRatio < native then
                    m = 1
                    n = math.clamp(targetRatio / native, 0.1, 1)
                else
                    m = math.clamp(native / targetRatio, 0.1, 1)
                    n = 1
                end
            end
            local step = math.clamp(dt or 0.0167, 0.0005, 0.1)
            local k = math.clamp(1 - math.exp(-12 * step), 0.001, 1)
            AR_Cur.m = AR_Cur.m + (m - AR_Cur.m) * k
            AR_Cur.n = AR_Cur.n + (n - AR_Cur.n) * k
            if math.abs(AR_Cur.m - 1) < 0.002 and math.abs(AR_Cur.n - 1) < 0.002 then
                AR_Cur.m, AR_Cur.n = 1, 1
                return
            end
            local c = cam.CFrame
            local p = c.Position
            if p.X ~= p.X or p.Y ~= p.Y or p.Z ~= p.Z then return end
            if c.RightVector.Magnitude < 0.01 or c.UpVector.Magnitude < 0.01 then return end
            local clean = CFrame.fromMatrix(p, c.RightVector.Unit, c.UpVector.Unit)
            cam.CFrame = clean * CFrame.new(0, 0, 0, AR_Cur.m, 0, 0, 0, AR_Cur.n, 0, 0, 0, 1)
        end)
    end)
end

-- ============================================================
--  FORCE CURSOR SYSTEM (PC Only)
-- ============================================================
local function StartForceCursor()
    if UserInputService.TouchEnabled and not UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled then
        return
    end

    STATE.originalMouseIconEnabled = UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled
    STATE.originalMouseBehavior   = UserInputService.MouseBehavior

    UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled = true
    UserInputService.MouseBehavior     = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default

    if STATE.forceCursorConn1 then STATE.forceCursorConn1:Disconnect() end
    if STATE.forceCursorConn2 then STATE.forceCursorConn2:Disconnect() end

    STATE.forceCursorConn1 = UserInputService:GetPropertyChangedSignal("MouseIconEnabled"):Connect(function()
        if CONFIG.forceCursorEnabled and not UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled then
            UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled = true
        end
    end)

    STATE.forceCursorConn2 = UserInputService:GetPropertyChangedSignal("MouseBehavior"):Connect(function()
        if CONFIG.forceCursorEnabled and UserInputService.MouseBehavior == Enum.MouseBehavior.LockCenter then
            UserInputService.MouseBehavior = Enum.MouseBehavior.Default
        end
    end)
end

local function StopForceCursor()
    if STATE.forceCursorConn1 then
        STATE.forceCursorConn1:Disconnect()
        STATE.forceCursorConn1 = nil
    end
    if STATE.forceCursorConn2 then
        STATE.forceCursorConn2:Disconnect()
        STATE.forceCursorConn2 = nil
    end
    if STATE.originalMouseIconEnabled ~= nil then
        UserInputService.MouseIconEnabled = STATE.originalMouseIconEnabled
        STATE.originalMouseIconEnabled = nil
    end
    if STATE.originalMouseBehavior ~= nil then
        UserInputService.MouseBehavior = STATE.originalMouseBehavior
        STATE.originalMouseBehavior = nil
    end
end

-- ============================================================
--  PERFORMANCE MONITOR
-- ============================================================
local StartPerfMonitor, StopPerfMonitor, StartPrediction, StopPrediction
do
    local function CreatePerfWindow()
        local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        sg.Name = "BolongPerfMon"; sg.ResetOnSpawn = false
        sg.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling; sg.IgnoreGuiInset = false
        sg.DisplayOrder = 50
        pcall(function() sg.Parent = (gethui and gethui() or game:GetService("CoreGui")) end)
        if not sg.Parent then sg.Parent = PlayerGui end
        local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
        frame.Name = "PerfFrame"; frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(160, 28)
        frame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0); frame.Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 10)
        frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 14); frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
        frame.Active = true; frame.Parent = sg
        local corner = Instance.new("UICorner"); corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8); corner.Parent = frame
        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke"); stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 75)
        stroke.Thickness = 1; stroke.Parent = frame
        local infoLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        infoLabel.Name = "InfoLabel"; infoLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        infoLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1; infoLabel.Text = "FPS — | PING —"
        infoLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 210); infoLabel.TextSize = 12
        infoLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold; infoLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
        infoLabel.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center; infoLabel.RichText = true; infoLabel.Parent = frame
        local dragging, dragStart, startPos
        frame.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = true; dragStart = input.Position; startPos = frame.Position
            end
        end)
        frame.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = false
            end
        end)
        UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                local delta = input.Position - dragStart
                frame.Position = UDim2.new(1, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, 0, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
            end
        end)
        return sg, infoLabel
    end

    RegisterTask("PerfMonitor", 0.5, function()
        if not STATE._perfActive then return end
        local fps = math.floor(STATE._perfFrames / math.max(STATE._perfElapsed, 0.001))
        local ping = math.floor(Stats.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue())
        local fpsHex = fps >= 50 and "rgb(80,220,100)" or fps >= 30 and "rgb(255,200,60)" or "rgb(255,70,70)"
        local pingHex = ping <= 80 and "rgb(80,220,100)" or ping <= 150 and "rgb(255,200,60)" or "rgb(255,70,70)"
        if not STATE.perfGui or not STATE.perfGui.Parent then
            if STATE.perfGui then pcall(function() STATE.perfGui:Destroy() end) end
            local sg = CreatePerfWindow()
            STATE.perfGui = sg
        end
        if STATE.perfGui and STATE.perfGui.Parent then
            local label = STATE.perfGui:FindFirstChild("PerfFrame"):FindFirstChild("InfoLabel")
            if label then
                label.Text = string.format('<font color="%s">FPS %d</font> <font color="rgb(60,60,75)">|</font> <font color="%s">PING %d</font>', fpsHex, fps, pingHex, ping)
            end
        end
        STATE._perfElapsed = 0; STATE._perfFrames = 0
    end)

    RegisterTask("PerfCounter", 0, function(dt)
        if STATE._perfActive then
            STATE._perfFrames = STATE._perfFrames + 1
            STATE._perfElapsed = STATE._perfElapsed + dt
        end
    end)

    function StartPerfMonitor()
        if STATE.perfGui then STATE.perfGui:Destroy() end
        local sg, infoLabel = CreatePerfWindow()
        STATE.perfGui = sg; STATE._perfActive = true
        STATE._perfElapsed = 0; STATE._perfFrames = 0
    end

    function StopPerfMonitor()
        STATE._perfActive = false
        if STATE.perfGui then STATE.perfGui:Destroy(); STATE.perfGui = nil end
    end

    -- ============================================================
    --  PREDICTION SYSTEM (Killer & Map)
    -- ============================================================
    local function GetGameValue(obj, name)
        if not obj then return nil end
        local attr = obj:GetAttribute(name)
        if attr ~= nil then return attr end
        local child = obj:FindFirstChild(name)
        if child then
            local success, val = pcall(function() return child.Value end)
            if success then return val end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function CreatePredictionWindow()
        local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        sg.Name = "BolongPrediction"
        sg.ResetOnSpawn = false
        sg.IgnoreGuiInset = true
        sg.DisplayOrder = 51
        sg.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling

        local parentTarget = nil
        pcall(function() parentTarget = gethui() end)
        if not parentTarget then
            pcall(function() parentTarget = game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
        end
        if not parentTarget then
            parentTarget = PlayerGui
        end
        sg.Parent = parentTarget

        local infoLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        infoLabel.Name = "InfoLabel"
        infoLabel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
    
        if STATE.predLastPos then
            infoLabel.Position = STATE.predLastPos
        else
            infoLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 50)
        end
    
        infoLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        infoLabel.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY
        infoLabel.Text = "Map: — | Killer: —"
        infoLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(252, 235, 229)
        infoLabel.TextSize = 14
        infoLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        infoLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
        infoLabel.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
        infoLabel.RichText = true
        infoLabel.Active = true
        infoLabel.Parent = sg

        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        stroke.Name = "Outline"
        stroke.Color = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
        stroke.Thickness = 1
        stroke.Transparency = 0.2
        stroke.Parent = infoLabel

        local dragging = false
        local dragStart = Vector2.new()
        local startPos = UDim2.new()

        infoLabel.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = true
                dragStart = input.Position
                startPos = infoLabel.Position
            end
        end)

        infoLabel.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = false
            end
        end)

        UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                local delta = input.Position - dragStart
                local newPos = UDim2.new(
                    startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X,
                    startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y
                )
                infoLabel.Position = newPos
                STATE.predLastPos = newPos
            end
        end)

        return sg, infoLabel
    end

    local function SetupMapInfoHook()
        if STATE.predMapInfoConn then return end
        task.spawn(function()
            local ok, remote = pcall(function()
                return ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes", 10)
                    :WaitForChild("Messages", 10)
                    :WaitForChild("Mapinfo", 10)
            end)
            if ok and remote and remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                STATE.predMapInfoConn = remote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(mapName, mapId)
                    if type(mapName) == "string" and mapName ~= "" then
                        STATE.predMapName = mapName
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)
    end

    RegisterTask("PredictionMonitor", 1, function()
        if not STATE.predActive then return end
    
        if not STATE.predGui or not STATE.predGui.Parent then
            if STATE.predGui then STATE.predGui:Destroy() end
            local sg, infoLabel = CreatePredictionWindow()
            STATE.predGui = sg
            STATE.predInfoLabel = infoLabel
        end
    
        local infoLabel = STATE.predGui:FindFirstChild("InfoLabel")
        if not infoLabel then return end

        -- === KILLER PREDICTION ===
        local players = Players:GetPlayers()

        table.sort(players, function(a, b)
            local aAllow = GetGameValue(a, "AllowKiller")
            local bAllow = GetGameValue(b, "AllowKiller")

            local aBlocked = (aAllow == false)
            local bBlocked = (bAllow == false)

            if aBlocked ~= bBlocked then
                return not aBlocked
            end

            if not aBlocked and not bBlocked then
                return (GetGameValue(a, "KillerChance") or 0) > (GetGameValue(b, "KillerChance") or 0)
            end

            return (GetGameValue(a, "KillerChance") or 0) < (GetGameValue(b, "KillerChance") or 0)
        end)

        local nextKiller = players[1]
        local killerStr = '<font color="rgb(100,110,130)">—</font>'

        if nextKiller then
            local name = nextKiller.Name
            local selectedKiller = GetGameValue(nextKiller, "SelectedKiller")
            local allowKiller    = GetGameValue(nextKiller, "AllowKiller")

            if nextKiller == localPlayer then
                name = "YOU"
            end

            local nameColor
            if allowKiller == false then
                nameColor = "rgb(255,0,30)"
            else
                nameColor = "rgb(252,235,229)"
            end

            if selectedKiller and type(selectedKiller) == "string" and selectedKiller ~= "" then
                killerStr = string.format(
                    '<font color="%s">%s</font> <font color="rgb(255,0,30)">(%s)</font>',
                    nameColor, name, selectedKiller
                )
            else
                killerStr = string.format(
                    '<font color="%s">%s</font>',
                    nameColor, name
                )
            end
        end

        -- === FORMAT DISPLAY ===
        local mapDisplay = STATE.predMapName or "—"
        infoLabel.Text = string.format(
            '<font color="rgb(255,210,60)">Map:</font> <font color="rgb(252,235,229)">%s</font>  <font color="rgb(80,80,85)">|</font>  <font color="rgb(255,210,60)">Killer:</font> %s',
            mapDisplay, killerStr
        )
    end)

    function StartPrediction()
        if STATE.predGui then STATE.predGui:Destroy() end
        STATE.predMapName = nil
        SetupMapInfoHook()
    
        local sg, infoLabel = CreatePredictionWindow()
        STATE.predGui = sg
        STATE.predInfoLabel = infoLabel
        STATE.predActive = true
    end

    function StopPrediction()
        STATE.predActive = false
        if STATE.predMapInfoConn then
            pcall(function() STATE.predMapInfoConn:Disconnect() end)
            STATE.predMapInfoConn = nil
        end
        if STATE.predGui then
            STATE.predGui:Destroy()
            STATE.predGui = nil
        end
    end
end

-- ============================================================
--  SPECTATOR COUNT
-- ============================================================
local StartSpectatorCount, StopSpectatorCount
do
    local THRESHOLD = 0.001
    local STREAM_RATE = 2
    local BODY_PARTS = {
        "Head",
        "Torso", "UpperTorso", "LowerTorso",
        "Left Arm", "Right Arm", "Left Leg", "Right Leg",
        "LeftUpperArm", "LeftLowerArm", "LeftHand",
        "RightUpperArm", "RightLowerArm", "RightHand",
        "LeftUpperLeg", "RightUpperLeg", "LeftFoot", "RightFoot",
    }

    local Conn = STATE.specConns
    local Detected = STATE.specDetected
    local StreamTimes = STATE.specStreamTimes

    local function LocalIsSpectatorTeam()
        local ok, team = pcall(function() return localPlayer.Team end)
        if not ok or not team then return false end
        local ok2, name = pcall(function() return team.Name end)
        if not ok2 or type(name) ~= "string" then return false end
        return (name:lower():gsub("%s+", ""):find("spectator", 1, true)) ~= nil
    end

    local function IsSelfPlayer(player)
        if not player or not localPlayer then return false end
        if player == localPlayer then return true end
        return player.UserId == localPlayer.UserId
    end

    local function IsSelfCharacter(char)
        if not char or not localPlayer then return false end
        if char == localPlayer.Character then return true end
        if Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(char) == localPlayer then return true end
        return char:FindFirstChild("Bolong_Invis") ~= nil
    end

    local function Disconnect(key)
        local c = Conn[key]
        if c then
            pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
            Conn[key] = nil
        end
    end

    local function DisconnectPrefix(prefix)
        for key in next, Conn do
            if key:sub(1, #prefix) == prefix then
                local c = Conn[key]
                pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
                Conn[key] = nil
            end
        end
    end

    local function GetTransparentPart(char)
        for _, name in ipairs(BODY_PARTS) do
            local part = char:FindFirstChild(name)
            if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
                local t = part.Transparency
                local lt = part.LocalTransparencyModifier
                if t > THRESHOLD or lt > THRESHOLD then
                    return part, t, lt
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local function GetSpecCount()
        local count = 0
        for uid, data in next, Detected do
            if data.Player and data.Player.Parent == Players
                and not IsSelfPlayer(data.Player) and not IsSelfCharacter(data.Character) then
                count = count + 1
            else
                Detected[uid] = nil
            end
        end
        return count
    end

    local function UpdateGui()
        if not STATE.specGui or not STATE.specGui.Parent then return end
        local count = STATE.specPaused and 0 or GetSpecCount()
        local label = STATE.specGui:FindFirstChild("SpecFrame") and STATE.specGui.SpecFrame:FindFirstChild("InfoLabel")
        if label then
            local hex = count > 0 and "rgb(80,220,100)" or "rgb(150,150,165)"
            label.Text = string.format('<font color="%s">SPECTATORS %d</font>', hex, count)
        end
    end

    local function CheckPlayer(player)
        if not STATE.specActive or not player or IsSelfPlayer(player) then return end

        local uid = player.UserId
        local char = player.Character

        if LocalIsSpectatorTeam() then
            if Detected[uid] then
                Detected[uid] = nil
                UpdateGui()
            end
            return
        end

        if not char or IsSelfCharacter(char) then
            if Detected[uid] then
                Detected[uid] = nil
                UpdateGui()
            end
            return
        end

        local part, t, lt = GetTransparentPart(char)

        if part then
            if not Detected[uid] then
                Detected[uid] = { Player = player, Character = char, Part = part, T = t, LT = lt }
                UpdateGui()
            else
                local d = Detected[uid]
                d.Character, d.Part, d.T, d.LT = char, part, t, lt
            end
        elseif Detected[uid] then
            Detected[uid] = nil
            UpdateGui()
        end
    end

    local function HookPart(player, part, name)
        local prefix = "char_" .. player.UserId .. "_" .. name
        Conn[prefix .. "_T"] = part:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Transparency")
            :Connect(function() CheckPlayer(player) end)
        Conn[prefix .. "_LT"] = part:GetPropertyChangedSignal("LocalTransparencyModifier")
            :Connect(function() CheckPlayer(player) end)
    end

    local function WatchCharacter(player, char)
        if IsSelfPlayer(player) or IsSelfCharacter(char) then return end

        local prefix = "char_" .. player.UserId .. "_"
        DisconnectPrefix(prefix)

        for _, name in ipairs(BODY_PARTS) do
            local part = char:FindFirstChild(name)
            if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
                HookPart(player, part, name)
            end
        end

        Conn[prefix .. "added"] = char.ChildAdded:Connect(function(obj)
            if not obj:IsA("BasePart") then return end
            for _, name in ipairs(BODY_PARTS) do
                if obj.Name == name then
                    HookPart(player, obj, name)
                    task.defer(function() CheckPlayer(player) end)
                    break
                end
            end
        end)

        task.defer(function() CheckPlayer(player) end)
    end

    local function WatchPlayer(player)
        if IsSelfPlayer(player) then return end

        local uid = player.UserId
        Disconnect("player_" .. uid)

        Conn["player_" .. uid] = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
            Detected[uid] = nil
            UpdateGui()
            task.wait(0.15)
            if STATE.specActive then WatchCharacter(player, char) end
        end)

        if player.Character then
            WatchCharacter(player, player.Character)
        end
    end

    local function UnwatchPlayer(player)
        if not player then return end
        local uid = player.UserId
        Disconnect("player_" .. uid)
        DisconnectPrefix("char_" .. uid .. "_")
        Detected[uid] = nil
        StreamTimes[uid] = nil
        UpdateGui()
    end

    local function TryStream(player)
        if not STATE.specActive or not player or IsSelfPlayer(player) then return end
        if STATE.specPaused or LocalIsSpectatorTeam() then return end
        local char = player.Character
        local root = char and (
            char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or
            char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
        )
        if not root or not root:IsA("BasePart") then return end

        local uid = player.UserId
        local now = os.clock()
        if StreamTimes[uid] and now - StreamTimes[uid] < STREAM_RATE then return end

        StreamTimes[uid] = now
        task.spawn(function()
            pcall(function()
                localPlayer:RequestStreamAroundAsync(root.Position, STREAM_RATE)
            end)
        end)
    end

    local streamAcc = 0

    local function RefreshPauseState()
        local inLobby = LocalIsSpectatorTeam()
        if inLobby ~= STATE.specPaused then
            STATE.specPaused = inLobby
            if inLobby then
                table.clear(Detected)
                table.clear(StreamTimes)
            end
        end
        UpdateGui()
    end

    RegisterTask("SpectatorCount", 0.25, function(dt)
        if not STATE.specActive then return end
        local inLobby = LocalIsSpectatorTeam()
        if inLobby ~= STATE.specPaused then
            STATE.specPaused = inLobby
            if inLobby then
                table.clear(Detected)
                table.clear(StreamTimes)
                streamAcc = 0
                UpdateGui()
                return
            end
            streamAcc = 0
        end
        if inLobby then
            UpdateGui()
            return
        end
        local list = Players:GetPlayers()
        for _, p in ipairs(list) do
            if not IsSelfPlayer(p) then CheckPlayer(p) end
        end
        if not STATE.specGui or not STATE.specGui.Parent then
            if STATE.specGui then pcall(function() STATE.specGui:Destroy() end) end
            STATE.specGui = CreateSpecWindow()
        end
        streamAcc = streamAcc + dt
        if streamAcc >= STREAM_RATE then
            streamAcc = 0
            for _, p in ipairs(list) do
                if not IsSelfPlayer(p) then TryStream(p) end
            end
        end
        UpdateGui()
    end)

    local function CreateSpecWindow()
        local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        sg.Name = "BolongSpecCount"; sg.ResetOnSpawn = false
        sg.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling; sg.IgnoreGuiInset = false
        sg.DisplayOrder = 49
        pcall(function() sg.Parent = (gethui and gethui() or game:GetService("CoreGui")) end)
        if not sg.Parent then sg.Parent = PlayerGui end
        local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
        frame.Name = "SpecFrame"; frame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(170, 28)
        frame.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 1); frame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 1, -10)
        frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 14); frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
        frame.Active = true; frame.Parent = sg
        local corner = Instance.new("UICorner"); corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8); corner.Parent = frame
        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke"); stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 75)
        stroke.Thickness = 1; stroke.Parent = frame
        local infoLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        infoLabel.Name = "InfoLabel"; infoLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        infoLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1; infoLabel.Text = "SPECTATORS 0"
        infoLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 210); infoLabel.TextSize = 12
        infoLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold; infoLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
        infoLabel.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center; infoLabel.RichText = true; infoLabel.Parent = frame
        local icon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
        icon.Name = "EyeIcon"; icon.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(16, 16)
        icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1; icon.Image = "rbxassetid://104977598392154"
        icon.ImageColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 210); icon.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0.5, -8)
        icon.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit; icon.Parent = frame
        local dragging, dragStart, startPos
        frame.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = true; dragStart = input.Position; startPos = frame.Position
            end
        end)
        frame.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = false
            end
        end)
        UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                local delta = input.Position - dragStart
                frame.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
            end
        end)
        return sg
    end

    function StartSpectatorCount()
        if STATE.specActive then return end
        STATE.specActive = true
        STATE.specPaused = LocalIsSpectatorTeam()
        streamAcc = 0
        table.clear(Detected); table.clear(StreamTimes)
        if STATE.specGui then STATE.specGui:Destroy() end
        STATE.specGui = CreateSpecWindow()
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if not IsSelfPlayer(p) then WatchPlayer(p) end
        end
        Conn["onJoin"] = Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(p)
            if STATE.specActive then WatchPlayer(p) end
        end)
        Conn["onLeave"] = Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(UnwatchPlayer)
        Conn["selfTeam"] = localPlayer:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Team"):Connect(function()
            if not STATE.specActive then return end
            RefreshPauseState()
        end)
        UpdateGui()
    end

    function StopSpectatorCount()
        STATE.specActive = false
        STATE.specPaused = false
        for key in next, Conn do
            local c = Conn[key]
            pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
            Conn[key] = nil
        end
        table.clear(Detected)
        table.clear(StreamTimes)
        if STATE.specGui then
            STATE.specGui:Destroy()
            STATE.specGui = nil
        end
    end
end

-- ============================================================
--  CROSSHAIR SYSTEM v2
-- ============================================================
CROSSHAIR_STYLES = {
   "Dot", "Circle", "Circle + Dot", "Plus", "Cross (X)", "T-Shape", "Square"
}

local CrosshairInstance
do
    local CONTAINER_SIZE   = 80
    local CONTAINER_CENTER = CONTAINER_SIZE / 2

    -- ── Style Factory v5 (Solid Intersecting Shapes) ─────────────
    local StyleFactory = {}

    local function createPart(centerX, centerY, width, length, rotation, color, alpha, name)
        local f = Instance.new("Frame")
        f.Name               = name or "CH_Part"
        f.BackgroundColor3   = color
        f.BackgroundTransparency = alpha
        f.BorderSizePixel    = 0
        f.Size               = UDim2.fromOffset(math.max(1, math.round(width)), math.max(1, math.round(length)))
        f.AnchorPoint        = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        f.Position           = UDim2.fromOffset(math.round(centerX), math.round(centerY))
        if rotation ~= 0 then f.Rotation = rotation end
        return f
    end

    local function getScale(size)
        return 0.3 + (math.clamp(size, 1, 100) / 100) * 2.2
    end

    StyleFactory.Plus = function(size, color, alpha)
        local parts = {}
        local s = getScale(size)
        local thick = math.max(2, math.round(2 * s))
        local len = math.max(8, math.round(25 * s))
        local c = CONTAINER_CENTER

        table.insert(parts, createPart(c, c, thick, len, 0, color, alpha))
        table.insert(parts, createPart(c, c, len, thick, 0, color, alpha))
        return parts, {}, {}
    end

    StyleFactory["Cross (X)"] = function(size, color, alpha)
        local parts = {}
        local s = getScale(size)
        local thick = math.max(2, math.round(2 * s))
        local len = math.max(8, math.round(25 * s))
        local c = CONTAINER_CENTER

        table.insert(parts, createPart(c, c, thick, len, 45, color, alpha))
        table.insert(parts, createPart(c, c, thick, len, -45, color, alpha))
        return parts, {}, {}
    end

    StyleFactory.Dot = function(size, color, alpha)
        local parts = {}
        local s = getScale(size)
        local dotSize = math.max(3, math.round(8 * s))
        local c = CONTAINER_CENTER

        local dot = createPart(c, c, dotSize, dotSize, 0, color, alpha, "CH_Dot")
        local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        corner.Parent = dot
        table.insert(parts, dot)
        return parts, {}, {}
    end

    StyleFactory.Circle = function(size, color, alpha)
        local parts, strokes = {}, {}
        local s = getScale(size)
        local radius = math.max(4, math.round(12 * s))
        local thick = math.max(2, math.round(2 * s))
        local c = CONTAINER_CENTER

        local ring = Instance.new("Frame")
        ring.Name = "CH_Ring"
        ring.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        ring.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(radius * 2, radius * 2)
        ring.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        ring.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(c, c)
        
        local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        corner.Parent = ring
        
        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        stroke.Name = "CH_Stroke"
        stroke.Color = color
        stroke.Thickness = thick
        stroke.Transparency = alpha
        stroke.Parent = ring
        
        table.insert(parts, ring)
        table.insert(strokes, stroke)
        return parts, strokes, {}
    end

    StyleFactory["Circle + Dot"] = function(size, color, alpha)
        local parts, strokes, dots = {}, {}, {}
        local s = getScale(size)
        local radius = math.max(4, math.round(12 * s))
        local thick = math.max(2, math.round(2 * s))
        local dotSize = math.max(2, math.round(4 * s))
        local c = CONTAINER_CENTER

        local ring = Instance.new("Frame")
        ring.Name = "CH_Ring"
        ring.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        ring.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(radius * 2, radius * 2)
        ring.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        ring.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(c, c)
        
        local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        corner.Parent = ring
        
        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        stroke.Name = "CH_Stroke"
        stroke.Color = color
        stroke.Thickness = thick
        stroke.Transparency = alpha
        stroke.Parent = ring
        
        table.insert(parts, ring)
        table.insert(strokes, stroke)
        
        local dot = createPart(c, c, dotSize, dotSize, 0, color, alpha, "CH_Dot")
        local dc = Instance.new("UICorner")
        dc.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
        dc.Parent = dot
        table.insert(parts, dot)
        table.insert(dots, dot)
        
        return parts, strokes, dots
    end

    StyleFactory["T-Shape"] = function(size, color, alpha)
        local parts = {}
        local s = getScale(size)
        local thick = math.max(2, math.round(2 * s))
        local len = math.max(8, math.round(25 * s))
        local c = CONTAINER_CENTER
        local offset = math.round(len * 0.25)

        table.insert(parts, createPart(c, c - offset, len, thick, 0, color, alpha))
        table.insert(parts, createPart(c, c - offset + len/2, thick, len, 0, color, alpha))
        return parts, {}, {}
    end

    StyleFactory.Square = function(size, color, alpha)
        local parts = {}
        local s = getScale(size)
        local thick = math.max(2, math.round(2 * s))
        local side = math.max(8, math.round(22 * s))
        local arm = math.max(3, math.round(side * 0.4))
        local c = CONTAINER_CENTER
        local half = side / 2

        table.insert(parts, createPart(c - half + arm/2, c - half, arm, thick, 0, color, alpha))
        table.insert(parts, createPart(c - half, c - half + arm/2, thick, arm, 0, color, alpha))
        table.insert(parts, createPart(c + half - arm/2, c - half, arm, thick, 0, color, alpha))
        table.insert(parts, createPart(c + half, c - half + arm/2, thick, arm, 0, color, alpha))
        table.insert(parts, createPart(c - half + arm/2, c + half, arm, thick, 0, color, alpha))
        table.insert(parts, createPart(c - half, c + half - arm/2, thick, arm, 0, color, alpha))
        table.insert(parts, createPart(c + half - arm/2, c + half, arm, thick, 0, color, alpha))
        table.insert(parts, createPart(c + half, c + half - arm/2, thick, arm, 0, color, alpha))
        
        return parts, {}, {}
    end

    -- ── Crosshair Class ──────────────────────────────────────────
    local Crosshair = {}
    Crosshair.__index = Crosshair

    function Crosshair.new()
        local self = setmetatable({}, Crosshair)
        self.config = {
            enabled      = false,
            style        = "Plus",
            size         = 1,
            opacity      = 1.0,
            offsetX      = 0,
            offsetY      = 0,
            color        = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
            smooth       = true,
            smoothSpeed  = 0.25,
        }
        self.gui            = nil
        self.container      = nil
        self.parent         = nil
        self.parts          = { frames = {}, strokes = {}, dots = {} }
        self.connections    = {}
        self.renderConn     = nil
        self.currentOffset  = Vector2.new(0, 0)
        self.targetOffset   = Vector2.new(0, 0)
        self._frameCount    = 0
        return self
    end

    local function getSafeGuiParent()
        local parent
        pcall(function() if gethui then parent = gethui() end end)
        if parent and parent.Parent then return parent end
        pcall(function() parent = game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
        if parent and parent.Parent then return parent end
        return PlayerGui
    end

    function Crosshair:_createGui()
        if self.gui and self.gui.Parent then return end

        local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        sg.Name             = "BolongCrosshair"
        sg.ResetOnSpawn     = false
        sg.ZIndexBehavior   = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
        sg.IgnoreGuiInset   = true
        sg.DisplayOrder     = 999999

        local parent = getSafeGuiParent()
        sg.Parent = parent
        self.parent = parent

        local container = Instance.new("Frame")
        container.Name             = "Container"
        container.Size             = UDim2.fromOffset(CONTAINER_SIZE, CONTAINER_SIZE)
        container.AnchorPoint      = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        container.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        container.BorderSizePixel  = 0
        container.Parent = sg

        self.gui       = sg
        self.container = container

        self:_rebuild()

        self.targetOffset  = Vector2.new(self.config.offsetX, self.config.offsetY)
        self.currentOffset = self.targetOffset
        self:_updatePosition()
    end

    function Crosshair:_rebuild()
        if not self.container then return end

        for _, f in ipairs(self.parts.frames) do
            pcall(function() f:Destroy() end)
        end
        self.parts = { frames = {}, strokes = {}, dots = {} }

        local builder = StyleFactory[self.config.style]
        if not builder then return end

        local alpha = 1 - self.config.opacity
        local frames, strokes, dots = builder(self.config.size, self.config.color, alpha)

        for _, f in ipairs(frames) do
            f.Parent = self.container
            table.insert(self.parts.frames, f)
        end
        for _, s in ipairs(strokes) do
            table.insert(self.parts.strokes, s)
        end
        self.parts.dots = dots or {}
    end

    function Crosshair:_updateColors()
        local color = self.config.color
        local alpha = 1 - self.config.opacity

        local isDot = {}
        for _, d in ipairs(self.parts.dots) do isDot[d] = true end

        for _, f in ipairs(self.parts.frames) do
            if f and f.Parent then
                f.BackgroundColor3 = isDot[f] and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or color
                
                if not f:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("UIStroke") then
                    f.BackgroundTransparency = alpha
                end
            end
        end
        for _, s in ipairs(self.parts.strokes) do
            if s and s.Parent then
                s.Color        = color
                s.Transparency = alpha
            end
        end
    end

    function Crosshair:_updatePosition()
        if not self.container then return end
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not cam then return end
        local vp = cam.ViewportSize
        self.container.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(
            vp.X * 0.5 + self.currentOffset.X,
            vp.Y * 0.5 + self.currentOffset.Y
        )
    end

    function Crosshair:_hookCamera()
        if self.connections.camViewport then
            self.connections.camViewport:Disconnect()
            self.connections.camViewport = nil
        end
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if cam then
            self.connections.camViewport =
                cam:GetPropertyChangedSignal("ViewportSize"):Connect(function()
                    self:_updatePosition()
                end)
        end
    end

    function Crosshair:_setupConnections()
        self:_hookCamera()

        if not self.connections.camChange then
            self.connections.camChange =
                workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"):Connect(function()
                    self:_hookCamera()
                    self:_updatePosition()
                end)
        end

        if not self.connections.childRemoved then
            self.connections.childRemoved =
                self.parent.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
                    if child == self.gui and self.config.enabled then
                        self.gui       = nil
                        self.container = nil
                        self.parts     = { frames = {}, strokes = {}, dots = {} }
                        task.wait(0.05)
                        if self.config.enabled then
                            self:_createGui()
                        end
                    end
                end)
        end

        if not self.connections.touchChanged then
            self.connections.touchChanged =
                UserInputService:GetPropertyChangedSignal("TouchEnabled"):Connect(function()
                    self:_updatePosition()
                end)
        end
    end

    function Crosshair:_startRender()
        if self.renderConn then return end
        self.renderConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
            if not self.config.enabled then return end

            if not self.gui or not self.gui.Parent then
                self:_createGui()
                return
            end

            if not self.gui.Enabled then
                self.gui.Enabled = true
            end

            self._frameCount = self._frameCount + 1
            if self._frameCount % 30 == 0 then
                local valid = false
                for _, f in ipairs(self.parts.frames) do
                    if f and f.Parent then valid = true; break end
                end
                if not valid and #self.parts.frames > 0 then
                    self:_rebuild()
                end
            end

            if self.config.smooth then
                local diff = self.targetOffset - self.currentOffset
                if diff.Magnitude > 0.01 then
                    local lerpFactor =
                        1 - math.pow(1 - self.config.smoothSpeed, dt * 60)
                    self.currentOffset =
                        self.currentOffset:Lerp(self.targetOffset, lerpFactor)
                    self:_updatePosition()
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    function Crosshair:_stopRender()
        if self.renderConn then
            self.renderConn:Disconnect()
            self.renderConn = nil
        end
    end

    function Crosshair:SetEnabled(enabled)
        self.config.enabled = enabled
        if enabled then
            if not self.gui or not self.gui.Parent then
                self:_createGui()
                self:_setupConnections()
            end
            self.gui.Enabled = true
            self.targetOffset  = Vector2.new(self.config.offsetX, self.config.offsetY)
            self.currentOffset = self.targetOffset
            self:_updatePosition()
            self:_startRender()
        else
            self:_stopRender()
            if self.gui then
                self.gui.Enabled = false
            end
        end
    end

    function Crosshair:SetStyle(style)
        self.config.style = style
        if self.config.enabled and self.gui then
            self:_rebuild()
        end
    end

    function Crosshair:SetSize(size)
        self.config.size = size
        if self.config.enabled and self.gui then
            self:_rebuild()
        end
    end

    function Crosshair:SetOpacity(opacity)
        self.config.opacity = opacity
        if self.config.enabled and self.gui then
            self:_updateColors()
        end
    end

    function Crosshair:SetColor(color)
        self.config.color = color
        if self.config.enabled and self.gui then
            self:_updateColors()
        end
    end

    function Crosshair:SetOffsetX(x)
        self.config.offsetX = x
        self.targetOffset = Vector2.new(x, self.config.offsetY)
        if not self.config.smooth and self.config.enabled then
            self.currentOffset = self.targetOffset
            self:_updatePosition()
        end
    end

    function Crosshair:SetOffsetY(y)
        self.config.offsetY = y
        self.targetOffset = Vector2.new(self.config.offsetX, y)
        if not self.config.smooth and self.config.enabled then
            self.currentOffset = self.targetOffset
            self:_updatePosition()
        end
    end

    function Crosshair:Destroy()
        self.config.enabled = false
        self:_stopRender()
        for _, conn in pairs(self.connections) do
            pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
        end
        self.connections = {}
        if self.gui then
            pcall(function() self.gui:Destroy() end)
        end
        self.gui       = nil
        self.container = nil
        self.parts     = { frames = {}, strokes = {}, dots = {} }
    end

    CrosshairInstance = Crosshair.new()
end

-- ============================================================
--  PROTECT NAME
-- ============================================================
local StartProtectName, StopProtectName, _ApplyCopiedAvatarToUI, _HookAllAvatarSlots, _EnsureAvatarUiWatchers
do
    local function _GetOrAssignFakeName(player)
        if not STATE.pnameFakeNames[player] then
            STATE.pnameFakeNames[player] = _FAKE_NAME_POOL[1]
        end
        return STATE.pnameFakeNames[player]
    end

    local function _HideOverheadName(char, fakeName)
        if not char then return end
        local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if not head then return end
        for _, gui in ipairs(head:GetChildren()) do
            if gui:IsA("BillboardGui") then
                local lbl = gui:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel", true)
                if lbl then lbl.Text = fakeName end
            end
        end
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then pcall(function() hum.DisplayDistanceType = Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.None end) end
    end
