    local function _RestoreOverheadName(char, realName)
        if not char then return end
        local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if head then
            for _, gui in ipairs(head:GetChildren()) do
                if gui:IsA("BillboardGui") then
                    local lbl = gui:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("TextLabel", true)
                    if lbl then lbl.Text = realName end
                end
            end
        end
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then pcall(function() hum.DisplayDistanceType = Enum.HumanoidDisplayDistanceType.Limit end) end
    end

    local function _FindSurvivorSlotTextLabel(userId)
        local survivorMobGui = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("Survivor-mob")
        if not survivorMobGui then return nil end
        local frame = survivorMobGui:FindFirstChild("Frame")
        if not frame then return nil end
        for i = 1, 5 do
            local slot = frame:FindFirstChild("Survivor" .. i)
            if slot and slot:GetAttribute("UserId") == userId then
                return slot:FindFirstChild("TextLabel")
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function _ApplyProtectToPlayer(player)
        local isLocal = (player == localPlayer)
        local fakeName = _GetOrAssignFakeName(player)
        if not isLocal then
            if STATE.espObjects[player] then STATE.espObjects[player].nameLabel.Text = fakeName end
            if player.Character then _HideOverheadName(player.Character, fakeName) end
        end
        local lbl = _FindSurvivorSlotTextLabel(player.UserId)
        if lbl then lbl.Text = fakeName end
        if not isLocal and not STATE.pnameNameConns[player] then
            STATE.pnameNameConns[player] = player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
                if not STATE.pnameEnabled then return end
                local fn = _GetOrAssignFakeName(player)
                task.wait(0.5)
                _HideOverheadName(char, fn)
                if STATE.espObjects[player] then STATE.espObjects[player].nameLabel.Text = fn end
                local lblRespawn = _FindSurvivorSlotTextLabel(player.UserId)
                if lblRespawn then lblRespawn.Text = fn end
            end)
        end
    end

    local function _RestorePlayerName(player)
        local isLocal = (player == localPlayer)
        local realName = player.Name
        if not isLocal then
            if STATE.espObjects[player] then STATE.espObjects[player].nameLabel.Text = realName end
            if player.Character then _RestoreOverheadName(player.Character, realName) end
            if STATE.pnameNameConns[player] then
                STATE.pnameNameConns[player]:Disconnect()
                STATE.pnameNameConns[player] = nil
            end
        end
        local lbl = _FindSurvivorSlotTextLabel(player.UserId)
        if lbl then lbl.Text = realName end
    end

    local function _HookSlotLabel(slot, lbl)
        local key = slot.Name
        if STATE.pnameSlotConns[key] then
            STATE.pnameSlotConns[key]:Disconnect()
            STATE.pnameSlotConns[key] = nil
        end
        local uid = slot:GetAttribute("UserId")
        if not uid then return end
        local owner
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if p.UserId == uid then owner = p; break end
        end
        if not owner then return end
        local fn = _GetOrAssignFakeName(owner)
        STATE.pnameSlotConns[key] = lbl:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
            if not STATE.pnameEnabled then return end
            local curUid = slot:GetAttribute("UserId")
            if not curUid then return end
            local curOwner
            for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if p.UserId == curUid then curOwner = p; break end
            end
            if not curOwner then return end
            local curFn = _GetOrAssignFakeName(curOwner)
            if lbl.Text ~= curFn then lbl.Text = curFn end
        end)
    end

    local function _HookAllSlots()
        for key, conn in pairs(STATE.pnameSlotConns) do
            conn:Disconnect()
            STATE.pnameSlotConns[key] = nil
        end
        local survivorMobGui = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("Survivor-mob")
        if not survivorMobGui then return end
        local frame = survivorMobGui:FindFirstChild("Frame")
        if not frame then return end
        for i = 1, 5 do
            local slot = frame:FindFirstChild("Survivor" .. i)
            if slot then
                local lbl = slot:FindFirstChild("TextLabel")
                if lbl then _HookSlotLabel(slot, lbl) end
            end
        end
    end

    local function _GenFakeText(text)
        if type(text) ~= "string" or text == "" then return nil end
        local cands = {}
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            for _, nm in ipairs({ p.Name, p.DisplayName }) do
                if type(nm) == "string" and #nm >= 3 then
                    cands[#cands + 1] = { nm = nm, fake = _GetOrAssignFakeName(p) }
                end
            end
        end
        table.sort(cands, function(a, b) return #a.nm > #b.nm end)
        for _, c in ipairs(cands) do
            if string.find(text, c.nm, 1, true) then
                local esc = c.nm:gsub("(%W)", "%%%1")
                return (text:gsub(esc, c.fake, 1))
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function _GenUnderSurvivorMob(obj)
        local par = obj.Parent
        for _ = 1, 6 do
            if not par then break end
            if par.Name == "Survivor-mob" then return true end
            par = par.Parent
        end
        return false
    end

    local function _GenProcess(obj)
        if not STATE.pnameEnabled then return end
        local ok, isText = pcall(function()
            return obj:IsA("TextLabel") or obj:IsA("TextButton") or obj:IsA("TextBox")
        end)
        if not ok or not isText then return end
        if _GenUnderSurvivorMob(obj) then return end
        local okT, cur = pcall(function() return obj.Text end)
        if not okT or type(cur) ~= "string" then return end
        local want = _GenFakeText(cur)
        if not want or want == cur then return end
        STATE.pnameGenConns = STATE.pnameGenConns or {}
        STATE.pnameGenOrig = STATE.pnameGenOrig or {}
        STATE.pnameGenCount = STATE.pnameGenCount or 0
        if STATE.pnameGenOrig[obj] == nil and STATE.pnameGenCount < 200 then
            STATE.pnameGenOrig[obj] = cur
            STATE.pnameGenCount = STATE.pnameGenCount + 1
        end
        pcall(function() obj.Text = want end)
        if not STATE.pnameGenConns[obj] then
            STATE.pnameGenConns[obj] = obj:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
                if not STATE.pnameEnabled then return end
                local orig = STATE.pnameGenOrig[obj]
                if not orig then return end
                local wantNow = _GenFakeText(orig)
                if wantNow then
                    local okC, curNow = pcall(function() return obj.Text end)
                    if okC and curNow ~= wantNow then
                        pcall(function() obj.Text = wantNow end)
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end

    function StartProtectName()
        STATE.pnameEnabled = true
        for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do _ApplyProtectToPlayer(player) end
        _HookAllSlots()
        pcall(function()
            local pg = localPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
            if pg then
                for _, desc in ipairs(pg:GetDescendants()) do _GenProcess(desc) end
            end
        end)
        if not STATE.pnameWatchConn then
            STATE.pnameWatchConn = PlayerGui.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                if child.Name == "Survivor-mob" then
                    task.wait(0.2)
                    if STATE.pnameEnabled then
                        _HookAllSlots()
                        for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do _ApplyProtectToPlayer(player) end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
        if not STATE.pnameAddedConn then
            STATE.pnameAddedConn = Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
                if STATE.pnameEnabled then
                    task.wait(1)
                    _ApplyProtectToPlayer(player)
                    _HookAllSlots()
                end
            end)
        end
        if not STATE.pnameGenConn then
            STATE.pnameGenConn = PlayerGui.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
                task.defer(function()
                    if STATE.pnameEnabled then _GenProcess(obj) end
                end)
            end)
        end
    end

    function StopProtectName()
        STATE.pnameEnabled = false
        for key, conn in pairs(STATE.pnameSlotConns) do
            conn:Disconnect()
            STATE.pnameSlotConns[key] = nil
        end
        if STATE.pnameWatchConn then
            STATE.pnameWatchConn:Disconnect()
            STATE.pnameWatchConn = nil
        end
        if STATE.pnameGenConn then
            pcall(function() STATE.pnameGenConn:Disconnect() end)
            STATE.pnameGenConn = nil
        end
        for lbl, conn in pairs(STATE.pnameGenConns or {}) do
            pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
        end
        STATE.pnameGenConns = {}
        for lbl, orig in pairs(STATE.pnameGenOrig or {}) do
            pcall(function()
                if lbl and lbl.Parent then lbl.Text = orig end
            end)
        end
        STATE.pnameGenOrig = {}
        STATE.pnameGenCount = 0
        for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do _RestorePlayerName(player) end
        STATE.pnameFakeNames = {}
        if STATE.pnameAddedConn then
            STATE.pnameAddedConn:Disconnect()
            STATE.pnameAddedConn = nil
        end
    end

    -- ============================================================
    --  AVATAR COPY -> SURVIVOR-MOB THUMBNAIL
    -- ============================================================
    local function _GetCopiedThumbnail(userId)
        if STATE.avatarUiImageId == userId and STATE.avatarUiImage then
            return STATE.avatarUiImage
        end
        local url = nil
        for _ = 1, 3 do
            local ok, img, ready = pcall(function()
                return Players:GetUserThumbnailAsync(userId, Enum.ThumbnailType.HeadShot, Enum.ThumbnailSize.Size420x420)
            end)
            if ok and img and img ~= "" then
                url = img
                if ready then break end
            end
            task.wait(1)
        end
        if url then
            STATE.avatarUiImage = url
            STATE.avatarUiImageId = userId
        end
        return url
    end

    local function _FindLocalAvatarSlot()
        local survivorMobGui = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("Survivor-mob")
        if not survivorMobGui then return nil, nil end
        local frame = survivorMobGui:FindFirstChild("Frame")
        if not frame then return nil, nil end
        for i = 1, 5 do
            local slot = frame:FindFirstChild("Survivor" .. i)
            if slot and slot:GetAttribute("UserId") == localPlayer.UserId then
                local img = slot:FindFirstChild("ImageLabel")
                if img then return slot, img end
            end
        end
        return nil, nil
    end

    function _ApplyCopiedAvatarToUI()
        if not STATE.avatarUiActive or not STATE.avatarUiTargetId then return end
        local _, img = _FindLocalAvatarSlot()
        if not img then return end
        local url = _GetCopiedThumbnail(STATE.avatarUiTargetId)
        if url and img.Parent and img.Image ~= url then
            pcall(function() img.Image = url end)
        end
    end

    local function _HookAvatarSlot(slot, img)
        local key = slot.Name
        local old = STATE.avatarUiSlotConns[key]
        if old then
            if type(old) == "table" then
                for _, c in ipairs(old) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
            else
                pcall(function() old:Disconnect() end)
            end
            STATE.avatarUiSlotConns[key] = nil
        end
        local conns = {}
        conns[#conns + 1] = img:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Image"):Connect(function()
            if not STATE.avatarUiActive or not STATE.avatarUiTargetId then return end
            if slot:GetAttribute("UserId") ~= localPlayer.UserId then return end
            local want = STATE.avatarUiImage
            if want and img.Parent and img.Image ~= want then
                pcall(function() img.Image = want end)
            end
        end)
        conns[#conns + 1] = slot:GetAttributeChangedSignal("UserId"):Connect(function()
            if not STATE.avatarUiActive or not STATE.avatarUiTargetId then return end
            task.wait(0.3)
            if slot:GetAttribute("UserId") == localPlayer.UserId then
                _ApplyCopiedAvatarToUI()
            end
        end)
        STATE.avatarUiSlotConns[key] = conns
    end

    function _HookAllAvatarSlots()
        for key, conns in pairs(STATE.avatarUiSlotConns) do
            if type(conns) == "table" then
                for _, c in ipairs(conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
            else
                pcall(function() conns:Disconnect() end)
            end
            STATE.avatarUiSlotConns[key] = nil
        end
        local survivorMobGui = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("Survivor-mob")
        if not survivorMobGui then return end
        local frame = survivorMobGui:FindFirstChild("Frame")
        if not frame then return end
        for i = 1, 5 do
            local slot = frame:FindFirstChild("Survivor" .. i)
            if slot then
                local img = slot:FindFirstChild("ImageLabel")
                if img then _HookAvatarSlot(slot, img) end
            end
        end
    end

    function _EnsureAvatarUiWatchers()
        if not STATE.avatarUiWatchConn then
            STATE.avatarUiWatchConn = PlayerGui.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                if child.Name == "Survivor-mob" then
                    task.wait(0.2)
                    if STATE.avatarUiActive and STATE.avatarUiTargetId then
                        _HookAllAvatarSlots()
                        _ApplyCopiedAvatarToUI()
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
        if not STATE.avatarUiCharConn then
            STATE.avatarUiCharConn = localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
                if not STATE.avatarUiTargetId then return end
                task.wait(1)
                if STATE.avatarUiTargetId then
                    STATE.avatarUiActive = true
                    CopyAvatarFromUsername(tostring(STATE.avatarUiTargetId))
                end
            end)
        end
    end
end

-- ============================================================
--  SKIP END SCREEN
-- ============================================================
local function StartSkipEndScreen(darknessMode)
    darknessMode = darknessMode or false
    local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
    local skipConns = STATE.skipEndScreenConns

    local _forcingCam = false
    local _forcingFov = false
    local _forcingAttr = false

    pcall(function()
        local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        if not remotes then return end

        local function blockRemote(remote)
            if remote and remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                for _, conn in ipairs(getconnections(remote.OnClientEvent)) do
                    conn:Disable()
                    conn:Disconnect()
                end
            end
        end

        local gameRemotes = remotes:FindFirstChild("Game")
        if gameRemotes then
            for _, name in ipairs({"cutscene", "cutsceneEnd", "cutsceneEnd2", "endscreencutscene", "cutsceneEndwithownchar", "shake"}) do
                blockRemote(gameRemotes:FindFirstChild(name))
            end
        end

        local killers = remotes:FindFirstChild("Killers")
        if killers then blockRemote(killers:FindFirstChild("Startmori")) end

        if darknessMode then
            blockRemote(remotes:FindFirstChild("Darkness2"))
        end
    end)

    local function forceCameraCustom()
        if not cam then return end
        if cam.CameraType == Enum.CameraType.Scriptable then
            _forcingCam = true
            cam.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
            _forcingCam = false
        end
    end

    if cam then
        forceCameraCustom()
        table.insert(skipConns, cam:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CameraType"):Connect(function()
            if not _forcingCam then forceCameraCustom() end
        end))

        table.insert(skipConns, cam:GetPropertyChangedSignal("FieldOfView"):Connect(function()
            if _forcingFov or CONFIG.lockFovEnabled then return end
            if cam.FieldOfView ~= 70 then
                _forcingFov = true
                cam.FieldOfView = 70
                _forcingFov = false
            end
        end))
    end

    table.insert(skipConns, Workspace:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CurrentCamera"):Connect(function()
        cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if cam then forceCameraCustom() end
    end))

    table.insert(skipConns, localPlayer:GetAttributeChangedSignal("isspectating"):Connect(function()
        if _forcingAttr then return end
        if localPlayer:GetAttribute("isspectating") then
            _forcingAttr = true
            localPlayer:SetAttribute("isspectating", false)
            _forcingAttr = false
        end
    end))

    table.insert(skipConns, localPlayer:GetAttributeChangedSignal("killerend"):Connect(function()
        if _forcingAttr then return end
        if localPlayer:GetAttribute("killerend") then
            _forcingAttr = true
            localPlayer:SetAttribute("killerend", false)
            _forcingAttr = false
        end
    end))

    local function instantPurgeEndScreen()
        local map = Workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
        if map then
            local endscreen = map:FindFirstChild("endscreen")
            if endscreen then
                pcall(function() endscreen.Parent = nil end)
            end
        end

        local bgSounds = Workspace:FindFirstChild("BackgroundSounds")
        if bgSounds then
            pcall(function() bgSounds:Destroy() end)
        end
    end

    instantPurgeEndScreen()

    table.insert(skipConns, Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(desc)
        if desc.Name == "endscreen" and desc.Parent and desc.Parent.Name == "Map" then
            task.wait(0.01)
            pcall(function() desc.Parent = nil end)
        elseif desc.Name == "BackgroundSounds" then
            pcall(function() desc:Destroy() end)
        end
    end))

    local function purgeGUI(gui)
        if not gui or not gui.Parent then return end
        pcall(function()
            if darknessMode and gui.Name == "Darkness" and gui:IsA("ScreenGui") then
                gui.Enabled = false
            end

            local function purgeDesc(desc)
                if desc:IsA("VideoFrame") then
                    desc:Destroy()
                elseif desc:IsA("Frame") and (desc.Name == "Frame2" or desc.Name == "blackout") then
                    desc.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                    if darknessMode then
                        desc.Visible = false
                        desc:GetPropertyChangedSignal("BackgroundTransparency"):Connect(function()
                            if desc.BackgroundTransparency < 1 then desc.BackgroundTransparency = 1 end
                        end)
                        desc:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Visible"):Connect(function()
                            if desc.Visible then desc.Visible = false end
                        end)
                    end
                elseif desc:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or desc:IsA("Beam") or desc:IsA("Trail") then
                    desc.Enabled = false
                end
                for _, child in ipairs(desc:GetChildren()) do purgeDesc(child) end
            end
            purgeDesc(gui)
        end)
    end

    for _, child in ipairs(PlayerGui:GetChildren()) do
        local n = child.Name
        if n == "Darkness" or n == "EndScreen" or n == "Cutscene" or n == "Results" then
            purgeGUI(child)
        end
    end

    table.insert(skipConns, PlayerGui.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
        local n = child.Name
        if n == "Darkness" or n == "EndScreen" or n == "Cutscene" or n == "Results" then
            task.wait(0.05)
            purgeGUI(child)
        end
    end))
end

local function StopSkipEndScreen()
    for _, connection in ipairs(STATE.skipEndScreenConns) do
        pcall(function() connection:Disconnect() end)
    end
    table.clear(STATE.skipEndScreenConns)
end

local function sharedGetKillerName(player,char)
    if not player or not char then
        return nil
    end

    local values=char:FindFirstChild("Values")
    if values then
        local killerNameVal=values:FindFirstChild("KillerName")
        if killerNameVal and killerNameVal:IsA("StringValue") and killerNameVal.Value~="" then
            return killerNameVal.Value
        end
    end

    local attr=player:GetAttribute("SelectedKiller")
    if typeof(attr)=="string" and attr~="" then
        return attr
    end

    return nil
end

local function sharedIsSpectatorTeam(player)
    if not player or not player.Team then
        return false
    end
    local teamName=player.Team.Name
    return typeof(teamName)=="string"
        and teamName:lower():gsub("%s+",""):find("spectator",1,true)~=nil
end

local survivorMobMarkerCache=setmetatable({}, {__mode="k"})
local survivorMobWatcher=setmetatable({}, {__mode="k"})

local function sharedNormalizeTargetName(value)
    return tostring(value or ""):lower():gsub("[%s%-%_]","")
end

local function sharedIsSurvivorMobName(value)
    local name=sharedNormalizeTargetName(value)
    return name=="survimob" or name=="survivormob"
end

local function sharedHasSurvivorMobMarker(char)
    if not char then
        return false
    end

    if sharedIsSurvivorMobName(char.Name) then
        return true
    end

    local cached=survivorMobMarkerCache[char]
    if cached then
        return cached.value
    end

    local markerCount=0
    for _,child in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
        if sharedIsSurvivorMobName(child.Name) then
            markerCount=markerCount+1
        end
    end

    local entry={count=markerCount,value=markerCount>0}
    survivorMobMarkerCache[char]=entry

    if not survivorMobWatcher[char] then
        survivorMobWatcher[char]=true
        char.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            if sharedIsSurvivorMobName(child.Name) then
                local current=survivorMobMarkerCache[char]
                if current then
                    current.count=current.count+1
                    current.value=current.count>0
                end
            end
        end)
        char.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
            if sharedIsSurvivorMobName(child.Name) then
                local current=survivorMobMarkerCache[char]
                if current then
                    current.count=math.max(0,current.count-1)
                    current.value=current.count>0
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    return entry.value
end

local function sharedShouldIgnoreVisualTarget(player,char)
    if player and player.Team then
        local teamName=sharedNormalizeTargetName(player.Team.Name)
        if teamName:find("spectator",1,true)~=nil
        or teamName:find("survivor",1,true)~=nil then
            return true
        end
    end

    if player and sharedIsSpectatorTeam(player) then
        return true
    end

    if sharedHasSurvivorMobMarker(char) then
        return true
    end

    return false
end

local function sharedIsKillerTeam(player)
    if not player or not player.Team or sharedShouldIgnoreVisualTarget(player,player.Character) then
        return false
    end
    local teamName=player.Team.Name
    return typeof(teamName)=="string" and teamName:lower():find("killer",1,true)~=nil
end

local function sharedHasKillerMobMarker(char)
    if not char or not char.Parent or sharedHasSurvivorMobMarker(char) then
        return false
    end

    local direct=char:FindFirstChild("Killer-mob")
        or char:FindFirstChild("KillerMob")
        or char:FindFirstChild("killer-mob")
        or char:FindFirstChild("killerMob")

    if direct then
        return true
    end

    return false
end

    local cachedParryButton=nil

    local function getParryButton()

            return cachedParryButton
        end

        local mobGui=PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("Survivor-mob")
        local controls=mobGui and mobGui:FindFirstChild("Controls")
        local btn=controls and controls:FindFirstChild("Gui-mob")

        if btn and btn:IsA("ImageButton") then
            cachedParryButton=btn
            return btn
        end

        cachedParryButton=nil
        return nil
    end

    local function sendParryInput()
        local char=localPlayer.Character
        if not char then
            return
        end

        local btn=getParryButton()
        local sent=false

        if btn and type(firesignal)=="function" then
            sent=pcall(function()
                firesignal(btn.MouseButton1Down)
                task.delay(0.05, function()
                    if btn and btn.Parent then
                        firesignal(btn.MouseButton1Up)
                    end
                end)
            end)
        end

        if not sent and type(getconnections)=="function" then
            local okObj,obj=pcall(function()
                local o=Instance.new("InputObject")
                o.UserInputType=Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2
                o.UserInputState=Enum.UserInputState.Begin
                return o
            end)
            if okObj and obj then
                pcall(function()
                    for _,conn in ipairs(getconnections(UserInputService.InputBegan)) do
                        conn:Fire(obj,false)
                    end
                end)
                sent=true
            end
        end

        if not sent then
            pcall(function()
                VirtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(0,0,1,true,game,0)
                task.delay(0.01, function()
                    pcall(function()
                        VirtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(0,0,1,false,game,0)
                    end)
                end)
            end)
        end

        local root=char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if root then
            task.defer(function()
                pcall(function()
                    if CollectionService:HasTag(root,"doing action") then
                        CollectionService:RemoveTag(root,"doing action")
                    end
                end)
            end)
        end
    end

    local PARRY_COOLDOWN=0.35
    local PROFILE_PARRY_COOLDOWN=0.22

    local function parryInputAvailable(now,cooldown)
        local lastParry=math.max(
            STATE.lastParryTime or 0,
            STATE.lastSkillParryTime or 0
        )

        local activeCooldown=cooldown or PARRY_COOLDOWN
        if lastParry>0 and now-lastParry<activeCooldown then
            return false
        end

        return true
    end

    local function getPalletModelFromPart(part)
        if not part then
            return nil
        end

        local current=part
        for _=1,6 do
            if not current then
                break
            end

            local name=string.lower(current.Name or "")
            if name:find("pallet",1,true) or name:find("plank",1,true) then
                if current:IsA("Model") then
                    return current
                end

                local parent=current.Parent
                if parent and parent:IsA("Model") then
                    return parent
                end
            end

            current=current.Parent
        end

        return nil
    end

    local function isStandingPallet(model)
        if not model or not model.Parent then
            return false
        end

        local ok,_,size=pcall(function()
            local cf,bounds=model:GetBoundingBox()
            return cf,bounds
        end)

        if not ok or not size then
            return false
        end

        local horizontal=math.max(size.X,size.Z,0.01)
        return size.Y>=horizontal*0.50
    end

    local function getPalletContext(hitPart)
        local model=getPalletModelFromPart(hitPart)
        if not model then
            return nil
        end

        return {
            model=model,
            standing=isStandingPallet(model),
            dropped=not isStandingPallet(model)
        }
    end

    local function getObstacleState(origin,target,extraIgnore)
        local localChar=localPlayer.Character
        local params=RaycastParams.new()
        params.FilterType=Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        params.FilterDescendantsInstances={localChar,extraIgnore}
        params.IgnoreWater=true

        local direction=target-origin

        if direction.Magnitude<=0.05 then
            return {
                clear=true,
                blocked=false,
                vault=false,
                pallet=false,
                standingPallet=false,
                droppedPallet=false,
                clearCount=1,
                blockedCount=0,
                directBlocked=false
            }
        end

        local flat=Vector3.new(direction.X,0,direction.Z)
        local flatUnit=flat.Magnitude>0.05 and flat.Unit or Vector3.zero
        local right=flatUnit:Cross(Vector3.new(0,1,0))

        local sideOffset=0.90
        local verticalOffset=0.75
        local rayData={
            {origin,target,true},
            {origin+right*sideOffset,target+right*sideOffset,false},
            {origin-right*sideOffset,target-right*sideOffset,false},
            {origin+Vector3.new(0,verticalOffset,0),target+Vector3.new(0,verticalOffset,0),false},
            {origin-Vector3.new(0,verticalOffset,0),target-Vector3.new(0,verticalOffset,0),false},
        }

        local localHead=localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("Head")
        local killerHead=extraIgnore and extraIgnore:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if localHead and killerHead then
            table.insert(rayData,{localHead.Position,killerHead.Position,false})
        end

        local clearCount=0
        local blockedCount=0
        local vaultHit=false
        local palletHit=false
        local standingPalletHit=false
        local droppedPalletHit=false
        local palletBlockedCount=0
        local directBlocked=false

        for _,pair in ipairs(rayData) do
            local result=Workspace:Raycast(pair[1],pair[2]-pair[1],params)

            if not result then
                clearCount=clearCount+1
            else
                blockedCount=blockedCount+1
                if pair[3] then
                    directBlocked=true
                end

                local hit=result.Instance
                if hit then
                    local n=string.lower(hit.Name or "")

                    if n:find("vault",1,true)
                    or n:find("window",1,true)
                    or n:find("ledge",1,true) then
                        vaultHit=true
                    end

                    if n:find("pallet",1,true)
                    or n:find("plank",1,true) then
                        palletHit=true
                        palletBlockedCount=palletBlockedCount+1

                        local pallet=getPalletContext(hit)
                        if pallet then
                            if pallet.standing then
                                standingPalletHit=true
                            else
                                droppedPalletHit=true
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local sampleCount=#rayData
        local clear=clearCount>=math.max(4,math.ceil(sampleCount*0.66))
        local blocked=blockedCount>=math.ceil(sampleCount*0.50)

        return {
            clear=clear,
            blocked=blocked,
            vault=vaultHit,
            pallet=palletHit,
            standingPallet=standingPalletHit,
            droppedPallet=droppedPalletHit,
            palletBlockedCount=palletBlockedCount,
            clearCount=clearCount,
            blockedCount=blockedCount,
            directBlocked=directBlocked
        }
    end

    local function doParry(force,killerChar,distance,allowPredictedRange,allowEdgePulse)
        if not STATE.autoParryEnabled then
            return false
        end

        local now=tick()
        local profileData=killerChar and STATE.activeAttackers[killerChar]
        local isAttackProfile=profileData and profileData.profile~=nil
        local cooldown=isAttackProfile and PROFILE_PARRY_COOLDOWN or PARRY_COOLDOWN

        if not parryInputAvailable(now,cooldown) then
            return false
        end

        if killerChar and distance then
            local allowedRange=STATE.autoParryRadius
            local allowVault=false
            local data=profileData

            if data and data.profile and data.profile.type=="lungehold" then
                allowVault=true
            end

            if distance>allowedRange and not allowPredictedRange then
                return false
            end

            local localChar=localPlayer.Character
            local localRoot=localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local killerRoot=killerChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

            if not localRoot or not killerRoot then
                return false
            end

            local obstacle=getObstacleState(
                localRoot.Position,
                killerRoot.Position,
                killerChar
            )

            local edgePulseAllowed=false
            if allowEdgePulse then
                edgePulseAllowed=(
                    obstacle.clearCount>=3
                    and obstacle.blockedCount>=1
                    and obstacle.blockedCount<=math.ceil(6*0.75)
                )
            end

            if obstacle.directBlocked and not edgePulseAllowed then
                return false
            end

            if (obstacle.blocked or not obstacle.clear) and not edgePulseAllowed then
                return false
            end
        end

        STATE.lastParryTime=now
        STATE.lastSkillParryTime=now
        sendParryInput()

        return true
    end

    local function doSkillParry()
        if not STATE.autoParryEnabled then
            return false
        end

        local now=tick()

        if not parryInputAvailable(now) then
            return false
        end

        STATE.lastSkillParryTime=now
        STATE.lastParryTime=now
        sendParryInput()

        return true
    end

    local function getSkillDistance(char)
        local localChar=localPlayer.Character
        local localRoot=localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local killerRoot=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if not localRoot or not killerRoot then
            return math.huge
        end

        local a=localRoot.Position
        local b=killerRoot.Position

        return (Vector3.new(a.X,0,a.Z)-Vector3.new(b.X,0,b.Z)).Magnitude
    end

    local function getPredictedDistance(char,maxTime)
        local localChar=localPlayer.Character
        local localRoot=localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local killerRoot=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if not localRoot or not killerRoot then
            return math.huge
        end

        local localPosition=localRoot.Position
        local killerPosition=killerRoot.Position

        local localVelocity=localRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local killerVelocity=killerRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity

        local localFlatVelocity=Vector3.new(localVelocity.X,0,localVelocity.Z)
        local killerFlatVelocity=Vector3.new(killerVelocity.X,0,killerVelocity.Z)

        local ping=math.clamp(localPlayer:GetNetworkPing(),0,0.3)
        local baseTime=ping+0.04
        local bestDistance=math.huge

        local samples={
            math.clamp(baseTime,0,maxTime),
            math.clamp(baseTime+0.05,0,maxTime),
            math.clamp(baseTime+0.10,0,maxTime),
            math.clamp(baseTime+0.16,0,maxTime),
            math.clamp(baseTime+0.22,0,maxTime),
            math.clamp(baseTime+0.30,0,maxTime),
            math.clamp(baseTime+0.38,0,maxTime)
        }

        for _,predictionTime in ipairs(samples) do
            local predictedLocal=localPosition+localFlatVelocity*predictionTime
            local predictedKiller=killerPosition+killerFlatVelocity*predictionTime

            local predictedDistance=(
                Vector3.new(predictedLocal.X,0,predictedLocal.Z)-
                Vector3.new(predictedKiller.X,0,predictedKiller.Z)
            ).Magnitude

            if predictedDistance<bestDistance then
                bestDistance=predictedDistance
            end
        end

        return bestDistance
    end

    local function getMaskedDirectionalPrediction(char)
        local localChar=localPlayer.Character
        local localRoot=localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local killerRoot=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if not localRoot or not killerRoot then
            return math.huge
        end

        local localPosition=localRoot.Position
        local killerPosition=killerRoot.Position

        local localVelocity=localRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local killerVelocity=killerRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity

        local localFlat=Vector3.new(localVelocity.X,0,localVelocity.Z)
        local killerFlat=Vector3.new(killerVelocity.X,0,killerVelocity.Z)

        local killerLook=killerRoot.CFrame.LookVector
        local killerLookFlat=Vector3.new(killerLook.X,0,killerLook.Z)

        if killerLookFlat.Magnitude>0.05 then
            killerLookFlat=killerLookFlat.Unit
        else
            killerLookFlat=Vector3.zero
        end

        local killerSpeed=killerFlat.Magnitude

        if killerSpeed>0.05 and killerLookFlat.Magnitude>0 then
            local forwardSpeed=killerFlat:Dot(killerLookFlat)

            if forwardSpeed>0 then
                killerFlat=killerFlat+killerLookFlat*math.min(forwardSpeed*0.45,6)
            end
        end

        local relative=killerPosition-localPosition
        local relativeFlat=Vector3.new(relative.X,0,relative.Z)
        local distance=relativeFlat.Magnitude

        if distance<=0.05 then
            return 0
        end

        local direction=relativeFlat.Unit
        local relativeVelocity=killerFlat-localFlat
        local closingSpeed=math.max(0,relativeVelocity:Dot(direction))

        local ping=math.clamp(localPlayer:GetNetworkPing(),0,0.3)
        local base=ping+0.025
        local best=distance

        local samples={
            base,
            base+0.02,
            base+0.04,
            base+0.07,
            base+0.10,
            base+0.14,
            base+0.19,
            base+0.25,
            base+0.32,
            base+0.40,
            base+0.50,
            base+0.60
        }

        for _,t in ipairs(samples) do
            local pLocal=localPosition+localFlat*t
            local pKiller=killerPosition+killerFlat*t

            local d=(Vector3.new(pLocal.X,0,pLocal.Z)-Vector3.new(pKiller.X,0,pKiller.Z)).Magnitude

            if d<best then
                best=d
            end
        end

        if closingSpeed>0 then
            local impactTime=math.max(distance-STATE.autoParryRadius,0)/closingSpeed

            if impactTime<=0.55 then
                best=math.min(best,STATE.autoParryRadius-0.05)
            end
        end

        return best
    end


    local MOVEMENT_HISTORY_WINDOW=0.24
    local MOVEMENT_REVERSAL_DOT=-0.20
    local MOVEMENT_REVERSAL_MIN_SPEED=2.0
    local MOVEMENT_REVERSAL_RANGE=1.35
    local MOVEMENT_REVERSAL_PREDICT=0.18
    local LANDING_ASSIST_WINDOW=0.22

    local movementHistory={
        localSample=nil,
        killers={}
    }

    local cornerMovementHistory={
        localSample=nil,
        killers={}
    }

    local TURN_PARRY_ANGLE_DEGREES=42
    local TURN_PARRY_SAMPLE_INTERVAL=0.10
    local TURN_PARRY_MAX_SAMPLE_AGE=0.16
    local TURN_PARRY_ACTIVE_WINDOW=0.16
    local TURN_PARRY_EXTRA_RANGE=1.35
    local localTurnHistory={
        look=nil,
        sampledAt=0,
        activeUntil=0
    }

    local function updateTurnParryWindow(localRoot,now)
        if not localRoot then
            return false
        end

        local look=localRoot.CFrame.LookVector
        local flatLook=Vector3.new(look.X,0,look.Z)
        if flatLook.Magnitude<=0.001 then
            return now<=localTurnHistory.activeUntil
        end
        flatLook=flatLook.Unit

        if not localTurnHistory.look then
            localTurnHistory.look=flatLook
            localTurnHistory.sampledAt=now
            return false
        end

        if now-localTurnHistory.sampledAt>=TURN_PARRY_SAMPLE_INTERVAL then
            local elapsed=now-localTurnHistory.sampledAt
            local dot=math.clamp(localTurnHistory.look:Dot(flatLook),-1,1)
            local angle=math.deg(math.acos(dot))

            if elapsed<=TURN_PARRY_MAX_SAMPLE_AGE
            and angle>=TURN_PARRY_ANGLE_DEGREES then
                localTurnHistory.activeUntil=now+TURN_PARRY_ACTIVE_WINDOW
            end

            localTurnHistory.look=flatLook
            localTurnHistory.sampledAt=now
        end

        return now<=localTurnHistory.activeUntil
    end

    local function flatVelocity(root)
        if not root then
            return Vector3.zero
        end

        local v=root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        return Vector3.new(v.X,0,v.Z)
    end

    local function updateMovementHistory(killerChar,localRoot,killerRoot)
        local now=tick()
        local localVel=flatVelocity(localRoot)
        local killerVel=flatVelocity(killerRoot)

        local previousLocal=movementHistory.localSample
        local previousKiller=movementHistory.killers[killerChar]

        movementHistory.localSample={
            velocity=localVel,
            time=now
        }

        movementHistory.killers[killerChar]={
            velocity=killerVel,
            time=now
        }

        return previousLocal,previousKiller,now,localVel,killerVel
    end

    local function getMovementReversalAssist(killerChar,distance,predictedDistance)
        local localChar=localPlayer.Character
        local localRoot=localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local killerRoot=killerChar and killerChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if not localRoot or not killerRoot then
            return false
        end

        local previousLocal,previousKiller,now,localVel,killerVel=
            updateMovementHistory(killerChar,localRoot,killerRoot)

        local localReversed=false
        local killerReversed=false

        if previousLocal
        and now-previousLocal.time<=MOVEMENT_HISTORY_WINDOW
        and previousLocal.velocity.Magnitude>=MOVEMENT_REVERSAL_MIN_SPEED
        and localVel.Magnitude>=MOVEMENT_REVERSAL_MIN_SPEED then
            local dot=previousLocal.velocity.Unit:Dot(localVel.Unit)
            localReversed=dot<=MOVEMENT_REVERSAL_DOT
        end

        if previousKiller
        and now-previousKiller.time<=MOVEMENT_HISTORY_WINDOW
        and previousKiller.velocity.Magnitude>=MOVEMENT_REVERSAL_MIN_SPEED
        and killerVel.Magnitude>=MOVEMENT_REVERSAL_MIN_SPEED then
            local dot=previousKiller.velocity.Unit:Dot(killerVel.Unit)
            killerReversed=dot<=MOVEMENT_REVERSAL_DOT
        end

        local humanoid=localChar and localChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local state=humanoid and humanoid:GetState()
        local landingState=(state==Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed
            or state==Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running
            or state==Enum.HumanoidStateType.RunningNoPhysics)

        local recentlyAirborne=false
        if humanoid then
            local y=localRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y
            recentlyAirborne=math.abs(y)>=7
                or state==Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall
                or state==Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping
        end

        if not (localReversed or killerReversed or recentlyAirborne) then
            return false
        end

        if distance>STATE.autoParryRadius+MOVEMENT_REVERSAL_RANGE then
            return false
        end

        local offset=localRoot.Position-killerRoot.Position
        local flatOffset=Vector3.new(offset.X,0,offset.Z)
        if flatOffset.Magnitude<=0.05 then
            return true
        end

        local direction=flatOffset.Unit

        local compensatedLocal=localVel
        if localReversed and previousLocal then
            compensatedLocal=previousLocal.velocity*0.55+localVel*0.45
        end

        local compensatedKiller=killerVel
        if killerReversed and previousKiller then
            compensatedKiller=previousKiller.velocity*0.55+killerVel*0.45
        end

        local relative=compensatedKiller-compensatedLocal
        local compensatedClosing=relative:Dot(direction)

        local predictedLocal=localRoot.Position+compensatedLocal*MOVEMENT_REVERSAL_PREDICT
        local predictedKiller=killerRoot.Position+compensatedKiller*MOVEMENT_REVERSAL_PREDICT
        local compensatedDistance=(
            Vector3.new(predictedLocal.X,0,predictedLocal.Z)-
            Vector3.new(predictedKiller.X,0,predictedKiller.Z)
        ).Magnitude

        if compensatedDistance<=STATE.autoParryRadius+0.55 then
            return true
        end

        if compensatedClosing>=0.75
        and (predictedDistance<=STATE.autoParryRadius+MOVEMENT_REVERSAL_RANGE
            or distance<=STATE.autoParryRadius+0.55) then
            return true
        end

        if landingState and recentlyAirborne
        and distance<=STATE.autoParryRadius+0.65 then
            return true
        end

        return false
    end

    local function getFaceKillerThreshold()
        local sensitivity=math.clamp(STATE.faceKillerSensitivity,-1,1)

        return 0.10+((sensitivity+1)/2)*0.55
    end

    local FOLLOW_CONTACT_ASSIST_ENABLED=true
    local FOLLOW_CONTACT_PREDICTION=0.14
    local FOLLOW_CONTACT_MIN_CLOSING=0.65
    local FOLLOW_CONTACT_MIN_KILLER_SPEED=1.25
    local FOLLOW_CONTACT_ALIGNMENT=0.12

    local function followContactAssist(killerChar)
        if not FOLLOW_CONTACT_ASSIST_ENABLED then
            return false
        end

        local localChar=localPlayer.Character
        local localRoot=localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local killerRoot=killerChar and killerChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if not localRoot or not killerRoot then
            return false
        end

        local offset=localRoot.Position-killerRoot.Position
        local flatOffset=Vector3.new(offset.X,0,offset.Z)
        local distance=flatOffset.Magnitude

        if distance<=0.05 then
            return true
        end

        local direction=flatOffset.Unit
        local killerVelocity=killerRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local survivorVelocity=localRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity

        local killerFlat=Vector3.new(killerVelocity.X,0,killerVelocity.Z)
        local survivorFlat=Vector3.new(survivorVelocity.X,0,survivorVelocity.Z)
        local relativeVelocity=killerFlat-survivorFlat
        local closingSpeed=relativeVelocity:Dot(direction)

        local predictedKiller=killerRoot.Position+killerFlat*FOLLOW_CONTACT_PREDICTION
        local predictedSurvivor=localRoot.Position+survivorFlat*FOLLOW_CONTACT_PREDICTION
        local predictedOffset=predictedSurvivor-predictedKiller
        local predictedDistance=Vector3.new(
            predictedOffset.X,0,predictedOffset.Z
        ).Magnitude

        if closingSpeed>=FOLLOW_CONTACT_MIN_CLOSING
        and predictedDistance<distance then
            return true
        end

        if killerFlat.Magnitude>=FOLLOW_CONTACT_MIN_KILLER_SPEED then
            local alignment=killerFlat.Unit:Dot(direction)
            if alignment>=FOLLOW_CONTACT_ALIGNMENT
            and predictedDistance<distance then
                return true
            end
        end

        local velocityDelta=(killerFlat-survivorFlat).Magnitude
        if killerFlat.Magnitude>=FOLLOW_CONTACT_MIN_KILLER_SPEED
        and velocityDelta>=0.65
        and predictedDistance<distance then
            return true
        end

        return false
    end


    local function isFacingLocalPlayer(killerChar)
        local localChar=localPlayer.Character
        local localRoot=localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local killerRoot=killerChar and killerChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if not localRoot or not killerRoot then
            return false
        end

        local direction=localRoot.Position-killerRoot.Position
        local flatDirection=Vector3.new(direction.X,0,direction.Z)

        if flatDirection.Magnitude<=0.05 then
            return true
        end

        local look=killerRoot.CFrame.LookVector
        local flatLook=Vector3.new(look.X,0,look.Z)

        if flatLook.Magnitude<=0.05 then
            return true
        end

        local dot=flatLook.Unit:Dot(flatDirection.Unit)
        local threshold=getFaceKillerThreshold()

        local sensitivity=math.clamp(STATE.faceKillerSensitivity,-1,1)
        local strictPercent=(sensitivity+1)/2
        local allowedAngle=80-(strictPercent*42)
        local aimThreshold=math.cos(math.rad(allowedAngle))

        if dot>=math.max(threshold,aimThreshold) then
            return true
        end

        local velocity=killerRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local flatVelocity=Vector3.new(velocity.X,0,velocity.Z)

        if flatVelocity.Magnitude>6 then
            local velocityDot=flatVelocity.Unit:Dot(flatDirection.Unit)
            local velocityThreshold=0.20+(strictPercent*0.35)

            if velocityDot>=velocityThreshold then
                return true
            end
        end

        return false
    end

    local PUBLIC_SERVER_GAP_BASE=0.75
    local PUBLIC_SERVER_GAP_MAX=4.25
    local PUBLIC_SERVER_ONEWAY_MAX=0.24
    local PUBLIC_SERVER_SPEED_FACTOR=0.90
    local PUBLIC_SERVER_EXTRA_LEAD=0.045

    local function getServerProximityAssist(killerChar,distance,predictedDistance,closingSpeed)
        if not killerChar or not distance then
            return false
        end

        local localChar=localPlayer.Character
        local localRoot=localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local killerRoot=killerChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not localRoot or not killerRoot then
            return false
        end

        local killerVelocity=killerRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local localVelocity=localRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity

        local killerFlat=Vector3.new(killerVelocity.X,0,killerVelocity.Z)
        local localFlat=Vector3.new(localVelocity.X,0,localVelocity.Z)

        local offset=Vector3.new(
            localRoot.Position.X-killerRoot.Position.X,
            0,
            localRoot.Position.Z-killerRoot.Position.Z
        )
        local offsetMagnitude=offset.Magnitude
        if offsetMagnitude<=0.05 then
            return true
        end

        local direction=offset.Unit
        local relativeVelocity=localFlat-killerFlat
        local approachSpeed=math.max(0,relativeVelocity:Dot(-direction))

        local ping=math.clamp(localPlayer:GetNetworkPing(),0,0.45)
        local oneWay=math.clamp(ping*0.5,0.025,PUBLIC_SERVER_ONEWAY_MAX)
        local lead=oneWay+PUBLIC_SERVER_EXTRA_LEAD

        local killerToward=math.max(0,killerFlat:Dot(direction))
        local killerAdvance=killerToward*lead
        local playerToward=math.max(0,localFlat:Dot(-direction))
        local playerAdvance=playerToward*lead

        local authorityGap=PUBLIC_SERVER_GAP_BASE
            +math.min(
                killerAdvance+playerAdvance,
                PUBLIC_SERVER_GAP_MAX-PUBLIC_SERVER_GAP_BASE
            )

        authorityGap=authorityGap
            +math.min(
                approachSpeed*lead*PUBLIC_SERVER_SPEED_FACTOR,
                1.35
            )

        authorityGap=math.clamp(authorityGap,PUBLIC_SERVER_GAP_BASE,PUBLIC_SERVER_GAP_MAX)

        local effectiveDistance=distance-authorityGap
        local effectivePredicted=(predictedDistance or math.huge)-authorityGap*0.65

        local approachEvidence=(
            approachSpeed>=2.0
            or (closingSpeed and closingSpeed>=2.0)
            or killerToward>=2.0
        )

        if not approachEvidence then
            return false
        end

        return (
            effectiveDistance<=STATE.autoParryRadius
            or effectivePredicted<=STATE.autoParryRadius
        )
    end

    local function canNormalAttackParry(killerChar,distance,closingSpeed,predictedDistance)
        if distance>STATE.autoParryRadius then
            if followContactAssist(killerChar) then
                return true
            end
            if getMovementReversalAssist(killerChar,distance,predictedDistance) then
                return true
            end
            if getServerProximityAssist(killerChar,distance,predictedDistance,closingSpeed) then
                return true
            end
            return false
        end

        if getMovementReversalAssist(killerChar,distance,predictedDistance) then
            return true
        end

        if getServerProximityAssist(killerChar,distance,predictedDistance,closingSpeed) then
            return true
        end

        if followContactAssist(killerChar) then
            return true
        end

        if closingSpeed and predictedDistance then
            if closingSpeed>=3.5 and predictedDistance<=STATE.autoParryRadius then
                return true
            end
        end

        local localChar=localPlayer.Character
        local killerRoot=killerChar and killerChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local localRoot=localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if localRoot and killerRoot then
            local toLocal=localRoot.Position-killerRoot.Position
            local flatToLocal=Vector3.new(toLocal.X,0,toLocal.Z)
            if flatToLocal.Magnitude>0.05 then
                local velocity=killerRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                local flatVelocity=Vector3.new(velocity.X,0,velocity.Z)
                if flatVelocity.Magnitude>=2.0 then
                    local moveDot=flatVelocity.Unit:Dot(flatToLocal.Unit)
                    if moveDot>=0.25 then
                        return true
                    end
                end

            end
        end

        if isFacingLocalPlayer(killerChar) then
            return true
        end

        local localChar=localPlayer.Character
        local localRoot=localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local killerRoot=killerChar and killerChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if not localRoot or not killerRoot then
            return false
        end

        local direction=localRoot.Position-killerRoot.Position
        local flatDirection=Vector3.new(direction.X,0,direction.Z)

        if flatDirection.Magnitude<=2.0 then
            return true
        end

        return false
    end

    local function registerAbysswalkerAttack(char,kName,track)
        if STATE.ignoredTheAbysswalker then
            return
        end

        local data={
            name=kName or "The Abysswalker",
            track=track,
            type="abysswalker",
            started=tick(),
            consumed=false,
            skillId=ABYSSWALKER_ANIM_ID
        }

        STATE.activeSkillAttackers[char]=data

        local conn

        conn=track.Stopped:Connect(function()
            if conn then
                conn:Disconnect()
                conn=nil
            end

            task.defer(function()
                if STATE.activeSkillAttackers[char]
                and STATE.activeSkillAttackers[char].track==track then
                    STATE.activeSkillAttackers[char]=nil
                end
            end)
        end)

        local distance=getSkillDistance(char)

        if distance<=ABYSSWALKER_RANGE then
            if doSkillParry() then
                data.consumed=true
            end
        end
    end

    local function getMaskedWalkTracks(char)
        local tracks={}
        local humanoid=char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local animator=humanoid and humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")

        if not animator then
            return tracks
        end

        for _,track in ipairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
            if track and track.IsPlaying and track.Animation then
                local id=getAnimNumericId(track.Animation.AnimationId)

                if id==MASKED_WALK_ID then
                    tracks[track]=true
                end
            end
        end

        return tracks
    end

    local function clearMaskedSequence(char)
        STATE.maskedSequence[char]=nil
        STATE.maskedLungeArmed[char]=nil
    end

    local function getMaskedPredictedDistance(char)
        return getMaskedDirectionalPrediction(char)
    end

    local function maskedCanParryNow(char,seq)
        if not char or not char.Parent or not seq then
            return false
        end

        if STATE.ignoredTheMaskedDuck
        or seq.consumed
        or not seq.lungeReachedMinTime
        or not seq.lungeFinished
        or not seq.walkSeen then
            return false
        end

        local distance=getSkillDistance(char)

        if distance<=STATE.autoParryRadius then
            return true
        end

        local predictedDistance=getMaskedPredictedDistance(char)

        if predictedDistance<=STATE.autoParryRadius then
            return true
        end

        local localChar=localPlayer.Character
        local localRoot=localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local killerRoot=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if localRoot and killerRoot then
            local relative=killerRoot.Position-localRoot.Position
            local relativeFlat=Vector3.new(relative.X,0,relative.Z)

            if relativeFlat.Magnitude>0.05 then
                local localVelocity=localRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                local killerVelocity=killerRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity

                local relativeVelocity=Vector3.new(
                    killerVelocity.X-localVelocity.X,
                    0,
                    killerVelocity.Z-localVelocity.Z
                )

                local closingSpeed=math.max(0,relativeVelocity:Dot(relativeFlat.Unit))

                if closingSpeed>=3 then
                    local timeToRadius=math.max(distance-STATE.autoParryRadius,0)/closingSpeed

                    if timeToRadius<=0.25 then
                        return true
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        return false
    end

    local function maskedTryLungeParry(char,seq)
        if not char or not char.Parent or not seq then
            return false
        end

        if STATE.ignoredTheMaskedDuck
        or seq.consumed
        or not seq.lungeReachedMinTime then
            return false
        end

        local distance=getSkillDistance(char)
        local predictedDistance=getMaskedPredictedDistance(char)

        if distance<=STATE.autoParryRadius then
            if doParry(true,char,distance) then
                seq.consumed=true
                seq.parried=true
                clearMaskedSequence(char)
                return true
            end

            return false
        end

        if predictedDistance<=STATE.autoParryRadius then
            if doParry(true,char,distance,true) then
                seq.consumed=true
                seq.parried=true
                clearMaskedSequence(char)
                return true
            end
        end

        return false
    end

    local function maskedTryWalkParry(char,seq)
        if not seq
        or seq.consumed
        or not seq.lungeReachedMinTime
        or not seq.lungeFinished
        or not seq.walkSeen then
            return false
        end

        if not maskedCanParryNow(char,seq) then
            return false
        end

        local distance=getSkillDistance(char)

        if distance<=STATE.autoParryRadius then
            if doParry(true,char,distance) then
                seq.consumed=true
                seq.parried=true
                clearMaskedSequence(char)
                return true
            end
        else
            local predictedDistance=getMaskedPredictedDistance(char)

            if predictedDistance<=STATE.autoParryRadius then
                if doParry(true,char,distance,true) then
                    seq.consumed=true
                    seq.parried=true
                    clearMaskedSequence(char)
                    return true
                end
            end
        end

        return false
    end

    local function maskedWalkIsNew(seq,track)
        if not seq or not track or seq.consumed then
            return false
        end

        if seq.acceptedWalkTracks and seq.acceptedWalkTracks[track] then
            return false
        end

        if seq.lungeTrack==track then
            return false
        end

        return true
    end

    local function registerMaskedWalkPreparation(char,seq,track)
        if not seq or seq.consumed or not track then
            return false
        end

        if not maskedWalkIsNew(seq,track) then
            return false
        end

        if tick()>seq.expires then
            return false
        end

        if not seq.lungeReachedMinTime or not seq.lungeFinished then
            seq.pendingWalkTrack=track
            return false
        end

        seq.acceptedWalkTracks=seq.acceptedWalkTracks or {}
        seq.acceptedWalkTracks[track]=true
        seq.walkTrack=track
        seq.walkStarted=tick()
        seq.walkSeen=true
        seq.prepared=true

        maskedTryWalkParry(char,seq)

        return true
    end

    local function registerMaskedFastAttack(char,kName,track,profile,animId)
        if STATE.ignoredTheMaskedDuck then
            return
        end

        local numericId=typeof(animId)=="number"
            and animId
            or getAnimNumericId(track.Animation and track.Animation.AnimationId)

        if not numericId then
            return
        end

        if numericId==MASKED_LUNGEHOLD_ID then
            local now=tick()
            local actualLength=track.Length

            if not actualLength or actualLength<=0 then
                actualLength=MASKED_EXTRA_ANIMS[MASKED_LUNGEHOLD_ID].length
            end

            local seq={
                armed=false,
                armedAt=nil,
                expires=now+MASKED_SEQUENCE_TIME,
                lungeTrack=track,
                lungeReachedMinTime=false,
                lungeFinished=false,
                lungePosition=0,
                lungeLength=actualLength,
                lungeStoppedAt=nil,
                walkTrack=nil,
                pendingWalkTrack=nil,
                walkStarted=nil,
                walkSeen=false,
                prepared=false,
                consumed=false,
                parried=false,
                acceptedWalkTracks={}
            }

            STATE.maskedSequence[char]=seq
            STATE.maskedLungeArmed[char]=seq

            local conn

            conn=track.Stopped:Connect(function()
                if conn then
                    conn:Disconnect()
                    conn=nil
                end

                task.defer(function()
                    if STATE.maskedSequence[char]~=seq then
                        return
                    end

                    seq.lungeFinished=true
                    seq.lungeStoppedAt=tick()

                    local currentWalkTracks=getMaskedWalkTracks(char)

                    for walkTrack in pairs(currentWalkTracks) do
                        registerMaskedWalkPreparation(char,seq,walkTrack)

                        if STATE.maskedSequence[char]~=seq then
                            return
                        end
                    end

                    if seq.pendingWalkTrack and seq.pendingWalkTrack.IsPlaying then
                        registerMaskedWalkPreparation(char,seq,seq.pendingWalkTrack)
                    end

                    if not seq.lungeReachedMinTime then
                        clearMaskedSequence(char)
                    end
                end)
            end)

            return
        end

        if numericId==MASKED_WALK_ID then
            local seq=STATE.maskedSequence[char]

            if seq then
                if seq.lungeTrack and seq.lungeTrack.IsPlaying then
                    seq.pendingWalkTrack=track
                else
                    seq.lungeFinished=true
                    registerMaskedWalkPreparation(char,seq,track)
                end
            end

            return
        end

        if numericId==MASKED_ATTACK_ID or numericId==MASKED_ATTACKDONE_ID then
            return
        end
    end

    local function maskedHeartbeatMonitor()
        if not STATE.autoParryEnabled then
            return
        end

        for killerChar,seq in pairs(STATE.maskedSequence) do
            if not killerChar or not killerChar.Parent or not seq then
                clearMaskedSequence(killerChar)
            elseif seq.consumed then
                clearMaskedSequence(killerChar)
            elseif tick()>seq.expires then
                clearMaskedSequence(killerChar)
            else
                local lungeTrack=seq.lungeTrack

                if lungeTrack then
                    if lungeTrack.IsPlaying then
                        local position=lungeTrack.TimePosition or 0
                        seq.lungePosition=position

                        if position>=MASKED_MIN_LUNGE_TIME then
                            seq.lungeReachedMinTime=true

                            if not seq.armed then
                                seq.armed=true
                                seq.armedAt=tick()
                                STATE.maskedLungeArmed[killerChar]=seq
                            end

                            if maskedTryLungeParry(killerChar,seq) then
                                continue
                            end
                        end
                    elseif seq.lungeReachedMinTime then
                        seq.lungeFinished=true

                        local distance=getSkillDistance(killerChar)
                        local predictedDistance=getMaskedPredictedDistance(killerChar)

                        if distance<=STATE.autoParryRadius
                        or predictedDistance<=STATE.autoParryRadius then
                            if maskedTryLungeParry(killerChar,seq) then
                                continue
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end

                if seq.lungeFinished and seq.lungeReachedMinTime then
                    if seq.pendingWalkTrack and seq.pendingWalkTrack.IsPlaying then
                        registerMaskedWalkPreparation(killerChar,seq,seq.pendingWalkTrack)

                        if not STATE.maskedSequence[killerChar] then
                            continue
                        end
                    end

                    local currentWalkTracks=getMaskedWalkTracks(killerChar)

                    for walkTrack in pairs(currentWalkTracks) do
                        registerMaskedWalkPreparation(killerChar,seq,walkTrack)

                        if not STATE.maskedSequence[killerChar] then
                            break
                        end
                    end

                    local current=STATE.maskedSequence[killerChar]

                    if current and current.walkSeen then
                        maskedTryWalkParry(killerChar,current)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local boundCharacters={}

    local function cleanupCharacter(char)
        boundCharacters[char]=nil
        STATE.killerCharacters[char]=nil
        STATE.activeAttackers[char]=nil
        STATE.activeSkillAttackers[char]=nil
        STATE.maskedLungeArmed[char]=nil
        STATE.maskedSequence[char]=nil
    end

    local function OnParryCharacterAdded(player,char)
        if not player or not char or player==localPlayer then
            return
        end

        if boundCharacters[char] then
            return
        end

        boundCharacters[char]=true

        local kName=getKillerName(player,char) or "Unknown Killer"

        local function resolveKiller()
            if not char.Parent then
                return false
            end

            local detectedName=getKillerName(player,char)
            local teamKiller=isKillerTeam(player)
            local known=STATE.killerCharacters[char]

            if detectedName then
                kName=detectedName

                if not known then
                    STATE.killerCharacters[char]={
                        player=player,
                        name=kName
                    }
                else
                    known.player=player
                    known.name=kName
                end

                return true
            end

            if teamKiller then
                if not known then
                    STATE.killerCharacters[char]={
                        player=player,
                        name=kName
                    }
                else
                    known.player=player

                    if known.name==nil
                    or known.name==""
                    or known.name=="Unknown Killer" then
                        known.name=kName
                    end
                end

                return true
            end

            if known and known.player==player then
                kName=known.name or kName
                return true
            end

            return false
        end

        resolveKiller()

        char.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_,parent)
            if not parent then
                cleanupCharacter(char)
            end
        end)

        task.spawn(function()
            for _=1,12 do
                if not char.Parent then
                    return
                end

                resolveKiller()
                task.wait(0.5)
            end
        end)

        local humanoid=char:WaitForChild("Humanoid",3)

        if not humanoid then
            return
        end

        local animator=humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator",3)

        if not animator then
            return
        end

        animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
            if not STATE.autoParryEnabled then
                return
            end

            if not track or not track.Animation then
                return
            end

            local numericId=getAnimNumericId(track.Animation.AnimationId)

            if not numericId then
                return
            end

            local detectedName=getKillerName(player,char)

            if detectedName then
                kName=detectedName

                local known=STATE.killerCharacters[char]

                if known then
                    known.name=detectedName
                else
                    STATE.killerCharacters[char]={
                        player=player,
                        name=detectedName
                    }
                end
            else
                local known=STATE.killerCharacters[char]

                if known and known.name then
                    kName=known.name
                end
            end

            local currentName=kName or "Unknown Killer"

            if numericId==MASKED_LUNGEHOLD_ID
            or numericId==MASKED_WALK_ID
            or numericId==MASKED_ATTACK_ID
            or numericId==MASKED_ATTACKDONE_ID then

                local profile=MASKED_EXTRA_ANIMS[numericId] or {
                    type="maskedfast",
                    length=1,
                    speed=1
                }

                registerMaskedFastAttack(
                    char,
                    currentName,
                    track,
                    profile,
                    numericId
                )

                if not STATE.killerCharacters[char] then
                    STATE.killerCharacters[char]={
                        player=player,
                        name=currentName
                    }
                end

                return
            end

            if numericId==ABYSSWALKER_ANIM_ID then
                registerAbysswalkerAttack(char,currentName,track)

                if not STATE.killerCharacters[char] then
                    STATE.killerCharacters[char]={
                        player=player,
                        name=currentName
                    }
                end

                return
            end

            local profile=ATTACK_PROFILES[numericId]

            if not profile then
                return
            end

            local known=STATE.killerCharacters[char]

            if not known then
                STATE.killerCharacters[char]={
                    player=player,
                    name=currentName
                }
            elseif currentName~="Unknown Killer" then
                known.name=currentName
            end

            local data={
                name=currentName,
                track=track,
                type=profile.type,
                id=numericId,
                profile=profile,
                started=tick(),
                consumed=false,
                lastTimePosition=-1
            }

            STATE.activeAttackers[char]=data

            local conn

            conn=track.Stopped:Connect(function()
                if conn then
                    conn:Disconnect()
                    conn=nil
                end

                if STATE.activeAttackers[char]
                and STATE.activeAttackers[char].track==track then
                    STATE.activeAttackers[char]=nil
                end
            end)
        end)
    end

    local function OnParryPlayerAdded(player)
        if player==localPlayer then
            return
        end

        if player.Character then
            task.spawn(function()
                OnParryCharacterAdded(player,player.Character)
            end)
        end

        player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
            task.spawn(function()
                OnParryCharacterAdded(player,char)
            end)
        end)
    end

    for _,player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        OnParryPlayerAdded(player)
    end

    Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(OnParryPlayerAdded)

    RegisterTask("CloseRangeMonitor",0,function()
        if not STATE.autoParryEnabled then
            return
        end

        local localChar=localPlayer.Character
        local localRoot=localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if not localRoot then
            return
        end

        local monitorNow=tick()
        local turnParryWindowOpen=updateTurnParryWindow(localRoot,monitorNow)

        for killerChar,data in pairs(STATE.activeAttackers) do
            if killerChar
            and killerChar.Parent
            and data
            and not data.consumed
            and data.track
            and data.track.IsPlaying
            and data.profile then

                local position=data.track.TimePosition or 0
                local triggerTime=(data.profile.triggerTime or 0)/(data.profile.speed or 1)

                if data.profile.type=="attack" then
                    local reactionWindow=data.profile.reactionWindow
                        or tuning.reactionWindow
                        or ATTACK_TRIGGER_GRACE

                    if position>triggerTime+reactionWindow then
                        STATE.activeAttackers[killerChar]=nil
                        continue
                    end
                end

                local killerRoot=killerChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                if killerRoot then
                    if data.profile.faceRequired and not isFacingLocalPlayer(killerChar) then
                        continue
                    end

                    local offset=localRoot.Position-killerRoot.Position
                    local flatOffset=Vector3.new(offset.X,0,offset.Z)
                    local distance=flatOffset.Magnitude

                    local killerVelocity=killerRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                    local localVelocity=localRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity

                    local killerFlatVelocity=Vector3.new(killerVelocity.X,0,killerVelocity.Z)
                    local localFlatVelocity=Vector3.new(localVelocity.X,0,localVelocity.Z)

                    local relativeVelocity=killerFlatVelocity-localFlatVelocity
                    local closingSpeed=0

                    if distance>0.05 then
                        closingSpeed=relativeVelocity:Dot(flatOffset.Unit)
                    end

                    local timeToImpact=closingSpeed>0 and distance/closingSpeed or math.huge

                    local predictedDistance=getPredictedDistance(killerChar,0.40)

                    local length=data.track.Length

                    if not length or length<=0 then
                        length=data.profile.length
                    end

                    local speed=data.profile.speed or 1
                    local progress=length>0 and (position*speed)/length or 0
                    local triggerTime=(data.profile.triggerTime or 0)/speed
                    local triggerProgress=data.profile.triggerProgress or 0

                    local animationReady=position>=math.max(0,triggerTime-EARLY_ATTACK_LEAD)
                        or progress>=math.max(0,triggerProgress-EARLY_ATTACK_LEAD)

                    local attackWindowOpen=true

                    if data.profile.type=="attack" then
                        local reactionWindow=data.profile.reactionWindow
                            or tuning.reactionWindow
                            or ATTACK_TRIGGER_GRACE
                        attackWindowOpen=position<=triggerTime+reactionWindow+EARLY_REACTION_BONUS
                    end

                    local lungeReady=closingSpeed>=5 and timeToImpact<=0.30
                    local critical=closingSpeed>=3.5 and timeToImpact<=0.15

                    if (animationReady and attackWindowOpen)
                    or lungeReady
                    or critical then

                        local turnParryTrigger=turnParryWindowOpen
                            and data.profile.type=="attack"
