                            and animationReady
                            and attackWindowOpen
                            and distance<=STATE.autoParryRadius+TURN_PARRY_EXTRA_RANGE

                        if turnParryTrigger or canNormalAttackParry(
                            killerChar,
                            distance,
                            closingSpeed,
                            predictedDistance
                        ) then

                            local trigger=turnParryTrigger

                            if distance<=STATE.autoParryRadius then
                                trigger=true
                            elseif closingSpeed>=2.5
                            and math.max(distance-3.5,0)/closingSpeed<=0.24 then
                                trigger=true
                            elseif closingSpeed>=5
                            and math.max(distance-3.5,0)/closingSpeed<=0.32 then
                                trigger=true
                            elseif predictedDistance<=STATE.autoParryRadius
                            and closingSpeed>=2 then
                                trigger=true
                            elseif animationReady
                            and attackWindowOpen
                            and predictedDistance<=STATE.autoParryRadius+1.75 then
                                trigger=true
                            end

                            if trigger then
                                local allowPredictedOrTurnRange=(
                                    predictedDistance<=STATE.autoParryRadius+tuning.predictionExtra
                                    or turnParryTrigger
                                )
                                if doParry(
                                    true,
                                    killerChar,
                                    distance,
                                    allowPredictedOrTurnRange,
                                    false
                                ) then
                                    data.consumed=true
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    local function normalAttackHeartbeatMonitor()
        if not STATE.autoParryEnabled then
            return
        end

        local localChar=localPlayer.Character
        local localRoot=localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if not localRoot then
            return
        end

        local localPos=localRoot.Position

        for killerChar,data in pairs(STATE.activeSkillAttackers) do
            if not killerChar or not killerChar.Parent or not data or not data.track then
                STATE.activeSkillAttackers[killerChar]=nil
            elseif not data.track.IsPlaying then
                STATE.activeSkillAttackers[killerChar]=nil
            elseif not data.consumed and data.skillId==ABYSSWALKER_ANIM_ID then
                if not STATE.ignoredTheAbysswalker then
                    local killerRoot=killerChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                    if killerRoot then
                        local killerPos=killerRoot.Position

                        local flatOffset=Vector3.new(
                            localPos.X-killerPos.X,
                            0,
                            localPos.Z-killerPos.Z
                        )

                        if flatOffset.Magnitude<=ABYSSWALKER_RANGE then
                            if doSkillParry() then
                                data.consumed=true
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if STATE.autoParryEnabled then
            normalAttackHeartbeatMonitor()
        end
    end)

    local function normalAttackersMonitor()
        if not STATE.autoParryEnabled then
            return
        end

        local localChar=localPlayer.Character
        local localRoot=localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if not localRoot then
            return
        end

        for killerChar,data in pairs(STATE.activeAttackers) do
            if killerChar
            and killerChar.Parent
            and killerChar~=localChar
            and data
            and not data.consumed
            and data.track
            and data.track.IsPlaying
            and data.profile then

                local position=data.track.TimePosition or 0
                local triggerTime=(data.profile.triggerTime or 0)/(data.profile.speed or 1)

                if data.profile.type=="attack" then
                    local reactionWindow=data.profile.reactionWindow
                        or tuning.reactionWindow
                        or ATTACK_TRIGGER_GRACE

                    if position>triggerTime+reactionWindow then
                        STATE.activeAttackers[killerChar]=nil
                        continue
                    end
                end

                local killerRoot=killerChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                if killerRoot then
                    local killerVelocity=killerRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                    local killerFlatVelocity=Vector3.new(killerVelocity.X,0,killerVelocity.Z)

                    local localVelocity=localRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                    local localFlatVelocity=Vector3.new(localVelocity.X,0,localVelocity.Z)

                    local offset=localRoot.Position-killerRoot.Position
                    local flatOffset=Vector3.new(offset.X,0,offset.Z)
                    local distance=flatOffset.Magnitude

                    local relativeVelocity=killerFlatVelocity-localFlatVelocity

                    local closingSpeed=0

                    if flatOffset.Magnitude>0 then
                        closingSpeed=relativeVelocity:Dot(flatOffset.Unit)
                    end

                    local ping=math.clamp(localPlayer:GetNetworkPing(),0,PUBLIC_PING_MAX)
                    local predictionTime=math.clamp(
                        ping+tuning.predictionLead,
                        tuning.predictionLead,
                        PUBLIC_PING_MAX+tuning.predictionLead
                    )

                    local predictedKiller=killerRoot.Position+
                        killerFlatVelocity*predictionTime

                    local predictedLocal=localRoot.Position+
                        localFlatVelocity*predictionTime

                    local predictedDistance=(
                        Vector3.new(predictedLocal.X,0,predictedLocal.Z)-
                        Vector3.new(predictedKiller.X,0,predictedKiller.Z)
                    ).Magnitude

                    local position=data.track.TimePosition or 0
                    local realLength=data.track.Length
                    local length=(realLength and realLength>0)
                        and realLength
                        or data.profile.length

                    local speed=data.profile.speed or 1
                    local progress=length>0 and (position*speed)/length or 0
                    local triggerTime=(data.profile.triggerTime or 0)/speed
                    local triggerProgress=data.profile.triggerProgress or 0

                    local animationReady=position>=math.max(0,triggerTime-EARLY_ATTACK_LEAD)
                        or progress>=math.max(0,triggerProgress-EARLY_ATTACK_LEAD)

                    local attackWindowOpen=true

                    if data.profile.type=="attack" then
                        local reactionWindow=data.profile.reactionWindow
                            or tuning.reactionWindow
                            or ATTACK_TRIGGER_GRACE
                        attackWindowOpen=position<=triggerTime+reactionWindow+EARLY_REACTION_BONUS
                    end

                    local emergencyContactWindow =
                        data.profile.type~="idle"
                        and data.profile.type~="unknown"
                        and position>=0
                        and position<=math.min(length,0.82)
                        and distance<=math.min(STATE.autoParryRadius,7.5)
                        and closingSpeed>=4.0
                        and predictedDistance<=STATE.autoParryRadius+tuning.predictionExtra

                    if (animationReady and attackWindowOpen)
                    or emergencyContactWindow then
                        if canNormalAttackParry(
                            killerChar,
                            distance,
                            closingSpeed,
                            predictedDistance
                        ) then

                            local trigger=false

                            if distance<=STATE.autoParryRadius then
                                trigger=true
                            elseif closingSpeed>=2
                            and predictedDistance<=STATE.autoParryRadius then
                                trigger=true
                            elseif closingSpeed>=3
                            and predictedDistance<=STATE.autoParryRadius+1.5 then
                                trigger=true
                            elseif distance<=STATE.autoParryRadius
                            and predictedDistance<=STATE.autoParryRadius+1.75 then
                                trigger=true
                            end

                            if trigger then
                                if doParry(
                                    true,
                                    killerChar,
                                    distance,
                                    predictedDistance<=STATE.autoParryRadius+tuning.predictionExtra,
                                    false
                                ) then
                                    data.consumed=true
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end


    local CORNER_PUBLIC_MAX_PING=0.45
    local CORNER_BASE_LEAD=0.055
    local CORNER_MIN_LEAD=0.055
    local CORNER_HORIZON=0.42
    local CORNER_CONTACT_MARGIN=0.90
    local CORNER_PREDICT_MARGIN=1.35
    local CORNER_ATTACK_ALIGNMENT=0.30
    local CORNER_SIDE_MARGIN=1.20

    local function getCornerAssist(killerChar)
        local localChar=localPlayer.Character
        local localRoot=localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local killerRoot=killerChar and killerChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if not localRoot or not killerRoot then
            return false, math.huge, 0
        end

        local localPos=localRoot.Position
        local killerPos=killerRoot.Position

        local offset=localPos-killerPos
        local flatOffset=Vector3.new(offset.X,0,offset.Z)
        local distance=flatOffset.Magnitude

        if distance<=0.05 then
            return true,distance,0
        end

        local currentLocal=flatVelocity(localRoot)
        local currentKiller=flatVelocity(killerRoot)

        local previousLocal=cornerMovementHistory.localSample
        local previousKiller=cornerMovementHistory.killers[killerChar]

        local localMove=currentLocal
        local killerMove=currentKiller

        if previousLocal
        and tick()-previousLocal.time<=MOVEMENT_HISTORY_WINDOW then
            localMove=previousLocal.velocity*0.35+currentLocal*0.65
        end

        if previousKiller
        and tick()-previousKiller.time<=MOVEMENT_HISTORY_WINDOW then
            killerMove=previousKiller.velocity*0.35+currentKiller*0.65
        end

        local ping=math.clamp(localPlayer:GetNetworkPing(),0,CORNER_PUBLIC_MAX_PING)
        local lead=math.clamp(
            ping+CORNER_BASE_LEAD,
            CORNER_MIN_LEAD,
            CORNER_PUBLIC_MAX_PING+CORNER_BASE_LEAD
        )

        local horizon=math.clamp(
            CORNER_HORIZON+ping*0.20,
            0.28,
            0.52
        )

        local relativeVelocity=localMove-killerMove
        local relativeSpeedSq=relativeVelocity:Dot(relativeVelocity)

        local closestTime=0
        if relativeSpeedSq>0.25 then
            closestTime=math.clamp(
                -flatOffset:Dot(relativeVelocity)/relativeSpeedSq,
                0,
                horizon
            )
        end

        local closestRelative=flatOffset+relativeVelocity*closestTime
        local closestDistance=closestRelative.Magnitude

        local leadLocal=localPos+localMove*lead
        local leadKiller=killerPos+killerMove*lead
        local leadOffset=Vector3.new(
            leadLocal.X-leadKiller.X,
            0,
            leadLocal.Z-leadKiller.Z
        )
        local predictedDistance=leadOffset.Magnitude

        local direction=flatOffset.Unit

        local look=killerRoot.CFrame.LookVector
        local attackDir=Vector3.new(look.X,0,look.Z)

        if attackDir.Magnitude<=0.05 then
            attackDir=direction
        else
            attackDir=attackDir.Unit
        end

        if killerMove.Magnitude>=5 then
            local moveDir=killerMove.Unit
            local blended=attackDir*0.62+moveDir*0.38
            if blended.Magnitude>0.05 then
                attackDir=blended.Unit
            end
        end

        local alignment=attackDir:Dot(direction)

        local params=RaycastParams.new()
        params.FilterType=Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        params.FilterDescendantsInstances={localChar,killerChar}
        params.IgnoreWater=true

        local clearFuture=0
        local bestPathDistance=math.huge
        local bestAttackAlignment=alignment

        local sampleTimes={
            lead,
            math.clamp(lead+0.035,0,horizon),
            math.clamp(lead+0.075,0,horizon),
            math.clamp(lead+0.12,0,horizon),
            math.clamp(lead+0.18,0,horizon)
        }

        for _,t in ipairs(sampleTimes) do
            local pK=killerPos+killerMove*t
            local pL=localPos+localMove*t

            local pKFlat=Vector3.new(pK.X,0,pK.Z)
            local pLFlat=Vector3.new(pL.X,0,pL.Z)
            local path=pLFlat-pKFlat
            local pathDistance=path.Magnitude

            if pathDistance<bestPathDistance then
                bestPathDistance=pathDistance
            end

            if pathDistance>0.05 then
                local futureDirection=path.Unit
                local futureAlignment=attackDir:Dot(futureDirection)
                if futureAlignment>bestAttackAlignment then
                    bestAttackAlignment=futureAlignment
                end

                local hit=Workspace:Raycast(
                    pK,
                    pL-pK,
                    params
                )

                if not hit then
                    clearFuture=clearFuture+1
                end
            end
        end

        local directHit=Workspace:Raycast(
            killerPos,
            localPos-killerPos,
            params
        )

        local directClear=directHit==nil

        local lateralVector=direction-attackDir*direction:Dot(attackDir)
        local lateralDistance=lateralVector.Magnitude

        local contactPossible=(
            closestDistance<=STATE.autoParryRadius+CORNER_CONTACT_MARGIN
            or predictedDistance<=STATE.autoParryRadius+CORNER_PREDICT_MARGIN
            or bestPathDistance<=STATE.autoParryRadius+CORNER_CONTACT_MARGIN
        )

        local attackCorridor=(
            bestAttackAlignment>=CORNER_ATTACK_ALIGNMENT
            and lateralDistance<=STATE.autoParryRadius+CORNER_SIDE_MARGIN
        )

        local pingCompensatedClosing=(
            relativeVelocity:Dot(direction)
        )

        local imminent=(
            pingCompensatedClosing>=1.0
            or closestDistance<=STATE.autoParryRadius+0.55
            or predictedDistance<=STATE.autoParryRadius
        )

        local pathCondition=directClear
            or clearFuture>=2

        local assist=(
            contactPossible
            and attackCorridor
            and imminent
            and pathCondition
        )

        return assist,predictedDistance,pingCompensatedClosing
    end

    local function edgeCornerParryPulse()
        if not STATE.autoParryEnabled then
            return
        end

        local localChar=localPlayer.Character
        local localRoot=localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not localRoot then
            return
        end

        for killerChar,data in pairs(STATE.activeAttackers) do
            if killerChar
            and killerChar.Parent
            and killerChar~=localChar
            and data
            and not data.consumed
            and data.track
            and data.track.IsPlaying
            and data.profile then

                local profile=data.profile
                local profileType=profile.type

                local position=data.track.TimePosition or 0
                local realLength=data.track.Length
                local length=(realLength and realLength>0)
                    and realLength
                    or (profile.length or 1)
                local speed=profile.speed or 1
                local triggerTime=(profile.triggerTime or 0)/speed
                local tuning=getProfileTuning(profile)
                local reactionWindow=profile.reactionWindow
                    or tuning.reactionWindow
                    or ATTACK_TRIGGER_GRACE

                local attackTimingReady=(
                    position>=math.max(0,triggerTime-0.045)
                    and (
                        profileType~="attack"
                        or position<=triggerTime+reactionWindow
                    )
                )

                if attackTimingReady
                and (
                    profileType=="attack"
                    or profileType=="lungehold"
                    or profileType=="corrupt"
                    or profileType=="spearlunge"
                ) then

                    local killerRoot=killerChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if killerRoot then
                        local offset=localRoot.Position-killerRoot.Position
                        local flat=Vector3.new(offset.X,0,offset.Z)
                        local distance=flat.Magnitude

                        local cornerAssist,cornerPredictedDistance,cornerClosingSpeed=
                            getCornerAssist(killerChar)

                        if distance<=STATE.autoParryRadius+3.5 or cornerAssist then
                            local killerVelocity=killerRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                            local localVelocity=localRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                            local kFlat=Vector3.new(killerVelocity.X,0,killerVelocity.Z)
                            local lFlat=Vector3.new(localVelocity.X,0,localVelocity.Z)

                            local closingSpeed=0
                            if flat.Magnitude>0.05 then
                                closingSpeed=(kFlat-lFlat):Dot(flat.Unit)
                            end

                            local ping=math.clamp(
                                localPlayer:GetNetworkPing(),
                                0,
                                CORNER_PUBLIC_MAX_PING
                            )
                            local tuningLead=tuning.predictionLead or CORNER_BASE_LEAD
                            local predictionTime=math.clamp(
                                ping+tuningLead,
                                tuningLead,
                                CORNER_PUBLIC_MAX_PING+tuningLead
                            )
                            local predictedKiller=killerRoot.Position+kFlat*predictionTime
                            local predictedLocal=localRoot.Position+lFlat*predictionTime

                            local predictedDistance=(
                                Vector3.new(predictedLocal.X,0,predictedLocal.Z)-
                                Vector3.new(predictedKiller.X,0,predictedKiller.Z)
                            ).Magnitude

                            local obstacle=getObstacleState(
                                localRoot.Position,
                                killerRoot.Position,
                                killerChar
                            )

                            local partialEdge=(
                                obstacle.clearCount>=2
                                and obstacle.blockedCount>=1
                                and (
                                    obstacle.vault
                                    or obstacle.standingPallet
                                    or obstacle.blockedCount>=1
                                )
                            )

                            if partialEdge or cornerAssist then
                                local imminent=(
                                    distance<=STATE.autoParryRadius
                                    or predictedDistance<=STATE.autoParryRadius+1.5
                                    or closingSpeed>=2.0
                                    or cornerClosingSpeed>=1.5
                                    or cornerPredictedDistance<=STATE.autoParryRadius+1.5
                                )

                                if imminent then
                                    local allowRange=(
                                        predictedDistance<=STATE.autoParryRadius+1.5
                                        or cornerPredictedDistance<=STATE.autoParryRadius+1.5
                                    )

                                    if doParry(
                                        true,
                                        killerChar,
                                        distance,
                                        allowRange,
                                        true
                                    ) then
                                        data.consumed=true
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local function refreshPublicMovementHistory()
        if not STATE.autoParryEnabled then
            return
        end

        local localChar=localPlayer.Character
        local localRoot=localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if not localRoot then
            return
        end

        local now=tick()
        local localVel=flatVelocity(localRoot)

        cornerMovementHistory.localSample=movementHistory.localSample

        for killerChar,_ in pairs(STATE.activeAttackers) do
            if killerChar and killerChar.Parent then
                cornerMovementHistory.killers[killerChar]=
                    movementHistory.killers[killerChar]
            end
        end

        movementHistory.localSample={
            velocity=localVel,
            time=now
        }

        for killerChar,_ in pairs(STATE.activeAttackers) do
            if killerChar and killerChar.Parent then
                local killerRoot=killerChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if killerRoot then
                    movementHistory.killers[killerChar]={
                        velocity=flatVelocity(killerRoot),
                        time=now
                    }
                end
            end
        end
    end

    if RunService.PreSimulation then
        RunService.PreSimulation:Connect(function()
            if STATE.autoParryEnabled then
                refreshPublicMovementHistory()
                edgeCornerParryPulse()
            end
        end)
    end

    RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if STATE.autoParryEnabled then
            normalAttackersMonitor()
            maskedHeartbeatMonitor()
        end
    end)

    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if STATE.autoParryEnabled then
            normalAttackersMonitor()
        end
    end)

    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if STATE.autoParryEnabled then
        end
    end)

    RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        if STATE.autoParryEnabled then
            maskedHeartbeatMonitor()
        end
    end)

    if RunService.PreSimulation then
        RunService.PreSimulation:Connect(function()
            if STATE.autoParryEnabled then
                normalAttackHeartbeatMonitor()
                normalAttackersMonitor()
                maskedHeartbeatMonitor()
            end
        end)
    end

    local espRing={}
    local espLoop=nil
    local espCache={}
    local SEGMENTS=32
    local lastRadius=-1

    local function buildRing()
        for _,p in ipairs(espRing) do
            if p and p.Parent then
                p:Destroy()
            end
        end

        espRing={}
        espCache={}

        local step=(2*math.pi)/SEGMENTS

        for i=1,SEGMENTS do
            local angle=step*(i-1)
            local nextAngle=step*i

            espCache[i]={
                cx=math.cos(angle),
                cz=math.sin(angle),
                nx=math.cos(nextAngle),
                nz=math.sin(nextAngle)
            }

            local seg=Instance.new("Part")

            seg.Shape=Enum.PartType.Block
            seg.Anchored=true
            seg.CanCollide=false
            seg.CanQuery=false
            seg.CastShadow=false
            seg.Material=Enum.Material.Neon
            seg.Color=Color3.fromRGB(248,248,255)
            seg.Transparency=0.15
            seg.Size=Vector3.new(0.08,0.08,0.1)
            seg.Name="BolongESP_Seg"
            seg.Parent=Workspace

            espRing[i]=seg
        end
    end

    local function updateSegmentSizes(radius)
        local arcLen=(2*math.pi*radius)/SEGMENTS

        for _,seg in ipairs(espRing) do
            if seg and seg.Parent then
                seg.Size=Vector3.new(0.08,0.08,arcLen+0.02)
            end
        end
    end

    local function destroyRing()
        for _,p in ipairs(espRing) do
            if p and p.Parent then
                p:Destroy()
            end
        end

        espRing={}
        espCache={}
        lastRadius=-1
    end

    function toggleAutoParryESP(state)
        if state then
            buildRing()

            espLoop=RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                if not localPlayer.Character then
                    return
                end

                local root=localPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                if not root then
                    return
                end

                local radius=math.max(0,STATE.autoParryRadius)

                if radius~=lastRadius then
                    updateSegmentSizes(radius)
                    lastRadius=radius
                end

                local center=root.Position-Vector3.new(0,root.Size.Y/2+0.6,0)

                for i,seg in ipairs(espRing) do
                    if seg and seg.Parent then
                        local c=espCache[i]
                        local pos=center+Vector3.new(c.cx*radius,0,c.cz*radius)
                        local nxt=center+Vector3.new(c.nx*radius,0,c.nz*radius)

                        seg.CFrame=CFrame.lookAt(pos,nxt)*CFrame.new(0,0,-seg.Size.Z/2)

                        seg.Transparency=0.15
                        seg.Size=Vector3.new(0.08,0.08,seg.Size.Z)
                    end
                end
            end)
        else
            if espLoop then
                espLoop:Disconnect()
                espLoop=nil
            end

            destroyRing()
        end
    end
end
KillerAimingFeature=(function()
    local activeTracks={}
    local trackConnections={}
    local alwaysLines={}
    local alwaysYellowLines={}
    local heavenParts={}
    local killerWatchConnections={}
    local lineAlways=false
    local lastRegistryScan=0

    local MAX_LENGTH=100
    local LINE_THICKNESS=0.09
    local HEAVEN_DISTANCE=18.0
    local HEAVEN_HEIGHT=0.33
    local HEAVEN_WIDTH=7.85
    local WHITE=Color3.fromRGB(248,248,255)
    local YELLOW=Color3.fromRGB(199,21,133)
    local MEDIUM_VIOLET_RED=Color3.fromRGB(199,21,133)

    local function numericId(track)
        if not track then
            return nil
        end

        local raw=nil
        pcall(function()
            if track.Animation then
                raw=track.Animation.AnimationId
            end
        end)

        if raw==nil then
            pcall(function()
                raw=track.AnimationId
            end)
        end

        local text=tostring(raw or "")
        local id=text:match("%d+")
        return id and tonumber(id) or nil
    end

    local function scanKillerRegistry()
        for _,player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if player~=localPlayer
            and player.Character
            and player.Character.Parent
            and not sharedShouldIgnoreVisualTarget(player,player.Character) then
                local char=player.Character
                local detectedName=sharedGetKillerName(player,char)
                local teamKiller=sharedIsKillerTeam(player)
                local killerMob=sharedHasKillerMobMarker(char)
                local known=STATE.killerCharacters[char]

                if detectedName or teamKiller or killerMob then
                    STATE.killerCharacters[char]={
                        player=player,
                        name=detectedName or (known and known.name) or "Killer"
                    }
                elseif known and known.player==player and known.name
                and known.name~="Unknown Killer" then
                    STATE.killerCharacters[char]=known
                end
            end
        end

        for char,data in pairs(STATE.killerCharacters) do
            local player=data and data.player
            if not char or not char.Parent
            or not player or player.Character~=char
            or sharedShouldIgnoreVisualTarget(player,char) then
                STATE.killerCharacters[char]=nil
            end
        end
    end

    local function isKillerCharacter(char)
        if not char or not char.Parent or char==localPlayer.Character then
            return false
        end

        local player=Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(char)
        if sharedShouldIgnoreVisualTarget(player,char) then
            return false
        end

        if player then
            if not player.Team or not sharedIsKillerTeam(player) then
                return false
            end

            local data=STATE.killerCharacters[char]
            local detectedName=sharedGetKillerName(player,char)
            STATE.killerCharacters[char]={
                player=player,
                name=detectedName or (data and data.name) or "Killer"
            }
            return true
        end

        return sharedHasKillerMobMarker(char)
    end

    local function setBeamColor(beam,color)
        if not beam or not beam.Parent then
            return false
        end
        beam.Color=ColorSequence.new(color)
        return true
    end

    local function destroyLineData(data)
        if not data then
            return
        end
        if data.beam and data.beam.Parent then
            data.beam:Destroy()
        end
        if data.endpointAttachment and data.endpointAttachment.Parent then
            data.endpointAttachment:Destroy()
        end
        if data.endpointPart and data.endpointPart.Parent then
            data.endpointPart:Destroy()
        end
        if data.startAttachment and data.startAttachment.Parent then
            data.startAttachment:Destroy()
        end
    end

    local function clearLine(store,char)
        local data=store[char]
        if data then
            destroyLineData(data)
            store[char]=nil
        end
    end

    local function createLine(store,char,color,prefix)
        if not char or not char.Parent then
            return nil
        end
        local head=char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if not head then
            return nil
        end

        clearLine(store,char)

        local endpointPart=Instance.new("Part")
        endpointPart.Name=prefix.."Endpoint"
        endpointPart.Anchored=true
        endpointPart.CanCollide=false
        endpointPart.CanTouch=false
        endpointPart.CanQuery=false
        endpointPart.CastShadow=false
        endpointPart.Shape=Enum.PartType.Ball
        endpointPart.Material=Enum.Material.Neon
        endpointPart.Color=color or WHITE
        endpointPart.Transparency=0.04
        endpointPart.Size=Vector3.new(0.26,0.26,0.26)
        endpointPart.CFrame=CFrame.new(head.Position)
        endpointPart.Parent=Workspace

        local startAttachment=Instance.new("Attachment")
        startAttachment.Name=prefix.."Start"
        startAttachment.Position=Vector3.zero
        startAttachment.Parent=head

        local endpointAttachment=Instance.new("Attachment")
        endpointAttachment.Name=prefix.."End"
        endpointAttachment.Position=Vector3.zero
        endpointAttachment.Parent=endpointPart

        local beam=Instance.new("Beam")
        beam.Name=prefix
        beam.Attachment0=startAttachment
        beam.Attachment1=endpointAttachment
        beam.FaceCamera=true
        beam.LightEmission=1
        beam.LightInfluence=0
        beam.Segments=1
        beam.Width0=0.12
        beam.Width1=0.12
        beam.Transparency=NumberSequence.new(0.45)
        setBeamColor(beam,color or WHITE)
        beam.Parent=endpointPart

        local raycastParams=RaycastParams.new()
        raycastParams.FilterType=Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
        raycastParams.IgnoreWater=true
        local localChar=localPlayer.Character
        local ignored={char}
        if localChar and localChar~=char then
            table.insert(ignored,localChar)
        end
        raycastParams.FilterDescendantsInstances=ignored

        local data={
            beam=beam,
            endpointPart=endpointPart,
            endpointAttachment=endpointAttachment,
            startAttachment=startAttachment,
            raycastParams=raycastParams,
            raycastLocalCharacter=localChar,
            lastRaycastAt=0,
            lastRayLength=nil,
            color=color or WHITE
        }
        store[char]=data
        return data
    end

    local function updateLine(data,char,forcedLength,dt)
        if not data or not char or not char.Parent
        or not data.endpointPart or not data.endpointPart.Parent then
            return
        end
        local head=char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        local root=char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not head or not root then
            return
        end

        local direction=root.CFrame.LookVector
        if direction.Magnitude<=0.001 then
            return
        end
        direction=direction.Unit

        local origin=head.Position
        local length=math.clamp(tonumber(forcedLength) or tonumber(STATE.alwaysLineLength) or 50,10,100)
        local targetLength=tonumber(data.targetLength) or length

        local now=tick()
        local localChar=localPlayer.Character
        if data.raycastLocalCharacter~=localChar then
            data.raycastLocalCharacter=localChar
            local ignored={char}
            if localChar and localChar~=char then
                table.insert(ignored,localChar)
            end
            data.raycastParams.FilterDescendantsInstances=ignored
            data.lastRaycastAt=0
        end

        local rayInterval=0.055
        local shouldRaycast=(data.lastRaycastAt==0)
            or (now-data.lastRaycastAt>=rayInterval)
            or (not data.lastRayLength)
            or (math.abs(data.lastRayLength-length)>=0.05)

        local result=nil

        if shouldRaycast then
            result=Workspace:Raycast(origin,direction*length,data.raycastParams)
            if result then
                local endMarkerRadius=data.endpointPart.Size.X*0.5
                targetLength=math.clamp(result.Distance-endMarkerRadius,0.12,length)
            else
                targetLength=length
            end

            data.lastRaycastAt=now
            data.lastRayLength=length
            data.targetLength=targetLength
        end

        local currentLength=tonumber(data.currentLength) or length
        if typeof(dt)=="number" and dt>0 then
            local alpha=1-math.exp(-18*math.clamp(dt,0,0.1))
            currentLength=currentLength+(targetLength-currentLength)*alpha
            if math.abs(targetLength-currentLength)<0.015 then
                currentLength=targetLength
            end
        else
            currentLength=targetLength
        end

        data.currentLength=currentLength
        data.targetLength=targetLength
        data.obstructed=result~=nil
        data.endpointPart.CFrame=CFrame.new(origin+direction*currentLength)
        data.length=currentLength
        data.startAttachment.Position=Vector3.zero
        data.endpointAttachment.Position=Vector3.zero
    end

    local function clearAlwaysLine(char)
        clearLine(alwaysLines,char)
        clearLine(alwaysYellowLines,char)
    end

    local function createAlwaysLine(char)
        local data=createLine(alwaysLines,char,WHITE,"LineOfSightAlways")
        if data then
            data.color=WHITE
        end
        return data
    end

    local function createAlwaysYellowLine(char)
        local data=alwaysYellowLines[char]
        if data then return data end
        data=createLine(alwaysYellowLines,char,YELLOW,"LineOfSightAlwaysYellow")
        if data and data.beam then
            data.beam.Width0=0.12
            data.beam.Width1=0.12
            data.beam.Enabled=true
        end
        return data
    end


    local SPECIAL_VISUAL_ANIM_IDS={
        [92098503722633]=true,[84093948968516]=true,[138045669415653]=true,
        [137688077908355]=true,[117886494230451]=true
    }
    local function getLiveAnimationColor(char)
        local humanoid=char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local animator=humanoid and humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
        if not animator then
            return WHITE
        end
        for _,track in ipairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
            if track and track.IsPlaying then
                local id=numericId(track)
                if id and SPECIAL_VISUAL_ANIM_IDS[id] then
                    return MEDIUM_VIOLET_RED
                end
            end
        end
        return WHITE
    end
    local function bindAll() end

    local function hasActiveYellowAnimation(char)
        local liveColor=getLiveAnimationColor(char)
        if liveColor==MEDIUM_VIOLET_RED then
            return true
        end

        local tracks=activeTracks[char]
        if not tracks then
            return false
        end

        local yellowActive=false
        for track,data in pairs(tracks) do
            if not track or not track.IsPlaying then
                tracks[track]=nil
                local conn=trackConnections[track]
                if conn then
                    conn:Disconnect()
                    trackConnections[track]=nil
                end
            elseif data and data.special then
                yellowActive=true
            end
        end

        if next(tracks)==nil then
            activeTracks[char]=nil
        end

        return yellowActive
    end

    local SHARED_WHITE_ANIMS = {
        [75258958842388]=true,
        [96744338559260]=true,
        [137846825408335]=true,
        [139928639611415]=true
    }

    local SHARED_SPECIAL_ANIMS = {
        [92098503722633]=true,
        [84093948968516]=true,
        [138045669415653]=true,
        [137688077908355]=true,
        [117886494230451]=true,
        }

    local function getSharedLineVisualColor(char)
        local humanoid=char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local animator=humanoid and humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
        if not animator then
            return WHITE
        end

        local hasWhite=false

        for _,track in ipairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
            if track and track.IsPlaying then
                local id=numericId(track)

                if id and SHARED_SPECIAL_ANIMS[id] then
                    return MEDIUM_VIOLET_RED
                end

                if id and SHARED_WHITE_ANIMS[id] then
                    hasWhite=true
                end
            end
        end

        if hasWhite then
            return BASE_VISUAL_COLOR
        end

        return WHITE
    end

    local function getAlwaysLineSpecialColor(char)
        local color=getSharedLineVisualColor(char)
        if color==MEDIUM_VIOLET_RED then
            return color
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function updateAlwaysLine(char,dt)
        if not char or not char.Parent then
            return
        end

        local data=alwaysLines[char]
        if not data
        or not data.beam
        or not data.beam.Parent
        or not data.endpointPart
        or not data.endpointPart.Parent
        or not data.startAttachment
        or not data.startAttachment.Parent
        or not data.endpointAttachment
        or not data.endpointAttachment.Parent then
            data=createAlwaysLine(char)
        end

        if not data then
            return
        end

        updateLine(data,char,nil,dt)

        local color=getSharedLineVisualColor(char)
        data.color=color

        if data.beam and data.beam.Parent then
            setBeamColor(data.beam,color)
            data.beam.Width0=0.12
            data.beam.Width1=0.12
            data.beam.Transparency=NumberSequence.new(0.45)
            data.beam.Enabled=true
        end
        if data.endpointPart and data.endpointPart.Parent then
            data.endpointPart.Color=color
            data.endpointPart.Material=Enum.Material.Neon
            data.endpointPart.Transparency=0.04
        end

        clearLine(alwaysYellowLines,char)
    end

    local function clearHeavenParts(char)
        local list=heavenParts[char]
        if list then
            for _,part in ipairs(list) do
                if part and part.Parent then
                    part:Destroy()
                end
            end
        end
        heavenParts[char]=nil
    end

    local function makeHeavenPart(name)
        local part=Instance.new("Part")
        part.Name=name
        part.Anchored=true
        part.CanCollide=false
        part.CanTouch=false
        part.CanQuery=false
        part.CastShadow=false
        part.Material=Enum.Material.Neon
        part.Color=WHITE
        part.Transparency=0.05
        part.Size=Vector3.new(.0825,.0825,.1)
        part.Parent=Workspace
        return part
    end

    local function pointPart(part,a,b)
        local delta=b-a
        local length=delta.Magnitude
        if length<=.001 then
            part.Transparency=1
            return
        end
        part.Transparency=.05
        part.Size=Vector3.new(.0825,.0825,length)
        part.CFrame=CFrame.lookAt((a+b)*0.5,b)
    end

    local function heavenGroundPoint(char,point)
        local root=char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not root then
            return point
        end
        return Vector3.new(point.X,root.Position.Y-3.0,point.Z)
    end

    local function ensureHeavenParts(char)
        local list=heavenParts[char]
        if list and #list==9 then
            return list
        end

        if list then
            for _,part in ipairs(list) do
                if part then
                    part:Destroy()
                end
            end
        end

        list={}
        for i=1,9 do
            list[i]=makeHeavenPart("HeavenEyesLine")
        end
        heavenParts[char]=list
        return list
    end

    local function updateHeavenEyes(char)
        if not STATE.heavenEyesEnabled or not char or not char.Parent then
            clearHeavenParts(char)
            return
        end

        local root=char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not root then
            return
        end

        local flatForward=Vector3.new(root.CFrame.LookVector.X,0,root.CFrame.LookVector.Z)
        if flatForward.Magnitude<=0.01 then
            return
        end
        flatForward=flatForward.Unit

        local right=Vector3.new(-flatForward.Z,0,flatForward.X)
        local groundY=root.Position.Y-3.0

        local halfWidth=7.85
        local depth=11.5
        local arcBulge=3.0

        local apex=Vector3.new(
            root.Position.X + flatForward.X*0.8,
            groundY,
            root.Position.Z + flatForward.Z*0.8
        )

        local leftEnd=Vector3.new(
            root.Position.X + flatForward.X*depth - right.X*halfWidth,
            groundY,
            root.Position.Z + flatForward.Z*depth - right.Z*halfWidth
        )

        local rightEnd=Vector3.new(
            root.Position.X + flatForward.X*depth + right.X*halfWidth,
            groundY,
            root.Position.Z + flatForward.Z*depth + right.Z*halfWidth
        )

        local parts=ensureHeavenParts(char)

        pointPart(parts[1],apex,leftEnd)
        pointPart(parts[2],apex,rightEnd)

        local arcPoints={}
        for i=0,7 do
            local t=i/7
            local lateral=(-halfWidth)+(2*halfWidth*t)
            local bulge=math.sin(math.pi*t)*arcBulge
            local p=root.Position
                + flatForward*(depth+bulge)
                + right*lateral
            arcPoints[i+1]=Vector3.new(p.X,groundY,p.Z)
        end

        for i=1,7 do
            pointPart(parts[i+2],arcPoints[i],arcPoints[i+1])
        end

        for _,part in ipairs(parts) do
            part.Color=WHITE
            part.Material=Enum.Material.Neon
            part.Transparency=0.02
        end
    end

    local function setEnabled(v)
        scanKillerRegistry()
        if not v then
            return
        end
        bindAll()
        if lineAlways then
            for _,player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if player~=localPlayer and player.Character and isKillerCharacter(player.Character) then
                    local char=player.Character
                    if not alwaysLines[char] then
                        createAlwaysLine(char)
                    end
                    updateAlwaysLine(char)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local function setLineAlways(v)
        lineAlways=v
        scanKillerRegistry()

        if lineAlways then
            bindAll()
        end

        for _,player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if player~=localPlayer and player.Character then
                local char=player.Character
                if isKillerCharacter(char) then
                    if lineAlways then
                        if not alwaysLines[char] then
                            createAlwaysLine(char)
                        end
                        updateAlwaysLine(char)
                    else
                        clearAlwaysLine(char)
                    end
                else
                    clearAlwaysLine(char)
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local function setAlwaysLineLength(v)
        local length=math.clamp(tonumber(v) or 50,10,100)
        STATE.alwaysLineLength=length
        for char,data in pairs(alwaysLines) do
            if data then data.length=length end
            if char and char.Parent then
                updateAlwaysLine(char)
            end
        end
        for char,data in pairs(alwaysYellowLines) do
            if data then data.length=length end
            if char and char.Parent then
                updateLine(data,char)
            end
        end
    end

    local function setHeavenEyes(v)
        STATE.heavenEyesEnabled=v
        scanKillerRegistry()
        if not v then
            for char in pairs(heavenParts) do
                clearHeavenParts(char)
            end
            return
        end

        bindAll()

        for _,player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if player~=localPlayer and player.Character and isKillerCharacter(player.Character) then
                updateHeavenEyes(player.Character)
            end
        end
    end

    RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
        local now=tick()
        if now-lastRegistryScan>=0.30 then
            scanKillerRegistry()
            lastRegistryScan=now
        end

        pcall(function()
            if lineAlways then
                for _,player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if player~=localPlayer and player.Character then
                        local char=player.Character
                        if isKillerCharacter(char) then
                            if not alwaysLines[char]
                            or not alwaysLines[char].beam
                            or not alwaysLines[char].beam.Parent then
                                createAlwaysLine(char)
                            end
                            updateAlwaysLine(char,dt)

                        else
                            clearAlwaysLine(char)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            for char in pairs(alwaysLines) do
                if not char or not char.Parent or not STATE.killerCharacters[char] or not lineAlways then
                    clearAlwaysLine(char)
                end
            end
        end)

        pcall(function()
            if STATE.heavenEyesEnabled then
                for char in pairs(heavenParts) do
                    local confirmed=(char and char.Parent and isKillerCharacter(char))
                    if not confirmed then
                        clearHeavenParts(char)
                    end
                end

                for _,player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                    if player~=localPlayer and player.Character then
                        local char=player.Character
                        if isKillerCharacter(char) then
                            updateHeavenEyes(char)
                        else
                            clearHeavenParts(char)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end)

    return {
        setEnabled=setEnabled,
        setHeavenEyes=setHeavenEyes,
        setLineAlways=setLineAlways,
        setAlwaysLineLength=setAlwaysLineLength,
    }
end)()
end)()
end
-- ============================================================
--  GHOST GATE SYSTEM
-- ============================================================
local function RefreshGhostGate()
    for _, gate in ipairs(STATE.cachedMapObjects.Gates) do
        ApplyGhostGateToObj(gate, STATE.ghostGateEnabled)
    end
end

-- ============================================================
--  TELEPORT SYSTEM
-- ============================================================
local function TeleportToFinishLine()
    local char = localPlayer.Character
    local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not root then Notify("Teleport", "Karakter tidak ditemukan!", 2); return end
    local finishPart = workspace:FindFirstChild("Fininshline", true)
    if not finishPart then Notify("Teleport", "Fininshline tidak ditemukan di map!", 2); return end
    local finishPos = finishPart.Position
    local rayParams = RaycastParams.new()
    rayParams.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
    rayParams.FilterDescendantsInstances = {char, finishPart.Parent}
    local safePos = nil
    local testPos = finishPos + Vector3.new(0, 10, 0)
    local hit = workspace:Raycast(testPos, Vector3.new(0, -30, 0), rayParams)
    if hit then
        local headCheck = workspace:Raycast(hit.Position + Vector3.new(0, 5, 0), Vector3.new(0, 5, 0), rayParams)
        if not headCheck then safePos = hit.Position + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0) end
    end
    if safePos then
        pcall(function() root.CFrame = CFrame.new(safePos) end)
    else
        pcall(function() root.CFrame = finishPart.CFrame end)
    end
end

-- ============================================================
--  AUTO CROUCH SYSTEM
-- ============================================================
local setCrouch
do
    local AUTOCROUCH_TARGET_ANIM_ID = "80411309607666"
    local function getAnimIdCrouch(animId)
        if not animId then return "" end
        return tostring(animId):match("%d+") or ""
    end
    local function getCrouchButton()
        local pGui = localPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
        if not pGui then return nil end
        local sMob = pGui:FindFirstChild("Survivor-mob")
        if not sMob then return nil end
        local ctrl = sMob:FindFirstChild("Controls")
        if not ctrl then return nil end
        local btn = ctrl:FindFirstChild("crouch")
        if btn and btn:IsA("GuiButton") then return btn end
        return nil
    end
    function setCrouch(state)
        if STATE.autoCrouchIsCrouching ~= state then
            STATE.autoCrouchIsCrouching = state
            local char = localPlayer.Character
            if char then
                char:SetAttribute("Crouchingserver", state)
                char:SetAttribute("Crouching", state)
            end
            pcall(function()
                ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.Mechanics.ChangeAttribute:FireServer("Crouchingserver", state)
                ReplicatedStorage.Remotes.Mechanics.ChangeAttribute:FireServer("Crouching", state)
            end)
            local isPC = not UserInputService.TouchEnabled and UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled
            if isPC then
                VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(state, Enum.KeyCode.C, false, game)
                VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(state, Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl, false, game)
            else
                local btn = getCrouchButton()
                if btn and type(firesignal) == "function" then
                    if state then
                        firesignal(btn.MouseButton1Down)
                        if btn.MouseButton1Click then firesignal(btn.MouseButton1Click) end
                    else
                        firesignal(btn.MouseButton1Up)
                        if btn.MouseButton1Click then firesignal(btn.MouseButton1Click) end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end
    local function hookKillerAnimationsCrouch(char)
        if not char then return end
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hum then return end
        if STATE.autoCrouchAnimConns[hum] then return end
        STATE.autoCrouchAnimConns[hum] = hum.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
            if not CONFIG.autoCrouchEnabled then return end
            local anim = track.Animation
            if anim then
                local trackId = getAnimIdCrouch(anim.AnimationId)
                if trackId == AUTOCROUCH_TARGET_ANIM_ID then
                    STATE.autoCrouchActiveSlashers[char] = true
                    track.Stopped:Connect(function()
                        task.wait(0.2)
                        STATE.autoCrouchActiveSlashers[char] = nil
                    end)
                end
            end
        end)
         hum.Died:Connect(function() 
          STATE.autoCrouchActiveSlashers[char] = nil 
    
           if STATE.autoCrouchAnimConns[hum] then
             STATE.autoCrouchAnimConns[hum]:Disconnect()
             STATE.autoCrouchAnimConns[hum] = nil
           end
        end)
    end
    RegisterTask("AutoCrouch", 0, function()
        if not CONFIG.autoCrouchEnabled then return end
        local localChar = localPlayer.Character
        if not localChar then return end
        local localRoot = localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not localRoot then return end
        local shouldCrouch = false
        for killerChar, _ in pairs(STATE.autoCrouchActiveSlashers) do
            if killerChar and killerChar.Parent then
                local kRoot = killerChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local kHum = killerChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if kRoot and kHum and kHum.Health > 0 then
                    local dist = (kRoot.Position - localRoot.Position).Magnitude
                    if dist <= CONFIG.autoCrouchRadius then
                        shouldCrouch = true
                        break
                    end
                end
            else
                STATE.autoCrouchActiveSlashers[killerChar] = nil
            end
        end
        setCrouch(shouldCrouch)
    end)
    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= localPlayer and player.Character then hookKillerAnimationsCrouch(player.Character) end
        if player ~= localPlayer then
            player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
                task.wait(1)
                hookKillerAnimationsCrouch(newChar)
            end)
        end
    end
    Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
        player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(newChar)
            task.wait(1)
            if CONFIG.autoCrouchEnabled then hookKillerAnimationsCrouch(newChar) end
        end)
    end)
end

-- ============================================================
--  AVATAR COPY SYSTEM
-- ============================================================
local function CopyAvatarFromUsername(input)
    if not input or input:gsub("%s+", "") == "" then return end
    input = input:gsub("%s+", "")

    task.spawn(function()
        local userId = tonumber(input)
        if not userId then
            local okId = pcall(function() userId = Players:GetUserIdFromNameAsync(input) end)
            if not okId or not userId then return end
        end

        local char = localPlayer.Character
        local humanoid = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not char or not humanoid then return end

        local desc = Players:GetHumanoidDescriptionFromUserId(userId)
        if not desc then return end

        local targetModel = Players:CreateHumanoidModelFromDescription(desc, humanoid.RigType)
        if not targetModel then return end

        for _, obj in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if obj:IsA("Accessory") or obj:IsA("Shirt") or obj:IsA("Pants") or obj:IsA("ShirtGraphic") or obj:IsA("BodyColors") or obj:IsA("CharacterMesh") then
                obj:Destroy()
            end
        end
        for _, dstPart in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
            if dstPart:IsA("BasePart") then
                for _, child in ipairs(dstPart:GetChildren()) do
                    if child:IsA("SpecialMesh") or child:IsA("Decal") or child:IsA("Texture") or child:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") then
                        child:Destroy()
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local srcBodyColors = targetModel:FindFirstChildOfClass("BodyColors")
        if srcBodyColors then
            srcBodyColors:Clone().Parent = char
            local colorMap = {
                ["Head"] = srcBodyColors.HeadColor3, ["Torso"] = srcBodyColors.TorsoColor3,
                ["Left Arm"] = srcBodyColors.LeftArmColor3, ["Right Arm"] = srcBodyColors.RightArmColor3,
                ["Left Leg"] = srcBodyColors.LeftLegColor3, ["Right Leg"] = srcBodyColors.RightLegColor3,
                ["UpperTorso"] = srcBodyColors.TorsoColor3, ["LowerTorso"] = srcBodyColors.TorsoColor3,
                ["LeftHand"] = srcBodyColors.LeftArmColor3, ["RightHand"] = srcBodyColors.RightArmColor3,
                ["LeftLowerArm"] = srcBodyColors.LeftArmColor3, ["RightLowerArm"] = srcBodyColors.RightArmColor3,
                ["LeftUpperArm"] = srcBodyColors.LeftArmColor3, ["RightUpperArm"] = srcBodyColors.RightArmColor3,
                ["LeftFoot"] = srcBodyColors.LeftLegColor3, ["RightFoot"] = srcBodyColors.RightLegColor3,
                ["LeftLowerLeg"] = srcBodyColors.LeftLegColor3, ["RightLowerLeg"] = srcBodyColors.RightLegColor3,
                ["LeftUpperLeg"] = srcBodyColors.LeftLegColor3, ["RightUpperLeg"] = srcBodyColors.RightLegColor3,
            }
            for partName, color in pairs(colorMap) do
                local part = char:FindFirstChild(partName)
                if part then pcall(function() part.Color = color end) end
            end
        end

        for _, obj in ipairs(targetModel:GetChildren()) do
            if obj:IsA("Shirt") or obj:IsA("Pants") or obj:IsA("ShirtGraphic") or obj:IsA("CharacterMesh") then
                obj:Clone().Parent = char
            end
        end

        for _, srcPart in ipairs(targetModel:GetChildren()) do
            if srcPart:IsA("BasePart") then
                local dstPart = char:FindFirstChild(srcPart.Name)
                if dstPart and dstPart:IsA("BasePart") then
                    if srcPart:IsA("MeshPart") and dstPart:IsA("MeshPart") then
                        pcall(function()
                            dstPart.MeshId = srcPart.MeshId
                            dstPart.TextureID = srcPart.TextureID
                            dstPart.Color = srcPart.Color
                            dstPart.Transparency = srcPart.Transparency
                        end)
                    end
                    for _, child in ipairs(srcPart:GetChildren()) do
                        if child:IsA("SpecialMesh") or child:IsA("Decal") or child:IsA("Texture") or child:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") then
                            child:Clone().Parent = dstPart
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local function WeldAccessory(acc)
            local clone = acc:Clone()
            clone.Parent = char
            local handle = clone:FindFirstChild("Handle")
            if not handle then return end

            handle.Anchored = false
            handle.CanCollide = false
            pcall(function() handle.Massless = true end)
            
            for _, child in ipairs(handle:GetChildren()) do
                if child:IsA("Weld") or child:IsA("WeldConstraint") or child:IsA("Motor6D") then
                    child:Destroy()
                end
            end

            pcall(function() humanoid:AddAccessory(clone) end)
            
            local isWelded = false
            for _, child in ipairs(handle:GetChildren()) do
                if (child:IsA("Weld") or child:IsA("WeldConstraint")) and child.Part1 and child.Part1:IsDescendantOf(char) then
                    isWelded = true
                    break
                end
            end
            
            if not isWelded then
                local accAtt = handle:FindFirstChildOfClass("Attachment")
                local weldPart = nil
                local matchAtt = nil

                if accAtt then
                    local found = char:FindFirstChild(accAtt.Name, true)
                    if found and found:IsA("Attachment") then
                        weldPart = found.Parent
                        matchAtt = found
                    end
                end

                if not weldPart then weldPart = char:FindFirstChild("Head") or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") end

                if weldPart then
                    if matchAtt and accAtt then
                        handle.CFrame = weldPart.CFrame * matchAtt.CFrame * accAtt.CFrame:Inverse()
                    else
                        handle.CFrame = weldPart.CFrame
                    end
                    local weld = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
                    weld.Name = "BolongWeld"
                    weld.Part0 = handle
                    weld.Part1 = weldPart
                    weld.Parent = handle
                end
            end
        end

        for _, obj in ipairs(targetModel:GetChildren()) do
            if obj:IsA("Accessory") then
                pcall(function() WeldAccessory(obj) end)
            end
        end

        targetModel:Destroy()

        if userId then
            if STATE.avatarUiTargetId ~= userId then
                STATE.avatarUiTargetId = userId
                STATE.avatarUiImage = nil
                STATE.avatarUiImageId = nil
            end
            STATE.avatarUiActive = true
            _EnsureAvatarUiWatchers()
            _HookAllAvatarSlots()
            _ApplyCopiedAvatarToUI()
        end
    end)
end

-- ============================================================
--  LIGHTING HELPERS
-- ============================================================
local applyFullbright, restoreLighting, removeVisualEffects, restoreVisualEffects
do
    local lightingBackup = {
        Ambient = Lighting.Ambient, 
        OutdoorAmbient = Lighting.OutdoorAmbient,
        ColorShift_Bottom = Lighting.ColorShift_Bottom, 
        ColorShift_Top = Lighting.ColorShift_Top,
        Brightness = Lighting.Brightness, 
        ClockTime = Lighting.ClockTime,
        GlobalShadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows, 
        FogStart = Lighting.FogStart,
        FogEnd = Lighting.FogEnd, 
        FogColor = Lighting.FogColor,
        ExposureCompensation = Lighting.ExposureCompensation,
        EnvironmentDiffuseScale = Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale,
        EnvironmentSpecularScale = Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale,
    }
    local savedEffectStates = {}

    function applyFullbright()
        Lighting.Ambient = Color3.fromRGB(178, 178, 178)
        Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = Color3.fromRGB(178, 178, 178)
        Lighting.ColorShift_Bottom = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
        Lighting.ColorShift_Top = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
        Lighting.Brightness = 3; Lighting.ClockTime = 12; Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
        Lighting.FogStart = 9e9; Lighting.FogEnd = 9e9; Lighting.ExposureCompensation = 0
        Lighting.EnvironmentDiffuseScale = 0; Lighting.EnvironmentSpecularScale = 0
    end

    function restoreLighting()
        for k, v in pairs(lightingBackup) do pcall(function() Lighting[k] = v end) end
    end

    function removeVisualEffects()
        savedEffectStates = {}
        for _, effect in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
            if effect:IsA("PostEffect") or effect:IsA("Clouds") or effect:IsA("Atmosphere") or effect:IsA("Sky") then
                savedEffectStates[effect] = { Enabled = effect.Enabled, Parent = effect.Parent }
                pcall(function()
                    if effect:IsA("Sky") then effect.Parent = nil else effect.Enabled = false end
                end)
            end
        end
    end

    function restoreVisualEffects()
        for inst, data in pairs(savedEffectStates) do
            if inst then 
                pcall(function() 
                    if inst:IsA("Sky") then inst.Parent = data.Parent else inst.Enabled = data.Enabled end
                end) 
            end
        end
        savedEffectStates = {}
    end

    RegisterTask("LightingEnforcer", 0.5, function()
        if STATE.fullbrightEnabled then applyFullbright() end
        if STATE.removeVfxEnabled then
            for _, effect in ipairs(Lighting:GetChildren()) do
                if effect:IsA("PostEffect") or effect:IsA("Clouds") or effect:IsA("Atmosphere") or effect:IsA("Sky") then
                    pcall(function()
                        if effect:IsA("Sky") then
                            if effect.Parent then effect.Parent = nil end
                        else
                            if effect.Enabled then effect.Enabled = false end
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    Lighting.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
        if STATE.removeVfxEnabled then
            if child:IsA("PostEffect") or child:IsA("Clouds") or child:IsA("Atmosphere") or child:IsA("Sky") then
                task.spawn(function()
                    pcall(function()
                        if child:IsA("Sky") then child.Parent = nil else child.Enabled = false end
                    end)
                end)
            end
        end
    end)
end

-- ============================================================
--  SILENT AIM SYSTEM
-- ============================================================
do
    local function CreateFOVCircle()
        local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        sg.Name = "BolongFOV"; sg.ResetOnSpawn = false; sg.DisplayOrder = 999999
        pcall(function() sg.Parent = (gethui and gethui() or game:GetService("CoreGui")) end)
        if not sg.Parent then sg.Parent = PlayerGui end
        STATE.FOVCircle = Instance.new("Frame")
        STATE.FOVCircle.Size = UDim2.new(0, CONFIG.silentAimFovRadius * 2, 0, CONFIG.silentAimFovRadius * 2)
        STATE.FOVCircle.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
        STATE.FOVCircle.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        STATE.FOVCircle.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        STATE.FOVCircle.BackgroundTransparency = 1; STATE.FOVCircle.Visible = false
        STATE.FOVCircle.Parent = sg
        local corner = Instance.new("UICorner"); corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0); corner.Parent = STATE.FOVCircle
        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        stroke.Color = ACCENT_COLOR; stroke.Thickness = 2; stroke.Transparency = 0.2; stroke.Parent = STATE.FOVCircle
    end
    CreateFOVCircle()

    local function GetTorsoCenter(char)
        if not char then return nil end
        local upperTorso = char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
        if upperTorso and upperTorso:IsA("BasePart") then return upperTorso.Position end
        local torso = char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
        if torso and torso:IsA("BasePart") then return torso.Position end
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if root then return root.Position end
        return nil
    end

    local function GetGunMuzzlePosition()
        local char = localPlayer.Character
        if not char then return nil end
        local ok, gun = pcall(function()
            return char:FindFirstChild("Twist of Fate"):FindFirstChild("Right Arm"):FindFirstChild("gun"):FindFirstChild("gun")
        end)
        if ok and gun and gun:IsA("BasePart") then return gun.Position end
        local rArm = char:FindFirstChild("Right Arm") or char:FindFirstChild("RightHand")
        if rArm then return rArm.Position end
        return nil
    end

    local TwistOfFateRemote = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes"):WaitForChild("Items"):WaitForChild("Twist of Fate"):WaitForChild("Fire")

    local originalNamecall
    pcall(function()
        originalNamecall = hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", function(self, ...)
            local method = getnamecallmethod()
            if method == "FireServer" and rawequal(self, TwistOfFateRemote) then
                local args = table.pack(...)
                if STATE.silentAimEnabled and typeof(STATE.silentAimLookVector) == "Vector3" then
                    if args.n >= 3 and typeof(args[3]) == "Vector3" then
                        args[3] = STATE.silentAimLookVector
                        if STATE.laserEspEnabled then STATE.triggerLaser = true end
                        local si = STATE.silentAimLastShot
                        if si then
                            print(string.format("[ToF] fire dist=%.0f tof=%.2f lead=%.1f vel=%.1f pred=%d damp=%d zig=%d dot=%.2f ping=%.0fms", si.dist or 0, si.tof or 0, si.lead or 0, si.vel or 0, si.pred or 0, si.damp or 0, si.zig or 0, si.dot or 0, (si.ping or 0) * 1000))
                        end
                    elseif args.n >= 2 and typeof(args[2]) == "Vector3" then
                        args[2] = STATE.silentAimLookVector
                        if STATE.laserEspEnabled then STATE.triggerLaser = true end
                        local si = STATE.silentAimLastShot
                        if si then
                            print(string.format("[ToF] fire dist=%.0f tof=%.2f lead=%.1f vel=%.1f pred=%d damp=%d zig=%d dot=%.2f ping=%.0fms", si.dist or 0, si.tof or 0, si.lead or 0, si.vel or 0, si.pred or 0, si.damp or 0, si.zig or 0, si.dot or 0, (si.ping or 0) * 1000))
                        end
                    end
                end
                return originalNamecall(self, table.unpack(args, 1, args.n))
            end
            return originalNamecall(self, ...)
        end)
    end)

    do
        local tofDownRemote = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes"):WaitForChild("Items"):WaitForChild("Twist of Fate"):WaitForChild("Fire")
        local lastDownShot = 0
        local function IsDownBusy()
            local char = localPlayer.Character
            if not char then return false end
            if localPlayer:GetAttribute("IsDead") then return true end
            if char:GetAttribute("IsCarried") then return true end
            if char:GetAttribute("IsHooked") then return true end
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum and hum.Health < hum.MaxHealth * 0.5 then return true end
            local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if root and root:HasTag("doing action") then return true end
            if char:GetAttribute("Knocked") == true then return true end
            return false
        end
        local function GetTofItem()
            local char = localPlayer.Character
            if char then
                local ok, gun = pcall(function()
                    return char:FindFirstChild("Twist of Fate"):FindFirstChild("Right Arm"):FindFirstChild("gun")
                end)
                if ok and gun then return gun end
                local it = char:FindFirstChild("Twist of Fate")
                if it then
                    local rarm = it:FindFirstChild("Right Arm")
                    if rarm and rarm:FindFirstChild("gun") then return rarm.gun end
                    return it
                end
            end
            return nil
        end
        local function FireTofDown()
            if not CONFIG.twistShootDownEnabled then return end
            if not IsDownBusy() then return end
            local now = tick()
            if now - lastDownShot < 1 then return end
            local item = GetTofItem()
            local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
            if not item or not cam then return end
            lastDownShot = now
            pcall(function()
                local dir = cam.CFrame.LookVector
                if STATE.silentAimEnabled and typeof(STATE.silentAimLookVector) == "Vector3" then
                    dir = STATE.silentAimLookVector
                end
                tofDownRemote:FireServer(item, dir)
            end)
        end
        UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gp)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                if CONFIG.twistShootDownEnabled and IsDownBusy() then
                    local pos = input.Position
                    for _, obj in ipairs(PlayerGui:GetDescendants()) do
                        if obj:IsA("ImageButton") and obj.Name == "Gui-mob" and obj.Visible then
                            local ap, as = obj.AbsolutePosition, obj.AbsoluteSize
                            if pos.X >= ap.X and pos.X <= ap.X + as.X and pos.Y >= ap.Y and pos.Y <= ap.Y + as.Y then
                                FireTofDown()
                                break
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
                return
            end
            if gp then return end
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                FireTofDown()
            elseif input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1 and input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonR2 then
                FireTofDown()
            end
        end)
    end

    RegisterTask("UpdateSilentAimTarget", 0.02, function()
        if STATE.FOVCircle then
            STATE.FOVCircle.Visible = STATE.silentAimFovVisible
            local targetSize = CONFIG.silentAimFovRadius * 2
            if STATE.FOVCircle.Size.X.Offset ~= targetSize then
                STATE.FOVCircle.Size = UDim2.new(0, targetSize, 0, targetSize)
            end
        end
        if not STATE.silentAimEnabled then 
            STATE.silentAimTarget = nil; STATE.silentAimLookVector = nil; STATE.silentAimPredictedPos = nil
            STATE.silentAimTrackChar = nil; STATE.silentAimHistory = {}; STATE.silentAimLastShot = nil; return 
        end
        local myChar = localPlayer.Character
        if not myChar then return end
        local myRoot = myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not myRoot then return end
        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then return end
        local bestPos = nil
        local bestDist = math.huge
        local bestChar = nil
        local targetMode = CONFIG.silentAimTargetType or "Killer"

        if targetMode == "Zombie" then
            if tick() - zombieCacheTime > 0.5 then RefreshZombieCache() end
            for i = 1, #zombieCache do
                local model = zombieCache[i]
                if model and model.Parent then
                    local root = GetScpModelRoot(model)
                    if root then
                        local pos = root.Position
                        local dist = (pos - myRoot.Position).Magnitude
                        if dist < bestDist then bestDist = dist; bestPos = pos; bestChar = model end
                    end
                end
            end
        else
            for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if player ~= localPlayer then
                    if targetMode == "Killer" and getRole(player) ~= "killer" then continue end
                    if targetMode == "Survivor" and getRole(player) ~= "survivor" then continue end
                    local pChar = player.Character
                    if pChar then
                        local pHum = pChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        local pTorso = GetTorsoCenter(pChar)
                        if pHum and pHum.Health > 0 and typeof(pTorso) == "Vector3" then
                            local dist = (pTorso - myRoot.Position).Magnitude
                            if dist < bestDist then bestDist = dist; bestPos = pTorso; bestChar = pChar end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end

        local now = tick()
        local smoothedVel = STATE.silentAimSmoothedVel
        if typeof(smoothedVel) ~= "Vector3" then smoothedVel = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0) end
        local silentAimDampDot = 0
        local silentAimZig = false
        if typeof(bestPos) == "Vector3" then
            if STATE.silentAimTrackChar ~= bestChar then
                STATE.silentAimTrackChar = bestChar
                STATE.silentAimHistory = {}
                smoothedVel = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
            end
            local hist = STATE.silentAimHistory
            table.insert(hist, { pos = bestPos, t = now })
            while #hist > 1 and hist[1].t < now - 0.4 do table.remove(hist, 1) end
            if #hist >= 2 then
                local a = hist[#hist - 1]
                local b = hist[#hist]
                local dt = b.t - a.t
                if dt > 0.001 then
                    local dp = (b.pos - a.pos) / dt
