                    local inst = Vector3.new(dp.X, 0, dp.Z)
                    local root = bestChar and bestChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if root and root:IsA("BasePart") then
                        local ok, av = pcall(function() return root.AssemblyLinearVelocity end)
                        if ok and typeof(av) == "Vector3" then
                            local ha = Vector3.new(av.X, 0, av.Z)
                            if ha.Magnitude > 0.5 and ha.Magnitude < 100 then
                                inst = inst:Lerp(ha, 0.5)
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    if inst.Magnitude > 65 then inst = inst.Unit * 65 end
                    if CONFIG.silentAimDamping and smoothedVel.Magnitude > 2 and inst.Magnitude > 2 then
                        silentAimDampDot = smoothedVel:Dot(inst) / (smoothedVel.Magnitude * inst.Magnitude)
                        if silentAimDampDot < 0 then
                            inst = inst * 0.7
                            silentAimZig = true
                        end
                    end
                    smoothedVel = smoothedVel:Lerp(inst, 0.45)
                end
            end
            local hvel = Vector3.new(smoothedVel.X, 0, smoothedVel.Z)
            if hvel.Magnitude > 65 then hvel = hvel.Unit * 65 end
            smoothedVel = hvel
        else
            STATE.silentAimTrackChar = nil
            STATE.silentAimHistory = {}
            smoothedVel = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        end
        STATE.silentAimTargetVel = smoothedVel
        STATE.silentAimSmoothedVel = smoothedVel

        if typeof(bestPos) == "Vector3" then
            local kTorso = bestPos
            if typeof(kTorso) == "Vector3" then
                local inFov = true
                local screenPos, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(kTorso)
                if onScreen then
                    local center = Vector2.new(camera.ViewportSize.X / 2, camera.ViewportSize.Y / 2)
                    local dist2D = (Vector2.new(screenPos.X, screenPos.Y) - center).Magnitude
                    if dist2D > CONFIG.silentAimFovRadius then inFov = false end
                else
                    inFov = false
                end
                if inFov then
                    local muzzlePos = GetGunMuzzlePosition()
                    local selfBusy = false
                    pcall(function()
                        local c = localPlayer.Character
                        if c then
                            if localPlayer:GetAttribute("IsDead") then selfBusy = true end
                            if c:GetAttribute("IsCarried") then selfBusy = true end
                            if c:GetAttribute("IsHooked") then selfBusy = true end
                            if c:GetAttribute("Knocked") == true then selfBusy = true end
                            local h = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                            if h and h.Health < h.MaxHealth * 0.5 then selfBusy = true end
                        end
                    end)
                    if selfBusy then
                        local r = myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if r then
                            muzzlePos = r.Position
                        else
                            muzzlePos = camera.CFrame.Position
                        end
                    end
                    if typeof(muzzlePos) ~= "Vector3" then muzzlePos = camera.CFrame.Position end
                    local aimBase = kTorso - Vector3.new(0, 1.2, 0)
                    local rawVec = aimBase - muzzlePos
                    local mag = rawVec.Magnitude
                    if mag > 0.1 then
                        local aimPos = aimBase
                        local tofFinal = mag / 200 + 0.1
                        local leadMag = 0
                        local velMag = 0
                        if typeof(STATE.silentAimSmoothedVel) == "Vector3" then
                            velMag = Vector3.new(STATE.silentAimSmoothedVel.X, 0, STATE.silentAimSmoothedVel.Z).Magnitude
                        end
                        if CONFIG.silentAimPrediction then
                            local targetVel = STATE.silentAimSmoothedVel
                            local leadLag = 0.1
                            local tof = mag / 200 + leadLag
                            aimPos = aimBase + targetVel * tof
                            local predVec = aimPos - muzzlePos
                            if predVec.Magnitude > 0.1 then
                                tof = predVec.Magnitude / 200 + leadLag
                                aimPos = aimBase + targetVel * tof
                            end
                            tofFinal = tof
                            leadMag = (aimPos - aimBase).Magnitude
                            STATE.silentAimPredictedPos = aimPos
                        else
                            STATE.silentAimPredictedPos = nil
                        end
                        local aimVec = aimPos - muzzlePos
                        local aimMag = aimVec.Magnitude
                        if aimMag > 0.1 then
                            STATE.silentAimTarget = kTorso
                            STATE.silentAimLookVector = Vector3.new(aimVec.X / aimMag, aimVec.Y / aimMag, aimVec.Z / aimMag)
                            STATE.currentMuzzlePos = muzzlePos; STATE.currentTargetPos = aimPos
                            local pingSec = 0
                            pcall(function()
                                if typeof(Spear_PingSec) == "function" then pingSec = Spear_PingSec() or 0 end
                            end)
                            STATE.silentAimLastShot = {
                                dist = mag, tof = tofFinal, lead = leadMag, vel = velMag,
                                pred = CONFIG.silentAimPrediction and 1 or 0,
                                damp = CONFIG.silentAimDamping and 1 or 0,
                                zig = silentAimZig and 1 or 0, dot = silentAimDampDot or 0,
                                ping = pingSec, t = tick(),
                            }
                        else
                            STATE.silentAimTarget = nil; STATE.silentAimLookVector = nil
                            STATE.silentAimLastShot = nil
                        end
                    else
                        STATE.silentAimTarget = nil; STATE.silentAimLookVector = nil
                        STATE.silentAimLastShot = nil
                    end
                else
                    STATE.silentAimTarget = nil; STATE.silentAimLookVector = nil
                    STATE.silentAimLastShot = nil
                end
            else
                STATE.silentAimTarget = nil; STATE.silentAimLookVector = nil
                STATE.silentAimLastShot = nil
            end
        else
            STATE.silentAimTarget = nil; STATE.silentAimLookVector = nil
            STATE.silentAimLastShot = nil
        end
    end)

    local aimLaser = nil

    RegisterTask("DrawLaserESP", 0, function()
        if STATE.triggerLaser then
            STATE.triggerLaser = false
            local startP = STATE.currentMuzzlePos
            local endP = STATE.currentTargetPos
            if typeof(startP) == "Vector3" and typeof(endP) == "Vector3" then
                local distance = (startP - endP).Magnitude
                if distance >= 0.1 then
                    local laser = Instance.new("Part")
                    laser.Name = "SilentLaser"; laser.Anchored = true; laser.CanCollide = false
                    laser.Material = Enum.Material.Neon; laser.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
                    laser.Transparency = 0.3; laser.Size = Vector3.new(0.15, 0.15, distance)
                    laser.CFrame = CFrame.new(startP, endP) * CFrame.new(0, 0, -distance / 2)
                    laser.Parent = workspace
                    task.delay(0.4, function() if laser then laser:Destroy() end end)
                end
            end
        end

        local char = localPlayer.Character
        local aiming = STATE.laserEspEnabled
            and STATE.silentAimEnabled
            and char ~= nil
            and char:GetAttribute("Aiming") == true
        local startP = aiming and STATE.currentMuzzlePos or nil
        local endP = aiming and STATE.currentTargetPos or nil
        local distance = (typeof(startP) == "Vector3" and typeof(endP) == "Vector3")
            and (startP - endP).Magnitude or 0
        if distance < 0.1 then
            if aimLaser then aimLaser:Destroy(); aimLaser = nil end
            return
        end
        if not aimLaser or not aimLaser.Parent then
            aimLaser = Instance.new("Part")
            aimLaser.Name = "SilentLaserAim"; aimLaser.Anchored = true; aimLaser.CanCollide = false
            aimLaser.Material = Enum.Material.Neon; aimLaser.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
            aimLaser.Transparency = 0.3
            aimLaser.Parent = workspace
        end
        aimLaser.Size = Vector3.new(0.15, 0.15, distance)
        aimLaser.CFrame = CFrame.new(startP, endP) * CFrame.new(0, 0, -distance / 2)
    end)

end

-- ============================================================
--  AIMLOCK FLASHLIGHT SYSTEM
-- ============================================================
do
    local FLASH_AIM_RANGE = 30

    local function HasFlashlightItem(char)
        if not char then return false end
        if char:FindFirstChild("Flashlight") then return true end
        for _, child in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if child.Name == "Flashlight" and (child:IsA("Model") or child:IsA("Tool") or child:IsA("Accessory")) then
                return true
            end
        end
        return false
    end

    local function IsValidFlashTarget(character)
        if not character then return false end
        local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 50 then return false end
        local hooked = character:GetAttribute("IsHooked") or character:GetAttribute("isHooked")
        if hooked == true then return false end
        return true
    end

    local function GetFlashAimHead(char)
        if not char then return nil end
        local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if head and head:IsA("BasePart") then return head end
        return char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
            or char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
            or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    end

    local function GetFlashAimTarget()
        local localChar = localPlayer.Character
        local localRoot = localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not localRoot then return nil end
        local bestPart, bestDist = nil, math.huge
        for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if player ~= localPlayer and player.Character and IsValidFlashTarget(player.Character) then
                if getRole(player) == "killer" then
                    local part = GetFlashAimHead(player.Character)
                    if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
                        local dist = (localRoot.Position - part.Position).Magnitude
                        if dist <= FLASH_AIM_RANGE and dist < bestDist then
                            bestDist = dist
                            bestPart = part
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return bestPart
    end

    local flashActivateRemote = nil
    pcall(function()
        local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        local items = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Items")
        local flashlight = items and items:FindFirstChild("Flashlight")
        local activate = flashlight and flashlight:FindFirstChild("Activate")
        if activate and activate:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
            flashActivateRemote = activate
        end
    end)

    local originalFlashNamecall
    pcall(function()
        originalFlashNamecall = hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", function(self, ...)
            local method = getnamecallmethod()
            if method == "FireServer" and flashActivateRemote and rawequal(self, flashActivateRemote) then
                local args = table.pack(...)
                pcall(function() STATE.flashAimActive = (args[2] == true) end)
            end
            return originalFlashNamecall(self, ...)
        end)
    end)

    RegisterTask("FlashAimLock", 0.02, function()
        if not STATE.flashAimEnabled then return end
        local hasFlash = HasFlashlightItem(localPlayer.Character)
        local isSurv = getRole(localPlayer) ~= "killer"
        if not (hasFlash and isSurv and STATE.flashAimActive) then return end
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not cam then return end
        local targetPart = GetFlashAimTarget()
        if not targetPart then return end
        local targetPos = targetPart.Position
        local smooth = math.clamp(tonumber(STATE.flashAimSmooth) or 0.35, 0.05, 1)
        pcall(function()
            cam.CFrame = cam.CFrame:Lerp(CFrame.new(cam.CFrame.Position, targetPos), smooth)
        end)
        pcall(function()
            local char = localPlayer.Character
            local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp then
                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position, Vector3.new(targetPos.X, hrp.Position.Y, targetPos.Z))
            end
        end)
    end)
end

-- ============================================================
--  INVISIBLE SYSTEM
-- ============================================================
local SetVisibleMode
do
    local LIFT_OFFSET = Vector3.new(0, -6000, 0)
    local VISIBLE_TRANS = 0.5
    local FAIL_LIMIT = 5

    local visibleParts = {}

    local function ResolveLookRemote()
        local ok, ev = pcall(function()
            local rs = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
            local remotes = rs:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
            if not remotes then return nil end
            local g = remotes:FindFirstChild("Game")
            if not g then return nil end
            local e = g:FindFirstChild("UpdateCharacterLook")
            if e and e:IsA("RemoteEvent") then return e end
            return nil
        end)
        if ok then return ev end
        return nil
    end

    local function ShiftPoseArgs(...)
        local out = {}
        local n = select("#", ...)
        for i = 1, n do
            local v = select(i, ...)
            if typeof(v) == "CFrame" then
                v = v + LIFT_OFFSET
            end
            out[i] = v
        end
        return table.unpack(out, 1, n)
    end

    local function FireSpoofBurst()
        if not STATE.invisOn then return false end
        local cache = STATE.invisCacheReal
        if type(cache) ~= "table" or #cache ~= 5 then return false end
        local ev = ResolveLookRemote()
        if not ev then return false end
        local res = table.pack(pcall(ShiftPoseArgs, table.unpack(cache)))
        if not res[1] then return false end
        local RealFire = ev.FireServer
        if typeof(RealFire) ~= "function" then return false end
        local okSend = pcall(RealFire, ev, table.unpack(res, 2, res.n))
        if okSend then
            STATE.invisSendFails = 0
            STATE.invisSpoofs = STATE.invisSpoofs + 1
        else
            STATE.invisSendFails = STATE.invisSendFails + 1
        end
        return okSend
    end

    local function FireRealReplay()
        if STATE.invisOn then return false end
        local cache = STATE.invisCacheReal
        if type(cache) ~= "table" or #cache ~= 5 then return false end
        local ev = ResolveLookRemote()
        if not ev then return false end
        local RealFire = ev.FireServer
        if typeof(RealFire) ~= "function" then return false end
        return pcall(RealFire, ev, table.unpack(cache))
    end

    local function ClearVisibleFx(character)
        if character == nil then return end
        for _, child in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
            if child.Name == "Bolong_Invis" and child:IsA("Highlight") then
                pcall(function() child:Destroy() end)
            end
        end
    end

    local function DestroyVisibleTag(character)
        if STATE.invisTagConn then
            pcall(function() STATE.invisTagConn:Disconnect() end)
            STATE.invisTagConn = nil
        end
        if character == nil then return end
        local head = character:FindFirstChild("Head") or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if head then
            local old = head:FindFirstChild("Bolong_InvisTag")
            if old then pcall(function() old:Destroy() end) end
        end
    end

    local function SyncVisibleTag(character, on)
        if character == nil then return end
        DestroyVisibleTag(character)
        if not on then return end
        local head = character:FindFirstChild("Head") or character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not head then return end
        local ok, bb = pcall(function()
            local labelText = "[ Invisible ]"
            local textSize = 12
            local textFont = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            local textW = 110
            pcall(function()
                local ts = game:GetService("TextService")
                textW = ts:GetTextSize(labelText, textSize, textFont, Vector2.new(1000, 26)).X
            end)
            local iconSize, gap, glowR, margin = 14, 4, 1.5, 5
            local labelH = 14
            local totalW = math.ceil(margin * 2 + iconSize + gap + textW)
            local totalH = iconSize + margin * 2
            local gui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
            gui.Name = "Bolong_InvisTag"
            gui.AlwaysOnTop = true
            gui.Size = UDim2.new(0, totalW, 0, totalH)
            gui.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0)
            gui.Adornee = head
            local iconX, iconY = margin, margin
            local labelX = margin + iconSize + gap
            local labelY = margin + math.floor((iconSize - labelH) / 2)
            local function px(v, k) return math.floor(v * k + 0.5) end
            local icon = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
            icon.Name = "Icon"
            icon.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(iconX, iconY)
            icon.Size = UDim2.new(0, iconSize, 0, iconSize)
            icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            icon.Image = "rbxassetid://84034353458936"
            icon.ZIndex = 2
            icon.Parent = gui
            local halos = {}
            local haloPads = { { 2, 0.6 }, { 4, 0.85 } }
            for i, hp in ipairs(haloPads) do
                local pad, transp = hp[1], hp[2]
                local h = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
                h.Name = "Halo" .. i
                h.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(iconX - pad, iconY - pad)
                h.Size = UDim2.new(0, iconSize + pad * 2, 0, iconSize + pad * 2)
                h.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                h.Image = "rbxassetid://84034353458936"
                h.ImageColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
                h.ImageTransparency = transp
                h.ZIndex = 1
                h.Parent = gui
                halos[#halos + 1] = { o = h, pad = pad }
            end
            local glows = {}
            local glowDefs = {
                { glowR, 0 }, { -glowR, 0 }, { 0, glowR }, { 0, -glowR },
                { glowR * 0.7, glowR * 0.7 }, { glowR * 0.7, -glowR * 0.7 },
                { -glowR * 0.7, glowR * 0.7 }, { -glowR * 0.7, -glowR * 0.7 },
            }
            for i, gd in ipairs(glowDefs) do
                local g = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                g.Name = "Glow" .. i
                g.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(labelX + gd[1], labelY + gd[2])
                g.Size = UDim2.new(0, textW, 0, labelH)
                g.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                g.Text = labelText
                g.TextColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
                g.TextTransparency = 0.8
                g.TextSize = textSize
                g.Font = textFont
                g.ZIndex = 1
                g.Parent = gui
                glows[#glows + 1] = { o = g, dx = gd[1], dy = gd[2] }
            end
            local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            lbl.Name = "Label"
            lbl.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(labelX, labelY)
            lbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, textW, 0, labelH)
            lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            lbl.Text = labelText
            lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            lbl.TextTransparency = 0
            lbl.TextSize = textSize
            lbl.Font = textFont
            lbl.ZIndex = 2
            lbl.Parent = gui
            gui.Parent = head
            local refDist, minK, maxK = 25, 0.45, 1.35
            if STATE.invisTagConn then
                pcall(function() STATE.invisTagConn:Disconnect() end)
                STATE.invisTagConn = nil
            end
            STATE.invisTagConn = game:GetService("RunService").RenderStepped:Connect(function()
                if not gui.Parent then return end
                local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
                if not cam then return end
                local hp = head.Parent and head.Position or nil
                if not hp then return end
                local dist = (cam.CFrame.Position - hp).Magnitude
                if dist < 1 then dist = 1 end
                local k = refDist / dist
                if k < minK then k = minK elseif k > maxK then k = maxK end
                gui.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(px(totalW, k), px(totalH, k))
                icon.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(px(iconX, k), px(iconY, k))
                icon.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(px(iconSize, k), px(iconSize, k))
                for _, hh in ipairs(halos) do
                    hh.o.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(px(iconX - hh.pad, k), px(iconY - hh.pad, k))
                    hh.o.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(px(iconSize + hh.pad * 2, k), px(iconSize + hh.pad * 2, k))
                end
                for _, gg in ipairs(glows) do
                    gg.o.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(px(labelX + gg.dx, k), px(labelY + gg.dy, k))
                    gg.o.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(px(textW, k), px(labelH, k))
                    gg.o.TextSize = px(textSize, k)
                end
                lbl.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(px(labelX, k), px(labelY, k))
                lbl.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(px(textW, k), px(labelH, k))
                lbl.TextSize = px(textSize, k)
            end)
            return gui
        end)
        if not ok or not bb then
            DestroyVisibleTag(character)
        end
    end

    local function PaintVisible(character, on)
        if character == nil then return end
        ClearVisibleFx(character)
        if not on then
            DestroyVisibleTag(character)
            for _, part in ipairs(visibleParts) do
                if part and part.Parent then
                    pcall(function() part.Transparency = 0 end)
                end
            end
            visibleParts = {}
            return
        end
        visibleParts = {}
        for _, desc in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
            if desc:IsA("BasePart") and desc.Transparency == 0 then
                visibleParts[#visibleParts + 1] = desc
            end
        end
        for _, part in ipairs(visibleParts) do
            pcall(function() part.Transparency = VISIBLE_TRANS end)
        end
        local ok, inst = pcall(function() return Instance.new("Highlight") end)
        if ok and inst then
            inst.Name = "Bolong_Invis"
            inst.Adornee = character
            inst.FillColor = Color3.fromRGB(184, 106, 4)
            inst.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
            inst.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
            inst.FillTransparency = 1
            inst.OutlineTransparency = 0
            inst.Parent = character
        end
        SyncVisibleTag(character, true)
    end

    local function InstallLookHook()
        if STATE.invisHooked then return end
        local ok = pcall(function()
            assert(typeof(hookmetamethod) == "function", "hookmetamethod missing")
            assert(typeof(getnamecallmethod) == "function", "getnamecallmethod missing")
            assert(typeof(checkcaller) == "function", "checkcaller missing")
            local oldNamecall
            oldNamecall = hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", function(self, ...)
                if checkcaller() then
                    return oldNamecall(self, ...)
                end
                if getnamecallmethod() ~= "FireServer" then
                    return oldNamecall(self, ...)
                end
                local okName, nm = pcall(function() return self.Name end)
                if not okName or typeof(nm) ~= "string" then
                    return oldNamecall(self, ...)
                end
                if string.lower(nm) ~= "updatecharacterlook" then
                    return oldNamecall(self, ...)
                end
                if not STATE.invisOn then
                    if select("#", ...) == 5 then
                        local a, b, c, d, e = ...
                        if typeof(a) == "CFrame" and typeof(b) == "CFrame" and typeof(c) == "CFrame" and typeof(d) == "CFrame" and typeof(e) == "CFrame" then
                            STATE.invisCacheReal = { a, b, c, d, e }
                        end
                    end
                    return oldNamecall(self, ...)
                end
                local charNow = localPlayer.Character
                local hrpNow = charNow and charNow:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if hrpNow == nil then
                    return oldNamecall(self, ...)
                end
                if select("#", ...) ~= 5 then
                    STATE.invisDropCount = STATE.invisDropCount + 1
                    return nil
                end
                local res = table.pack(pcall(ShiftPoseArgs, ...))
                if not res[1] then
                    return oldNamecall(self, ...)
                end
                if STATE.invisSendFails >= FAIL_LIMIT then
                    return oldNamecall(self, table.unpack(res, 2, res.n))
                end
                local RealFire = self.FireServer
                local okSend = false
                if typeof(RealFire) == "function" then
                    okSend = pcall(RealFire, self, table.unpack(res, 2, res.n))
                end
                if okSend then
                    STATE.invisSendFails = 0
                    STATE.invisSpoofs = STATE.invisSpoofs + 1
                else
                    STATE.invisSendFails = STATE.invisSendFails + 1
                end
                return nil
            end)
        end)
        if ok then STATE.invisHooked = true end
    end

    function SetVisibleMode(on)
        STATE.invisOn = on and true or false
        local char = localPlayer.Character
        if char then PaintVisible(char, STATE.invisOn) end
        if STATE.invisOn then
            FireSpoofBurst()
        else
            FireRealReplay()
        end
    end

    if not STATE.invisCharConn then
        STATE.invisCharConn = localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
            STATE.invisCacheReal = nil
            if STATE.invisOn then
                task.spawn(function()
                    task.wait(1)
                    if STATE.invisOn then PaintVisible(char, true) end
                end)
            end
        end)
    end

    pcall(function()
        local char = localPlayer.Character
        if char then
            ClearVisibleFx(char)
            DestroyVisibleTag(char)
        end
    end)
    STATE.invisOn = false
    InstallLookHook()
end

-- ============================================================
--  AIMLOCK M2 HIDDEN SYSTEM
-- ============================================================
local StartHiddenM2Hook, StopHiddenM2Hook
do
    local HIDDEN_M2_RANGE = 50
    local HIDDEN_M2_WINDOW = 1.5

    local function IsValidHiddenM2Target(character)
        if not character then return false end
        local humanoid = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not humanoid or humanoid.Health <= 0 then return false end
        if character:GetAttribute("Knocked") then return false end
        if character:GetAttribute("IsHooked") then return false end
        if character:GetAttribute("IsCarried") then return false end
        return true
    end

    local function GetHiddenM2Target()
        local localChar = localPlayer.Character
        local localRoot = localChar and localChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not localRoot then return nil end
        local bestPart, bestDist = nil, math.huge
        for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if player ~= localPlayer and player.Character and IsValidHiddenM2Target(player.Character) then
                if getRole(player) ~= "killer" then
                    local part = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        or player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                    if part and part:IsA("BasePart") then
                        local dist = (localRoot.Position - part.Position).Magnitude
                        if dist <= HIDDEN_M2_RANGE and dist < bestDist then
                            bestDist = dist
                            bestPart = part
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return bestPart
    end

    local function OpenHiddenM2Window()
        if not STATE.hiddenM2AimEnabled then return end
        local okRole, isKiller = pcall(function() return getRole(localPlayer) == "killer" end)
        if not okRole or not isKiller then return end
        STATE.hiddenM2LockUntil = tick() + HIDDEN_M2_WINDOW
    end

    function StartHiddenM2Hook()
        if STATE.hiddenM2PrepareConn then return end
        pcall(function()
            local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
            local killers = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Killers")
            local hidden = killers and killers:FindFirstChild("Hidden")
            local prepare = hidden and hidden:FindFirstChild("preparem2")
            if prepare and prepare:IsA("BindableEvent") then
                STATE.hiddenM2PrepareConn = prepare.Event:Connect(OpenHiddenM2Window)
            elseif prepare and prepare:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                STATE.hiddenM2PrepareConn = prepare.OnClientEvent:Connect(OpenHiddenM2Window)
            else
                Notify("AIMLOCK M2", "preparem2 tidak ditemukan", 2)
            end
        end)
    end

    function StopHiddenM2Hook()
        if STATE.hiddenM2PrepareConn then
            pcall(function() STATE.hiddenM2PrepareConn:Disconnect() end)
            STATE.hiddenM2PrepareConn = nil
        end
        STATE.hiddenM2LockUntil = 0
    end

    RegisterTask("HiddenM2Aimlock", 0.02, function()
        if not STATE.hiddenM2AimEnabled then return end
        if tick() > STATE.hiddenM2LockUntil then return end
        local okRole, isKiller = pcall(function() return getRole(localPlayer) == "killer" end)
        if not okRole or not isKiller then return end
        local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not cam then return end
        local targetPart = GetHiddenM2Target()
        if not targetPart then return end
        local targetPos = targetPart.Position
        local smooth = math.clamp(tonumber(STATE.hiddenM2AimSmooth) or 1.0, 0.05, 1)
        pcall(function()
            cam.CFrame = cam.CFrame:Lerp(CFrame.new(cam.CFrame.Position, targetPos), smooth)
        end)
        pcall(function()
            local char = localPlayer.Character
            local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp then
                hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(hrp.Position, Vector3.new(targetPos.X, hrp.Position.Y, targetPos.Z))
            end
        end)
    end)
end

-- ============================================================

do
    local SpearVelSt = { hist = {}, char = nil, sm = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0) }
    local function Spear_GetTorsoCenter(char)
        if not char then return nil end
        local upperTorso = char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
        if upperTorso and upperTorso:IsA("BasePart") then return upperTorso.Position end
        local torso = char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
        if torso and torso:IsA("BasePart") then return torso.Position end
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if root then return root.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0.5, 0) end
        return nil
    end
    local function Spear_IsTargetInFOV(camera, targetPosition, fovRadius)
        local screenPos, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(targetPosition)
        if not onScreen then return false end
        local center = Vector2.new(camera.ViewportSize.X / 2, camera.ViewportSize.Y / 2)
        local targetScreenPos = Vector2.new(screenPos.X, screenPos.Y)
        local dist2D = (targetScreenPos - center).Magnitude
        return dist2D <= fovRadius
    end
    local function Spear_CreateFOVCircle()

        sg.Name = "BolongSpearFOV"; sg.ResetOnSpawn = false; sg.DisplayOrder = 999999

        STATE.SpearFOVCircle.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)

        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        stroke.Color = ACCENT_COLOR; stroke.Thickness = 2; stroke.Transparency = 0.2; stroke.Parent = STATE.SpearFOVCircle
    end
    Spear_CreateFOVCircle()

        STATE.SpearStatusText = Instance.new("TextLabel")

    Spear_CreateIndicatorUI()

    local function Spear_CreateSnapLineDrawings()

        STATE.SnapLineTextLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        STATE.SnapLineTextLabel.Text = ""
        STATE.SnapLineTextLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        STATE.SnapLineTextLabel.TextSize = 12; STATE.SnapLineTextLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        STATE.SnapLineTextLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
        STATE.SnapLineTextLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.new(0, 0, 0)
        STATE.SnapLineTextLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
        STATE.SnapLineTextLabel.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
        STATE.SnapLineTextLabel.Visible = false; STATE.SnapLineTextLabel.Parent = STATE.SnapLineGui
    end
    Spear_CreateSnapLineDrawings()

    local function Spear_EstimateProjectileHeight(horizontalDist, angle, speed, gravity)
        local cosA = math.cos(angle)
        if cosA <= 0.015 then return nil, nil end
        local travelTime = horizontalDist / (speed * cosA)
        if travelTime ~= travelTime or travelTime <= 0 then return nil, nil end
        local frameDt = 1 / 60
        local discreteDropFix = 0.5 * gravity * frameDt * travelTime
        local height = speed * math.sin(angle) * travelTime - (0.5 * gravity * travelTime * travelTime) - discreteDropFix
        return height, travelTime
    end
    local function Spear_ScorePitch(horizontalDist, heightDiff, angle, speed, gravity)
        local predictedHeight, travelTime = Spear_EstimateProjectileHeight(horizontalDist, angle, speed, gravity)
        if not predictedHeight or not travelTime then return nil end
        if travelTime < 0.025 or travelTime > 5 then return nil end
        local heightError = math.abs(predictedHeight - heightDiff)
        local highArcPenalty = math.max(angle - RAD_42, 0) * 0.35
        local timePenalty = math.max(travelTime - 1.3, 0) * 0.25
        return heightError + highArcPenalty + timePenalty, heightError, travelTime
    end
    local function Spear_SolveBallisticDirection(launchPos, targetPos, speed, gravity)
        if speed <= 0 then speed = 142.5 end
        if gravity <= 0 then gravity = 196.2 end
        local delta = targetPos - launchPos
        local horizontal = Vector3.new(delta.X, 0, delta.Z)
        local horizontalDist = horizontal.Magnitude
        local heightDiff = delta.Y
        if delta.Magnitude <= 0.001 then return nil end
        if horizontalDist <= 0.35 or gravity <= 0.001 then
            return delta.Unit, math.clamp(delta.Magnitude / speed, 0.025, 5)
        end
        local horizontalDir = horizontal.Unit
        local lineAngle = math.atan2(heightDiff, horizontalDist)
        local minAngle = math.max(-RAD_28, lineAngle - RAD_18)
        local maxAngle = RAD_72
        local bestAngle, bestScore, bestError, bestTime = nil, math.huge, math.huge, nil
        local farShot = horizontalDist > 80
        local samples = farShot and 44 or 30
        local goodEnough = farShot and 0.08 or GOOD_ENOUGH_ERROR
        for i = 0, samples do
            local angle = minAngle + (maxAngle - minAngle) * (i / samples)
            local score, heightError, travelTime = Spear_ScorePitch(horizontalDist, heightDiff, angle, speed, gravity)
            if score and score < bestScore then
                bestScore, bestError, bestAngle, bestTime = score, heightError, angle, travelTime
                if bestError < goodEnough then break end
            end
        end
        if bestAngle then
            local range = (maxAngle - minAngle) / samples * 2.5
            local maxRefineIters = (bestError < goodEnough) and 1 or (farShot and 6 or 4)
            for _ = 1, maxRefineIters do
                local localBestAngle, localBestScore, localBestError, localBestTime = bestAngle, bestScore, bestError, bestTime
                for j = -3, 3 do
                    local angle = math.clamp(bestAngle + range * (j / 3), minAngle, maxAngle)
                    local score, heightError, travelTime = Spear_ScorePitch(horizontalDist, heightDiff, angle, speed, gravity)
                    if score and score < localBestScore then
                        localBestScore, localBestError, localBestAngle, localBestTime = score, heightError, angle, travelTime
                    end
                end
                bestAngle, bestScore, bestError, bestTime = localBestAngle, localBestScore, localBestError, localBestTime
                if bestError < goodEnough then break end
                range = range * 0.38
            end
        end
        if not bestAngle then
            local directTime = math.clamp(horizontalDist / speed, 0.025, 5)
            local lift = math.min(0.5 * gravity * directTime * directTime, 15)
            local aimPos = targetPos + Vector3.new(0, lift, 0)
            local fallback = aimPos - launchPos
            if fallback.Magnitude <= 0.001 then return nil end
            return fallback.Unit, directTime
        end
        local dir = horizontalDir * math.cos(bestAngle) + Vector3.new(0, math.sin(bestAngle), 0)
        if dir.Magnitude <= 0.001 then return nil end
        return dir.Unit, bestTime
    end
    local function Spear_CanHit(horizontalDist, heightDiff, speed, gravity)
        if speed <= 0 or gravity <= 0 then return false end
        local x, y, v, g = horizontalDist, heightDiff, speed, gravity
        local term = (g * x * x) / (2 * v * v)
        if term <= 0 then return false end
        local a, b, c = term, -x, y + term
        return (b*b - 4*a*c) >= 0
    end
    local function Spear_GetIdealHoldTime(horizontalDist, heightDiff, gravity)
        for t = 0.1, 3.0, 0.05 do
            local s = 23
            if t < 1 then s = math.max(23, t * 150)
            elseif t < 2 then s = 142.5
            else s = 165 end
            if Spear_CanHit(horizontalDist, heightDiff, s, gravity) then return t end
        end
        return nil
    end

    do
        local SpearRemote = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes"):WaitForChild("Killers"):WaitForChild("Veil"):WaitForChild("Spearthrow")
        local VisualizeRemote = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes"):WaitForChild("Mechanics"):WaitForChild("visualize")
        VisualizeRemote.OnClientEvent:Connect(function(ownerChar, spearDir, spearSpeed, gravityMult)
            if ownerChar == localPlayer.Character then
                if type(spearSpeed) == "number" and spearSpeed >= 15 and spearSpeed <= 800 then STATE.lastSpearSpeed = spearSpeed end
                if type(gravityMult) == "number" and gravityMult > 0.2 and gravityMult <= 5 then STATE.lastGravityMult = gravityMult end
                local si = STATE.spearLastShot
                if si and (tick() - (si.t or 0)) < 5 and (si.speed or 0) > 1
                    and type(spearSpeed) == "number" and spearSpeed >= 15 and spearSpeed <= 800 then
                    local ratio = spearSpeed / si.speed
                    if ratio > 0.2 and ratio < 5 then
                        local cal = tonumber(STATE.spearSpeedCal) or 1.0
                        STATE.spearSpeedCal = math.clamp(cal + (ratio - 1) * 0.3, 0.5, 2.0)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
        local OldSpearNamecall
        OldSpearNamecall = hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", function(...)
            local method = getnamecallmethod()
            if method == "FireServer" then
                local args = table.pack(...)
                local self = args[1]
                if typeof(self) == "Instance" and rawequal(self, SpearRemote) then
                    if STATE.silentSpearEnabled and typeof(STATE.silentSpearLookVector) == "Vector3" then
                        if args.n >= 4 and typeof(args[2]) == "Vector3" and typeof(args[4]) == "Vector3" then
                            args[2] = STATE.silentSpearLookVector
                            local si = STATE.spearLastShot
                            if si then
                                print(string.format("[Spear] fire dist=%.0f spd=%.0f(raw%.0f,cal%.2f) tof=%.2f lead=%.1f ping=%.0fms grav=%.2f stab=%.2f", si.dist or 0, si.speed or 0, si.raw or 0, tonumber(STATE.spearSpeedCal) or 1, si.tof or 0, si.lead or 0, (si.ping or 0) * 1000, si.grav or 1, si.stab == nil and -1 or si.stab))
                            end
                            return OldSpearNamecall(table.unpack(args, 1, args.n))
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            return OldSpearNamecall(...)
        end)
        local OldSpearFireServer
        OldSpearFireServer = hookmetamethod(game, "__index", function(t, k)
            if k == "FireServer" and typeof(t) == "Instance" and rawequal(t, SpearRemote) then
                return newcclosure(function(...)
                    local args = table.pack(...)
                    if STATE.silentSpearEnabled and typeof(STATE.silentSpearLookVector) == "Vector3" then
                        if args.n >= 4 and typeof(args[2]) == "Vector3" and typeof(args[4]) == "Vector3" then
                            args[2] = STATE.silentSpearLookVector
                            local si = STATE.spearLastShot
                            if si then
                                print(string.format("[Spear] fire dist=%.0f spd=%.0f(raw%.0f,cal%.2f) tof=%.2f lead=%.1f ping=%.0fms grav=%.2f stab=%.2f", si.dist or 0, si.speed or 0, si.raw or 0, tonumber(STATE.spearSpeedCal) or 1, si.tof or 0, si.lead or 0, (si.ping or 0) * 1000, si.grav or 1, si.stab == nil and -1 or si.stab))
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    return OldSpearFireServer(t, k)(table.unpack(args, 1, args.n))
                end)
            end
            return OldSpearFireServer(t, k)
        end)
    end

    do
        local hookedSpearMobileButtons = {}
        local function Spear_HasAncestorNamed(inst, name)
            local current = inst and inst.Parent
            while current do
                if current.Name == name then return true end
                current = current.Parent
            end
            return false
        end
        local function Spear_IsSpearAttackButton(inst)
            if not (inst and inst:IsA("GuiButton")) then return false end
            if inst.Name ~= "attack" and inst.Name ~= "Gui-mob" then return false end
            if not Spear_HasAncestorNamed(inst, "Slasher-mob") and not Spear_HasAncestorNamed(inst, "Survivor-mob") then return false end
            if not Spear_HasAncestorNamed(inst, "Control") and not Spear_HasAncestorNamed(inst, "Controls") then return false end
            return true
        end
        local function Spear_HookMobileButton(btn)
            if not Spear_IsSpearAttackButton(btn) then return end
            if hookedSpearMobileButtons[btn] then return end
            hookedSpearMobileButtons[btn] = true
            btn.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
                local t = input.UserInputType
                if t ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch and t ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then return end
                local char = localPlayer.Character
                if char and char:GetAttribute("spearmode") then
                    STATE.spearIsHolding = true; STATE.spearHoldStartTime = tick()
                end
            end)
            btn.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
                local t = input.UserInputType
                if t ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch and t ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then return end
                STATE.spearIsHolding = false; STATE.spearHoldStartTime = nil
            end)
        end
        local function Spear_ScanMobileButtons()
            local pg = localPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui") or PlayerGui
            if not pg then return end
            pcall(function()
                local function scanSpearScreen(screenName)
                    local screen = pg:FindFirstChild(screenName, true)
                    if not screen then return end
                    local control = screen:FindFirstChild("Control") or screen:FindFirstChild("Controls")
                    if control then
                        local function walkControl(parent)
                            for _, child in ipairs(parent:GetChildren()) do
                                Spear_HookMobileButton(child)
                                walkControl(child)
                            end
                        end
                        walkControl(control)
                    end
                    local guiMob = screen:FindFirstChild("Gui-mob", true)
                    if guiMob and guiMob:IsA("GuiButton") then Spear_HookMobileButton(guiMob) end
                end
                scanSpearScreen("Slasher-mob")
                scanSpearScreen("Survivor-mob")
            end)
            pg.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(desc) Spear_HookMobileButton(desc) end)
        end
        UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gp)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                local char = localPlayer.Character
                if char and char:GetAttribute("spearmode") then
                    STATE.spearIsHolding = true; STATE.spearHoldStartTime = tick()
                end
            end
        end)
        UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 then
                STATE.spearIsHolding = false; STATE.spearHoldStartTime = nil
            end
        end)
        Spear_ScanMobileButtons()
    end

    RegisterTask("Spear_UpdateSnapLine", 0, function(dt)
        local SNAPLINE = STATE.SPEAR_SNAPLINE
        if not SNAPLINE.enabled then
            if STATE.SnapLineFrame then STATE.SnapLineFrame.Visible = false end
            if STATE.SnapLineDot then STATE.SnapLineDot.Visible = false end
            if STATE.SnapLineTextLabel then STATE.SnapLineTextLabel.Visible = false end
            SNAPLINE.locked = false; SNAPLINE.lockedTarget = nil; SNAPLINE.lockedPlayerName = ""
            return
        end
        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then
            if STATE.SnapLineFrame then STATE.SnapLineFrame.Visible = false end
            if STATE.SnapLineDot then STATE.SnapLineDot.Visible = false end
            if STATE.SnapLineTextLabel then STATE.SnapLineTextLabel.Visible = false end
            return
        end
        local myChar = localPlayer.Character
        local isSpearMode = myChar and myChar:GetAttribute("spearmode")
        if not isSpearMode then
            if STATE.SnapLineFrame then STATE.SnapLineFrame.Visible = false end
            if STATE.SnapLineDot then STATE.SnapLineDot.Visible = false end
            if STATE.SnapLineTextLabel then STATE.SnapLineTextLabel.Visible = false end
            return
        end
        local viewportSize = camera.ViewportSize
        local center = Vector2.new(viewportSize.X / 2, viewportSize.Y / 2)
        local myRoot = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local function FindNearestInFOV()
            local bestPlayer = nil
            local bestDist2D = math.huge
            local bestDist3D = math.huge
            for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if player ~= localPlayer and getRole(player) == "survivor" then
                    local sChar = player.Character
                    if sChar then
                        local sHum = sChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                        local sTorso = Spear_GetTorsoCenter(sChar)
                        if sTorso and sHum and sHum.Health > 0 and sHum.Health > 50 and not sChar:GetAttribute("IsHooked") then
                            local dist3D = myRoot and (sTorso - myRoot.Position).Magnitude or 0
                            if dist3D <= CONFIG.spearSnaplineMaxDistance then
                                local screenPos, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(sTorso)
                                if onScreen then
                                    local dist2D = (Vector2.new(screenPos.X, screenPos.Y) - center).Magnitude
                                    if dist2D <= CONFIG.spearFovRadius then
                                        if dist2D < bestDist2D then
                                            bestDist2D = dist2D; bestDist3D = dist3D; bestPlayer = player
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            return bestPlayer, bestDist2D, bestDist3D
        end
        if STATE.spearIsHolding and not SNAPLINE.locked then
            local bestPlayer, _, bestDist3D = FindNearestInFOV()
            if bestPlayer then
                SNAPLINE.locked = true; SNAPLINE.lockedTarget = bestPlayer
                SNAPLINE.lockedPlayerName = bestPlayer.Name; SNAPLINE.currentNearestDist = bestDist3D
            end
        end
        if not STATE.spearIsHolding then
            SNAPLINE.locked = false; SNAPLINE.lockedTarget = nil; SNAPLINE.lockedPlayerName = ""
        end
        local lockedTargetValid = false
        local lockedTargetScreenPos = nil
        local lockedTargetDist3D = 0
        local lockedTargetTorsoPos = nil
        if SNAPLINE.locked and SNAPLINE.lockedTarget then
            local sChar = SNAPLINE.lockedTarget.Character
            if sChar then
                local sHum = sChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                local sTorso = Spear_GetTorsoCenter(sChar)
                if sTorso and sHum and sHum.Health > 50 and not sChar:GetAttribute("IsHooked") then
                    local screenPos, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(sTorso)
                    if onScreen then
                        local dist2D = (Vector2.new(screenPos.X, screenPos.Y) - center).Magnitude
                        if dist2D <= CONFIG.spearFovRadius * 1.15 then
                            lockedTargetScreenPos = Vector2.new(screenPos.X, screenPos.Y)
                            lockedTargetDist3D = myRoot and (sTorso - myRoot.Position).Magnitude or 0
                            lockedTargetTorsoPos = sTorso; lockedTargetValid = true
                            SNAPLINE.currentNearestDist = lockedTargetDist3D
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            if not lockedTargetValid then
                SNAPLINE.locked = false; SNAPLINE.lockedTarget = nil; SNAPLINE.lockedPlayerName = ""
            end
        end
        local guideTargetScreenPos = nil
        local guideTargetDist3D = 0
        local guideTargetTorsoPos = nil
        local guideTargetPlayer = nil
        if not SNAPLINE.locked then
            local bestPlayer, _, bestDist3D = FindNearestInFOV()
            if bestPlayer then
                local sTorso = Spear_GetTorsoCenter(bestPlayer.Character)
                local screenPos, _ = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(sTorso)
                guideTargetScreenPos = Vector2.new(screenPos.X, screenPos.Y)
                guideTargetDist3D = bestDist3D; guideTargetTorsoPos = sTorso
                guideTargetPlayer = bestPlayer; SNAPLINE.currentNearestDist = bestDist3D
            end
        end
        local isReleaseTime = false
        if STATE.spearIsHolding and STATE.spearHoldStartTime and myRoot then
            local targetTorso = lockedTargetTorsoPos or (guideTargetPlayer and Spear_GetTorsoCenter(guideTargetPlayer.Character))
            if targetTorso then
                local launchPos = camera.CFrame.Position
                local delta = targetTorso - launchPos
                local horizontal = Vector3.new(delta.X, 0, delta.Z)
                local horizontalDist = horizontal.Magnitude
                local heightDiff = delta.Y
                local gravity = Workspace.Gravity * (STATE.lastGravityMult or 1)
                local idealHold = Spear_GetIdealHoldTime(horizontalDist, heightDiff, gravity)
                if idealHold then
                    local currentHold = tick() - STATE.spearHoldStartTime
                    if currentHold >= idealHold then isReleaseTime = true end
                end
            end
        end
        local finalTargetPos = nil
        local finalTargetName = ""
        local finalDist3D = 0
        if SNAPLINE.locked and lockedTargetValid then
            finalTargetPos = lockedTargetScreenPos; finalTargetName = SNAPLINE.lockedPlayerName
            finalDist3D = lockedTargetDist3D
        elseif guideTargetScreenPos then
            finalTargetPos = guideTargetScreenPos; finalTargetName = guideTargetPlayer and guideTargetPlayer.Name or ""
            finalDist3D = guideTargetDist3D
        end
        local ok = pcall(function()
            if not STATE.SnapLineFrame or not STATE.SnapLineDot or not STATE.SnapLineTextLabel or not camera then
                if STATE.SnapLineFrame then STATE.SnapLineFrame.Visible = false end
                if STATE.SnapLineDot then STATE.SnapLineDot.Visible = false end
                if STATE.SnapLineTextLabel then STATE.SnapLineTextLabel.Visible = false end
                return
            end
            if not finalTargetPos then
                STATE.SnapLineFrame.Visible = false; STATE.SnapLineDot.Visible = false
                STATE.SnapLineTextLabel.Visible = false; SNAPLINE.currentNearestDist = math.huge
                return
            end
            local startPos = center; local endPos = finalTargetPos
            local deltaVec = endPos - startPos; local length = deltaVec.Magnitude
            if length < 2 then
                STATE.SnapLineFrame.Visible = false; STATE.SnapLineDot.Visible = false
                STATE.SnapLineTextLabel.Visible = false; return
            end
            local isLocked = isReleaseTime
            local lineColor = isLocked and Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 120) or Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 0)
            local lineAlpha = isLocked and 0.08 or 0.35
            STATE.SnapLineFrame.BackgroundColor3 = lineColor
            STATE.SnapLineFrame.BackgroundTransparency = lineAlpha
            STATE.SnapLineFrame.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(length, isLocked and 2 or 1)
            STATE.SnapLineFrame.Position = UDim2.fromOffset((startPos.X + endPos.X) * 0.5, (startPos.Y + endPos.Y) * 0.5)
            STATE.SnapLineFrame.Rotation = math.deg(math.atan2(deltaVec.Y, deltaVec.X))
            STATE.SnapLineFrame.Visible = true
            STATE.SnapLineDot.BackgroundColor3 = lineColor
            STATE.SnapLineDot.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(endPos.X, endPos.Y)
            STATE.SnapLineDot.Visible = true
            if CONFIG.spearSnaplineShowText and finalTargetName ~= "" then
                STATE.SnapLineTextLabel.Text = string.format("%s [%dm]", finalTargetName, math.floor(finalDist3D))
                STATE.SnapLineTextLabel.TextColor3 = lineColor
                STATE.SnapLineTextLabel.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(endPos.X, endPos.Y - 14)
                STATE.SnapLineTextLabel.Visible = true
            else
                STATE.SnapLineTextLabel.Visible = false
            end
        end)
        if not ok then
            if STATE.SnapLineFrame then STATE.SnapLineFrame.Visible = false end
            if STATE.SnapLineDot then STATE.SnapLineDot.Visible = false end
            if STATE.SnapLineTextLabel then STATE.SnapLineTextLabel.Visible = false end
        end
    end)

    RegisterTask("Spear_UpdateSpearSystem", 0, function()
        if STATE.SpearFOVCircle then
            STATE.SpearFOVCircle.Visible = STATE.spearFovCircleEnabled
            local targetSize = CONFIG.spearFovRadius * 2
            if STATE.SpearFOVCircle.Size.X.Offset ~= targetSize then
                STATE.SpearFOVCircle.Size = UDim2.new(0, targetSize, 0, targetSize)
            end
        end
        if not STATE.silentSpearEnabled then
            STATE.silentSpearTargetTorso = nil; STATE.silentSpearLookVector = nil
        end
        local myChar = localPlayer.Character
        local isSpearMode = myChar and myChar:GetAttribute("spearmode")
        if not STATE.spearIndicatorEnabled or not isSpearMode then
            if STATE.SpearIndicatorGui then STATE.SpearIndicatorGui.Enabled = false end
            if not STATE.silentSpearEnabled or not isSpearMode then return end
        end
        local myRoot = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not myRoot then return end
        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if not camera then return end
        if STATE.spearIndicatorEnabled and isSpearMode and STATE.SpearIndicatorGui then
            STATE.SpearIndicatorGui.Enabled = true
        end
        local bestPlayer = nil
        local bestDist2D = math.huge
        local bestDist3D = math.huge
        local bestInFov = false
        local viewportCenter = Vector2.new(camera.ViewportSize.X / 2, camera.ViewportSize.Y / 2)
        for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if player ~= localPlayer and getRole(player) == "survivor" then
                local sChar = player.Character
                if sChar then
                    local sHum = sChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    local sTorso = Spear_GetTorsoCenter(sChar)
                    local sRoot = sChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if sHum and sHum.Health > 50 and typeof(sTorso) == "Vector3" and sRoot and not sChar:GetAttribute("IsHooked") then
                        local dist3D = (sTorso - myRoot.Position).Magnitude
                        local screenPos, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(sTorso)
                        if onScreen then
                            local dist2D = (Vector2.new(screenPos.X, screenPos.Y) - viewportCenter).Magnitude
                            if dist2D <= CONFIG.spearFovRadius then
                                if dist2D < bestDist2D then
                                    bestDist2D = dist2D; bestDist3D = dist3D
                                    bestPlayer = player; bestInFov = true
                                end
                            elseif not bestInFov and dist3D < bestDist3D then
                                bestDist3D = dist3D; bestPlayer = player
                            end
                        elseif not bestInFov and dist3D < bestDist3D then
                            bestDist3D = dist3D; bestPlayer = player
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if bestPlayer and bestPlayer.Character then
            local sTorso = Spear_GetTorsoCenter(bestPlayer.Character)
            if sTorso then bestInFov = Spear_IsTargetInFOV(camera, sTorso, CONFIG.spearFovRadius) end
        end
        if STATE.spearIndicatorEnabled and isSpearMode then
            if not bestPlayer or not bestPlayer.Character then
                STATE.SpearStatusText.Text = "NO TARGET"
                STATE.SpearStatusText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
                STATE.SpearAccentBar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 80, 80)
                STATE.SpearInfoText.Text = "Aim to Survivor"; return
            end
            local sTorso = Spear_GetTorsoCenter(bestPlayer.Character)
            local launchPos = camera.CFrame.Position
            local delta = sTorso - launchPos
            local horizontal = Vector3.new(delta.X, 0, delta.Z)
            local horizontalDist = horizontal.Magnitude
            local heightDiff = delta.Y
            local dist3D = delta.Magnitude
            local gravity = Workspace.Gravity * (STATE.lastGravityMult or 1)
            local idealHold = Spear_GetIdealHoldTime(horizontalDist, heightDiff, gravity)
            local nameStr = bestPlayer.Name
            local distStr = string.format("%.0f", dist3D)
            if not idealHold then
                STATE.SpearStatusText.Text = "OUT OF RANGE"
                STATE.SpearStatusText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 80)
                STATE.SpearAccentBar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 50)
                STATE.SpearInfoText.Text = string.format("%s | %s studs", nameStr, distStr)
            elseif not bestInFov then
                STATE.SpearStatusText.Text = "AIM AT TARGET"
                STATE.SpearStatusText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 150, 150)
                STATE.SpearAccentBar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 100)
                local screenPos, onScreen = camera:WorldToViewportPoint(sTorso)
                local ctr = Vector2.new(camera.ViewportSize.X / 2, camera.ViewportSize.Y / 2)
                local dist2D = (Vector2.new(screenPos.X, screenPos.Y) - ctr).Magnitude
                local fovDist = string.format("%.0f", dist2D - CONFIG.spearFovRadius)
                if not onScreen then
                    STATE.SpearInfoText.Text = "Target di belakang kamera"
                else
                    STATE.SpearInfoText.Text = string.format("Masuk FOV: %s px lagi", fovDist)
                end
            elseif not STATE.spearIsHolding then
                STATE.SpearStatusText.Text = "READY"
                STATE.SpearStatusText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                STATE.SpearAccentBar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 200)
                STATE.SpearInfoText.Text = string.format("%s | %s studs | Hold: %ss", nameStr, distStr, string.format("%.2f", idealHold))
            else
                local currentHold = tick() - STATE.spearHoldStartTime
                local currStr = string.format("%.2f", currentHold)
                local idealStr = string.format("%.2f", idealHold)
                local stillInFov = Spear_IsTargetInFOV(camera, sTorso, CONFIG.spearFovRadius)
                if not stillInFov then
                    STATE.SpearStatusText.Text = "AIM AT TARGET"
                    STATE.SpearStatusText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 150, 150)
                    STATE.SpearAccentBar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 100)
                    STATE.SpearInfoText.Text = "Target keluar FOV!"
                elseif currentHold >= idealHold then
                    local stabNow = STATE.spearLastStab
                    if stabNow ~= nil and stabNow < 0.35 then
                        STATE.SpearStatusText.Text = "UNSTABLE!"
                        STATE.SpearStatusText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 150, 50)
                        STATE.SpearAccentBar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 150, 50)
                        STATE.SpearInfoText.Text = "Target bermanuver, tahan dulu..."
                    else
                        STATE.SpearStatusText.Text = "RELEASE!"
                        STATE.SpearStatusText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(80, 255, 120)
                        STATE.SpearAccentBar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 255, 100)
                        STATE.SpearInfoText.Text = string.format("Hold: %ss | Ideal: %ss", currStr, idealStr)
                    end
                else
                    STATE.SpearStatusText.Text = "HOLD..."
                    STATE.SpearStatusText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 100)
                    STATE.SpearAccentBar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 50)
                    STATE.SpearInfoText.Text = string.format("Hold: %ss | Ideal: %ss", currStr, idealStr)
                end
            end
        end
        if STATE.silentSpearEnabled and isSpearMode and bestPlayer and bestPlayer.Character then
            local sTorso = Spear_GetTorsoCenter(bestPlayer.Character)
            local sRoot = bestPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if typeof(sTorso) == "Vector3" and sRoot then
                local inFov = Spear_IsTargetInFOV(camera, sTorso, CONFIG.spearFovRadius)
                if inFov then
                    local launchPos = camera.CFrame.Position
                    local rawVec = sTorso - launchPos
                    local mag = rawVec.Magnitude
                    if mag > 0.1 then
                        local currentSpeed = 142.5
                        if STATE.spearIsHolding and STATE.spearHoldStartTime then
                            local ht = tick() - STATE.spearHoldStartTime
                            if ht >= 2 then currentSpeed = 165
                            elseif ht >= 1 then currentSpeed = 142.5
                            else currentSpeed = math.max(23, ht * 150) end
                        end
                        local rawSpeed = currentSpeed
                        currentSpeed = rawSpeed * (tonumber(STATE.spearSpeedCal) or 1.0)
                        local gravity = Workspace.Gravity * (STATE.lastGravityMult or 1)
                        local horizontalVel, stabNow = nil, 1
                        if CONFIG.spearPrediction then
                            horizontalVel, stabNow = Spear_EstVel(SpearVelSt, bestPlayer.Character, sTorso)
                        else
                            SpearVelSt.char, SpearVelSt.hist, SpearVelSt.sm = nil, {}, Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
                            SpearVelSt.turn = 0
                            local vel = sRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity
                            horizontalVel = Vector3.new(vel.X, 0, vel.Z)
                            if horizontalVel.Magnitude > 65 then horizontalVel = horizontalVel.Unit * 65 end
                        end
                        STATE.silentSpearTargetVel = horizontalVel
                        STATE.spearLastStab = math.clamp(stabNow or 1, 0, 1)
                        local leadScale = 1
                        if CONFIG.spearDamping then leadScale = 0.55 + 0.45 * STATE.spearLastStab end
                        local aimDir, travelTime = Spear_SolveBallisticDirection(launchPos, sTorso, currentSpeed, gravity)
                        if aimDir and travelTime then
                            local pingExtra = Spear_PingSec() * (tonumber(CONFIG.spearPingLead) or 1.0)
                            local lead = STATE.silentSpearTargetVel * ((travelTime + pingExtra) * leadScale)
                            local maxLead = math.max(mag * 0.4, 4)
                            if lead.Magnitude > maxLead then lead = lead.Unit * maxLead end
                            local predictedPos = sTorso + lead
                            local finalDir, finalTime = Spear_SolveBallisticDirection(launchPos, predictedPos, currentSpeed, gravity)
                            if finalDir and finalTime and finalTime > 0.35 then
                                local leadB = STATE.silentSpearTargetVel * ((finalTime + pingExtra) * leadScale)
                                if leadB.Magnitude > maxLead then leadB = leadB.Unit * maxLead end
                                local predictedPos2 = sTorso + leadB
                                local finalDir2 = Spear_SolveBallisticDirection(launchPos, predictedPos2, currentSpeed, gravity)
                                if finalDir2 then finalDir, predictedPos = finalDir2, predictedPos2 end
                            end
                            if finalDir then
                                STATE.silentSpearTargetTorso = predictedPos; STATE.silentSpearLookVector = finalDir
                                STATE.spearLastShot = { dist = mag, speed = currentSpeed, raw = rawSpeed, tof = travelTime, lead = lead.Magnitude, ping = pingExtra, t = tick(), grav = (STATE.lastGravityMult or 1), stab = STATE.spearLastStab }
                            else
                                STATE.silentSpearTargetTorso = nil; STATE.silentSpearLookVector = nil
                                STATE.spearLastShot = nil
                            end
                        else
                            STATE.silentSpearTargetTorso = nil; STATE.silentSpearLookVector = nil
                        end
                    else
                        STATE.silentSpearTargetTorso = nil; STATE.silentSpearLookVector = nil
                    end
                else
                    STATE.silentSpearTargetTorso = nil; STATE.silentSpearLookVector = nil
                end
            else
                STATE.silentSpearTargetTorso = nil; STATE.silentSpearLookVector = nil
            end
        else
            STATE.silentSpearTargetTorso = nil; STATE.silentSpearLookVector = nil
        end
    end)
end

-- ============================================================
--  JERK OFF SYSTEM
-- ============================================================
local StartJerk, StopJerk
do
    local jerkTool = nil
    local jerkLoopTask = nil
    local jerkAnim = nil
    local track = nil
    local jorkin = false

    local function stopTomfoolery()
        jorkin = false
        if track then
            pcall(function() track:Stop() end)
            track = nil
        end
    end

    function StopJerk()
        stopTomfoolery()
        if jerkLoopTask then
            pcall(function() task.cancel(jerkLoopTask) end)
            jerkLoopTask = nil
        end
        if jerkTool then
            pcall(function() jerkTool:Destroy() end)
            jerkTool = nil
        end
    end

    function StartJerk()
        StopJerk()
        
        local char = localPlayer.Character
        if not char then return end
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local backpack = localPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
        if not hum or not backpack then return end

        jerkTool = Instance.new("Tool")
        jerkTool.Name = "Jerk Off"
        jerkTool.ToolTip = 'in the stripped club. straight up "jorking it" . and by "it" , haha, well. let\'s justr say. My peanits.'
        jerkTool.RequiresHandle = false
        jerkTool.Parent = backpack

        jerkTool.Equipped:Connect(function() jorkin = true end)
        jerkTool.Unequipped:Connect(stopTomfoolery)
        hum.Died:Connect(stopTomfoolery)

        jerkLoopTask = task.spawn(function()
            while task.wait() do
                if not jorkin then continue end

                local currentChar = localPlayer.Character
                local currentHum = currentChar and currentChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if not currentHum then continue end

                local isR15 = currentHum.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15

                if not track then
                    jerkAnim = Instance.new("Animation")
                    jerkAnim.AnimationId = not isR15 and "rbxassetid://72042024" or "rbxassetid://698251653"
                    pcall(function()
                        track = currentHum:LoadAnimation(jerkAnim)
                    end)
                end

                if track then
                    pcall(function()
                        track:Play()
                        track:AdjustSpeed(isR15 and 0.7 or 0.65)
                        track.TimePosition = 0.6
                    end)
                    
                    task.wait(0.1)
                    
                    while track and track.TimePosition < (not isR15 and 0.65 or 0.7) do 
                        task.wait(0.1) 
                    end
                    
                    if track then
                        pcall(function() track:Stop() end)
                        track = nil
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end

-- ============================================================
--  NOCLIP SYSTEM
-- ============================================================
local StartNoclip, StopNoclip
do
    local noclipConn = nil
    local noclipOrig = {}
    local NC_GEN_STORE = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
    NC_GEN_STORE.BolongNoclipGen = (tonumber(NC_GEN_STORE.BolongNoclipGen) or 0) + 1
    local NC_GEN = NC_GEN_STORE.BolongNoclipGen

    local function setNoclipCollide(char, collideOn)
        if not char then return end
        if not noclipOrig[char] then noclipOrig[char] = {} end
        local rec = noclipOrig[char]
        if collideOn then
            for part, _ in pairs(rec) do
                if part and part.Parent then
                    pcall(function() part.CanCollide = true end)
                end
            end
            noclipOrig[char] = {}
        else
            for _, v in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
                if v:IsA("BasePart") and rec[v] == nil then
                    local ok, cur = pcall(function() return v.CanCollide end)
                    if ok and cur then
                        rec[v] = true
                        pcall(function() v.CanCollide = false end)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    local function sweepNoclipDead()
        local stale = {}
        for char, _ in pairs(noclipOrig) do
            if not char or not char.Parent then stale[#stale + 1] = char end
        end
        for _, char in ipairs(stale) do noclipOrig[char] = nil end
    end

    function StartNoclip()
        StopNoclip()
        STATE.noclipOn = true
        noclipConn = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
            if NC_GEN ~= NC_GEN_STORE.BolongNoclipGen then
                pcall(function() noclipConn:Disconnect() end)
                noclipConn = nil
                return
            end
            if not STATE.noclipOn then return end
            local char = localPlayer.Character
            if char then setNoclipCollide(char, false) end
        end)
    end

    function StopNoclip()
        STATE.noclipOn = false
        if noclipConn then
            pcall(function() noclipConn:Disconnect() end)
            noclipConn = nil
        end
        sweepNoclipDead()
        local char = localPlayer.Character
        if char then setNoclipCollide(char, true) end
    end

    localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        sweepNoclipDead()
    end)

    sweepNoclipDead()
    pcall(function()
        local char = localPlayer.Character
        if char then setNoclipCollide(char, true) end
    end)
end

-- ============================================================
--  FLY GUI SYSTEM
-- ============================================================
local StartFlyGui, StopFlyGui
do
    local FLY_URL = "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/b00l08g87b/trbg/refs/heads/main/FlyGuiV3.lua"

    local function restoreFlyPhysics()
        local char = localPlayer.Character
        if not char then return end
        local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if hrp then
            pcall(function() hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero end)
        end
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then
            pcall(function() hum.PlatformStand = false end)
            for _, st in ipairs(Enum.HumanoidStateType:GetEnumItems()) do
                if st ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.None then
                    pcall(function() hum:SetStateEnabled(st, true) end)
                end
            end
        end
        for _, desc in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if desc:IsA("BodyGyro") or desc:IsA("BodyVelocity")
                or desc:IsA("BodyPosition") or desc:IsA("BodyForce") then
                pcall(function() desc:Destroy() end)
            end
        end
        local animate = char:FindFirstChild("Animate")
        if animate then pcall(function() animate.Disabled = false end) end
    end

    function StartFlyGui()
        pcall(function()
            local old = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("main")
            if old then old:Destroy() end
        end)
        local ok, err = pcall(function()
            loadstring(game:HttpGet(FLY_URL))()
        end)
        if not ok then
            Notify("Fly GUI", "Gagal memuat: " .. tostring(err), 2)
        end
    end

    function StopFlyGui()
        pcall(function()
            local g = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
            g.nowe = false
            g.tpwalking = false
        end)
        pcall(function()
            local gui = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("main")
            if gui then gui:Destroy() end
        end)
        restoreFlyPhysics()
    end

    pcall(function()
        local g = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
        g.nowe = false
        g.tpwalking = false
    end)
end

-- ============================================================
--  CENTRALIZED PLAYER EVENT HANDLERS
-- ============================================================
do
    local function OnPlayerCharacterAdded(player, char)
        if player == localPlayer then return end
        if not char or not char.Parent or player.Character ~= char then return end
        AttachESPToChar(player, char)
        AttachOutlineToChar(player, char)
        if STATE.autoParryEnabled or (AP_State and AP_State.Enabled) then
            if AP_HookKiller then AP_HookKiller(player, char) end
        end
    end

    local function OnPlayerAdded(player)
        if player == localPlayer then return end
        refreshRole(player)
        CreatePlayerESP(player)
        CreatePlayerOutline(player)
        player:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Team"):Connect(function()
            refreshRole(player)
            task.wait(0.2)
            if STATE.autoParryEnabled or (AP_State and AP_State.Enabled) then
                if AP_HookKiller then AP_HookKiller(player) end
            end
        end)
        if player.Character then
            task.spawn(function() OnPlayerCharacterAdded(player, player.Character) end)
        end
        player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
            task.spawn(function() OnPlayerCharacterAdded(player, char) end)
        end)
    end

    local function OnPlayerRemoving(player)
    end

    Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(OnPlayerAdded)
    Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(OnPlayerRemoving)

    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if player ~= localPlayer then OnPlayerAdded(player) end
    end

    -- ============================================================
