    --  LOCAL PLAYER CHARACTER HANDLER
    -- ============================================================
    local function OnLocalCharacterAdded(char)
        STATE.HoldLockActive = false; STATE.godLastHP = nil
        STATE.CV_HoldingPC = false; STATE.CV_HoldingMobile = false
        CV_ClearLock()
        if STATE.CV_Enabled then task.defer(CV_ScanAttackButtons) end
        task.spawn(SetupMovementForChar, char)
        if STATE.godEnabled then
            task.wait(0.3); StopGodMode(); StartGodMode()
        end
        if CONFIG.lockFovEnabled then
            task.wait(0.1); STATE.originalFOV = nil; applyCustomFov(CONFIG.customFovValue)
        end
        if CONFIG.cameraZoomEnabled then
            task.wait(0.1); STATE.originalMaxZoom = nil; applyCameraZoom(CONFIG.cameraZoomValue)
        end
        if CONFIG.genBoostEnabled then
            task.delay(1, function()
                pcall(DestroyGenBoostButton)
                pcall(CreateGenBoostButton)
            end)
        end
        if getRole(localPlayer) ~= "killer" and FP_ReArm then
            task.delay(1, function()
                if getRole(localPlayer) ~= "killer" then pcall(FP_ReArm) end
            end)
        end
    end

    if localPlayer.Character then
        task.spawn(OnLocalCharacterAdded, localPlayer.Character)
    end
    localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
        task.spawn(OnLocalCharacterAdded, char)
    end)
end

-- ============================================================
--  FAST VAULT & FLOWSTATE SYSTEM
-- ============================================================
do
    local fastVaultRemote    = nil
    local vaultCompletePart1 = nil
    local function FindVaultRemotes()
        local window = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes") and ReplicatedStorage.Remotes:FindFirstChild("Window")
        if not window then return end
        fastVaultRemote    = window:FindFirstChild("fastvault")
        vaultCompletePart1 = window:FindFirstChild("VaultCompleteEventpart1")
    end
    pcall(FindVaultRemotes)

    -- ============================================================
    --  FLOWSTATE COOLDOWN
    -- ============================================================
    local function TriggerFlowstateCooldown()
        if not CONFIG.flowstateEnabled then return end
        local now = tick()
        if now < STATE.flowstateCooldownUntil then return end
        STATE.flowstateActiveUntil = now + 0.5
        STATE.flowstateCooldownUntil = now + (CONFIG.flowstateCooldown or 15)
    end

    local function SetupVaultCache()
        local vaultCacheReady = false
        local function IsVaultDesc(desc)
            return desc:IsA("BasePart") and (desc.Name == "VaultTrigger" or desc.Name == "VaultPoint")
        end
        local function AddVaultPoint(desc)
            if not IsVaultDesc(desc) then return end
            for _, vp in ipairs(STATE.fastVaultPoints) do
                if vp == desc then return end
            end
            table.insert(STATE.fastVaultPoints, desc)
        end
        local function RemoveVaultPoint(desc)
            for i, vp in ipairs(STATE.fastVaultPoints) do
                if vp == desc then table.remove(STATE.fastVaultPoints, i) break end
            end
        end
        if vaultCacheReady then return end
        vaultCacheReady = true
        for _, tagged in ipairs(CollectionService:GetTagged("VaultPoint")) do
            AddVaultPoint(tagged)
        end
        Workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(AddVaultPoint)
        Workspace.DescendantRemoving:Connect(RemoveVaultPoint)
    end
    SetupVaultCache()

    local function FindNearestVault(pos, radius)
        local nearest, best = nil, radius
        for _, vp in ipairs(STATE.fastVaultPoints) do
            if vp and vp.Parent then
                local d = (vp.Position - pos).Magnitude
                if d < best then
                    best = d
                    nearest = vp
                end
            end
        end
        return nearest
    end

    -- ============================================================
    --  FAST VAULT: paksa sprint flag + dorong velocity
    -- ============================================================
    RegisterTask("FastVault", 0.05, function()
        if not CONFIG.alwaysFastVault then return end
        if getRole(localPlayer) == "killer" then return end

        local char = localPlayer.Character
        local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not char or not root then return end

        if FindNearestVault(root.Position, 6) then
            pcall(function()
                char:SetAttribute("Sprinting", true)
                char:SetAttribute("IsRunning", true)
            end)
        end
    end)

    UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
        if not CONFIG.alwaysFastVault then return end
        if getRole(localPlayer) == "killer" then return end
        if gameProcessed then return end

        local key = input.KeyCode
        if key ~= Enum.KeyCode.Space and key ~= Enum.KeyCode.E then return end

        local char = localPlayer.Character
        local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not char or not root then return end

        local vp = FindNearestVault(root.Position, 9)
        if not vp then return end

        local dir = vp.Position - root.Position
        dir = Vector3.new(dir.X, 0, dir.Z)
        if dir.Magnitude > 0.05 then
            dir = dir.Unit
        else
            dir = Vector3.new(root.CFrame.LookVector.X, 0, root.CFrame.LookVector.Z).Unit
        end

        local vel = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        local speed = Vector3.new(vel.X, 0, vel.Z).Magnitude
        if speed < 13 then
            pcall(function()
                char:SetAttribute("Sprinting", true)
                char:SetAttribute("IsRunning", true)
            end)
            root.CFrame = CFrame.new(root.Position, root.Position + dir)
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = dir * 22
        end
    end)

    -- ============================================================
    --  FAST VAULT: replace startVault di module SurvivorActions
    -- ============================================================
    local ok, SurvivorActions = pcall(function()
        local modules = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Modules", true)
        local sa = modules and modules:FindFirstChild("SurvivorActions", true)
        return sa and require(sa)
    end)
    if ok and SurvivorActions and SurvivorActions.startVault then
        local origStartVault = SurvivorActions.startVault
        SurvivorActions.startVault = newcclosure(function(controller, vaultPoint)
            TriggerFlowstateCooldown()
            pcall(FP_MarkVault)
            if not CONFIG.alwaysFastVault then
                return origStartVault(controller, vaultPoint)
            end
            pcall(function()
                local char = controller and controller.character
                if char then
                    char:SetAttribute("Sprinting", true)
                    char:SetAttribute("IsRunning", true)
                    local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    if root then
                        local vp = vaultPoint and (vaultPoint.Position or (vaultPoint:IsA("Model") and vaultPoint.PrimaryPart and vaultPoint.PrimaryPart.Position))
                        if vp then
                            local dir = vp - root.Position
                            dir = Vector3.new(dir.X, 0, dir.Z)
                            if dir.Magnitude > 0.05 then
                                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = dir.Unit * 22
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end)
            local results = { origStartVault(controller, vaultPoint) }
            if fastVaultRemote then
                pcall(function() fastVaultRemote:FireServer(localPlayer) end)
            end
            if vaultCompletePart1 then
                pcall(function() vaultCompletePart1:FireServer() end)
            end
            return unpack(results)
        end)
    end

    -- ============================================================
    --  FAST VAULT: override _isFacingStraightEnough
    -- ============================================================
    pcall(function()
        local modules = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Modules", true)
        local sac = modules and modules:FindFirstChild("SurvivorAnimationsController", true)
        local animCtrl = sac and require(sac)
        if animCtrl and animCtrl._isFacingStraightEnough then
            local origFacing = animCtrl._isFacingStraightEnough
            animCtrl._isFacingStraightEnough = newcclosure(function(...)
                if not CONFIG.alwaysFastVault then
                    return origFacing(...)
                end
                return true
            end)
        end
    end)

    -- ============================================================
    --  KILLER PERKS INFO
    -- ============================================================
    local function SetupKillerPerksInfo()
        local KPI_STATE = {
        gui      = nil,
        panel    = nil,
        rows     = nil,
        taskName = "KillerPerksInfo",
        lastKey  = nil,
        dragConn = {},
        perkSet  = {},
        cacheBuilt = false,
        toggleRef  = nil,
        taskRegistered = false,
    }

    local KPI_PERK_SEED = {
        "Predator", "Eternal Torment", "Play With Your Food", "Terror Spread",
        "Sloppy Mess", "Resentment Clinger", "Echo Location", "Enhanced Senses",
        "Next in Line", "Corrupted Path", "Abyssal Covenant", "Shadow Trace",
        "Piercing Reverie", "Blood Between Worlds", "Echo Of The Void",
        "Excitement", "Brutal Strength", "Offscreen Scare", "Hard Swing",
        "Combo Streak", "Crackdown", "Sustenance", "Foundation Staff",
        "Desire For The Living", "Deep Wound", "All Seeing Eye", "Touch Of Death",
        "Murderous Acrobatics", "Eyes Of Hell", "Stage Fright",
        "Trade Off", "Containment", "King's Scourge",
    }

    local function KPI_Normalize(name)
        if not name then return "" end
        return tostring(name):lower():gsub("[^%a%d]", ""):gsub("excitment", "excitement")
    end

    local KPI_PERK_BLOCKED = {
        exposuretherapy = true,
    }

    local function KPI_AddPerk(name)
        if not name or name == "" then return end
        local clean = KPI_Normalize(name)
        if clean == "" then return end
        if KPI_PERK_BLOCKED[clean] then return end
        KPI_STATE.perkSet[clean] = name
    end

    local function KPI_BuildCache()
        if KPI_STATE.cacheBuilt then return end
        KPI_STATE.cacheBuilt = true
        for _, perk in ipairs(KPI_PERK_SEED) do
            KPI_AddPerk(perk)
        end
        pcall(function()
            local folders = {
                ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Killers"),
                ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("ShopKillers"),
                ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Perks"),
                ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("KillerPerks"),
            }
            for _, folder in ipairs(folders) do
                if folder then
                    for _, child in ipairs(folder:GetChildren()) do
                        if folder.Name == "Perks" or folder.Name == "KillerPerks" then
                            KPI_AddPerk(child.Name)
                        else
                            local sub = child:FindFirstChild("Perks")
                            if sub then
                                for _, s in ipairs(sub:GetChildren()) do
                                    KPI_AddPerk(s.Name)
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    local function KPI_FindKiller()
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if getRole(p) == "killer" then
                return p
            end
        end
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            local ok, team = pcall(function() return p.Team and p.Team.Name or "" end)
            if ok and tostring(team):lower():find("killer") then
                return p
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function KPI_MatchFromSource(clean, results)
        if clean == "" then return end
        for perkClean, perkName in pairs(KPI_STATE.perkSet) do
            if clean == perkClean or clean:find(perkClean, 1, true) or perkClean:find(clean, 1, true) then
                if not results[perkName] then
                    results[perkName] = true
                end
                return
            end
        end
    end

    local function KPI_ScanPerks(killer)
        local results = {}
        local function feed(name)
            KPI_MatchFromSource(KPI_Normalize(name), results)
        end
        local char = killer.Character

        pcall(function()
            for _, v in pairs(killer:GetAttributes()) do
                feed(tostring(v))
            end
            for name in pairs(killer:GetAttributes()) do
                feed(name)
            end
        end)

        pcall(function()
            local perksFolder = killer:FindFirstChild("EquippedPerks") or killer:FindFirstChild("Perks")
            if perksFolder then
                for _, child in ipairs(perksFolder:GetChildren()) do
                    feed(child.Name)
                    if child:IsA("ValueBase") and type(child.Value) == "string" then
                        feed(child.Value)
                    end
                end
            end
        end)

        if char then
            pcall(function()
                for _, v in pairs(char:GetAttributes()) do
                    feed(tostring(v))
                end
                for name in pairs(char:GetAttributes()) do
                    feed(name)
                end
            end)
            pcall(function()
                for _, child in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                    feed(child.Name)
                end
            end)
        end
        return results
    end

    local function KPI_GetGuiParent()
        local ok, parent = pcall(function()
            if gethui then return gethui() end
            return nil
        end)
        if ok and parent then return parent end
        local ok2, core = pcall(function() return game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
        if ok2 and core then return core end
        return PlayerGui
    end

    local function KPI_CreateGui()
        local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
        sg.Name = "BolongKillerPerksInfo"
        sg.ResetOnSpawn = false
        sg.IgnoreGuiInset = true
        sg.DisplayOrder = 48
        sg.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
        sg.Parent = KPI_GetGuiParent()

        local panel = Instance.new("Frame")
        panel.Name = "Panel"
        panel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 14)
        panel.BackgroundTransparency = 0.06
        panel.BorderSizePixel = 0
        panel.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(220, 0)
        panel.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        panel.Position = STATE.kpiPos or UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 12)
        panel.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
        panel.Active = true
        panel.Parent = sg

        local corner = Instance.new("UICorner")
        corner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
        corner.Parent = panel

        local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 75)
        stroke.Thickness = 1
        stroke.Parent = panel

        local padding = Instance.new("UIPadding")
        padding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8)
        padding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8)
        padding.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 6)
        padding.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 6)
        padding.Parent = panel

        local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 3)
        layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        layout.Parent = panel

        local header = Instance.new("Frame")
        header.Name = "Header"
        header.LayoutOrder = 1
        header.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        header.BorderSizePixel = 0
        header.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16)
        header.Parent = panel

        local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        title.Name = "Title"
        title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        title.BorderSizePixel = 0
        title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 16)
        title.Text = "KILLER"
        title.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        title.TextSize = 11
        title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        title.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
        title.RichText = true
        title.Parent = header

        local minBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        minBtn.Name = "Minimize"
        minBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        minBtn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        minBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16)
        minBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 0)
        minBtn.Text = "-"
        minBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(160, 162, 170)
        minBtn.TextSize = 13
        minBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        minBtn.Parent = header

        minBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            KPI_STATE.minimized = not KPI_STATE.minimized
            if KPI_STATE.rows then
                KPI_STATE.rows.Visible = not KPI_STATE.minimized
            end
            local div = panel:FindFirstChild("Divider")
            if div then div.Visible = not KPI_STATE.minimized end
        end)

        local divider = Instance.new("Frame")
        divider.Name = "Divider"
        divider.LayoutOrder = 1.5
        divider.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 75)
        divider.BackgroundTransparency = 0.4
        divider.BorderSizePixel = 0
        divider.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 1)
        divider.Parent = panel

        local rows = Instance.new("Frame")
        rows.Name = "Rows"
        rows.LayoutOrder = 3
        rows.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        rows.BorderSizePixel = 0
        rows.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
        rows.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        rows.Parent = panel

        local rowsLayout = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
        rowsLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 2)
        rowsLayout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        rowsLayout.Parent = rows

        local dragging, dragStart, startPos
        header.Active = true
        header.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = true
                dragStart = input.Position
                startPos = panel.Position
            end
        end)
        header.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
            if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                local delta = input.Position - dragStart
                panel.Position = UDim2.new(1, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, 0, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
            end
        end)
        header.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
            if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = false
                STATE.kpiPos = panel.Position
            end
        end)

        KPI_STATE.gui = sg
        KPI_STATE.panel = panel
        KPI_STATE.rows = rows
    end

    local function KPI_UpdateDisplay()
        if not KPI_STATE.gui or not KPI_STATE.gui.Parent then return end

        local killer = KPI_FindKiller()
        local key, text
        if not killer then
            key = "none"
            text = "<font color=\"rgb(160,162,170)\">Tidak ada Killer.</font>"
        else
            local perks = KPI_ScanPerks(killer)
            local perkNames = {}
            for name in pairs(perks) do
                table.insert(perkNames, name)
            end
            table.sort(perkNames)
            key = killer.Name .. "|" .. table.concat(perkNames, ",")
            local lines = {}
            for _, name in ipairs(perkNames) do
                table.insert(lines, "<font color=\"rgb(200,200,210)\">- " .. name .. "</font>")
            end
            if #lines == 0 then
                text = "<font color=\"rgb(160,162,170)\">Tidak ada perk terdeteksi.</font>"
            else
                text = table.concat(lines, "\n")
            end
            key = key .. "#" .. tostring(killer.Character and killer.Character.Name or "")
        end

        if key == KPI_STATE.lastKey then return end
        KPI_STATE.lastKey = key

        local title = KPI_STATE.panel and KPI_STATE.panel:FindFirstChild("Header") and KPI_STATE.panel:FindFirstChild("Header"):FindFirstChild("Title")
        if title then
            title.Text = killer and ("KILLER <font color=\"rgb(255,80,80)\">•</font> <font color=\"rgb(252,235,229)\">" .. killer.Name .. "</font>") or "KILLER"
        end

        if KPI_STATE.rows then
            KPI_STATE.rows.Visible = not KPI_STATE.minimized
            for _, child in ipairs(KPI_STATE.rows:GetChildren()) do
                if child:IsA("TextLabel") then
                    child:Destroy()
                end
            end
            local order = 1
            for line in string.gmatch(text, "[^\n]+") do
                local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                label.BorderSizePixel = 0
                label.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.XY
                label.LayoutOrder = order
                order = order + 1
                label.Text = line
                label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 210)
                label.TextSize = 12
                label.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
                label.RichText = true
                label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
                label.Parent = KPI_STATE.rows
            end
        end
    end

    function StartKillerPerksInfo()
        if not CONFIG.killerPerksInfoEnabled then return end
        KPI_BuildCache()
        if KPI_STATE.gui and KPI_STATE.gui.Parent then
            KPI_STATE.gui.Enabled = true
        else
            KPI_CreateGui()
        end
        KPI_UpdateDisplay()
        if not KPI_STATE.taskRegistered then
            RegisterTask(KPI_STATE.taskName, 2, function()
                if not CONFIG.killerPerksInfoEnabled then return end
                KPI_UpdateDisplay()
            end)
            KPI_STATE.taskRegistered = true
        end
    end

    function StopKillerPerksInfo()
        if KPI_STATE.gui then
            pcall(function() KPI_STATE.gui:Destroy() end)
            KPI_STATE.gui = nil
            KPI_STATE.panel = nil
            KPI_STATE.rows = nil
        end
        KPI_STATE.lastKey = nil
    end

    function SetKPI_ToggleRef(toggle)
        KPI_STATE.toggleRef = toggle
    end
    end
    SetupKillerPerksInfo()

    -- ============================================================
    --  FLOWSTATE: paksa atribut Flowstate + cooldown
    -- ============================================================
    RegisterTask("Flowstate", 0.1, function()
        if not CONFIG.flowstateEnabled then return end
        if getRole(localPlayer) == "killer" then return end

        local char = localPlayer.Character
        local now = tick()
        local active = not (now < STATE.flowstateCooldownUntil) or (now < STATE.flowstateActiveUntil)

        local targets = { localPlayer, char }
        local climbNames = { "climb_obsessing", "climb_collisoning", "climb_collisioning", "climb_colliding" }
        local climbing = false
        for _, name in ipairs(climbNames) do
            local climbModel = Workspace:FindFirstChild(name)
            if climbModel then
                local sub = climbModel:FindFirstChild(localPlayer.Name)
                if sub and sub:IsA("Model") then
                    climbing = true
                    table.insert(targets, sub)
                end
            end
        end
        if climbing then TriggerFlowstateCooldown() end

        for _, target in ipairs(targets) do
            if target then
                local cur = target:GetAttribute("Flowstate")
                if cur ~= active then
                    pcall(function() target:SetAttribute("Flowstate", active) end)
                end
            end
        end
    end)

    -- ============================================================
    --  FLOWSTATE UI: tampilan cooldown
    -- ============================================================
    RegisterTask("FlowstateUI", 0.2, function()
        local pg = localPlayer:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")
        if not pg then return end

        local gui = pg:FindFirstChild("FlowstateUIGui")
        local show = CONFIG.flowstateEnabled and not CONFIG.hideFlowstateUI

        if not show then
            if gui then pcall(function() gui:Destroy() end) end
            return
        end

        if not gui then
            gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
            gui.Name = "FlowstateUIGui"
            gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
            gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
            gui.Parent = pg

            local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
            label.Name = "FlowstateCooldownLabel"
            label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
            label.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
            label.TextScaled = false
            label.TextSize = 16
            label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
            label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            label.Size = UDim2.new(0, 220, 0, 22)
            label.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 55)
            label.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
            label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
            label.Parent = gui
        end

        local label = gui:FindFirstChild("FlowstateCooldownLabel")
        if label then
            local now = tick()
            if now < STATE.flowstateCooldownUntil then
                label.Text = "FLOWSTATE: " .. tostring(math.ceil(STATE.flowstateCooldownUntil - now)) .. "s"
                label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 60, 60)
            else
                label.Text = "FLOWSTATE: READY"
                label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 120)
            end
        end
    end)
end

-- ============================================================
--  FAKE PERKS
-- ============================================================
do
    STATE.fpBuffs = STATE.fpBuffs or {}
    STATE.fpConns = STATE.fpConns or {}
    STATE.fpOn = STATE.fpOn or {}
    if STATE.fpCooldown == nil then STATE.fpCooldown = 10 end
    if STATE.fpLastEnd == nil then STATE.fpLastEnd = 0 end
    if STATE.fpMinFallTime == nil then STATE.fpMinFallTime = 0.25 end
    if STATE.fpMinFallDrop == nil then STATE.fpMinFallDrop = 3 end
    if STATE.fpVaultWindow == nil then STATE.fpVaultWindow = 1.0 end

    local function fpChar()
        return localPlayer.Character
    end

    local function fpTotalBuff()
        local total = 0
        local now = tick()
        for _, b in pairs(STATE.fpBuffs or {}) do
            if now < b.endTime then total = total + b.amt end
        end
        return total
    end

    local function fpApply()
        if getRole(localPlayer) == "killer" then return end
        local char = fpChar()
        local total = fpTotalBuff()
        if char then
            pcall(function()
                char:SetAttribute("speedboost", total > 0 and (1 + total / 14) or 1)
            end)
        end
    end

    function FP_TryBuff(name, amt, dur)
        if getRole(localPlayer) == "killer" then return end
        if STATE.fpBuffs[name] then return end
        if tick() - STATE.fpLastEnd < (STATE.fpCooldown or 10) and next(STATE.fpBuffs) == nil then return end
        STATE.fpBuffs[name] = { amt = amt, endTime = tick() + dur }
        if name == "PerfectLanding" then STATE.fpPLAnnounced = false end
        fpApply()
        Notify("Fake Perks", "[" .. name .. "] Aktif! +" .. math.floor(amt / 14 * 100) .. "% Speed (" .. dur .. "s)", 3)
    end

    local function fpDisconnect(name)
        local list = STATE.fpConns[name]
        if list then
            for _, c in ipairs(list) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
            STATE.fpConns[name] = nil
        end
    end

    local function fpReg(name, conn)
        if conn == nil then return end
        if not STATE.fpConns[name] then STATE.fpConns[name] = {} end
        table.insert(STATE.fpConns[name], conn)
    end

    local function fpHookCharAdded(name, fn)
        fpReg(name, localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(fn))
        local cur = fpChar()
        if cur then pcall(fn, cur) end
    end

    -- ============================================================
    --  PERFECT LANDING vs VAULT
    -- ============================================================

    function FP_MarkVault()
        STATE.fpVaultUntil = tick() + (STATE.fpVaultWindow or 1)
    end

    function FP_IsVaultNow()
        if (STATE.fpVaultUntil or 0) > tick() then return true end
        local char = localPlayer.Character
        if not char then return true end
        local ci = char:FindFirstChild("CheckInterrable")
        if ci and ci:GetAttribute("isVaulting") then return true end
        if IsPlayerDoingAction() then return true end
        if char:GetAttribute("IsHooked") or char:GetAttribute("IsCarried") then return true end
        return false
    end

    if not STATE.fpVaultHooked then
        STATE.fpVaultHooked = true
        pcall(function()
            local r = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
            local w = r and r:FindFirstChild("Window")
            local ve = w and w:FindFirstChild("VaultEvent")
            if ve and ve.OnClientEvent then
                STATE.fpVaultConn = ve.OnClientEvent:Connect(function()
                    STATE.fpVaultUntil = tick() + (STATE.fpVaultWindow or 1)
                end)
            end
        end)
    end

    function FP_SetPerk(name, on)
        if name == "Flowstate" then STATE.fpOn.Flowstate = nil; return end
        STATE.fpOn[name] = on and true or false
        if not on then
            fpDisconnect(name)
            STATE.fpBuffs[name] = nil
            if name == "PerfectLanding" then STATE.fpPLAnnounced = false end
            fpApply()
            return
        end
        if name == "QuickRecovery" then
            local function onVault(_, sprintFlag)
                if not STATE.fpOn.QuickRecovery then return end
                if sprintFlag ~= true then return end
                FP_TryBuff("QuickRecovery", 5.6, 3)
            end
            pcall(function()
                local r = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
                local w = r and r:FindFirstChild("Window")
                local vb = w and w:FindFirstChild("Vaultbindable")
                if vb and vb:IsA("BindableEvent") then fpReg(name, vb.Event:Connect(onVault)) end
            end)
        elseif name == "PerfectLanding" then
            fpHookCharAdded(name, function(ch)
                local hum = ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if not hum then return end
                local root = ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                STATE.fpWasFalling = false
                STATE.fpFallStart = 0
                STATE.fpFallY = 0
                fpReg(name, hum.StateChanged:Connect(function(_, new)
                    if not STATE.fpOn.PerfectLanding then return end
                    if new == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall then
                        STATE.fpWasFalling = true
                        STATE.fpFallStart = tick()
                        STATE.fpFallY = root and root.Position.Y or 0
                    elseif STATE.fpWasFalling and (new == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed
                        or new == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) then
                        STATE.fpWasFalling = false
                        if FP_IsVaultNow() then return end
                        if tick() - (STATE.fpFallStart or 0) < (STATE.fpMinFallTime or 0.25) then return end
                        if root then
                            local drop = (STATE.fpFallY or root.Position.Y) - root.Position.Y
                            if drop < (STATE.fpMinFallDrop or 3) then return end
                        end
                        FP_TryBuff("PerfectLanding", 5.6, 3)
                    elseif STATE.fpWasFalling and (new == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Swimming
                        or new == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Climbing
                        or new == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics
                        or new == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Seated) then
                        STATE.fpWasFalling = false
                    end
                end))
            end)
        elseif name == "AdrenalineRush" then
            fpHookCharAdded(name, function(ch)
                local hum = ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if not hum then return end
                local lastHP = hum.Health
                fpReg(name, hum.HealthChanged:Connect(function(newHP)
                    if STATE.fpOn.AdrenalineRush and newHP < lastHP and newHP <= 50 and newHP > 0 then
                        FP_TryBuff("AdrenalineRush", 4, 5)
                    end
                    lastHP = newHP
                end))
            end)
        end
    end

    function FP_ReArm()
        for name, on in pairs(STATE.fpOn or {}) do
            if on then
                pcall(FP_SetPerk, name, false)
                pcall(FP_SetPerk, name, true)
            end
        end
    end

    RegisterTask("FakePerks", 0.1, function()
        if next(STATE.fpBuffs) == nil then return end
        local now = tick()
        local expired = false
        for pname, b in pairs(STATE.fpBuffs) do
            if now >= b.endTime then
                STATE.fpBuffs[pname] = nil
                expired = true
            end
        end
        if expired and next(STATE.fpBuffs) == nil then
            STATE.fpLastEnd = now
        end
        local pl = STATE.fpBuffs.PerfectLanding
        if pl and now < pl.endTime then
            local char = localPlayer.Character
            if char then
                pcall(function()
                    if char:GetAttribute("MovementLocked") then
                        char:SetAttribute("MovementLocked", false)
                        if not STATE.fpPLAnnounced then
                            STATE.fpPLAnnounced = true
                            print("[FP] MovementLocked ditahan OFF (PerfectLanding)")
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
        fpApply()
    end)
end

-- ============================================================
--  SELF HEAL & BANDAGE PROGRESS BOOST
-- ============================================================
local HEAL_SelfHealOnce, HEAL_BoostBandage, HEAL_SetNoAnim, HEAL_Abort, HEAL_SetSessionEndHook
do
    local HEAL_STOP_PCT   = 95
    local HEAL_DONE_PCT   = 100
    local HEAL_MAX_WAIT   = 8

    local PHASE_IDLE, PHASE_HEAL, PHASE_BANDAGE = "idle", "heal", "bandage"

    local phase       = PHASE_IDLE
    local runChar     = nil
    local healLatched = false
    local bandageFired = false
    local bandageArg  = nil
    local runStart    = 0
    local conns       = {}
    local attrConn    = nil
    local boostMode   = false
    local sawProgress = false
    local HEAL_Tick

    local noAnimEnabled = false
    local healNoAnim    = false
    local healHookedFns = {}

    local function HEAL_DisconnectAll()
        if attrConn then
            pcall(function() attrConn:Disconnect() end)
            attrConn = nil
        end
        for i = 1, #conns do
            pcall(function() conns[i]:Disconnect() end)
        end
        table.remove(conns, 1, #conns)
    end

    local function HEAL_SendHealEvent(on)
        local char = runChar or localPlayer.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not hrp then return false end
        local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        local healing = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Healing")
        local healEvent = healing and healing:FindFirstChild("HealEvent")
        if not healEvent then return false end
        return (pcall(function() healEvent:FireServer(hrp, on) end))
    end

    local function HEAL_ClearRun()
        phase = PHASE_IDLE
        runChar = nil
        healLatched = false
        bandageFired = false
        bandageArg = nil
        runStart = 0
        sawProgress = false
    end

    local sessionEndCb = nil

    local function HEAL_Reset()
        HEAL_DisconnectAll()
        boostMode = false
        HEAL_ClearRun()
        healNoAnim = false
        if sessionEndCb then pcall(sessionEndCb) end
    end

    local function HEAL_InstallNoAnimHook()
        pcall(function()
            local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
            local healing = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Healing")
            if not healing then return end
            for _, name in ipairs({ "HealAnim", "HealAnimRec" }) do
                local ev = healing:FindFirstChild(name)
                if ev then
                    for _, conn in ipairs(getconnections(ev.OnClientEvent)) do
                        local fn = conn.Function
                        if fn and not healHookedFns[fn] and hookfunction then
                            healHookedFns[fn] = true
                            local old
                            old = hookfunction(fn, function(arg)
                                if healNoAnim and arg then
                                    return
                                end
                                return old(arg)
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    function HEAL_SetNoAnim(v)
        noAnimEnabled = v and true or false
        if noAnimEnabled then
            if not hookfunction then
                Notify("No-Anim Heal", "Executor tidak support hookfunction.", 3)
                noAnimEnabled = false
                return
            end
            HEAL_InstallNoAnimHook()
        else
            healNoAnim = false
        end
    end

    local function HEAL_ReadProgress()
        local char = runChar or localPlayer.Character
        local v = nil
        if char then v = char:GetAttribute("HealProgress") end
        if type(v) ~= "number" then v = localPlayer:GetAttribute("HealProgress") end
        if type(v) ~= "number" then return nil end
        return v
    end

    local function HEAL_EquippedItemName()
        local char = runChar or localPlayer.Character
        if not char then return nil end
        local attr = char:GetAttribute("EquippedItem") or char:GetAttribute("Equippedltem")
        if attr == nil then
            attr = localPlayer:GetAttribute("EquippedItem") or localPlayer:GetAttribute("Equippedltem")
        end
        if type(attr) == "string" then return attr end
        if typeof(attr) == "Instance" then return attr.Name end
        return nil
    end

    local function HEAL_IsBandageEquipped()
        local name = HEAL_EquippedItemName()
        if type(name) ~= "string" then return false end
        return name:lower() == "bandage"
    end

    local function HEAL_BlockReason()
        local char = runChar or localPlayer.Character
        if not char then return "Character not found" end
        if char:GetAttribute("IsDead") then return "Kamu sudah mati" end
        if char:GetAttribute("IsHooked") then return "Kamu sedang hooked" end
        if char:GetAttribute("IsCarried") then return "Kamu sedang di-carry" end
        if CollectionService:HasTag(char, "Silenced") then return "Kamu sedang Silenced" end
        if CollectionService:HasTag(char, "Antiheal") then return "Kamu sedang Antiheal" end
        local ci = char:FindFirstChild("CheckInterractable")
        if ci then
            for _, attr in ipairs({
                "isVaulting", "isSliding", "isDroppingPallet",
                "isRepairing", "isHealing", "isUnhooking", "isExiting",
            }) do
                if ci:GetAttribute(attr) then
                    return "SedangBusy: " .. attr
                end
            end
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function HEAL_ResolveBandageArg()
        local char = runChar or localPlayer.Character
        if not char then return nil, nil end
        local tool = char:FindFirstChild("Bandage")
        if tool and tool:IsA("Tool") then
            local charge = tool:GetAttribute("charge")
            if type(charge) == "number" and charge <= 0 then
                return nil, "Bandage kehabisan charge"
            end
            return tool, nil
        end
        local arm = tool and tool:FindFirstChild("Right Arm")
        local mesh = arm and arm:FindFirstChild("Bandage")
        if mesh then return mesh, nil end
        return nil, "Bandage tidak ter-equip"
    end

    local function HEAL_SendBandageEvent(on, arg)
        local remotes = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("Remotes")
        local items = remotes and remotes:FindFirstChild("Items")
        local band = items and items:FindFirstChild("Bandage")
        local fire = band and band:FindFirstChild("Fire")
        if not fire then return false end
        return (pcall(function() fire:FireServer(on, arg) end))
    end

    local function HEAL_StartHealPhase(title)
        local char = localPlayer.Character
        if not char or not char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            return false
        end
        if getRole(localPlayer) == "killer" then
            Notify(title, "Fitur ini hanya untuk Survivor.", 2)
            return false
        end
        HEAL_DisconnectAll()
        HEAL_ClearRun()
        runChar = char
        runStart = tick()
        phase = PHASE_HEAL

        attrConn = char:GetAttributeChangedSignal("HealProgress"):Connect(function()
            HEAL_Tick()
        end)
        table.insert(conns, attrConn)

        if not HEAL_SendHealEvent(true) then
            HEAL_Reset()
            return false
        end
        healLatched = true
        return true
    end

    local function HEAL_FireBandageOnce(title)
        local reason = HEAL_BlockReason()
        if reason then
            Notify(title, "Boost bandage gagal: " .. reason, 3)
            return false
        end
        if not HEAL_IsBandageEquipped() then
            Notify(title, "Equip Item Bandage dulu sebelum pakai Boost Progress.", 3)
            return false
        end
        local arg, err = HEAL_ResolveBandageArg()
        if not arg then
            Notify(title, "Boost bandage gagal: " .. tostring(err), 3)
            return false
        end
        if not HEAL_SendBandageEvent(true, arg) then
            return false
        end
        bandageArg = arg
        bandageFired = true
        phase = PHASE_BANDAGE
        runStart = tick()
        return true
    end

    function HEAL_Tick()
        if phase == PHASE_IDLE then return end
        local char = runChar
        if not char or char ~= localPlayer.Character or not char.Parent then
            if healLatched then HEAL_SendHealEvent(false) end
            if bandageFired then HEAL_SendBandageEvent(false, bandageArg) end
            HEAL_Reset()
            return
        end
        local p = HEAL_ReadProgress()
        local elapsed = tick() - runStart
        if p and p > 0 then sawProgress = true end

        if phase == PHASE_HEAL then
            if (p and p >= HEAL_STOP_PCT) then
                if healLatched then HEAL_SendHealEvent(false) end
                healLatched = false
                phase = PHASE_IDLE
                if boostMode then
                    HEAL_FireBandageOnce("Boost Bandage")
                else
                    HEAL_Reset()
                end
            elseif elapsed > HEAL_MAX_WAIT and not sawProgress then
                if healLatched then HEAL_SendHealEvent(false) end
                HEAL_Reset()
            end
            return
        end

        if phase == PHASE_BANDAGE then
            if (p and p >= HEAL_DONE_PCT) or elapsed > HEAL_MAX_WAIT then
                if bandageFired then HEAL_SendBandageEvent(false, bandageArg) end
                HEAL_Reset()
            end
        end
    end

    function HEAL_SelfHealOnce()
        boostMode = false
        healNoAnim = noAnimEnabled
        if healNoAnim then HEAL_InstallNoAnimHook() end
        if not HEAL_StartHealPhase("Self Heal") then
            boostMode = false
            HEAL_Reset()
        end
    end

    function HEAL_BoostBandage()
        boostMode = true
        healNoAnim = noAnimEnabled
        if healNoAnim then HEAL_InstallNoAnimHook() end
        if not HEAL_StartHealPhase("Boost Bandage") then
            boostMode = false
            HEAL_Reset()
        end
    end

    function HEAL_Abort()
        if healLatched then HEAL_SendHealEvent(false) end
        if bandageFired then HEAL_SendBandageEvent(false, bandageArg) end
        HEAL_Reset()
    end

    function HEAL_SetSessionEndHook(fn)
        sessionEndCb = fn
    end

    RegisterTask("SelfHealWatch", 0.1, function()
        if phase ~= PHASE_IDLE then HEAL_Tick() end
    end)

    localPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        if phase ~= PHASE_IDLE then
            if healLatched then HEAL_SendHealEvent(false) end
            if bandageFired then HEAL_SendBandageEvent(false, bandageArg) end
            HEAL_Reset()
        end
    end)

end

-- ============================================================
--  POTATO GRAPHICS
-- ============================================================
local StartPotato, StopPotato
do
    local POTATO_BATCH = 800
    local POTATO_BUDGET = 0.008

    local ProtectedNames = {
        generator=true,generators=true,character=true,avatar=true,
        survivor=true,killer=true,window=true,vault=true,
        wood=true,wooden=true,plank=true,planks=true,
        log=true,logs=true,trunk=true,tree=true,
        equipment=true,item=true,items=true,tool=true,tools=true,
        hitbox=true,interaction=true,interact=true,prompt=true,
        attack=true,weapon=true,weapons=true,spear=true,spears=true,
    }
    local ProtectedClasses = {
        Humanoid=true,Animator=true,Animation=true,
        AnimationController=true,Tool=true,ProximityPrompt=true,
        ClickDetector=true,Weld=true,WeldConstraint=true,
        Motor6D=true,ManualWeld=true,
    }
    local KillerEffectClasses = {
        ParticleEmitter=true,Smoke=true,Fire=true,Beam=true,Sparkles=true,
    }
    local LeafNames = {
        leaf=true,leaves=true,leaflet=true,leaflets=true,
        leafmesh=true,leaf_mesh=true,leafpart=true,leaf_part=true,
        leaftexture=true,leaf_texture=true,leafcard=true,leaf_card=true,
        leafplane=true,leaf_plane=true,leafcluster=true,leaf_cluster=true,
        leafgroup=true,leaf_group=true,foliageleaf=true,foliage_leaf=true,
        treetop=true,tree_top=true,treeleaves=true,tree_leaves=true,
        leavesmesh=true,leaves_mesh=true,
    }
    local GrassNames = {
        grass=true,grassblade=true,grass_blade=true,
    }

    local ProtectedKeywords, LeafKeywords, GrassKeywords = {},{},{}
    for k in pairs(ProtectedNames) do ProtectedKeywords[#ProtectedKeywords+1] = k end
    for k in pairs(LeafNames) do LeafKeywords[#LeafKeywords+1] = k end
    for k in pairs(GrassNames) do GrassKeywords[#GrassKeywords+1] = k end

    local function lower(v)
        return string.lower(tostring(v))
    end

    local function contains(name, list)
        name = lower(name)
        for i = 1, #list do
            if string.find(name, list[i], 1, true) then
                return true
            end
        end
        return false
    end

    local EndingLiteKeywords = {
        "endscreen", "endscreencutscene", "cutsceneend",
        "cutsceneend2", "cutsceneendwithownchar", "endgame", "end_screen",
    }

    local function isEndingObjectLite(obj)
        if not obj then return false end
        local c = obj
        for _ = 1, 4 do
            if not c then break end
            if contains(c.Name, EndingLiteKeywords) then return true end
            c = c.Parent
        end
        return false
    end

    local ExitFogKeywords = {
        "fog", "mist", "haze", "smoke", "steam", "vapor",
    }

    local function isExitFogEffect(obj)
        if not obj then return false end
        if not (obj:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or obj:IsA("Smoke") or obj:IsA("Beam")) then
            return false
        end
        local c = obj
        for _ = 1, 12 do
            if not c then break end
            local name = lower(c.Name)
            if string.find(name, "exit", 1, true)
                or string.find(name, "escape", 1, true)
                or string.find(name, "door", 1, true) then
                if contains(name, ExitFogKeywords) then return true end
            end
            if contains(name, ExitFogKeywords) then
                local parent = c.Parent
                for _ = 1, 8 do
                    if not parent then break end
                    local parentName = lower(parent.Name)
                    if string.find(parentName, "exit", 1, true)
                        or string.find(parentName, "escape", 1, true)
                        or string.find(parentName, "door", 1, true) then
                        return true
                    end
                    parent = parent.Parent
                end
            end
            c = c.Parent
        end
        return false
    end

    local function registerCharacter(c)
        if c then STATE.potatoCharRoots[c] = true end
    end

    local function unregisterCharacter(c)
        if c then
            STATE.potatoCharRoots[c] = nil
            STATE.potatoKillerChars[c] = nil
        end
    end

    local function isKillerPlayer(p)
        return p and p.Team and p.Team.Name == "killer"
    end

    local function updateKiller(p)
        if not p then return end
        local c = p.Character
        if c then
            if isKillerPlayer(p) then
                STATE.potatoKillerChars[c] = true
            else
                STATE.potatoKillerChars[c] = nil
            end
        end
    end

    local function setupPlayer(p)
        if not p then return end
        if p.Character then
            registerCharacter(p.Character)
            updateKiller(p)
        end
        table.insert(STATE.potatoConns, p.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c)
            registerCharacter(c)
            task.defer(function() updateKiller(p) end)
        end))
        table.insert(STATE.potatoConns, p.CharacterRemoving:Connect(function(c)
            unregisterCharacter(c)
        end))
        table.insert(STATE.potatoConns, p:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Team"):Connect(function()
            updateKiller(p)
        end))
    end

    local function isInsideCharacter(obj)
        local c = obj
        for _ = 1, 20 do
            if not c then return false end
            if STATE.potatoCharRoots[c] then return true end
            c = c.Parent
        end
        return false
    end

    local function isKillerEffect(obj)
        if not obj or not KillerEffectClasses[obj.ClassName] then return false end
        local c = obj
        for _ = 1, 20 do
            if not c then return false end
            if STATE.potatoKillerChars[c] then return true end
            c = c.Parent
        end
        return false
    end

    local function isPallet(obj)
        local c = obj
        for _ = 1, 20 do
            if not c then return false end
            if string.find(lower(c.Name), "pallet", 1, true) then return true end
            c = c.Parent
        end
        return false
    end

    local function getPalletRoot(obj)
        if not obj then return nil end
        local c = obj
        for _ = 1, 20 do
            if not c then return nil end
            if string.find(lower(c.Name), "pallet", 1, true) then return c end
            c = c.Parent
        end
        return nil
    end

    local function isLeaf(obj)
        if not obj then return false end
        if contains(obj.Name, LeafKeywords) then return true end
        local p = obj.Parent
        for _ = 1, 4 do
            if not p then break end
            if contains(p.Name, LeafKeywords) then return true end
            p = p.Parent
        end
        return false
    end

    local function isProtected(obj)
        if not obj then return true end
        if ProtectedClasses[obj.ClassName] then return true end
        if isInsideCharacter(obj) then return true end
        if isPallet(obj) then return false end
        local c = obj
        for _ = 1, 7 do
            if not c then break end
            if contains(c.Name, ProtectedKeywords) then return true end
            c = c.Parent
        end
        return false
    end

    local function isCutsceneNow()
        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        return camera and camera.CameraType == Enum.CameraType.Scriptable
    end

    local function processPallet(obj)
        if not obj or not obj.Parent then return end
        if obj:IsA("MeshPart") then
            pcall(function()
                obj.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
                obj.CastShadow = false
                obj.TextureID = ""
            end)
        elseif obj:IsA("BasePart") then
            pcall(function()
                obj.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
                obj.CastShadow = false
            end)
        elseif obj:IsA("Decal") or obj:IsA("Texture") then
            pcall(function() obj.Transparency = 1 end)
        elseif obj:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") then
            pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end)
        elseif obj:IsA("SpecialMesh") then
            pcall(function() obj.TextureId = "" end)
        end
    end

    local function processLeaf(obj)
        if not obj or not obj.Parent then return end
        if ProtectedClasses[obj.ClassName] then return end
        if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
            pcall(function()
                obj.Transparency = 1
                obj.CanCollide = false
                obj.CanTouch = false
                obj.CanQuery = false
            end)
        elseif obj:IsA("Decal") or obj:IsA("Texture") then
            pcall(function() obj.Transparency = 1 end)
        elseif obj:IsA("SpecialMesh") then
            pcall(function() obj.TextureId = "" end)
        elseif obj:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") then
            pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end)
        elseif obj:IsA("ParticleEmitter")
            or obj:IsA("Beam")
            or obj:IsA("Smoke")
            or obj:IsA("Fire")
            or obj:IsA("Sparkles") then
            pcall(function() obj.Enabled = false end)
        end
    end

    local endingHold = false

    local function process(obj)
        if not obj or not obj.Parent then return end
        if STATE.potatoProcessed[obj] then return end
        if isEndingObjectLite(obj) then
            STATE.potatoProcessed[obj] = true
            return
        end
        if isExitFogEffect(obj) then
            pcall(function() obj.Enabled = false end)
            STATE.potatoProcessed[obj] = true
            return
        end
        if isCutsceneNow() then return end
        if isPallet(obj) then
            local palletRoot = getPalletRoot(obj)
            if palletRoot then
                STATE.potatoPallets[palletRoot] = true
                processPallet(obj)
                STATE.potatoProcessed[obj] = true
                return
            end
        end
        if isKillerEffect(obj) then
            pcall(function() obj.Enabled = false end)
            STATE.potatoProcessed[obj] = true
            return
        end
        if isInsideCharacter(obj) then
            STATE.potatoProcessed[obj] = true
            return
        end
        if ProtectedClasses[obj.ClassName] then
            STATE.potatoProcessed[obj] = true
            return
        end
        if isLeaf(obj) then
            processLeaf(obj)
            STATE.potatoProcessed[obj] = true
            return
        end
        if isProtected(obj) then
            STATE.potatoProcessed[obj] = true
            return
        end
        if contains(obj.Name, GrassKeywords) then
            STATE.potatoProcessed[obj] = true
            return
        end
        if obj:IsA("BasePart") then
            pcall(function()
                obj.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
                obj.CastShadow = false
            end)
            STATE.potatoProcessed[obj] = true
            return
        end
        if obj:IsA("Decal") or obj:IsA("Texture") then
            local p = obj.Parent
            if not p or not contains(p.Name, ProtectedKeywords) then
                pcall(function() obj.Transparency = 1 end)
            end
            STATE.potatoProcessed[obj] = true
            return
        end
        if obj:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") then
            local p = obj.Parent
            if p and isLeaf(p) then
                pcall(function() obj:Destroy() end)
            end
            STATE.potatoProcessed[obj] = true
            return
        end
        if obj:IsA("SpecialMesh") then
            local p = obj.Parent
            if p and isLeaf(p) then
                pcall(function() obj.TextureId = "" end)
            end
            STATE.potatoProcessed[obj] = true
            return
        end
        STATE.potatoProcessed[obj] = true
    end

    local function validRoot(root)
        if not root then return false end
        if root == localPlayer.Character then return false end
        if STATE.potatoCharRoots[root] then return false end
        if root:IsA("Camera") or root:IsA("Terrain") then return false end
        return root:IsA("Model")
            or root:IsA("Folder")
            or (root:IsA("BasePart") and root.Parent == workspace)
    end

    local function queueObject(obj)
        if not STATE.potatoRunning or not obj or not obj.Parent then return end
        if endingHold then
            STATE.potatoProcessed[obj] = true
            return
        end
        if STATE.potatoProcessed[obj] or STATE.potatoQSet[obj] then return end
        if isEndingObjectLite(obj) then
            STATE.potatoProcessed[obj] = true
            return
        end
        if isExitFogEffect(obj) then
            pcall(function() obj.Enabled = false end)
            STATE.potatoProcessed[obj] = true
            return
        end
        if ProtectedClasses[obj.ClassName] then return end
        if isInsideCharacter(obj) and not isKillerEffect(obj) then return end
        STATE.potatoQTail = STATE.potatoQTail + 1
        STATE.potatoQueue[STATE.potatoQTail] = obj
        STATE.potatoQSet[obj] = true
    end

    local function resetQueueIfEmpty()
        if STATE.potatoQHead > STATE.potatoQTail then
            STATE.potatoQueue = {}
            STATE.potatoQHead = 1
            STATE.potatoQTail = 0
            STATE.potatoQSet = {}
        end
    end

    local function wipePotatoWork()
        STATE.potatoQueue = {}
        STATE.potatoQHead, STATE.potatoQTail, STATE.potatoQSet = 1, 0, {}
        STATE.potatoRoots = {}
        STATE.potatoCursorList = nil
        STATE.potatoCursorIndex = 1
    end

    local function tripEndingHold()
        if endingHold then return end
        endingHold = true
        wipePotatoWork()
    end

    local function isEndScreenSignal(obj)
        return obj ~= nil and obj.Name == "endscreen"
            and obj.Parent ~= nil and obj.Parent.Name == "Map"
    end

    local function budgetLeft(start, count)
        if count >= POTATO_BATCH then return false end
        if os.clock() - start >= POTATO_BUDGET then return false end
        if isCutsceneNow() then return false end
        return true
    end

    function StartPotato()
        if STATE.potatoRunning then return end
        STATE.potatoRunning = true
        endingHold = false
        pcall(function()
            local map = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
            if map and map:FindFirstChild("endscreen") then
                tripEndingHold()
            end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            STATE.potatoOrigQuality = settings().Rendering.QualityLevel
            settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = Enum.QualityLevel.Level01
        end)
        pcall(function()
            local L = game:GetService("Lighting")
            STATE.potatoOrigShadows = L.GlobalShadows
            STATE.potatoOrigFogEnd = L.FogEnd
            L.GlobalShadows = false
            L.FogEnd = 1000000
        end)
        STATE.potatoOrigPostFx = {}
        pcall(function()
            for _, obj in ipairs(game:GetService("Lighting"):GetChildren()) do
                if obj:IsA("PostEffect") and obj.Enabled then
                    obj.Enabled = false
                    STATE.potatoOrigPostFx[#STATE.potatoOrigPostFx + 1] = obj
                end
            end
        end)
        for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do setupPlayer(p) end
        table.insert(STATE.potatoConns, Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(setupPlayer))
        table.insert(STATE.potatoConns, Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(p)
            if p.Character then unregisterCharacter(p.Character) end
        end))
        table.insert(STATE.potatoConns, workspace.ChildAdded:Connect(function(root)
            if not STATE.potatoRunning then return end
            if endingHold then return end
            if isEndingObjectLite(root) then return end
            if validRoot(root) then
                STATE.potatoRoots[#STATE.potatoRoots + 1] = root
            end
        end))
        table.insert(STATE.potatoConns, workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
            if not STATE.potatoRunning or not obj or not obj.Parent then return end
            if isEndScreenSignal(obj) then
                tripEndingHold()
                return
            end
            if endingHold then
                STATE.potatoProcessed[obj] = true
                return
            end
            if STATE.potatoProcessed[obj] or STATE.potatoQSet[obj] then return end
            if isEndingObjectLite(obj) then
                STATE.potatoProcessed[obj] = true
                return
            end
            if isExitFogEffect(obj) then
                pcall(function() obj.Enabled = false end)
                STATE.potatoProcessed[obj] = true
                return
            end
            if isInsideCharacter(obj) and not isKillerEffect(obj) then return end
            if ProtectedClasses[obj.ClassName] then return end
            queueObject(obj)
        end))
        pcall(function()
            for _, root in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
                if validRoot(root) then
                    STATE.potatoRoots[#STATE.potatoRoots + 1] = root
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    function StopPotato()
        STATE.potatoRunning = false
        endingHold = false
        for _, conn in ipairs(STATE.potatoConns) do
            pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
        end
        STATE.potatoConns = {}
        STATE.potatoRoots = {}
        STATE.potatoCursorList = nil
        STATE.potatoCursorIndex = 1
        STATE.potatoQueue = {}
        STATE.potatoQHead, STATE.potatoQTail, STATE.potatoQSet = 1, 0, {}
        STATE.potatoCharRoots = {}
        STATE.potatoKillerChars = {}
        pcall(function()
            if STATE.potatoOrigQuality then
                settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = STATE.potatoOrigQuality
            end
        end)
        pcall(function()
            local L = game:GetService("Lighting")
            if STATE.potatoOrigShadows ~= nil then L.GlobalShadows = STATE.potatoOrigShadows end
            if STATE.potatoOrigFogEnd ~= nil then L.FogEnd = STATE.potatoOrigFogEnd end
            for _, obj in ipairs(STATE.potatoOrigPostFx) do
                if obj and obj.Parent then obj.Enabled = true end
            end
        end)
        STATE.potatoOrigPostFx = {}
    end

    RegisterTask("PotatoStream", 0, function()
        if not STATE.potatoRunning then return end
        if endingHold then return end
        if isCutsceneNow() then return end
        local start = os.clock()
        local count = 0
        while STATE.potatoQHead <= STATE.potatoQTail and budgetLeft(start, count) do
            local obj = STATE.potatoQueue[STATE.potatoQHead]
            STATE.potatoQueue[STATE.potatoQHead] = nil
            STATE.potatoQHead = STATE.potatoQHead + 1
            if obj then STATE.potatoQSet[obj] = nil end
            if obj and obj.Parent then process(obj) end
            count = count + 1
        end
        resetQueueIfEmpty()
        if STATE.potatoCursorList == nil and #STATE.potatoRoots > 0 and budgetLeft(start, count) then
            local root = table.remove(STATE.potatoRoots, 1)
            if root and root.Parent and validRoot(root) then
                local ok, objs = pcall(function() return root:GetDescendants() end)
                if ok and objs then
                    STATE.potatoCursorList = objs
                    STATE.potatoCursorIndex = 1
                end
            end
        end
        while STATE.potatoCursorList ~= nil and budgetLeft(start, count) do
            local list = STATE.potatoCursorList
            local idx = STATE.potatoCursorIndex
            local obj = list[idx]
            STATE.potatoCursorIndex = idx + 1
            if idx > #list then
                STATE.potatoCursorList = nil
                STATE.potatoCursorIndex = 1
            elseif obj and obj.Parent then
                process(obj)
            end
            count = count + 1
        end
    end)
end

-- ============================================================
--  START MASTER HEARTBEAT LOOP
-- ============================================================
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
    for i = 1, #MasterTasks do
        local t = MasterTasks[i]
        t.timer = t.timer + dt
        if t.timer >= t.interval then
            t.timer = 0
            t.fn(dt)
        end
    end
end)

-- ============================================================
--  UNHOOK YOURSELF SYSTEM
-- ============================================================
local UnhookToggle
local UnhookMaster
do
    local UNHOOK_ENABLED = false
    local UNHOOK_ARMED = false
    local UNHOOK_CONN
    local UNHOOK_HOOK_CF
    local SelfUnHookEvent = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes"):WaitForChild("Carry"):WaitForChild("SelfUnHookEvent")
    local RepairEvent = ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Remotes"):WaitForChild("Generator"):WaitForChild("RepairEvent")

    local function UnhookNearestHook(pos)
        local best = nil
        local bestD = math.huge
        for _, item in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
            if item.Name == "Hook" and (item:IsA("Model") or item:IsA("BasePart")) and item.Parent then
                local ok, cf = pcall(function()
                    if item:IsA("Model") then return item:GetPivot() end
                    return item.CFrame
                end)
                if ok and cf then
                    local dist = (cf.Position - pos).Magnitude
                    if dist < bestD then
                        bestD = dist
                        best = cf
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return best
    end

    local function UnhookRestore(hrp, char)
        if UNHOOK_CONN then
            UNHOOK_CONN:Disconnect()
            UNHOOK_CONN = nil
        end
        UNHOOK_ARMED = false
        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        if not UNHOOK_HOOK_CF then
            char:PivotTo(hrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 15, 0))
        else
            char:PivotTo(UNHOOK_HOOK_CF * CFrame.new(0, 3, 0))
        end
        task.wait(0.1)
        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
    end

    local function UnhookTrack()
        if localPlayer.Character then
            local killer = nil
            for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                local isKiller = p.Team
                if isKiller then
                    isKiller = (p.Team.Name == "Killer")
                end
                if isKiller then
                    killer = p
                    break
                end
            end
            if killer then
                if UNHOOK_CONN then
                    UNHOOK_CONN:Disconnect()
                    UNHOOK_CONN = nil
                end
                UNHOOK_CONN = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
                    local char = localPlayer.Character
                    local hrp = char
                    if hrp then
                        hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    end
                    local skip = not UNHOOK_ARMED
                    if not skip then
                        skip = not char
                    end
                    if not skip then
                        skip = not hrp
                    end
                    if not skip then
                        if char:GetAttribute("IsHooked") then
                            local kchar = killer.Character
                            local khrp = kchar
                            if khrp then
                                khrp = kchar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                            end
                            if khrp then
                                local anticamp = char:GetAttribute("anticampCharge")
                                if not anticamp then
                                    anticamp = 0
                                end
                                if not (anticamp >= 100) then
                                    hrp.CFrame = khrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -15, 10)
                                    return
                                else
                                    UnhookRestore(hrp, char)
                                    task.wait(0.9)
                                    SelfUnHookEvent:FireServer()
                                    return
                                end
                            else
                                UNHOOK_ARMED = false
                                return
                            end
                        else
                            UnhookRestore(hrp, char)
                            return
                        end
                    else
                        if UNHOOK_CONN then
                            UNHOOK_CONN:Disconnect()
                            UNHOOK_CONN = nil
                        end
                        return
                    end
                end)
                return
            else
                return
            end
        else
            return
        end
    end

    UnhookMaster = function()
        UNHOOK_ENABLED = true
        UnhookToggle()
    end

    UnhookToggle = function()
        if UNHOOK_ENABLED then
            local char = localPlayer.Character
            if char then
                if char:GetAttribute("IsHooked") then
                    UNHOOK_ARMED = not UNHOOK_ARMED
                    if not UNHOOK_ARMED then
                        if UNHOOK_CONN then
                            UNHOOK_CONN:Disconnect()
                            UNHOOK_CONN = nil
                        end
                        return
                    else
                        local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                        if hrp then
                            UNHOOK_HOOK_CF = UnhookNearestHook(hrp.Position)
                            task.wait(1)
                            local skip = not UNHOOK_ARMED
                            if not skip then
                                skip = not char:GetAttribute("IsHooked")
                            end
                            if not skip then
                                local gen = nil
                                for _, d in ipairs(Workspace:GetDescendants()) do
                                    local m = d.Name:match("^GeneratorPoint")
                                    if m then
                                        m = d:IsA("BasePart")
                                    end
                                    if m then
                                        gen = d
                                        break
                                    end
                                end
                                if gen then
                                    hrp.CFrame = gen.CFrame + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
                                    task.wait(0.1)
                                    RepairEvent:FireServer(gen, true)
                                    task.wait(0.25)
                                    RepairEvent:FireServer(gen, false)
                                    task.wait(0.1)
                                    UnhookTrack()
                                end
                                return
                            else
                                return
                            end
                        else
                            return
                        end
                    end
                else
                    return
                end
            else
                return
            end
        else
            return
        end
    end
end

    CVSection:AddToggle({

            CONFIG.cameraVeilEnabled = v

            if not ok then
                CONFIG.cameraVeilEnabled = false
                Notify("Camera Veil", "Gagal mengaktifkan Camera Veil", 2)
            end
        end,
    })
    CVSection:AddToggle({
        Title = "SnapLine ESP", Default = false,
        Callback = function(v)
            CONFIG.cameraVeilSnapLine = v
            if not v then
                if STATE.CV_SnapLine then STATE.CV_SnapLine.Visible = false end
                if STATE.CV_SnapDot then STATE.CV_SnapDot.Visible = false end
            end
        end,
    })
end

    AbyssRow:AddButton({
        Title = "Fire Slash (Abyss)",
        Callback = function()
            pcall(function()
                local Event = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes.Attacks.BasicAttack
                Event:FireServer(true)
            end)
        end

    -- === MAYERS (STALKER) NO COOLDOWN ===
    local MayersSection = KillerTab:AddSection("Mayers (Stalker) No Cooldown", nil)
    MayersSection:AddToggle({
        Title = "Enable Mayers No Cooldown",
        Content = "PC: Q | Mobile: Button Jambak",
        Default = false,
