    })
    local ObjColorRow3 = ObjESPSection:AddHStack()
    ObjColorRow3:AddColorPicker({
        Title = "Gate Color", Default = CONFIG.espGateColor,
        Callback = function(color)
            CONFIG.espGateColor = color
            for _, gate in ipairs(STATE.cachedMapObjects.Gates) do
                if gate and gate.Parent then
                    local h = gate:FindFirstChild("__BolongHL__")
                    if h then h.FillColor = color; h.OutlineColor = color end
                end
            end
        end,
    })
    local PredSection = VisualTab:AddSection("Prediction Map&Killer", nil)
    PredSection:AddToggle({
        Title = "Show Prediction Monitor",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(v)
            if v then StartPrediction() else StopPrediction() end
        end,
    })
    local WLD = VisualTab:AddSection("World Settings", nil)
    WLD:AddToggle({
        Title = "Fullbright", Default = false,
        Callback = function(v) 
            STATE.fullbrightEnabled = v
            if v then applyFullbright() else restoreLighting() end 
        end,
    })
    WLD:AddToggle({
        Title = "Remove Visual Effects", Content = "BoostFps", Default = false,
        Callback = function(v) 
            STATE.removeVfxEnabled = v
            if v then removeVisualEffects() else restoreVisualEffects() end 
        end,
    })
end

do
    -- === MiscTab ===
    local PotatoSection = MiscTab:AddSection("Potato Graphics By Ruv", nil)
    PotatoSection:AddParagraph({
        Title = "Potato Graphics",
        Content = "by ruvlegacys.",
    })
    PotatoSection:AddButton({
        Title = "Apply Potato Graphics",
        Callback = function()
            if STATE.potatoRunning then
                Notify("Potato Graphics", "Sudah aktif", 1.5)
                return
            end
            CONFIG.potatoEnabled = true
            StartPotato()
            Notify("Potato Graphics", "Potato mode diterapkan", 2)
        end,
    })
    local AntiAfkSection = MiscTab:AddSection("Anti AFK", nil)
    AntiAfkSection:AddToggle({
        Title = "Enable Anti AFK",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(v)
            ToggleAntiAfk(v)
        end,
    })
    local GS = MiscTab:AddSection("Killer Warn", nil)
    GS:AddToggle({
        Title = "Enable Killer Warn",
        Content = "The ! sign above your head when the killer is around you",
        Default = CONFIG.warnEnabled,
        Callback = function(v) CONFIG.warnEnabled = v end,
    })
    local STS = MiscTab:AddSection("Stun Timer", nil)
    STS:AddToggle({
        Title = "Enable Stun Timer",
        Content = "Show Stun x.xs countdown above stunned Killer head",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(v)
            if v then StartStunTimer() else StopStunTimer() end
        end,
    })
    STS:AddSlider({
        Title = "Max Distance",
        Min = 50, Max = 500, Default = 250, Increment = 10,
        Callback = function(v)
            STATE.stunTimerMaxDist = v
            for _, bb in pairs(STATE.stunTimerBoards) do
                pcall(function() bb.MaxDistance = v end)
            end
        end,
    })

    local PerfSection = MiscTab:AddSection("Performance Monitor", nil)
    PerfSection:AddToggle({
        Title = "Show Performance Window", Default = false,
        Callback = function(v) if v then StartPerfMonitor() else StopPerfMonitor() end end,
    })
    local SpecSection = MiscTab:AddSection("Spectator Count", nil)
    SpecSection:AddToggle({
        Title = "Show Spectator Counter", Default = false,
        Callback = function(v) if v then StartSpectatorCount() else StopSpectatorCount() end end,
    })
    local CAMS = MiscTab:AddSection("Camera Zoom", nil)
    CAMS:AddToggle({
        Title = "Max Camera Zoom", Default = CONFIG.cameraZoomEnabled,
        Callback = function(v)
            CONFIG.cameraZoomEnabled = v
            if v then applyCameraZoom(CONFIG.cameraZoomValue) else restoreCameraZoom() end
        end,
    })
    local STIFFS = MiscTab:AddSection("Camera Stiffness", nil)
    STIFFS:AddToggle({
        Title = "Smooth Camera", Default = CONFIG.camStiffEnabled,
        Content = "DBD Trend Camera (Hanya Work Didalam Match.",
        Callback = function(v)
            CONFIG.camStiffEnabled = v
            if v then
                if not applyCamStiffness(CONFIG.camStiffValue) then
                end
            else
                restoreCamStiffness()
            end
        end,
    })
    STIFFS:AddSlider({
        Title = "Stiffness", Content = "Lebih kecil = lebih smooth",
        Min = 1, Max = 10, Default = 5, Increment = 0.1,
        Callback = function(v)
            CONFIG.camStiffValue = v
        end,
    })
    local ARS = MiscTab:AddSection("Aspect Ratio", nil)
    ARS:AddToggle({
        Title = "Stretched Res", Default = CONFIG.aspectEnabled,
        Callback = function(v)
            CONFIG.aspectEnabled = v
            if v then AR_Start() else AR_Stop() end
        end,
    })
    local aspectSlider
    ARS:AddDropdown({
        Title = "Preset", Options = { "Normal 16:9", "4:3 Stretched", "16:10", "21:9 Ultrawide" }, Default = "Normal 16:9",
        Callback = function(v)
            local map = { ["Normal 16:9"] = 16 / 9, ["4:3 Stretched"] = 4 / 3, ["16:10"] = 16 / 10, ["21:9 Ultrawide"] = 21 / 9 }
            local r = map[v] or (16 / 9)
            CONFIG.aspectRatio = r
            if aspectSlider then pcall(function() aspectSlider:Set(r) end) end
        end,
    })
    aspectSlider = ARS:AddSlider({
        Title = "Custom Ratio",
        Min = 0.8, Max = 2.5, Default = 16 / 9, Increment = 0.01,
        Callback = function(v)
            CONFIG.aspectRatio = v
        end,
    })
    local CursorSection = MiscTab:AddSection("Force Cursor (PC Only)", nil)
    local forceCursorToggle = CursorSection:AddToggle({
        Title = "Force Mouse Cursor",
        Content = "Forces the mouse cursor to always appear on the screen.",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(v)
            CONFIG.forceCursorEnabled = v
            if v then
                StartForceCursor()
                Notify("Force Cursor", "Mouse cursor forced to appear!", 2)
            else
                StopForceCursor()
            end
        end,
    })
    CursorSection:AddKeybind({
        Title = "Toggle Keybind",
        Default = Enum.KeyCode.Y,
        Callback = function()
            local newState = not CONFIG.forceCursorEnabled
            forceCursorToggle:Set(newState)
        end
    })
    local ProtectNameSection = MiscTab:AddSection("Protect Name", nil)
    ProtectNameSection:AddToggle({
        Title = "Enable Protect Name", Default = false,
        Callback = function(v) if v then StartProtectName() else StopProtectName() end end,
    })
    local SkipEndScreenSection = MiscTab:AddSection("Skip Cutscene", nil)
    local _skipToggleGuard = false
    local skipEndScreenToggle, skipEndDarknessToggle

    skipEndScreenToggle = SkipEndScreenSection:AddToggle({
        Title = "Skip End Screen",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(v)
            if _skipToggleGuard then return end
            CONFIG.skipEndScreenEnabled = v
            if v then
                _skipToggleGuard = true
                CONFIG.skipLoadEndScreenEnabled = false
                if skipEndDarknessToggle then skipEndDarknessToggle:Set(false) end
                _skipToggleGuard = false
                StopSkipEndScreen()
                StartSkipEndScreen(false)
            else
                StopSkipEndScreen()
            end
        end,
    })

    skipEndDarknessToggle = SkipEndScreenSection:AddToggle({
        Title = "Skip Loading & End Screen",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(v)
            if _skipToggleGuard then return end
            CONFIG.skipLoadEndScreenEnabled = v
            if v then
                _skipToggleGuard = true
                CONFIG.skipEndScreenEnabled = false
                if skipEndScreenToggle then skipEndScreenToggle:Set(false) end
                _skipToggleGuard = false
                StopSkipEndScreen()
                StartSkipEndScreen(true)
            else
                StopSkipEndScreen()
            end
        end,
    })

    -- === AVATAR COPY SECTION ===
    local AvatarSection = MiscTab:AddSection("Avatar Copy (Visual)", nil)
    AvatarSection:AddPresetManager({
        Title = "Profile Avatar By Username / ID",
        Placeholder = "Jandel / 1234...",
        Default = "", 
        Presets = {
            ["Boy1"] = "kiicaine",
            ["Boy2"] = "444jamesss",
            ["Boy3"] = "KiLouo14",
            ["Girl1"] = "9kinb",
            ["Girl2"] = "winterilous",
            ["Girl3"] = "ellea_893"
        },
        Callback = function(text)
            currentAvatarInput = text
        end
    })
    AvatarSection:AddButton({
        Title = "Copy Avatar",
        Callback = function()
            if currentAvatarInput and currentAvatarInput ~= "" then
                CopyAvatarFromUsername(currentAvatarInput)
            else
                Notify("BolongHub", "Masukkan Username/ID terlebih dahulu!", 2)
            end
        end
    })
    -- === JERK OFF SECTION ===
    local JerkSection = MiscTab:AddSection("Jerk Off (Fun)", nil)
    JerkSection:AddToggle({
        Title = "Enable Jerk Off",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(v)
            if v then
                StartJerk()
            else
                StopJerk()
            end
        end
    })
    local MoveMiscSection = MiscTab:AddSection("Noclip & Fly", nil)
    MoveMiscSection:AddToggle({
        Title = "Enable Noclip",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(v)
            if v then
                StartNoclip()
            else
                StopNoclip()
            end
        end
    })
    MoveMiscSection:AddToggle({
        Title = "Fly GUI (V3)",
        Default = false,
        Callback = function(v)
            if v then
                StartFlyGui()
            else
                StopFlyGui()
            end
        end
    })
    local ServerSection = MiscTab:AddSection("Hop Server / Respawn", nil)
    ServerSection:AddParagraph({
        Title = "Info",
        Content = "Hop: pindah ke server random.\nRejoin: masuk ulang ke server ini.\nRespawn: mati dan hidup kembali.",
    })
    ServerSection:AddButton({
        Title = "Hop Server",
        Callback = function()
            local ok = pcall(function()
                game:GetService("TeleportService"):Teleport(game.PlaceId, localPlayer)
            end)
            Notify("Server", ok and "Teleporting ke server lain..." or "Gagal hop server", 2)
        end,
    })
    ServerSection:AddButton({
        Title = "Rejoin Server",
        Callback = function()
            local ok = pcall(function()
                game:GetService("TeleportService"):TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, game.JobId, localPlayer)
            end)
            Notify("Server", ok and "Rejoin server..." or "Gagal rejoin server", 2)
        end,
    })
    ServerSection:AddButton({
        Title = "Respawn",
        Callback = function()
            local fired = false
            pcall(function()
                local allow = {
                    Respawn = true, RequestRespawn = true, Spawn = true,
                    SpawnCharacter = true, RespawnCharacter = true,
                }
                local function tryFire(obj)
                    if fired then return end
                    if allow[obj.Name]
                        and (obj:IsA("RemoteEvent") or obj:IsA("RemoteFunction")) then
                        pcall(function()
                            if obj:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
                                obj:FireServer()
                            else
                                obj:InvokeServer()
                            end
                        end)
                        fired = true
                    end
                end
                for _, container in ipairs({ game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"), workspace }) do
                    if fired then break end
                    for _, obj in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do
                        tryFire(obj)
                        if fired then break end
                    end
                end
            end)
            pcall(function()
                local char = localPlayer.Character
                local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if hum then hum.Health = 0 end
            end)
            task.spawn(function()
                local t0 = os.clock()
                while os.clock() - t0 < 6 do
                    local char = localPlayer.Character
                    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if hum and hum.Health > 0 then
                        Notify("Server", "Respawn berhasil", 2)
                        return
                    end
                    task.wait(0.5)
                end
                Notify("Server", "Karakter mati - game menolak respawn paksa, tunggu round berikutnya", 3)
            end)
        end,
    })
end

do
    -- === ConfigManagerTab ===
    local ConfigSection = ConfigManagerTab:AddSection("Configuration", true)
    ConfigSection:AddConfig()
end
end

BuildUI()

print("BOLONGHUB LOADED!")
